- Main source addendum: frontend/src/components/floating-support-chat-widget.tsx also sends via /app-api/v1/support/threads and replies; its reply text is cleared before fetch and restored on handled errors, so unknown-commit/unmount loss needs explicit recovery. The widget supports image-URL attachments; v1328 drafts are TEXT-ONLY and must not silently strip, save or claim to restore attachments. Test both /support and floating widget, plus Android parity.

## Planning v1328 — F675-139 Explicit Member Support Draft Recovery and Verified Send (Feature Addition / DRAFT / RELEASE HOLD)

### Authority, cycle, source truth and alternatives
- Previous **Feature Improvement v1326** → Current **Feature Addition v1328** → Next **Feature Improvement v1330** (provisional). Remote main at start and mid: 0fe614b2ca1a7bc0df540514e54db78130479e2a. Main moved from v1326's 1c5b84a1... to 0fe614b2ca1a7bc0df540514e54db78130479e2a at 20:51 KST after integrating other workers' runtime commits. v1326 Draft PR #1452 remains unmerged; this proposal is stacked **only on its docs branch**, is not main-merge-ready and must be reconciled with changed main before any merge. Read latest main AGENTS.md, README.md, PROJECT_PLAN.md (canonical) and PROJECT_PLAN.ko.md; the old main plan is behind the v1326 draft stack. Latest completed QA v1299 targets the old main and is **not** an exact-SHA QA result for new main. No runtime/DB/Test/Production changes.
- Evidence: latest-main frontend/src/app/support/page.tsx renders plain new-thread and reply forms; actions.ts immediately submits to POST /api/v1/support/threads and POST /api/v1/support/threads/:id/messages with random UUID keys; support.controller.ts exposes no draft endpoint; Migration196 creates support_threads and support_messages but no draft table. No confirmed server-saved drafts or send receipts for drafts. Production draft-loss frequency UNKNOWN; this is a missing capability, not a proven outage.
- Considered (A) browser localStorage autosave (fast but shared-device/PII and cross-account leakage), (B) server autosave (silent sensitive data collection), (C) **explicit owner-controlled server Save/Discard/Send** (selected: transparent consent, cross-device availability, authoritative confirmation). Feature is opt-in and default OFF; do not add passive keystroke telemetry, attachments, AI summaries, notifications, or financial effects.

### User goal, permissions, routes, screens, CTA and states
- Goal: members composing a new inquiry or reply can deliberately save text and recover it on another authenticated device without mistaking a draft for a sent message. Target: signed-in, consented member; no guest, admin acting on member's behalf, or public visibility. Entry: /support new inquiry form and existing thread reply form, with a clearly separate **Save draft**, **Discard draft**, and **Send message**. New inquiry draft includes subject <=120 Unicode characters and body <=2,000; reply draft includes body <=2,000, immutable thread UUID scope; trim and NFC normalize on save, reject empty body for send, allow explicit blank/Discard. No file uploads. At most one durable draft per (owner, NEW) and one per (owner, thread); cap 20 live drafts/member, 30-day expiry (proposed, subject to privacy approval).
- UX states: NONE, EDITING_UNSAVED, SAVING, SAVED_CONFIRMED(version, timestamp), SYNC_CONFLICT, EXPIRED, SEND_PENDING, SENT_CONFIRMED(message/thread receipt), UNKNOWN_COMMIT, DISCARDED, SIGNED_OUT, UNAVAILABLE. GET or form focus must never save; page reload does not claim unsaved text persisted. On account switch/logout/consent revocation purge client memory, ignore stale async responses, never persist raw body in localStorage, analytics, URL, log, or public cache. Send requires explicit user click, confirmed current version and owner session; no send on Save, tab close, timeout, network reconnect or admin status change. On unknown outcome show 'Check sent messages' and re-query receipt; never silently retry with a new send key. If thread was removed or access revoked, do not send; preserve owner-safe draft until explicit deletion/expiry.
- Keyboard focus returns to editor after Save; destructive Discard confirmation; 320px/200%-zoom layout, touch >=44px, screen reader live announcements for actual server acknowledgments, reduced motion, WCAG 2.2 AA. Korean/English copy must distinguish '임시 저장됨' from '전송됨'. Private page noindex/nofollow, API Cache-Control: private, no-store; no sitemap/canonical index. Android App API must expose equivalent explicit Save/Get/Discard/Send and logout fencing; no implicit background upload.

### Exact API contract (PROPOSED, not implemented)
- GET /api/v1/support/drafts?threadId=<uuid|null> returns owner-only draft with draftId, scope, subject?, body, version, savedAt, expiresAt and ETag; absent 404, expired 410, inaccessible thread indistinguishable 404. No GET writes, no default creation, no unbounded list. Query UUID validation, response max 2,500 characters.
- PUT /api/v1/support/drafts with {threadId:null|uuid, subject?:string, body:string, expectedVersion:integer|null, idempotencyKey:uuid}; CSRF + authenticated/consented server session; expectedVersion=null only first create, later If-Match/expectedVersion required. 200 confirmed receipt {draftId, version, savedAt, expiresAt}; same key/digest replay original result; mismatch 409; stale 412; over-cap 409; validation 422; rate 429; unavailable/uncertain 503.
- DELETE /api/v1/support/drafts?threadId=... with expectedVersion and Idempotency-Key; returns tombstone version and receipt; never deletes a sent support message. POST /api/v1/support/drafts/send with {threadId:null|uuid, expectedVersion, idempotencyKey}; atomically sends exact saved subject/body via existing restricted support_create_thread or support_add_message, records immutable sent receipt (threadId,messageId,originalDraftVersion), tombstones draft and commits audit in one DB transaction. No client-supplied owner, arbitrary target member or edited send body. Require explicit recent read-back if server draft is stale. Existing direct send endpoints stay available and unchanged when flag OFF; no requirement to migrate historical form submissions.
- HTTP: 401 no session, 403 consent/CSRF, 404 foreign/not-found without existence oracle, 409 key digest/cap conflict, 410 expired, 412 stale version, 422 invalid input, 428 missing precondition, 429 bounded rate, 503 unknown commit/unavailable. BFF uses existing server-only API token, no public NestJS exposure; reject cross-origin CSRF and untrusted redirects. Rate proposal: <=30 draft mutations/hour/member and <=5 sends/min/member; do not count as live until enforced and tested.

### DB/transactions, admin, abuse, observability
- Forward-only proposed support_member_drafts: draft_id UUID PK, user_id UUID FK users ON DELETE CASCADE, thread_id UUID nullable FK support_threads ON DELETE CASCADE (evaluate thread hard-delete retention), subject/body, version BIGINT CHECK >=1, saved_at, expires_at, deleted_at, send_receipt_id; unique owner for NEW scope via partial UNIQUE(user_id) WHERE thread_id IS NULL, unique owner/thread via partial UNIQUE(user_id,thread_id) WHERE thread_id IS NOT NULL **without deleted_at predicate** so tombstones retain version and prevent ABA. CHECK subject/body bounds and NEW/REPLY consistency, indexes (user_id, updated_at DESC, draft_id DESC) and (expires_at) for bounded purge. Separate support_draft_requests with UNIQUE(user_id,idempotency_key), 32-byte digest, operation, original response, expires_at; immutable support_draft_send_receipts UNIQUE(draft_id,version), UNIQUE(user_id,idempotency_key), FK to actual sent thread/message and audit. Deletion retains only redacted receipt within reviewed retention; proposed 90 days, not approved legal period.
- Only reviewed SECURITY DEFINER functions with fixed search_path, explicit schema qualifiers, revoked PUBLIC EXECUTE, app-role EXECUTE only and actor/session binding at NestJS. **Actor UUID passed to SQL is not authentication.** No direct app-role table DML or broad grants. SAVE/DISCARD locks owner draft row and CAS version; REPLY SEND locks support thread then draft, validates ownership/status/version and reuses stable send idempotency key; NEW SEND locks owner then draft before existing create function. Every mutation + receipt + audit is atomic; failed audit rolls back send and tombstone. Race: two tabs editing same version => one 200, one 412; double Send => exactly one message/thread and same original receipt on identical replay; different digest =>409; expiry/consent/session revocation => fail closed. Lock ordering must be reconciled with v1326 support-resolution work before implementation.
- Admin: never display raw drafts in admin inbox, never allow administrator impersonated send; only redacted aggregate counts for cohort >=20 and deletion job health, no individual body/subject. Privacy: explicit save disclosure, access/delete/export rights mapped to privacy center, 30-day draft expiry and 90-day redacted receipt retention are **proposals pending privacy/legal review**. Encryption-at-rest/key-management must be verified in deployment, not assumed. No body in telemetry, search indexes, SEO, logs or error traces.
- KPI: explicit Save completion rate, draft restore success across authenticated devices, send receipt mismatch/unknown-commit rate, stale-CAS conflict, draft expiry/discard, support abandonment (all event counts only, no content). Targets are hypotheses: >=99.9% confirmed-send receipt integrity and 0 duplicate sends in 100-way concurrency fixture; no invented current baseline. No engagement dark patterns.

- **P1 SUP1328-06:** atomic versioned SECDEF save/discard/send, idempotency replay and consistent lock order with v1326.
- **P1 SUP1328-07:** NestJS DTO/API/owner guards/error contracts and existing support-send compatibility.
- **P1 SUP1328-08:** Next.js forms + Android App API parity, a11y/i18n, logout fencing, no client persistence.
- **P1 SUP1328-09:** privacy retention/export/erasure, admin k>=20 aggregates, SEO and content-free KPI.
- **P1 SUP1328-10:** isolated PostgreSQL real-role 100 concurrent SAVE/SEND, 100 BOLA, 10,001 synthetic draft/receipt paging rows (not a production cap), expiry/CSRF/unknown-commit/retry, 320px/200% WCAG, Test fail-closed flag OFF and rollback.
- Release: docs-only Draft now; engineering must branch separately after design review. Feature OFF -> additive DB with rollback -> exact candidate SHA CI/isolated PG -> official Test authenticated web/Android E2E + backup restore -> approved staged GitOps/canary -> monitored Production, rollback switch OFF without deleting saved drafts/ledger. No money/WLD/ledger mutation. If security, data correctness, audits or migration gate fails, RELEASE HOLD. Other Gemini/A/B/C/QA PRs untouched.

## v2026.10.26.1326 — F675-138 Resolution Epoch and Last-Reply Visibility Integrity (Feature Improvement / DRAFT / RELEASE HOLD)

**Cycle:** Previous Feature Addition v1324 → Current Feature Improvement v1326 → Next Feature Addition v1328 (provisional). Source-of-truth main `1c5b84a1c45baafcca83f375a6f99698121e9bb5` rechecked at start/mid; predecessor planning Draft #1449 OPEN and stacked on unmerged #1432. This is documentation-only; no runtime, DB, WLD, ledger, feature switch, Test or Production change.

### Actual source and correction to v1324 assumptions
- Migration `196-user-admin-support-chat.sql` returns member messages in `created_at ASC,id ASC` order with default 200/max 500; `/support/page.tsx` renders the returned slice without truncation evidence. A member can see an old 200-message slice while a newer administrator reply and resolution exist. Proposed v1324 acknowledgment must **not** become eligible merely because the thread's status is `resolved`; a display proof must cover the latest relevant administrator reply or explicitly say there was no administrator reply.
- `admin_support_set_status` currently executes `UPDATE ... status=p_status` even when already resolved; member `support_add_message` sets `open` and `admin_support_reply` sets `waiting_user`. There is no current closure event or revision in Migration196. A naive after-update trigger would mint duplicate resolution events for `resolved→resolved`, and a stale browser can submit feedback after reopen/re-resolve. These are source-confirmed *design risks*, not verified Production incidents.
- Read-only development DB aggregate rechecked this cycle: 2 resolved, 1 waiting_user threads; 2 administrator and 4 member messages (6 total). No thread exceeds 200 in this sample; this is not Production evidence.
- `support.repository.ts` uses the existing SECURITY DEFINER functions and `support.controller.ts` enforces session/consent/CSRF for writes. Do not expose private support APIs through a public edge path or trust a caller-supplied user UUID. Existing v1316/v1318 reply-attention and v1324 feedback proposals remain separate but must share a reviewed per-thread ordering/revision contract, not competing triggers.

### Corrected user journey and eligibility proof
- Entry stays `/support?thread=<uuid>` (member-only, noindex/no-store); show two independent facts: **Administrator marked resolved** and **Member explicitly confirmed resolved**. CTA `View latest administrator reply` fetches newest-first/keyset pages; `This resolved my question` becomes available only after the server returns an eligible closure card and, if present, the latest administrator reply associated with that closure. A 201+ history may label earlier messages `OLDER_HISTORY_PARTIAL` while still proving the final reply is included. Never show an old first-200 slice as complete.
- For a closure with no administrator reply, display `Closed without an administrator reply` and the actual closure timestamp; voluntary acknowledgment can be offered after displaying that closure card, but never fabricate a reply or human reading. A signed proof shows server-delivered eligible content, **not** that a human read it. `I still need help` uses the existing reply path and opens a new unresolved epoch; negative helpfulness alone never reopens.
- Explicit states: `ELIGIBLE_CURRENT`, `NO_ADMIN_REPLY`, `OLDER_HISTORY_PARTIAL`, `LEGACY_UNKNOWN`, `STALE_EPOCH`, `PROOF_EXPIRED`, `ALREADY_ACKNOWLEDGED`, `UNAVAILABLE`, `SESSION_EXPIRED`, `RATE_LIMITED`. Do not turn 404/503/partial into empty/confirmed; do not submit on GET, render, prefetch, navigation or refresh. Keyboard/320px/200% zoom/44px targets/ARIA live/reduced motion and Android parity remain mandatory.

### API contract and replay safety
- Owner GET `/api/v1/support/threads/:threadId/resolution-feedback` returns `{threadId,status,statusRevision,resolutionEventId,resolvedAt,lastAdminMessageId,lastMessageId,replyCoverage,memberAcknowledged,helpful,responseAt,displayProof,proofExpiresAt}`. Null means unknown, not false. `displayProof` is null for legacy, stale, partial-final-reply or backend failure; GET is read-only, private/no-store, bounded and server-side owner-authorized.
- Newest message page GET uses an opaque signed keyset over `(created_at,id)` at PostgreSQL microsecond precision, stable ordering, per-page limit <=100, and a response `lastReturnedMessageId`/coverage marker. For eligibility, server confirms the closure's authoritative last administrator message is in the delivered latest slice; do not infer completeness from a count. Older pages are optional and clearly marked partial. The closure event must record the authoritative `last_admin_message_id` and `last_message_id` snapshot under the same thread lock.
- Server issues HMAC-SHA256 proof with key ID, purpose `support-resolution-ack-v1`, member/session binding, thread, event, revision, last-admin-message snapshot, issuance/expiry <=600 seconds; rotate keys with bounded verification overlap, reject cross-session/cross-thread reuse, never log proof. The POST requires `resolutionEventId`, `statusRevision`, `displayProof`, UUID `idempotencyKey`, `acknowledged:true`, optional helpfulness/note <=500 characters; actor is derived from authenticated session. Missing proof 428; stale event/reopened/re-resolved or expired proof 412; conflicting key/payload or different response to same epoch 409; invalid 422; 401/403/404/429/503 as in v1324. No success before authoritative commit.
- Android `/app-api/v1/[...path]` already proxies the `support` group through `appGatewayPath` and forwards cookie/CSRF headers; new GET/POST must preserve `Cache-Control: private,no-store`, typed errors, client version policy and response-header parity. A group allowlist is not a replacement for NestJS owner authorization; do not add a public direct backend route or a new original-route-map entry.
- CSRF and consent for POST, rate limits by member/thread/session, response `201` on first durable submission or `200` on exact replay. The digest includes canonical event/revision/acknowledged/helpful/note (normalize Unicode once, distinguish null vs false) and key; validate byte/character bounds consistently across Next/NestJS/DB. Exact retry returns original stored receipt even after later reopen, **only after** current actor/session and original ownership checks; different payload must not overwrite history.

### PostgreSQL, concurrency and compatibility
- Forward-only migration; never edit applied Migration196 or Migration266. Under `SELECT ... FOR UPDATE` of `support_threads`, assign a monotonic `status_revision` on *actual* state change only. A genuine non-resolved→resolved transition creates one immutable `support_resolution_events` row with `(thread_id,sequence)` and `(thread_id,status_revision)` UNIQUE, admin actor, last message/admin reply snapshot, and atomic audit. `resolved→resolved` must be a no-op: zero new event, zero revision increment, no fake new timestamp; `resolved→open/waiting_user→resolved` creates a new event and eligibility epoch. Message writes and status changes must use the same lock order.
- Existing v1 `admin_support_set_status`, `support_add_message`, `admin_support_reply` signatures remain callable but must be routed through reviewed versioned shared transition logic in an additive migration; never depend on a trigger that guesses the authenticated admin actor from a client-controlled session variable. If any v1 writer bypasses epoch/revision/audit invariants, feature flag stays OFF and UI shows `LEGACY_UNKNOWN` rather than a fabricated event. Recheck old signatures/permissions against the isolated PostgreSQL instance before activation.
- Lock order: thread → current resolution event → response receipt; then re-read status/revision/event/last-admin-message while holding the lock. Lookup the idempotency receipt **after acquiring the thread lock**, not before: concurrent same-key requests otherwise both miss and race on UNIQUE. Same key/same digest returns immutable receipt; same key/different digest 409; different keys on same event return the existing response without changing it (409). `UNIQUE(resolution_event_id,user_id)`, `UNIQUE(user_id,idempotency_key)`, 32-byte digest CHECK, FK ownership, index `(thread_id,resolved_at DESC,id DESC)` and `(user_id,created_at DESC,id DESC)`; explicit PUBLIC EXECUTE revoke, app only versioned function EXECUTE, fixed SECDEF search_path and atomic audit. A caller-supplied actor UUID alone is not DB authentication; effective app-role ACL remains P0.
- Migration rollout: shadow-compare new event count to actual v2 transitions before flag ON; pre-existing `resolved` rows without proven event remain `LEGACY_UNKNOWN` and cannot be bulk backfilled from timestamps. Existing UI/endpoint remains available when feature OFF. Audit failure aborts the whole transition, never leaving `resolved` without event.

### Admin/privacy, SEO, KPIs, acceptance and release
- Admin console distinguishes administrative closure, voluntary acknowledgment, and missing feedback; no staff ranking, rewards or user-identifying public output. Aggregate only fixed non-overlapping cohorts with >=20 eligible events, suppress small cells and difference attacks across filters/time windows; notes require separately approved privileged access and audited reason, no note bodies or proof tokens in analytics. Define retention/erasure with privacy owner. Private SSR/Android responses `no-store`; account/admin routes `noindex`, sitemap excluded; do not render private feedback in structured data.
- KPIs: eligible proof deliveries, acknowledgment submissions, member-confirmation rate (confirmed/current eligible events), unacknowledged/legacy share, `stale_epoch_412`, `proof_expired_412`, `missing_last_reply_block`, duplicate-key conflicts, audit-rollback count, p95 owner GET/POST, follow-up replies within 7 days; denominator and cohort version pinned, all business impact HYPOTHESIS until observed. Target unauthorized acknowledgment=0, fabricated closure event=0, false reply-complete=0.
- Real isolated PostgreSQL acceptance: old/new 200/201/500/501/10,001 messages, newest admin reply visible while earlier pages partial, no-admin-reply closure, repeated resolved no-op, close→reply→close, 100-way simultaneous status/reply/ack races, 100 same-key submits, different digest, 100 foreign owners, 599/600/601s proof, session switch/revoke, audit insertion failure atomic rollback, out-of-order clock/UUID tie, 404 vs 503, Unicode 500/501, XSS, admin suppression, Android and WCAG. CI/docs checks do not substitute exact-SHA authenticated official Test E2E or isolated backup restore.
- Feature `support_resolution_feedback_v1` default OFF; additive DB, versioned endpoints, 1%→10%→100% staged rollout only after exact SHA CI, security audit, migration/ACL, verified restore, Test evidence, approved GitOps and owner authorization. Rollback OFF + previous compatible binaries; preserve immutable events, do not delete/edit ledger or history. QA v1299 IN PROGRESS with main frontend 1,397 pass/6 fail and main prod/full audits FAIL; candidate tests and production audit PASS but candidate full audit FAIL at latest checkpoint; root 99% (~1.7GiB free), data ~24GiB free, app-role ACL/Migration266 and Test/restore unresolved. Production BLOCKED.

### Prioritized development backlog — 4 P0 / 6 P1
- **P0 ENV1326-01:** root disk 99% and data volume headroom; owner-approved non-destructive capacity recovery, preserve other workers' worktrees and verify independent encrypted isolated restore.
- **P0 SEC1326-02:** reconcile effective app-role DML/PUBLIC EXECUTE (previous 235/241, bank3) and server-derived owner identity, BOLA/BFLA, HMAC/session/CSRF; no extra direct table grants.
- **P0 REL1326-03:** QA1299 tests/audits and Migration266 drift, exact HEAD CI, real-PG ACL, authenticated official Test exact-SHA and GitOps/restore release gate; no Production bypass.
- **P0 SUP1326-04:** never enable acknowledgment if latest relevant admin reply/closure card was not delivered; distinguish partial/legacy/503 and stale epoch from member-confirmed.
- **P1 SUP1326-05:** forward-only shared thread status revision/closure event and admin reply snapshot, v1-compatible transitions, no duplicate resolved→resolved event.
- **P1 SUP1326-06:** versioned SECDEF owner receipt transaction with thread-first lock, post-lock idempotency digest replay, epoch CAS and audit-atomic rollback.
- **P1 SUP1326-07:** newest keyset + GET coverage + HMAC 600s display proof + typed NestJS POST errors, OpenAPI/route inventory and rate limits.
- **P1 SUP1326-08:** Next.js explicit acknowledgment/partial/expired UX and Android App API parity, WCAG 2.2 AA, session-switch fencing.
- **P1 SUP1326-09:** privacy-safe admin fixed cohorts, retention/access controls, SEO noindex/no-store and versioned KPI definitions.
- **P1 SUP1326-10:** isolated real-PG 201/501/10,001 and 100-way replay/race/BOLA/audit-failure regression, flag-OFF rollback, exact-SHA Test proof.

**External independent reference gate:** [PostgreSQL row-level locking](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/explicit-locking.html) and [OWASP API Security Top 10](https://api-security.owasp.org/editions/2023/en/0x11-t10/) official publisher guidance reviewed as design context, newly independently accepted/deduplicated/classified entries **0**; cumulative independently approved **UNKNOWN / 10,000**, **BLOCKED / IN PROGRESS**. No 10,000-source completion claim. Engineering handoff: issue #1448; capacity #1170. No change to Gemini/Dev A/B/C/QA work.

- **v1324 Draft PR:** https://github.com/wtrdd1-hash/Woldeok-Moneyverse-Migration/pull/1449 (stacked on unmerged #1432; DRAFT / RELEASE HOLD). **Engineering issue:** https://github.com/wtrdd1-hash/Woldeok-Moneyverse-Migration/issues/1448.
## v2026.10.26.1324 — F675-138 Verified Member Support Resolution Acknowledgment and Feedback (Feature Addition / DRAFT / RELEASE HOLD)

**Cycle:** Previous Feature Improvement v1322 → Current Feature Addition v1324 → Next Feature Improvement v1326 (provisional). Source of truth: main 1c5b84a1c45baafcca83f375a6f99698121e9bb5 AGENTS.md, README.md, English canonical PROJECT_PLAN.md, Korean parity. Predecessor planning Draft PR #1432 is unmerged; engineering issue #1448. No runtime, database, Test or Production change.

### Verified gap, intent, users and scope
- Main frontend/src/app/support/page.tsx shows open/waiting_user/resolved based on server status, but has no explicit member confirmation or satisfaction feedback. backend/src/support/support.controller.ts exposes member thread/message and admin status endpoints; support.repository.ts calls functions from Migration 196. Migration 196 CHECK limits status to open, waiting_user, resolved; administrator may set resolved without the member acknowledging it. Read-only development DB: resolved 2 threads, waiting_user 1, messages 6. These aggregates do not establish member satisfaction, verified resolution, or Production state.
- **Problem:** administrative closure can look like customer-confirmed resolution; support cannot measure member-perceived outcome. **Target:** signed-in, consent-complete owner of a thread; authorized support administrator sees privacy-safe aggregate only. **Outcome:** distinct admin-closed vs member-confirmed status and one voluntary response per verifiable resolution event. No reward, WLD, ledger, automatic moderator performance ranking or public review.
- **Alternatives considered:** infer confirmation from page view (rejected as false consent); store a boolean on support_threads (rejected: loses history and concurrency semantics); append-only per-resolution event + response (selected, migration and rollout complexity accepted). Prior closed threads without an authenticated resolution event are LEGACY_UNKNOWN, never retroactively counted as confirmed.

### User journey, screens, CTAs and states
- Entry: existing /support thread selector; resolved thread displays “Administrator marked this resolved” with timestamp and neutral explanation; link to /support?thread=<UUID>&resolution=1, no new public route. CTAs: “This resolved my question” (explicit acknowledgment), optional “Helpful / Not helpful” and note (0–500 Unicode characters), “I still need help” (existing member reply form, which reopens via current authoritative support_add_message), “Skip feedback”, “Retry”. The negative-helpful answer is not itself a reopen instruction.
- States: NOT_ELIGIBLE(open/waiting_user), ELIGIBLE(resolved with current event), SUBMITTED, LEGACY_UNKNOWN(resolved without event), STALE_RESOLUTION(changed during submit), UNAVAILABLE(backend failure), SESSION_EXPIRED, RATE_LIMITED. No fake success/zero or implicit acknowledgment on GET, prefetch, rendering, link opening, logout or failed POST. A second genuine resolution event permits a new independent response; immutable earlier feedback is not overwritten.
- Admin /admin/support: show aggregate response count and helpful ratio only for cohorts n>=20, else “insufficient data”; no public user identities, note excerpts or leaderboard. Raw notes require separately approved privacy/support privilege and audited access. Support status continues to mean administrator workflow status, not independently verified outcome.

### API, identity, authorization and errors
- Proposed NestJS GET /api/v1/support/threads/:threadId/resolution-feedback: authenticated owner only; returns {threadId,status,resolutionEventId,resolvedAt,eligibility,memberAcknowledged,helpful,responseAt} with nulls for unknown. No GET write, no broad table access, no false fallback. GET 401 unauthenticated, 403 missing consent, 404 non-owner/nonexistent, 503 DB unavailable; Cache-Control: private,no-store.
- Proposed POST same path: body {resolutionEventId:UUID,idempotencyKey:UUID,acknowledged:true,helpful?:boolean,note?:string}; CSRF, active session, consent, same-origin; server derives actor from session, not caller UUID. Response 201 first write / 200 exact replay, 409 changed or already answered with different payload, 412 stale event/status, 422 malformed input, 429 throttled, 503 unavailable. Hash canonical request payload for idempotency; never leak another member’s existence. If member needs more help, reuse existing POST /support/threads/:id/messages, not a fake automatic reopen.
- Android App API: same authenticated contract and CSRF/session or approved app-native equivalent, route-map/OpenAPI parity, stable typed enums, null/legacy/error behavior; no mobile-only bypass.

### Forward-only PostgreSQL and transactional design
- Add support_threads.status_revision bigint NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 in the forward-only migration; increment only when status actually changes (including member/admin replies) via a reviewed DB trigger, without altering Migration196. New support_resolution_events(id uuid PK, thread_id uuid FK support_threads(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT, sequence bigint NOT NULL CHECK sequence>0, resolved_by uuid NOT NULL, resolved_at timestamptz NOT NULL, status_revision bigint NOT NULL, created_at timestamptz NOT NULL, UNIQUE(thread_id,sequence), UNIQUE(thread_id,status_revision)); indexes (thread_id, resolved_at DESC,id DESC). Do not synthesize events from old resolved status or timestamp alone.
- New support_resolution_feedback(id uuid PK, resolution_event_id uuid NOT NULL FK, thread_id uuid NOT NULL FK, user_id uuid NOT NULL FK, acknowledged boolean NOT NULL CHECK acknowledged=true, helpful boolean NULL, note text NULL CHECK char_length(note)<=500, idempotency_key uuid NOT NULL, request_digest bytea NOT NULL CHECK octet_length(request_digest)=32, created_at timestamptz NOT NULL, UNIQUE(resolution_event_id,user_id), UNIQUE(user_id,idempotency_key)); index (user_id,created_at DESC,id DESC); consistency of event/thread and owner enforced in SECDEF under lock, not trusted from client.
- New forward-only reviewed migration preserves Migration196 public function signatures; augment administrator transition-to-resolved function to append an immutable event **in the same transaction** only on a genuine non-resolved→resolved transition, with authenticated admin actor and monotonically assigned revision. Repeated resolved→resolved does not fabricate a new event. Audit/event insert failure aborts status change; no partial “resolved”. Do not modify applied Migration196 in place.
- Versioned SECDEF owner-read/submit functions use fixed search_path, owner+active user checks inside SQL, explicit PUBLIC EXECUTE revoke, only minimal moneyverse_app EXECUTE. Check a pre-existing identical idempotency key+digest first and return its stored result even if the thread subsequently reopened; otherwise lock thread then current resolution event in deterministic order, compare event id/status revision, and insert response and audit atomically. Unique constraints and digest make 100-way exact replay one durable response; different payload conflicts. Existing app-role 235/241 DML grants are an unresolved dev-DB P0, not permission to add more.
- Legacy compatibility: existing resolved threads without a resolution event remain LEGACY_UNKNOWN and may use ordinary support reply; do not bulk backfill invented events. Status transitions after feature installation get event; feature OFF hides new UI while existing /support stays functional. No financial tables, balance, ledger or settlement changes.

### Security, privacy, SEO, mobile, observability and delivery
- Threat model: cross-account BOLA/BFLA, forged actor/event, admin spoofing, stale close/reopen races, CSRF, replay/key reuse, rate abuse, stored XSS in note, inference from 404/403, sensitive note retention, mass export. Enforce session+consent+CSRF, least-privilege DB, constant-shaped non-owner 404, output encoding, no raw notes in analytics/logs, per-user/thread rate limits, no optional feedback incentives. Review retention/erasure with privacy owner before activation; no indefinite retention promise.
- Private page noindex/nofollow, no-store and no public structured data. Mobile 320px and 44px touch targets, semantic form labels, keyboard focus, ARIA live state, screen reader state text, reduced motion, WCAG 2.2 AA. Never show an unavailable request as a confirmed resolution.
- KPI instrumentation (aggregate only): eligible_resolution_events, feedback_submitted, member_ack_rate=submitted acknowledgments/eligible with event, helpful_yes_share=helpful_yes/non-null helpful, followup_reply_7d, feedback_error_rate, p95 POST latency, duplicate_conflict_count; cohort n>=20 and consented privacy policy. No claim of current rates or causal impact; target only after baseline, no reward gaming.
- Tests: isolated real PG migrations and negative grants, 100 foreign-user attempts, 100 concurrent idempotent submits, 10,001 synthetic resolution events, exact/different digest replay, status reopen race, admin same-status no event, audit failure full rollback, legacy no-event case, session/CSRF/consent failures, 429/503, XSS note, Android parity, WCAG keyboard/mobile, API contract, frontend/backend regression. No tests claimed run for new runtime code.
- Deployment: planning branch only; engineering implementation on separate owned branch, flag default OFF, additive migration/backward compatibility, isolated restore and exact-SHA CI + security audit + authenticated Test user flow, staging rollout 1%→10%→100% after error/permission gates, stop on unauthorized access or duplicate/event mismatch, rollback flag OFF and previous binaries while preserving append-only events. No Production promotion without GitOps approval.

### Prioritized actionable backlog (4 P0 + 6 P1)
- **P0 ENV1324-01:** recover root capacity (98%, ~2.0GiB free) with owner-approved non-destructive relocation, prove independent encrypted backup by isolated restore and verify worker worktrees preserved.
- **P0 SEC1324-02:** inventory real app-role effective grants (dev 235/241 DML and PUBLIC bank funcs), review forward-only least-privilege and owner/actor BOLA negatives before feature writes.
- **P0 REL1324-03:** block promotion until exact HEAD CI full tests, full dependency audit, real-PG migration/ACL, authenticated official Test exact-SHA, restore and GitOps approval PASS.
- **P0 SUP1324-04:** stop conflating administrator resolved status with member-confirmed outcome; legacy unknown/503/empty and race cases must not show fake member acceptance.
- **P1 SUP1324-05:** additive support_resolution_events/feedback schema, FK/unique/check/index and atomic admin status-transition provenance; no historical fake backfill.
- **P1 SUP1324-06:** SECDEF member submit with lock/CAS, exact idempotency+digest replay and atomic audit; 100-way race.
- **P1 SUP1324-07:** NestJS owner-only GET/POST, validation, error contracts, rate limits and route-map/OpenAPI changes.
- **P1 SUP1324-08:** Next.js /support confirmation CTAs and Android App API parity, keyboard/mobile WCAG regression.
- **P1 SUP1324-09:** administrator k>=20 aggregates, private notes, retention/erasure approval, noindex/no-store and opt-in KPI.
- **P1 SUP1324-10:** disposable PG 10,001-event/BOLA/race/legacy/audit-failure tests, migration compatibility, flag OFF rollback and exact-SHA Test evidence.

**Current QA/release:** QA v1297 FAILED/HOLD; main six frontend test failures, full dependency audit fail, migration266 checksum drift, dev app-role grants and PUBLIC bank funcs unresolved, official exact-SHA Test/isolated restore unverified; root 98%, ~2.0GiB available. Production BLOCKED. External 10,000 independent reference gate: newly approved 0, cumulative independently deduplicated and classified approved UNKNOWN / 10,000, BLOCKED / IN PROGRESS. No assertion of research completion.

## v2026.10.26.1322 — F675-137 Collection Provenance and Curation Integrity (Feature Improvement / DRAFT / RELEASE HOLD)

**Cycle:** Previous Feature Addition v1320 → Current Feature Improvement v1322 → Next Feature Addition v1324 (provisional). Source of truth is main 1c5b84a1c45baafcca83f375a6f99698121e9bb5 (AGENTS.md, README.md, PROJECT_PLAN.md canonical, PROJECT_PLAN.ko.md parity); prior v1320 Draft #1427 remains unmerged. Documentation only, isolated from Gemini/A/B/C/QA. No changes to WLD, ledger, runtime, DB, feature flags, Test or Production.

### Reproduced code boundaries and decision
- Main frontend/src/app/collections/curation-retention-flow.tsx hardcodes NEO_CYBER_ARCADE_TOKEN as a sample, while backend/src/collection/collection.repository.ts inserts NEO_CYBER_CASINO_CHIP for the analogous slot. Codes, labels, categories and history cannot be reconciled by string similarity. No alias should silently certify an award.
- listCollections GET calls ensureSeedPieces: a missing owner collection becomes four inserted sample rows, then returned as owned. getCurationStatus GET inserts a default ladder_step=1/timeline_day=D7 row, and its ON CONFLICT UPDATE refreshes updated_at even when only a read was requested. Both violate read-only expectations; concurrent GETs can race with legitimate updates.
- advanceCuration POST accepts caller-provided targetStep 1..7 and independently selectable D1/D3/D7, and the UI optimistically changes the day without checking response.ok. updatePiece PUT accepts either id::text or code, and the browser persists optimistic favorites/notes to a shared localStorage key even if the server rejects a write. UI must not call cached data a verified holding.
- Migration232 user_collections unique(user_id,code) has no authoritative grant event FK, while user_curation_progress contains no revision column. Read-only development DB: 8 collections/2 owners (2 each of FIRST_CAPITAL_BADGE, ANTIQUE_SAFE_KEY, NEO_CYBER_CASINO_CHIP, SMART_LOGISTICS_PERMIT); 2 progress rows both ladder_step=1/timeline_day=D7. These aggregates do not prove any particular award's origin or any elapsed seven-day journey; Production data unknown.
- Alternatives: A keep implicit seeding and add a badge (reject false entitlement); B delete legacy/rename codes (reject history loss); C separate examples, preserve legacy as UNKNOWN, make reads pure and mutations versioned (chosen). No retroactive VERIFIED based on code, date, browser cache, prose or a manual mapping alone.

### User journey, permissions, UI, states, CTAs
- Authenticated/consented owner enters private /collections; explicit tabs Verified holdings, Legacy records needing evidence, Illustrative examples (non-owned), and My curation. New /collections/[pieceId]/evidence remains the receipt detail. A piece can be VERIFIED only by v1320 immutable owner/source receipt, otherwise LEGACY_UNKNOWN, UNVERIFIED, REVOKED, UNAVAILABLE; empty verified list is distinct from 503 and local draft. An example has DEMO_NOT_OWNED and can never be shared as an entitlement.
- CTAs: View acquisition evidence; Report discrepancy; Retry; Edit note; Toggle favorite; Start curation; Advance one editorial step; Choose D1/D3/D7 view. Display a save-in-progress state, committed revision, conflict reload, offline draft and retry; never show Saved before HTTP 2xx with server revision. A chosen D7 is a view label, not proof of seven elapsed days or an acquisition date. Curation steps are editorial/self-reported, not a reward or entitlement; Step 1 'Have' may assert ownership only for VERIFIED receipts. Legacy pieces may be annotated as unverified personal drafts but cannot unlock a verified holding badge.
- Never initialize apparent ownership from DEFAULT_PIECES; render skeleton then complete owner response, genuine empty or explicit unavailable. Browser storage may hold notes as account-scoped unsynced drafts only, with logout/account-switch purge and no auto-promotion to verified state. Accessibility: 320px, 200% zoom, WCAG 2.2 AA, keyboard/ARIA live success/error, 44px touch targets, no color-only verification; Android App API parity.

### API, auth, database and transactions
- Preserve v1 endpoints while feature OFF; v2 owner GET /api/v1/collections/v2 and GET /api/v1/collections/v2/curation/status MUST NOT mutate any DB row (including updated_at). Missing status returns {state:UNINITIALIZED,revision:0,selectedDay:null}, not a synthetic persisted D7. Owner PATCH /api/v1/collections/v2/:uuid/curation-note with If-Match revision and Idempotency-Key; POST /api/v1/collections/v2/curation/advance with expectedRevision and exactly nextStep=currentStep+1, or explicit initialization with first verified receipt; PUT /api/v1/collections/v2/preferences/day is an independent non-achievement display preference. Do not allow arbitrary step regression/skip, client owner IDs or code-as-ID in v2. Validate max500-character note, bounded page limits and server-issued opaque UUIDs. App API and OpenAPI/Android share exact errors and response shapes.
- Use SessionGuard, AuthenticatedGuard, ConsentGuard and server-derived member identity; server CSRF and rate limits for mutations (e.g. 20/hour/member); 401 unauthenticated, 403 consent, 404 missing/foreign indistinguishable, 409 idempotency conflict, 412 revision mismatch, 428 required precondition missing, 429 throttled, 503 unavailable. Reads: Cache-Control private,no-store; no 200 empty on DB outage. Treat actor UUID passed to SECURITY DEFINER as insufficient independent DB authentication until app-role compromise threat is tested.
- Forward-only migration after 232 adds revision bigint NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 and an optional verified-receipt FK/entitlement link, without editing already-applied 232 or 266. Consider a separate curation_day_preferences(user_id PK FK,selected_day CHECK D1/D3/D7,revision>=0) to prevent day selection from altering milestone history. Curation action receipts: UUID PK, owner FK, expected/new revision, action kind, canonical payload digest, idempotency key, committed_at; UNIQUE(owner_id,idempotency_key), UNIQUE(owner_id,new_revision), CHECK new_revision>expected_revision. Enforce monotonic ladder 1..7 in transaction, with Step 1 owner-verified receipt requirement and later editorial events separately labeled. Existing (user_id,code) uniqueness retained; no automatic alias grant. Proposed read indexes: user_collections(user_id,id), receipt(owner_id,issued_at DESC,id DESC), curation_events(owner_id,committed_at DESC,id DESC); use CONCURRENTLY outside transaction only if supported by deployment and proven on isolated DB.
- Versioned SECURITY DEFINER functions collection_owner_list_v2, collection_curation_status_v2, collection_curation_advance_v2, collection_note_update_v2 with fixed pg_catalog/pg_temp search_path, fully qualified objects, REVOKE EXECUTE FROM PUBLIC and narrow EXECUTE grants. No direct app DML on collection/curation/receipt tables after compatibility validation. Each write locks owner progress row and entitlement in stable order, checks source/owner, revision and idempotency digest, updates exactly once and inserts audit+outbox in the same commit; same-key same-payload retry returns immutable original result, different payload 409. A conflict must not overwrite newer progress or optimistic local drafts.
- Alias discrepancy: NEO_CYBER_ARCADE_TOKEN and NEO_CYBER_CASINO_CHIP remain distinct historical codes unless a separately authorized evidence review proves identity. If approved, append a signed mapping decision and preserve original code, provenance, owner and acquisition timestamp; never mass UPDATE, silently merge two items or infer VERIFIED. Administrative dispute review uses separate approver and immutable audit, not direct verification toggles.

### Privacy, operations, analytics, acceptance and rollback
- Private evidence and curation pages noindex,nofollow, sitemap excluded, no-store; public showcase opt-in separate redacted projection only for VERIFIED items. No owner UUID, notes, source payloads or idempotency keys in analytics; aggregate admin statistics only with k>=20. KPIs: false VERIFIED=0, GET-side DB writes=0, lost user-note saves=0, cross-owner leaks=0, client/server item-code disagreement=0 after mapping decisions, 409/412/503 counts, % legacy UNKNOWN, p95 v2 GET<500ms in Test.
- Real isolated PostgreSQL fixtures: existing eight-row/two-owner dev-shape and 10,001 synthetic rows; 100 concurrent first GETs produce zero new collections/progress rows; GET status never changes updated_at; 100 concurrent CAS advances cannot skip/regress; 100 foreign-member probes reveal no rows; same/different idempotency payloads, duplicate alias, deleted/revoked source, note length 500/501, browser account switch, offline and HTTP 500, D7 label vs real elapsed days, empty vs 503, Android parity, WCAG 200% zoom. Assert v1 function signatures and existing member notes preserved.
- Release gate: root 98% used (~2.7GiB free) and data 87% used (~26GiB free) at start, QA v1297 in progress with failing main/candidate test/security gates; v1296 candidate full audit/bot failures. Main frontend 1,397 pass/6 fail; dev app role DML 235/241 tables, PUBLIC bank EXECUTE3, migration266 checksum mismatch, exact-SHA authenticated Test E2E and isolated backup restore unverified. Production BLOCKED. Keep collection_verified_receipts_v1 OFF, shadow reads, migration/privilege negative tests, Test exact-SHA and recovery drill; only separate engineering branch and approved GitOps 1%→10%→100%. Abort on any false VERIFIED, GET write, cross-owner leak, source mismatch or progress regression; rollback flag OFF and v1 routes, preserve receipts and original legacy data. No planning-run deploy.
- Independent external source gate: newly independently verified/approved 0, cumulative deduplicated/classified approved UNKNOWN / 10,000, BLOCKED / IN PROGRESS. Prior W3C/OWASP/PostgreSQL documents are design guidance, not accepted corpus entries. Engineering issue #1426.

**P0 4 / P1 6 development backlog:**
- P0 ENV1322-01 capacity + isolated restore; SEC1322-02 app DB grants/session actor boundary; REL1322-03 QA/exact-SHA security and GitOps; COL1322-04 GET-side seed and false verified ownership elimination.
- P1 COL1322-05 canonical code discrepancy and historical alias evidence; COL1322-06 pure read curation and forward-only schema; COL1322-07 monotonic CAS/idempotent owner mutation; COL1322-08 Next.js/Android/offline/a11y; COL1322-09 admin/privacy/SEO/KPI; COL1322-10 isolated PostgreSQL concurrency/regression/rollback.
- Machine-checkable IDs: ENV1322-01 SEC1322-02 REL1322-03 COL1322-04 COL1322-05 COL1322-06 COL1322-07 COL1322-08 COL1322-09 COL1322-10.


- **v1320 Draft PR: https://github.com/wtrdd1-hash/Woldeok-Moneyverse-Migration/pull/1427 (stacked on unmerged #1420; HOLD). Engineering issue:** https://github.com/wtrdd1-hash/Woldeok-Moneyverse-Migration/issues/1426 (Draft/RELEASE HOLD; predecessor #1420 unmerged).

## v2026.10.26.1320 — F675-137 Verified Collection Acquisition Evidence & Receipts (Feature Addition / DRAFT / RELEASE HOLD)

**Cycle:** Previous Feature Improvement v1318 → Current Feature Addition v1320 → Next Feature Improvement v1322 (provisional). Main 1c5b84a1c45baafcca83f375a6f99698121e9bb5 and EN canonical / KO parity checked at start and midpoint. Prior Draft #1420 is unmerged; this planning branch is documentation-only, isolated from A/B/C, Gemini and QA. Engineering issue #1426.

### Actual source boundary, user purpose and alternatives
- Member purpose: distinguish an actually granted collection item from a decorative example, and inspect the authoritative event that granted it without exposing another member's private activity. Success is zero unproven items labeled VERIFIED, zero cross-member receipts, and recoverable discrepancies.
- In main, frontend/src/app/collections/curation-retention-flow.tsx initializes four DEFAULT_PIECES as apparent holdings and can restore localStorage on fetch failure; backend/src/collection/collection.repository.ts ensureSeedPieces inserts four historical-sounding pieces when a member has none. Migration232 public.user_collections stores free-text provenance but no acquisition-event FK/receipt. These are observed code paths, NOT proof any individual historic row was fraudulently granted.
- Read-only development PostgreSQL: 8 user_collections rows / 2 owners / 8 matching the four seed codes. The moneyverse_app role currently has SELECT, INSERT, UPDATE and DELETE on this table in development. Do not extrapolate counts or privileges to Production.
- Alternatives considered: (A) retain seed with a small disclaimer (reject: still asserts ownership); (B) erase unknown legacy rows (reject: destroys potential legitimate member history); (C) additive evidence receipts with honest legacy UNKNOWN (chosen). Do not retroactively claim proof from matching item code, acquired_at, prose provenance or an unverified browser cache.

### Member flow, UI states and calls to action
- Existing private /collections → list of evidence-backed items and separately labeled legacy/unverified entries; new /collections/[pieceId]/evidence detail. Each card shows collection name, code, current status, source category, server-issued acquisition time when proven, and optional verified event reference; no wallet value or scarcity promise. New CTAs: View acquisition evidence, Report a discrepancy (opens authenticated /support draft with opaque item ID), Retry verification, View illustrative examples (separate non-owned demo, never in holdings/share cards).
- States: VERIFIED (owner + authoritative source event + immutable receipt + non-revoked grant all match), LEGACY_UNKNOWN (existing row with no independently validated source), UNVERIFIED (receipt/source mismatch), REVOKED (authoritative revocation), UNAVAILABLE (source read or network failure), EMPTY_VERIFIED (successful complete owner query returns no verified pieces), LOADING, AUTH_REQUIRED, FEATURE_OFF. A network error never becomes EMPTY_VERIFIED; neither localStorage nor DEFAULT_PIECES may set VERIFIED.
- Filters Verified / Needs review / Revoked; default excludes revoked from active holdings but allows evidence history. Public share CTA is disabled for anything not VERIFIED, and remains opt-in, public-safe and free of member notes, wallet, purchase metadata and user identifiers. Legacy rows stay visible with explicit uncertainty; no silent deletion or mass relabel.

### API and App API contract
- NestJS owner-only GET /api/v1/collections/v2?state=&limit=&cursor= (default20, max50), GET /api/v1/collections/v2/:pieceId/evidence; Next.js same-origin /app-api/v1 forwarding and Android OpenAPI/app-api parity. No client-supplied owner ID; SessionGuard + AuthenticatedGuard + ConsentGuard, server-derived identity; per-object owner check in DB. Existing v1 list/curation contracts remain unchanged until validated cutover.
- Response: {items:[{id,code,name,evidenceState,sourceType?,acquiredAt?,receiptId?,verifiedAt?,version}],nextCursor?,snapshotCutoff,hasMore,completeness:'COMPLETE'|'PARTIAL'}. Cursor signed/HMAC and bound to member, filter, cutoff, expiry (600s); keyset immutable (acquired_at,id) with PostgreSQL microsecond precision, not JS Date roundtrips. HTTP pages do not share a long-lived PostgreSQL snapshot; new events after cutoff appear on refresh. Return explicit 401, 403 consent, 404 cross-owner/not-found, 409 stale cursor, 429 throttled, 503 source unavailable; never return 200 empty on dependency error. Cache-Control private,no-store; never expose DB internal keys or raw source payload.
- No new member POST to create/verify evidence. Any future privileged acquisition adapter or administrator review uses a separately authorized POST with Idempotency-Key, If-Match/version and CSRF; replays must return the committed receipt, not create a second award.

### PostgreSQL constraints, transactions, and trust boundary
- Additive forward-only tables: collection_acquisition_receipts(id UUID PK, owner_id UUID FK, collection_id UUID FK, item_code text, source_kind enum/validated text, source_event_id UUID, source_event_version bigint, receipt_version bigint, issued_at timestamptz, source_digest bytea, created_at timestamptz); collection_receipt_events(id UUID PK, receipt_id FK, event_kind GRANTED|REVOKED|CORRECTED, seq bigint, actor_kind, created_at); collection_idempotency(owner_id, operation, key, request_digest, receipt_id, created_at). Unique (source_kind,source_event_id,owner_id), unique (receipt_id,seq), unique (owner_id,operation,key), and index (owner_id,issued_at DESC,id DESC), index (owner_id,item_code), index (receipt_id,created_at). CHECK bounded codes, non-null canonical references, monotonic version; no arbitrary user-entered source text used as proof.
- Only explicitly implemented adapters can attest actual shop purchase, quest award, event grant or independently approved manual award; each must verify authoritative source existence, owner, item code, committed status and any reversal. Use typed source registry and source-specific verifier rather than trusting polymorphic UUID alone. No evidence => LEGACY_UNKNOWN; inconsistent evidence => UNVERIFIED; revoked => REVOKED. Do not assume Migration232 seed rows can be backfilled from free text.
- A new grant and its authoritative source event, entitlement, receipt, audit and outbox must commit in one PostgreSQL transaction or all roll back. Lock owner/item/source rows in stable order; compare-and-swap version and unique idempotency request hash, fail closed on conflicting replay; a revoke appends a durable event rather than deleting receipt. This feature is read-only to members and never posts WLD, changes ledger balances or creates a monetary reward.
- New SECURITY DEFINER owner-scoped versioned functions with SET search_path=pg_catalog,pg_temp and schema-qualified relations; REVOKE EXECUTE FROM PUBLIC and grant only required app role in the same migration transaction. Remove direct app DML on collections and receipt tables only with audited compatibility adapters and real-PG negative tests; do not blanket-revoke unrelated worker grants. A supplied actor UUID is not itself DB authentication: trusted API session mapping, restricted DB execution and adversarial spoof tests are mandatory. No changes to already-applied migration232 or migration266.

### Administrator, security, privacy, SEO, mobile and KPI
- Admin discrepancy queue: compare receipt/source/owner/version; independently authorized operators may request investigation, not flip VERIFIED by editing a boolean. Manual awards require attributable independent approval and immutable audit; avoid unreviewed privileged override. BOLA/IDOR, guessed IDs, cursor tampering, CSRF, 20 req/min member reads, 5 req/day discrepancy reports, repeated share scraping and source adapter outage fail closed.
- Member pages and evidence API: noindex,nofollow, private/no-store, sitemap-excluded. Optional public showcase must use a separate explicitly consented, minimized projection; no automatic index of member holdings. WCAG 2.2 AA, 320px and 200% zoom, keyboard focus, aria-live state changes, 44px touch targets, reduced motion and locale-aware time; Android parity includes all five evidence states.
- KPIs: false VERIFIED count = 0; cross-owner leakage = 0; receipt/source mismatch backlog by cause; percent VERIFIED vs LEGACY_UNKNOWN (denominator explicitly eligible records), p95 owner GET <500ms in controlled Test, failed evidence reads, discrepancy median resolution time. Aggregate admin metrics k>=20; no raw member IDs/notes/source event payloads in analytics.
- Regression: synthetic 10,001 records, 100 concurrent duplicate grants/revokes, 100 cross-owner attempts, replay with same/different digest, source missing/reversed, seeded 8-row fixture, clock/microsecond cursor, 200% zoom and Android, outage 503 vs real empty, opt-in public projection, direct-DML deny and function allow on isolated PostgreSQL. No runtime tests performed in this planning cycle.
- Rollout: feature collection_verified_receipts_v1 OFF by default; additive migration and shadow read, source adapter audit, negative PG tests, verified source coverage measurement, Test exact-SHA auth E2E and rollback rehearsal, then owner-approved GitOps progressive 1%→10%→100% with abort on any false VERIFIED/BOLA/source mismatch. Rollback toggles OFF and preserves immutable receipts; no destructive schema/data rollback. No direct Test/Production deploy by planning.

### Prioritized development backlog and current blockers
- P0 ENV1320-01 root capacity + isolated backup restore; SEC1320-02 collection app DML/actor trust boundary; REL1320-03 exact-SHA Test/security/GitOps gate; COL1320-04 synthetic ownership and false-success suppression.
- P1 COL1320-05 schema/source registry; COL1320-06 atomic grants/revokes/idempotency; COL1320-07 owner API/cursor/errors; COL1320-08 Next.js/Android/a11y; COL1320-09 privacy/admin/SEO/KPI; COL1320-10 isolated PostgreSQL/release rollback.
- QA v1294 FAILED/HOLD: main frontend 1,397 pass/6 fail, full audit Critical2/High10 and production audit High2. Candidate PR #1412 exact HEAD 1,404 frontend pass and production audit zero, but full audit Critical2/High8 and bot Critical1/High5; main unchanged. Dev app DML 235/241, PUBLIC bank3, migration266 checksum mismatch, authenticated Test exact-SHA/isolated restore unverified. Root 97%/~3.3GiB. RELEASE HOLD.
- External evidence gate: W3C PROV-O, OWASP Authorization and PostgreSQL official function-privilege documentation reviewed as design candidates, NOT admitted to 10,000 independent classified corpus. Newly accepted 0; cumulative independently deduplicated/verified approved UNKNOWN / 10,000, BLOCKED / IN PROGRESS. Previously quoted aggregate UI screenshots are not independently validated source counts. Links: https://www.w3.org/TR/prov-o/ ; https://cheatsheetseries.owasp.org/cheatsheets/Authorization_Cheat_Sheet.html ; https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-createfunction.html . Engineering: https://github.com/wtrdd1-hash/Woldeok-Moneyverse-Migration/issues/1426 .

## v2026.10.26.1318 — F675-136 Support Reply Attention: Pagination and Read Integrity (Feature Improvement / DRAFT / RELEASE HOLD)

**Cycle:** Previous Feature Addition v1316 → Current Feature Improvement v1318 → Next Feature Addition v1320 provisional. Remote main 1c5b84a1c45baafcca83f375a6f99698121e9bb5 and canonical EN/KO checked at start/midpoint. Previous Draft #1416 remains unmerged. Documentation only.

### Verified source gap and user outcome
- Migration 196 `support_thread_messages` orders `created_at ASC,id ASC` and returns the **oldest 200 messages** by default (max 500); `backend/src/support/support.repository.ts` requests 200 and `frontend/src/app/support/page.tsx` renders that truncated slice. New administrator replies may be marked unread yet not appear in the member pane. `support_my_threads` defaults to 50 threads (max 100), so its list is not a global unread total.
- Read-only development DB: 3 threads, 6 messages, 2 administrator replies, zero threads >200 messages. This is a **code-boundary risk**, not a reproduced production incident. Test 201/501/10,001 synthetic messages. Legacy human read state is UNKNOWN.
- Goal: authenticated consented member on web/Android sees recorded **post-activation** administrator replies and explicitly acknowledges only a consecutive interval actually returned to the visible pane. A token cannot prove human reading, DOM paint or external delivery. F675-127 transcript/export and A/B/C/Gemini/QA changes remain separate.

### Member flow, CTA, state, mobile and SEO
- Existing `/support`: `View more threads`, `New recorded admin reply` badge, `Unread new replies` filter, `Load older/newer messages` and `Mark displayed replies viewed` CTA. Versioned owner-only message pages show newest messages rather than only legacy oldest 200; history display keyset `(created_at,id)` preserves PostgreSQL microseconds, but read ordering uses authoritative per-thread admin reply `seq`, never timestamps/UUID.
- Return consecutive post-activation events from `last_seen_seq+1` ascending, default20/max50, paired with actual message bodies. Sign **exact consecutive interval** and message-ID digest. Enable CTA only when that interval is presented in the active pane and member explicitly acts. Missing older page, offscreen/preloaded event or newer concurrent reply must not be marked read. GET/SSR/prefetch/background refresh never writes receipts.
- States `LEGACY_UNKNOWN`, `KNOWN_UNREAD`, `PARTIAL_HISTORY`, `CONTIGUOUS_READY`, `ACK_PENDING`, `ACKED_POST_ACTIVATION`, `STALE_VERSION`, `STALE_PROOF`, `STALE_LIST`, `AUTH_REQUIRED`, `UNAVAILABLE`, `FEATURE_OFF`. If only old capped endpoint works, warn of partial history and disable CTA. Failure/503 is not empty; 50 threads cannot justify global zero.
- Thread list cursor uses immutable `(thread.created_at,thread.id)` with fixed creation cutoff, not mutable last-message ordering. Unread filter changes across HTTP pages: include owner attention revision in signed cursor; if changed, 409 `STALE_LIST` and restart. No multi-request PostgreSQL snapshot claim. Full owner aggregate required for global unread zero. WCAG 2.2 AA, keyboard/ARIA live feedback, 44px targets, 320px/200% zoom, localized dates; `private,no-store`, `noindex,nofollow`, no sitemap/PII analytics.

### API, security and Android parity
- Preserve existing GET `/api/v1/support/threads` and GET `/api/v1/support/threads/:id/messages` signatures. Proposed owner GET `/api/v1/support/threads?view=attention&limit=20&cursor=...`, GET `/api/v1/support/threads/:id/messages-v2?limit=50&cursor=...`, GET `/api/v1/support/threads/:id/attention?fromSeq=...&limit=50`, POST `/api/v1/support/threads/:id/seen` JSON `{displayProof,idempotencyKey:uuid,expectedVersion:bigint}`. Route-map/contract review before implementation; Android member endpoints equivalent, no admin mutation endpoints.
- 600-second HMAC proof binds key version, member, live session fingerprint, thread, base seen seq, contiguous inclusive target seq, canonical message-ID digest, nonce and expiry. POST verifies session/consent/CSRF, owner, signature, committed contiguous events and CAS. Do not accept client-supplied max seq, put token in URL or log it. 401 expired session, 404 foreign/nonexistent indistinguishable, 409 key reuse with different payload/stale list, 412 CAS, 428 missing proof/version, 429 quota, 503 storage unavailable; 410 proof expiry only if consistently specified. Rate 20/member/hour, 60 global/min, page max50.

### PostgreSQL schema, concurrency and rollback
- Migration196 and existing function return types immutable. Add unique `support_admin_reply_events(thread_id,seq,message_id,committed_at)` with `UNIQUE(thread_id,seq)` and `UNIQUE(thread_id,message_id)`, seq>0; owner `support_member_read_watermarks(user_id,thread_id,last_seen_seq>=0,version>=0,updated_at)`; immutable `support_member_seen_receipts(user_id,idempotency_key,payload_hash,thread_id,from_seq,to_seq,committed_version,created_at)`; owner attention revision for filtered-list drift. Index `(thread_id,seq ASC) INCLUDE(message_id)` and `(user_id,thread_id)`, enforce FKs/retention, no bodies in receipts.
- New versioned `support_member_message_page_v2`, `support_member_reply_attention_v2`, `support_member_seen_v2` and `admin_support_reply_v2` are SECURITY DEFINER with fixed `search_path=pg_catalog,pg_temp`, PUBLIC EXECUTE revoked, minimal app EXECUTE and **no new direct app table grants**. Caller-supplied actor UUID is not independent DB authentication: bind to real server session, treat compromised shared DB role as unresolved P0.
- V2 admin reply locks thread `FOR UPDATE`, validates admin session, checks global idempotency key + canonical payload, then commits message, one contiguous seq event, status and audit atomically. Same-key race must serialize or roll back/re-read committed receipt after uniqueness conflict; different payload 409. Replay/audit failure must not increment seq. Legacy v1 reply path stays active while flag OFF; pre-v2 messages remain `LEGACY_UNKNOWN`, not retroactively unread.
- Seen transaction locks owner watermark, verifies all seq in `(currentSeen,target]` were returned consecutively and committed, checks expectedVersion, writes immutable receipt and monotonic watermark atomically. Same-key same-payload replay returns original receipt without rewinding after later ack; different payload 409. New concurrent reply stays unread. Thread `waiting_user` status is independent of read state.
- Additive schema and online indexes → shadow reads → isolated PostgreSQL tests → feature `support_reply_attention_v1` default OFF → v2 writer only after atomicity/legacy regression. Approved rollback switches OFF to unchanged v1 and hides badges, preserving receipts. While ON never silently lose an event: fail v2 transaction/alert. No destructive down migration or planning-run deploy.

### Admin, KPI, acceptance, release gate
- Admin keeps existing reply/status, may see only k>=20 anonymized coverage/error metrics; no member-level read surveillance. Privacy retention approval required. KPIs newest reply visibility, partial-history share, unknown-legacy ratio, false zero target 0, proof reject, 409/412/503 and p95 latency.
- Isolated real PostgreSQL: 201/501/10,001 synthetic messages and 10,001 events no gaps/duplicates; 100 distinct concurrent replies unique contiguous seq, 100 same-key retries one event, 100 foreign-owner probes no leaks; missing-page/prefetch/offscreen proof rejected, two-session CAS no rewind, 599/600/601s expiry, microsecond ties, 50/100 thread limits and stale list restart, audit/insert failure rollback, old functions unchanged, Android/a11y.
- QA v1293 **FAILED/HOLD**: main frontend 1,397 pass/6 fail, full audit 2 Critical/10 High, prod audit 2 High. Draft #1412 exact-SHA CI SUCCESS does not certify Test. Dev app DML 235/241, PUBLIC bank EXECUTE 3, migration266 checksum mismatch; authenticated exact-SHA Test/finance E2E and isolated restore missing. Root 97%/~3.4GiB free, data 84%/~31GiB. Production HOLD.

| P0 | ENV1318-01 | Root/data disk budget, collaborator preservation, isolated backup restore before migration. |

| P0 | REL1318-03 | QA v1293 main regressions/audits/migration266, exact-SHA Test and GitOps release gate. |

| P1 | SUP1318-06 | Thread-locked v2 admin reply/event/status/audit, race-safe idempotency. |

**10,000 independent-source gate:** new approved **0**, cumulative deduplicated/classified approved **UNKNOWN/10,000**, **BLOCKED / IN PROGRESS**. Consulted standards do not count as accepted records. No runtime/DB/WLD/ledger/Test/Production/automation changes.

## v2026.10.26.1316 — F675-136 Verified Member Support Reply Attention and Read Watermarks (Feature Addition / DRAFT / RELEASE HOLD)

**Authority and cycle:** Previous Feature Improvement v1314 → Current Feature Addition v1316 → Next Feature Improvement v1318 (provisional). Remote main `1c5b84a1c45baafcca83f375a6f99698121e9bb5` rechecked at start and midpoint; predecessor Draft #1411 unmerged. Main AGENTS.md, README.md, English PROJECT_PLAN.md and Korean parity are authoritative; this is an isolated docs-only proposal, not implemented behavior. Engineering issue [#1415](https://github.com/wtrdd1-hash/Woldeok-Moneyverse-Migration/issues/1415).

### Source-confirmed gap, purpose, audience, non-goals
- `frontend/src/app/support/page.tsx` renders support thread list, selected messages and replies but no member unread count or explicit acknowledgement. `backend/src/support/support.controller.ts` exposes member list/messages/reply and admin replies/status, not a read watermark. `packages/database/migrations/196-user-admin-support-chat.sql` has support_threads/support_messages, per-thread message order by `(created_at,id)`, member list capped at 50 and member messages at 200; there is no recorded seen state. Existing F675-127 support transcript/status-history Draft is **unmerged** and separate; do not duplicate transcript export or invent historic read receipts.
- **Goal:** let an authenticated member notice *new recorded administrator replies* and explicitly acknowledge the messages they actually displayed, without claiming a human read or email/push delivery. Target active member with consent, including Android. Admin can use existing support inbox but receives only privacy-preserving aggregate health metrics, not member read surveillance. No WLD, ledger, rewards, account changes, auto-resolution, external push or historical reconstruction.

### Entry, UI, CTA, state and error truth
- Entry: existing `/support` conversation list adds `New admin reply` indicator and `Unread replies` filter, and a thread detail `Mark replies viewed through here` button after the verified message pane renders. Do not mark viewed from GET, route prefetch, initial SSR, background polling, notification delivery or an offscreen tab. Show server-verified `knownNewReplies` count only for post-activation recorded admin events.
- States: `LOADING`, `UNTRACKED_LEGACY` (historic read state unknown), `KNOWN_UNREAD`, `ACK_PENDING`, `VIEWED_SINCE_ACTIVATION`, `STALE_VERSION`, `SESSION_EXPIRED`, `UNAVAILABLE`, `FEATURE_OFF`. Distinguish no post-activation events from historical `all read`; unknown/503 must never become a green 0 badge. After timeout, refetch owner read state before success toast; disable action until real rendered server messages and signed display proof exist.
- Use accessible status region, keyboard focus, 44px target where applicable, Korean date labels backed by ISO UTC, 320px mobile layout, 200% zoom and WCAG 2.2 AA. Member private route `noindex,nofollow`, `Cache-Control: private,no-store`, no sitemap, no PII in analytics or URL.

### Exact API, session and Android contract
- `GET /api/v1/support/attention?filter=all|unread&limit=20&cursor=...`: owner-scoped list with `threadId,subject,status,knownNewReplies,historyCoverage,readVersion,lastReplyAt,nextCursor`. HMAC cursor binds actor+session+filter+snapshot ceiling+last `(last_message_at,thread_id)` in full PostgreSQL microsecond precision, key version and expiry; pages are not a multi-request transaction snapshot, and a changed thread may reappear. `unread` filter includes only known post-activation replies; separately disclose untracked historical threads.
- `GET /api/v1/support/threads/:id/attention`: owner-only latest visible reply event and `displayProof` signed by backend for the **highest actually returned/renderable admin reply event**, bound to member/session/thread/seq, 10-minute expiry and server key version; an empty post-activation page can return signed baseline seq 0. Proof issuance is not a read event. Use the existing authenticated support message retrieval, not a new public message endpoint.
- `POST /api/v1/support/threads/:id/seen` JSON `{displayProof:string,idempotencyKey:uuid,expectedVersion:integer}`, session+active account+consent+CSRF. Check owner and session before DB write; verify proof signature, expiry, thread and max displayed seq, then advance only to `max(existingSeenSeq,proofSeq)` with CAS and canonical digest-bound idempotent receipt. Replayed identical key/payload returns the original receipt, different payload 409; no speculative success. 401 unauthenticated, 404 foreign/missing, 409 key reuse, 412 version stale, 428 missing version, 429 throttle, 503 unavailable; return `no-store`. Android `/app-api/v1` must have equivalent typed JSON/error/CSRF/session semantics, never bearer tokens in query strings. Do not change existing v1 support signatures.

### Forward-only DB, transactions and privilege boundaries
- Add `support_admin_reply_events(id uuid PK,thread_id uuid NOT NULL FK,message_id uuid NOT NULL UNIQUE,seq bigint NOT NULL CHECK(seq>0),committed_at timestamptz NOT NULL, UNIQUE(thread_id,seq))`, index `(thread_id,seq DESC)`; event must refer to a real persisted admin message. Add `support_member_read_watermarks(user_id uuid NOT NULL,thread_id uuid NOT NULL,last_seen_seq bigint NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK(last_seen_seq>=0),version bigint NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL,PRIMARY KEY(user_id,thread_id))` with owner/thread FK and check thread owner inside function. Add `support_seen_receipts(user_id,thread_id,request_key uuid UNIQUE,request_digest,committed_seen_seq,version,created_at)` for immutable replay receipts. Do not backfill guessed pre-feature read events; mark historical coverage `partial`.
- Forward-only migration must preserve migration 196 functions' signatures. Update `admin_support_reply` implementation only in a **separate engineering migration**, not this planning PR: lock `support_threads` row FOR UPDATE, perform existing idempotent message insert and status/audit, and append exactly one new admin reply event for each newly committed admin message with `seq=COALESCE(MAX(seq),0)+1` while holding the thread lock. Same-key replay reads the committed message/event and does not increment; cross-actor/different-body replay fails. If event/audit insert fails, the entire reply transaction rolls back; do not return success. Existing member replies and admin status-only changes do not increment reply seq. Ensure thread deletion/retention policy explicitly governs event/receipt deletion.
- Versioned `SECURITY DEFINER` functions with fixed `search_path=pg_catalog,pg_temp`, explicit PUBLIC revoke, app EXECUTE allowlist, no new direct table DML/SELECT. The `p_actor` UUID is not proof of identity because the app role is shared: server session binding plus database privilege hardening and 100 impersonation tests are P0. Owner checks happen on every read/write and event existence is not leaked through timing/status. Use an index supporting member list and `(thread_id,seq)` for O(log n) unread counts; EXPLAIN synthetic 10,001 rows. CAS lock watermark row, reject proof seq greater than committed event ceiling, keep monotonic watermark and receipt in one transaction.

### Abuse, admin, KPIs, tests and deployment
- Limit seen writes to 20/member/hour and 60/minute globally (tunable); max page 50, default 20, cursor TTL 10 minutes. Never include message bodies, subject text, account IDs, or admin identity in aggregate telemetry. Admin dashboard only aggregate coverage/503/p95 with k>=20, no member-by-member seen timestamps. Respect retention, deletion and merge policies; no silent ownership transfer.
- KPIs: known admin-reply attention coverage, explicit acknowledgment success rate, stale CAS rate, p95 list latency, unknown-legacy ratio, BOLA denial rate; do not equate seen acknowledgment to human reading or resolution.
- Isolated PostgreSQL acceptance: 100 simultaneous admin replies → unique contiguous seq for each committed new message, 100 same-key replays → one event, 100 foreign-thread reads/acks → no disclosure, 10,001 synthetic events paged without missing/duplicate rows, two sessions concurrent seen updates never decrease watermark, 599/600/601-second proof expiry boundary, SSR/prefetch never acknowledges, 401/404/409/412/428/429/503, admin revoke, message deletion, replay after unknown commit, migration rollback, Android contract and WCAG regression. **No runtime tests performed by this planning run.**
- Feature flag `support_reply_attention_v1` defaults OFF. Engineering branch separate from F675-127 and A/B/C/Gemini/QA. Before enabling: resolve dev app DML 235/241, PUBLIC bank EXECUTE 3, migration 266 checksum, QA v1292 main frontend 6 failures and dependency audits, root capacity, isolated backup restore, official exact-SHA CI + authenticated Test finance E2E, security/QA signoff and approved GitOps staged 1%→10%→100%. Rollback flag OFF and prior API/UI, retain append-only event history for audit; never modify applied migrations or Production data as part of planning.

| Priority | ID | Acceptance / owner handoff |
| --- | --- | --- |
| P0 | ENV1316-01 | Budget root/data space, preserve other worker worktrees, verify isolated DB backup restore before migration. |
| P0 | SEC1316-02 | Bind actor to live session; deny 100 cross-owner/impersonation attempts; remove unsafe app DML/PUBLIC privileges via reviewed forward-only fix. |
| P0 | REL1316-03 | QA v1292, audit, migration266, official exact-SHA CI, authenticated Test E2E and GitOps HOLD gate. |

| P1 | SUP1316-05 | Additive reply event/watermark/receipt schema, versioned owner SECDEF, indexes and explain plan. |
| P1 | SUP1316-06 | Thread-locked atomic admin reply/event/idempotency with replay and rollback proofs. |
| P1 | SUP1316-07 | Signed display proof, monotonic seen CAS, stable owner cursor and typed API failures. |
| P1 | SUP1316-08 | Next member CTA and Android App API parity, mobile keyboard/WCAG. |
