## v2026.10.26.1314 — F675-135 Privacy Case Materialization, Independent Authority and Evidence Integrity (Feature Improvement / DRAFT / RELEASE HOLD)

**Cycle and provenance:** Previous Feature Addition v1312 → Current Feature Improvement v1314 → Next Feature Addition v1316 (provisional). Rechecked remote main `1c5b84a1c45baafcca83f375a6f99698121e9bb5` and AGENTS.md, README.md, canonical PROJECT_PLAN.md and Korean parity at start/mid; predecessor Draft #1407 is unmerged. This is a design delta on F675-135, not a second feature or implementation. No runtime/DB/WLD/ledger/Test/Production write.

### Verified incompatibilities and threat model
- `packages/database/migrations/003-mvp-features.sql` creates `admin_role` enum operator/approver/server_operator; later superadmin designation materializes legacy roles. **No privacy_officer grant exists**. `057-superadmin-authority-and-admin-sessions.sql` explicitly retires generic two-person approval with SQLSTATE 55000. Do not reuse disabled approval functions or infer privacy authority from `AdminGuard` (any nonempty admin role), operator, approver or superadmin. A distinct privacy-specific authorization and reviewer enrollment/revocation workflow is a prerequisite, not an optional UI check.
- `015-privacy-data-subject-requests.sql` inserts owner-scoped idempotent `received` receipts with audit in the same transaction and exposes `privacy_list_my_requests(actor,limit)` (max100; controller currently defaults30). No case rows exist on main. Preserve original `status='received'`, signatures and submission audit. Existing `frontend/src/app/account/privacy/page.tsx` uses `apiOrNull`: a fetch failure is not a verified empty; older receipts beyond first30 are not absent.
- `backend/src/auth/guards/reauth.guard.ts` uses 900 seconds; v1312 sensitive case completion/denial promises <=600 seconds. Add a **privacy-disposition-only <=600s** server-clock guard, check both before transition and at DB commit authorization; do not silently change the global guard. Explicitly test 599/600/601 seconds, expired/revoked admin sessions and role removal during approval.
- PostgreSQL SECURITY DEFINER functions receiving `p_actor uuid` are **not proof of caller identity**: a compromised shared app DB role could pass another UUID. Treat actor binding as a P0 architectural threat. Separate least-privilege privacy workflow DB credential/service boundary, verify active admin session and explicit officer grant at each operation, forbid direct DML/PUBLIC EXECUTE, audit access and test impersonation with the actual app role. No release until the reviewed binding demonstrates fail-closed behavior; a caller-set custom GUC alone is not an authentication proof.

### Zero-fabrication materialization, queue and owner UX
- Implement additive `privacy_request_cases`, `privacy_request_case_events`, `privacy_request_case_receipts`, `privacy_officer_grants` (officer_id, can_review, can_approve, granted_by, granted_at, revoked_at, version; only privileged provisioning API can mutate). **Bootstrap requires two distinct approved active humans** with reviewed grant provenance. A single superadmin cannot act as both. Use case state `RECEIVED` only when a case row is actually materialized; missing case row returns `RECEIVED_NOT_TRIAGED` from the original owner receipt, not a false completed status. Do not expose internal grant/audit details to members.
- Forward-only rollout sequence: (1) create case schema, grants and bounded trigger in a short transaction with `lock_timeout` and retry/rollback; trigger creates an initial case only for **newly inserted** original receipts in the same transaction; (2) idempotently backfill historical receipts in <=500-row batches with `INSERT ... ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING`, checkpoint/progress counts, no rewrite of migration015; (3) run anti-join gap count `SELECT count(*) FROM privacy_data_subject_requests r LEFT JOIN privacy_request_cases c ON c.request_id=r.id WHERE c.request_id IS NULL` = 0, and orphan/event uniqueness/owner consistency checks under a stable verification boundary; (4) keep flag OFF until these checks and isolated restore PASS. If a batch fails, leave flag OFF and old receipt UI working; never silently drop historical requests. Install trigger before batches to avoid new-request gaps; prove no untracked insert during cutover. Avoid a long blocking backfill transaction; benchmark indexes and lock impact in isolated PostgreSQL. **Trigger failure also aborts the original v1 submission transaction**: failure-inject this path before activation, require an approved forward-only trigger-disable/repair runbook and prove old submission recovery. Feature flag OFF by itself does not disable a PostgreSQL trigger; the operational rollback must account for it.
- Owner route `/account/privacy/requests/[requestId]` shows verified original receipt and separate case timeline with **View case progress**, **Refresh**, **Back**, **New request**; no-case `RECEIVED_NOT_TRIAGED`, LOADING, VERIFIED_CASE, AUTH_REQUIRED, NOT_FOUND (foreign indistinguishable), SOURCE_UNAVAILABLE (503), STALE_VERSION. Admin `/admin/privacy/requests` uses queue/list/detail, **Assign**, **Review**, **Request info**, **Submit evidence digest**, **Request independent approval**, **Approve/Reject**, **Close unfulfilled**. Queue must include every unprocessed historical receipt after backfill; 200/empty only after authoritative successful query. Existing v1 GET/POST remain unchanged; avoid pagination gaps from 30-record legacy list by a new owner-only cursor query for history.
- Distinguish `AWAITING_APPROVAL` from `COMPLETED_VERIFIED`: submitter/first reviewer cannot be second approver; check two different active grants, fresh independent admin sessions and non-revoked roles at decision time. Require separately reviewed real-world fulfillment evidence **reference digest and evidence type**, independent second review, audit chain and evidence retention policy. A digest is a pointer/commitment, **not proof** the requested deletion/correction happened; show member-facing `COMPLETED_VERIFIED` only after approved manual fulfillment check. Do not automatically erase/export/account-restrict, and do not assert legal compliance or statutory deadlines.

### API, database, security and consistency acceptance
- Existing `GET/POST /api/v1/privacy/requests` unchanged. New owner `GET /api/v1/privacy/requests/:requestId/case`, `GET /api/v1/privacy/requests/history?limit&cursor`; operator `GET /api/v1/admin/privacy/requests?state&type&limit&cursor`, `GET /:id`, `POST /:id/transitions`, and separate grant-provisioning surface. Actor/role/session from server authentication only. `If-Match: "case-vN"` and UUID `Idempotency-Key`; 428 missing precondition, 412 stale version, 409 conflicting payload/disallowed transition, 403 missing officer grant, 404 foreign/missing, 401 reauth/session, 429 throttling with Retry-After, 503 source/audit failure. Never map 503 to `[]`, never echo free-text request detail in broad admin queue.
- DB: `privacy_request_cases(request_id PK FK RESTRICT,state,version bigint >=0,assignee,updated_at)`; append-only `privacy_request_case_events(request_id,version,actor,event_code,reason_code,evidence_type,evidence_digest,created_at, UNIQUE(request_id,version))`; immutable `privacy_request_case_receipts(request_id,idempotency_key,command_hash,committed_version, PRIMARY KEY(request_id,idempotency_key))`; grants revocable and separate from `admin_role`. FK/unique/check constraints, queue `(state,updated_at DESC,request_id DESC)`, events `(request_id,version DESC)`, owner `(requester_user_id,created_at DESC,id DESC)`. Versioned SECURITY DEFINER with `search_path=pg_catalog,pg_temp`, schema-qualified relations, migrator ownership, REVOKE PUBLIC and app direct table privileges, narrowly scoped EXECUTE.
- One atomic transition: verify actor/admin session/grant/recent reauth, lock original request then case and receipt in deterministic order, compare version, validate state, check idempotency hash, assert distinct second approver and evidence, insert immutable event, CAS version+state, insert immutable receipt, append redacted admin audit, COMMIT then success. Any audit/event/receipt failure ROLLBACK. Identical replay returns the exact committed receipt without duplicating event; different payload with same key => 409; role revoked before commit => fail. Do not claim row lock on UUID alone proves authorization. Queue HMAC keyset `(updated_at,id)` with PostgreSQL microseconds, role/filter/session epoch/TTL<=15min; no cross-HTTP MVCC snapshot promise.
- Privacy/accessibility/analytics: no sensitive staff notes, raw evidence, IDs in telemetry, IP/email/UA or PII in public API. Private `no-store`, `noindex,nofollow`, sitemap excluded, strict CSP. Member Android app-api parity only (no operator routes); keyboard/ARIA live error, 320px and 200% zoom, WCAG 2.2 AA. Admin aggregates only cohorts >=20 distinct users. KPI backlog age p50/p95, materialization gap count, approval separation violations, 401/403/409/412/503, audit failures; internal 72h target is not a legal SLA. Retention/legal-basis and out-of-band deleted-user requests need privacy counsel review.
- Isolated PG negative tests: 100 foreign-owner probes, 100 parallel transitions, 10,001 synthetic receipt/case cursor ties, replay same/different, no-case/legacy 30+ histories, 501-row batch boundary and mid-batch failure, concurrent original insert during trigger install/backfill, missing-case anti-join, active officer revocation, one-human two roles, superadmin-only denial, 599/600/601s reauth, app-role actor spoofing, audit failure rollback, privilege matrix and immutable evidence, 503-vs-empty, Android parity, 320px WCAG, no WLD/ledger/account mutations. Compare original migration015 signatures and checksum, verify approved isolated restore, CI/security and exact integrated SHA authenticated Test before authorized GitOps 1%→10%→100%. Flag `privacy_case_workflow_v1` OFF on any breach; forward-only repair, preserve historical receipts and audit.

### P0/P1 engineering backlog (4 P0 + 6 P1)
| Priority | ID | Acceptance |

## v2026.10.26.1312 — F675-135 Verified Privacy Request Case Triage & Member Status Timeline (Feature Addition / DRAFT / RELEASE HOLD)

**Cycle/provenance:** Previous Feature Improvement v1310 → Current Feature Addition v1312 → Next Feature Improvement v1314 (provisional). Authoritative remote main `1c5b84a1c45baafcca83f375a6f99698121e9bb5` rechecked at start/mid; read main AGENTS.md, README.md, canonical PROJECT_PLAN.md and Korean parity, and source code in the exact-main QA checkout. Stacked on unmerged v1310 Draft #1402; documentation-only, no production claim. Separate from Gemini, development A/B/C, QA and existing privacy submission code.

### Verified gap, purpose, boundaries
- Main `frontend/src/app/account/privacy/page.tsx` already displays a submission form and latest records, using `apiOrNull` for GET `/api/v1/privacy/requests`. `backend/src/privacy/privacy.controller.ts` exposes only authenticated GET and CSRF-protected POST; `packages/database/migrations/015-privacy-data-subject-requests.sql` constrains `status CHECK(status='received')`, provides `privacy_create_my_request` and `privacy_list_my_requests`, and explicitly has **no staff queue or fulfillment workflow**. Thus a recorded request is NOT evidence of review, action, completion, or legal compliance. Main `AdminGuard` accepts any nonempty admin role: it is NOT by itself a privacy-officer authorization policy.
- **Goal:** permit a specifically authorized privacy officer to triage an already-submitted request, record review and verified outcomes, and let its owner see a truthful, minimally disclosed timeline. **Targets:** authenticated submitting member, privacy officer, separate approving privacy officer, audited compliance operator. No automatic export, correction, deletion, consent withdrawal, account freeze, WLD/ledger mutation, outbound email, or legal-SLA guarantee in v1. Human-reviewed fulfillment stays a separately gated operational process.
- **Design choice:** keep immutable original submission `received` and existing v1 functions/signatures unchanged. Add a distinct case lifecycle and v2 owner read. Legacy `received` is the *submission receipt state*, never silently relabeled as processing outcome. The member page displays a separate case-state badge only from verified v2 evidence. Existing legacy clients remain compatible.

### User and operator flows, entry points, permissions, CTAs, states
- Member entry `/account/privacy` → existing request row → **View case progress** → `/account/privacy/requests/[requestId]`; show submitted timestamp/type, opaque receipt ID, case state, public-safe event timestamps and human-readable reason category (never staff notes or evidence contents). CTAs **Refresh status**, **Back to privacy center**, **Submit additional request** (new request only; no unreviewed mutation of prior submission). Do not promise notification or statutory deadline. Empty/no-case means **RECEIVED / NOT YET TRIAGED**, not COMPLETED.
- Operator entry `/admin/privacy/requests` → **Open case**, **Assign to me**, **Start review**, **Request information** (manual safe instruction category, no contact details), **Record verified fulfillment**, **Close unfulfilled with reason**, **Escalate**; all state-changing CTAs require confirmation, fresh CSRF and explicit version. Separate second privacy officer must approve verified completion; no self-approval or self-assignment privilege escalation. Queue filters by state/type/age, page size 25/max 50, server-side search by exact opaque request UUID only (no free-text sensitive detail search).
- Member UI state machine: LOADING → VERIFIED_RECEIPT_ONLY | VERIFIED_IN_REVIEW | VERIFIED_NEEDS_INFO | VERIFIED_COMPLETED | VERIFIED_CLOSED_UNFULFILLED; distinct AUTH_REQUIRED, CONSENT_REQUIRED, NOT_FOUND, SOURCE_UNAVAILABLE, STALE_VERSION. Admin: QUEUE_LOADING, QUEUE_EMPTY, QUEUE_ROWS, CLAIM_CONFLICT, APPROVAL_PENDING, ROLE_DENIED, SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE. Only successful owner-filtered 200/empty yields a verified empty; 401/403/404/409/412/428/429/503 and malformed bodies must not become empty/success. Logout/account switch invalidates in-flight data, caches, cursor and action state.
- **Case transitions:** RECEIVED → IN_REVIEW | NEEDS_INFORMATION; NEEDS_INFORMATION → IN_REVIEW | CLOSED_UNFULFILLED; IN_REVIEW → NEEDS_INFORMATION | AWAITING_APPROVAL | CLOSED_UNFULFILLED; AWAITING_APPROVAL → COMPLETED_VERIFIED (second officer, evidence prerequisite) | IN_REVIEW (rejected). CLOSED states terminal; reopen only by new, separately audited request. `COMPLETED_VERIFIED` is a **manually evidenced outcome**, not proof of platform-side automatic execution. Never claim fulfillment based on clicking a button alone.

### API, data model, transaction and authorization contract
- Existing POST/GET `/api/v1/privacy/requests` stay unchanged. New `GET /api/v1/privacy/requests/:requestId/case` returns owner-only `{requestId,submissionStatus:'received',caseState,version,updatedAt,publicEvents:[{state,occurredAt,reasonCategory}]}`; 404 for nonexistent/foreign IDs without existence oracle, 503 on DB failure. New admin `GET /api/v1/admin/privacy/requests?state&type&limit&cursor`, `GET /api/v1/admin/privacy/requests/:id`, `POST /api/v1/admin/privacy/requests/:id/transitions` body `{action,reasonCode,evidenceDigest?,idempotencyKey}` with `If-Match: "case-vN"`. API derives actor and session server-side, never accepts a caller-supplied actor/role. 428 missing If-Match, 412 stale, 409 replay-key changed payload or disallowed transition, 403 role/approval, 404 hidden foreign, 429 throttled, 503 fail-closed. No API returns original free-text `detail` to general admins.
- Add forward-only migration: `privacy_request_cases(request_id uuid PRIMARY KEY REFERENCES privacy_data_subject_requests(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT, state text CHECK enum, version bigint NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK(version>=0), assignee_user_id uuid NULL, updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL)`; `privacy_request_case_events(id uuid PK, request_id FK, version bigint NOT NULL, actor_user_id uuid NOT NULL, event_code enum, reason_code enum NULL, evidence_sha256 bytea NULL CHECK(octet_length=32), created_at timestamptz, UNIQUE(request_id,version))`; `privacy_request_case_receipts(request_id,idempotency_key UUID,command_hash bytea CHECK 32, committed_version bigint, PRIMARY KEY(request_id,idempotency_key))`. Never store raw evidence files, passwords, tokens, email addresses, IPs or arbitrary free-text staff comments in case tables. Index queue `(state,updated_at DESC,request_id DESC)`, owner timeline through existing submission owner index plus event `(request_id,version DESC)`. Check index/query plans in isolated PostgreSQL; no new index on Production without review.
- Add versioned owner-read, role-filtered queue/detail, and bounded transition `SECURITY DEFINER` functions; keep migration 015 signatures intact. Fixed `search_path=pg_catalog,pg_temp`, schema-qualified references, migrator owner, REVOKE PUBLIC and direct table privileges, grant minimal EXECUTE only. **P0 threat model:** `p_actor` UUID is not DB-authenticated identity; `AdminGuard` any-role is insufficient. Require independent DB-verified privacy-officer permission + active admin session and explicit authorization for second approver; test app-role compromise and privilege escalation. No blanket table DML/SELECT grants.
- Single transaction: verify authenticated actor/role/session and owner boundary, lock submission then case in consistent UUID order, validate `If-Match` version and state transition, check idempotency receipt payload hash, require distinct approver and a reviewed evidence digest for COMPLETED_VERIFIED, append immutable event, advance version, insert receipt, append redacted admin audit, COMMIT before 200. On audit failure rollback all. Identical replay returns original committed result; same key with different payload 409; 100 concurrent requests must yield one winning transition and no skipped version. Retain existing original request `status='received'`; do not overwrite migration 015.
- Queue cursor: signed HMAC `(updated_at,id)` microsecond-precise tuple, role/filters/session-epoch/expiry bound, `limit+1`, max 50, <=15min TTL; no cross-HTTP snapshot claim. Admin role loss or session revoke invalidates cursor and prepared UI. Rate limit operator mutations 20/hour/operator and owner refresh 60/hour/member; return Retry-After on 429. CSRF, admin console session, <=600s reauth for case completion/denial, dual control and independent audit required.

### Privacy, accessibility, analytics, tests and release
- Noindex/nofollow, `Cache-Control: private,no-store`, robots/sitemap exclusion, strict CSP, no sensitive telemetry; member sees own case only, admin dashboard aggregates by category with cohort >=20 and suppression of low counts. Retention/erasure, lawful bases and legal response deadlines require **separate privacy/legal review**; never imply this feature itself fulfills statutory rights. Account-deleted member access remains a reviewed out-of-band process; do not weaken current active-user boundary.
- Web: Korean clear state labels, keyboard-focus restore, accessible confirmation dialogs, 320px/200% zoom, WCAG 2.2 AA; mobile Android app-api parity for owner case detail and typed failures, no admin role surfaced in member app. KPI: submitted→triaged p50/p95, cases pending > internal 72h *operational target only*, completion-with-evidence ratio, 403/409/412/503, privacy incident count; no raw user IDs or request details in analytics.
- Isolated real-PG tests: migration 015 unchanged; 100 foreign-owner reads and admin role probes; no-case, deleted-account, 100 concurrent transitions, replay same/changed payload, stale version, admin role removal, dual-approval same actor forbidden, missing evidence, audit failure rollback, 10,001 synthetic case rows with microsecond/tie cursor, SQL injection, no sensitive admin/member payload, no-store/noindex, Android parity, CSRF/reauth at 599/600/601s, queue timeouts and 503-vs-empty. Confirm no WLD/ledger/account deletion mutation.
- Rollout: `privacy_case_workflow_v1` **OFF** by default; separate engineering branch and forward-only reviewed migration, isolated restore and negative ACL tests, green security audits/front regressions, official authenticated exact integrated-main SHA Test E2E and backup restore, authorized GitOps progressive 1%→10%→100% with error/queue/KPI guardrails. Flag OFF stops writes and hides new UI without deleting append-only events; rollback DB via forward-only compatible repair, not destructive migration. **No Production promotion from this planning PR.**

| Priority | ID | Independently testable acceptance |
| --- | --- | --- |
| P0 | ENV1312-01 | Root capacity 95%/~5.3GiB and separate volume 82%/~34GiB; owner-coordinated reversible mitigation, preserve worktrees, verify isolated backup restore and disk budget. |
| P0 | SEC1312-02 | DB app DML/PUBLIC bank function boundary, active session + explicit privacy-officer role (not any AdminGuard role), owner isolation and two-person completion authorization; 100 BOLA negatives. |
| P0 | REL1312-03 | QA v1290 FAILED/HOLD: frontend 1,397 PASS/6 FAIL; prod audit 2 High, full audit 2 Critical/10 High; migration266 provenance, exact-SHA authenticated Test/restore and GitOps HOLD. |

## v2026.10.26.1310 — F675-134 Security Activity Archive Trust and Export Reauthentication Integrity (Feature Improvement / DRAFT / RELEASE HOLD)

**Cycle and provenance:** Previous Feature Addition v1308 → Current Feature Improvement v1310 → Next Feature Addition v1312 (provisional). Authoritative remote main `1c5b84a1c45baafcca83f375a6f99698121e9bb5`; source AGENTS.md, README.md, PROJECT_PLAN.md (EN canonical) and PROJECT_PLAN.ko.md checked. Stacked on unmerged v1308 Draft #1399; this is a documentation-only change, not a release.

### Verified source gaps and correction of v1308 assumptions
- Main `backend/src/auth/guards/reauth.guard.ts` sets `REAUTHENTICATION_WINDOW_SECONDS=900` (15 min). v1308 promised **<=600 seconds (10 min)** for CSV export. Reusing the existing `ReauthGuard` alone violates the proposed policy; engineer a **dedicated export-only 600-second reauthentication policy**, without silently shortening the global 900-second guard for existing sensitive actions. Server clock is authoritative; `now - reauthenticated_at <= 600s` at authorization AND again before sending buffered bytes. If expiry or session revocation occurs during preparation, discard the buffer and return a typed 401 `reauthentication_required`/401 `session_expired`; never send partial CSV. Existing `CsrfGuard` must also pass. No claim of real download completion after HTTP success.
- Main `account-security.controller.ts` already applies SessionGuard/AuthenticatedGuard/ConsentGuard and uses `@Get('events')`; `account-security.repository.ts` calls `account_recent_security_events($1,20)`. Migration 214 returns only event_type,created_at, caps 50 and sorts by UUID tie-break internally; adding a UUID to its RETURNS TABLE via CREATE OR REPLACE would break PostgreSQL compatibility. **Add versioned `account_security_event_history_v1` and preserve existing function signature.**
- Main Next `/account/security/page.tsx` uses `apiOrNull`, `eventData?.events ?? []`, renders only `events.slice(0,5)`; it already shows a fetch-error message when `eventData === null`. New archive/BFF MUST preserve explicit 401/403/410/413/429/503 statuses, and must not infer VERIFIED_EMPTY from `apiOrNull` or a missing JSON key. Existing five-card preview is unaffected.
- Migration 180 `auth_security_events` contains UUID id, nullable user_id ON DELETE SET NULL, event_type, metadata JSONB and created_at; migrations 180/183/209/212/215 insert **local-email registration/login/password/email-change events**. No evidence that all OAuth logins, logouts, session revocations or suspicious attempts are written. Source coverage `complete:false` and clear 'recorded local-account events only where available' label; no missing-event fabrication, no metadata/IP/UA/email leakage. Read-only dev sample from v1308: 41 events/3 users; no claim of Production coverage.

### UX, entry point, permissions and status contract
- Existing authenticated /account/security → 'View recorded activity' → /account/security/activity. Search by UTC inclusive `from`, exclusive `to`, category enum ALL/SIGN_IN/CREDENTIAL/ACCOUNT_CHANGE/OTHER, default 90d/max 365d. GET page size default25/max50. Explicit Apply, Reset, Next, Prepare CSV, Reauthenticate and Retry CTAs; no free-text metadata search. Show last-known data only if clearly marked stale and never export stale cache.
- State machine LOADING → VERIFIED_ROWS | VERIFIED_EMPTY | FILTER_NO_MATCH; independent SOURCE_UNAVAILABLE, AUTH_REQUIRED, CONSENT_REQUIRED, REAUTH_REQUIRED, EXPORT_PREPARING, EXPORT_READY_SERVER_PREPARED, EXPORT_TOO_LARGE, RATE_LIMITED, SESSION_CHANGED. HTTP 200 with zero rows is the ONLY VERIFIED_EMPTY. 503/timeout or malformed body = SOURCE_UNAVAILABLE; failed export never becomes a downloaded/saved claim. On account switch/logout, cancel or fence in-flight fetch/export and clear cursor, prepared blob and visible private rows.
- Accessibility: 320px/200% zoom, keyboard focus and live-region error/prepare status, 44px preferred targets, WCAG 2.2 AA. Noindex/nofollow, no-store, private sitemap exclusion. App API for Android must use its native authenticated transport, equivalent typed errors and secure native download; never copy web cookies or persist CSV in shared storage.

### API, cursor, database and transaction acceptance
- `GET /api/v1/account/security/events/history?from&to&category&limit&cursor`: derive actor and session from verified server session, never request body or query; response `{items:[{id,type,category,occurredAt}],nextCursor,hasMore,coverage:{source:'auth_security_events',complete:false}}`. No metadata/userId/email/IP/UA. 400 malformed filters/cursor; 401 expired session; 403 consent; 410 expired or revoked cursor; 429 rate limit; 503 DB failure. BFF must preserve error classes and `Cache-Control: private,no-store`.
- Stable DESC keyset `(created_at,id)` from **raw PostgreSQL microsecond precision**, never JS `Date.toISOString()` as the cursor source. HMAC envelope versioned with key-id, actor, session epoch, normalized filter digest, last tuple, upper cutoff and <=15min expiry; constant-time signature check, reject cross-member/session/filter/key-version mismatch. `LIMIT limit+1`, tie-safe equal timestamp+UUID, bounded 10,001 synthetic rows. Cross-HTTP pages do not share an MVCC snapshot; late backfills may appear even below cutoff. Explicit refresh-on-change policy rather than 'immutable history' promise.
- Versioned SECURITY DEFINER `account_security_event_history_v1` with explicit public-qualified identifiers, fixed search_path, owner moneyverse_migrator, REVOKE PUBLIC and EXECUTE for minimum necessary role only; owner WHERE user_id=p_actor, NULL owner excluded. Benchmark optional `(user_id,created_at DESC,id DESC)` index using isolated PostgreSQL EXPLAIN, forward-only migration only, no applied migration edits. **An app-supplied actor UUID is not DB-authenticated identity**; API SessionGuard and negative cross-owner tests are necessary but not sufficient for an app-role compromise. P0 review the threat model and actual dev grants (235/241 writable; 3 PUBLIC bank EXECUTE), no false security boundary claim. No shared dev/Production DB mutations.
- `POST /api/v1/account/security/events/export` body `{from,to,category}`; verified session, consent, CSRF, **export-specific <=600s reauth**, rate 3/day/member and one concurrent preparation. Prepare UTF-8 fixed-schema `event_id,event_type,event_category,occurred_at_utc` entirely in bounded memory under a single READ ONLY REPEATABLE READ snapshot; <=5,000 rows, <=2MiB exact encoded bytes, <=30s end-to-end including gateway deadline. No root-disk spool. Validate and encode all rows before response headers, formula-injection guards for =,+,-,@,tabs/CR/LF plus proper quoting; spreadsheet behavior varies, never promise universal immunity. Exceed limit: 413 without partial success; source/timeout/audit error: 503 before body. SHA-256 only for the exact emitted byte sequence, `Digest` header only if end-to-end bytes match; `Content-Disposition: attachment`, `nosniff`, `no-store`. Prepared-only audit (count, hash, timestamp, no sensitive metadata) commits in separate transaction BEFORE response; on audit failure discard buffer. A repeated POST is a new preparation unless a reviewed immutable idempotency receipt design exists.
- Admin: only aggregate metrics at cohort >=20 distinct members, no member-level exports; retention and account deletion semantics unchanged. Analytics: preparation attempted/ready/failure, 413/429/503, p95 query <300ms at 10,001 isolated rows, no raw IDs/timestamps/CSV contents. Security negative tests: 100 foreign-owner probes, 100 concurrent prepares, 10,001 microsecond/tie rows, NULL owner, session/consent/CSRF/reauth boundaries at 599/600/601 seconds, session revoked during buffer, audit failure, 5,001 rows, 2MiB+1 byte, 30s+1, CSV formula/quotes/CRLF, BFF 503 vs empty, mobile, logout fencing, flag OFF rollback and original preview regression.

| Priority | ID | Owner acceptance |
| --- | --- | --- |
| P0 | ENV1310-01 | Root 94%/~6.5GiB free now (was 100%/~467MiB in QA v1289); determine external recovery ownership and sustained margin, preserve worktrees, verify independent backup restore; do not close capacity risk on one reading. |
| P0 | SEC1310-02 | Actor/session boundary and least privilege: 235/241 dev app DML and 3 PUBLIC bank EXECUTE; cross-owner/NULL owner negatives and privacy; no shared DB writes. |
| P0 | REL1310-03 | Main QA v1289 FAILED/HOLD, six frontend regressions, production/full/bot audits, migration266 checksum drift, exact-SHA CI/Test authenticated E2E, restore and GitOps release gates. |
| P0 | AUTH1310-04 | Export 600s reauth mismatch with existing 900s global guard, session-revocation/CSRF race, no false export success, source coverage and 503-vs-empty. |
| P1 | AUTH1310-05 | Additive versioned SECDEF owner read, PostgreSQL-compatible return signature, EXPLAIN-backed index and strict NULL owner exclusion. |
| P1 | AUTH1310-06 | HMAC microsecond-safe keyset cursor with session/filter/expiry/cutoff, late-backfill truth and 10,001-row fixture. |
| P1 | AUTH1310-07 | Memory-bounded complete-before-headers CSV, exact digest, 5k/2MiB/30s limits, audit failure and formula defenses. |
| P1 | AUTH1310-08 | Nest/Next/Android status parity, 320px WCAG, login/logout/account-switch response fencing and no-store. |

## v2026.10.26.1308 — F675-134 Verified Member Security Activity Archive and Safe CSV Export (Feature Addition / DRAFT / RELEASE HOLD)

**Cycle:** Previous Improvement v1306 / Current Addition v1308 / Next Improvement v1310 provisional. Remote main 1c5b84a1c45baafcca83f375a6f99698121e9bb5 checked start/mid, canonical AGENTS.md, README.md, PROJECT_PLAN.md and Korean parity. Predecessor v1306 Draft #1394 unmerged; docs-only isolated branch.

### Evidence, purpose and truthful scope
- Actual main: /account/security calls GET /api/v1/account/security/events; backend AccountSecurityRepository.recentEvents calls migration 214 account_recent_security_events(actor,20), but UI displays only events.slice(0,5). Function caps 50 and returns event_type,created_at, not UUID. Preserve existing endpoint/card.
- Migration 180 auth_security_events: UUID id PK, nullable user_id, event_type, metadata JSONB, created_at, index (user_id,created_at DESC). Dev PostgreSQL READ ONLY at 2026-10-26 10:10 KST: 41 rows/3 users, 39 local_login_succeeded, 2 local_registration_completed. NOT proof of all logins, OAuth, logout, device events or Production security incident. No metadata/IP/email/UA/secrets in output.
- Goal: members can browse/filter actual recorded account-security events beyond the five-card preview and safely prepare a private CSV. No synthetic events, WLD, ledger, account or session mutation. Distinct from F675-132 session aliases and F675-131 notification archive.

### Member experience, roles, states and accessibility
- Existing /account/security card gains View recorded activity CTA to new /account/security/activity, only for authenticated consented member. Headline says Recorded security activity — not a complete sign-in history. Date range default 90 days, max 365, UTC source and locale display. Server categories ALL, SIGN_IN, CREDENTIAL, ACCOUNT_CHANGE, OTHER; Apply/Clear/Next/Download CSV CTAs. No free-text search of metadata.
- States LOADING, VERIFIED_ROWS, VERIFIED_EMPTY (successful zero), FILTER_NO_MATCH, SOURCE_UNAVAILABLE (503/timeout, never empty), AUTH_REQUIRED, REAUTH_REQUIRED, EXPORT_PREPARING, EXPORT_READY (server bytes prepared, NOT user-saved), EXPORT_TOO_LARGE, RATE_LIMITED. Unknown type generic; no inferred device/location trust. 320px mobile, keyboard, screen reader/live status, reduced motion, WCAG 2.2 AA, 44px preferred touch targets.

### API, cursor, Android
- New GET /api/v1/account/security/events/history?from&to&category&limit&cursor. Actor from verified server session only. Default 25/max 50. Response items[{id,type,category,occurredAt}],nextCursor,hasMore,coverage{source:auth_security_events,complete:false,earliestAvailableAt:null-or-ISO}. complete:false until independently audited coverage. Exclude user_id, metadata, IP, UA, email, token.
- Keyset ORDER BY created_at DESC,id DESC with PostgreSQL microseconds; HMAC cursor binds actor, session, filters, high-water, last (timestamp,UUID), key version, <=15 min expiry. Reject tampered/filter mismatch 400; unauthenticated 401, consent 403, throttled 429, source failure 503. No JS millisecond exclusive cursor. HTTP pages do not share MVCC snapshot; late backdated inserts may appear, so no frozen snapshot promise.
- New POST /api/v1/account/security/events/export with JSON {from,to,category}, authenticated/consented + CSRF + reauth <=10min. UTF-8 CSV columns event_id,event_type,event_category,occurred_at_utc; 5,000 rows, 2MiB, 30s hard limits, 3 preparations/day/member, 1 concurrent. Buffer full validated bytes in bounded memory BEFORE headers/body, else 413/503 with no partial success. attachment/private no-store/nosniff; SHA-256 exact transmitted bytes, Digest header only if gateway verified. Quote/escape CSV and defend formula injection (=,+,-,@,TAB,CR,LF, separators/quotes); do not claim universal spreadsheet safety. No root disk spool.
- Optional prepared-only audit AFTER CSV snapshot and complete buffer, separate transaction; record count/hash without sensitive metadata, fail 503 before bytes if audit commit fails. Prepared != downloaded; no identical-retry guarantee without immutable file. Next.js BFF must preserve errors, not convert 503 to empty. Android App API same member/session DTO, errors and secure native download, not copied browser cookies.

### PostgreSQL, security, administrator
- New versioned SECURITY DEFINER account_security_event_history_v1(p_actor uuid,p_from timestamptz,p_to timestamptz,p_categories text[],p_before_at timestamptz,p_before_id uuid,p_limit int) returns event_id,event_type,created_at. Preserve existing migration 214 return type. WHERE user_id=p_actor AND created_at>=from AND created_at<to with (created_at,id) keyset, LIMIT limit+1, whitelist and bounds; NULL owner excluded. Explicit public schema, fixed search_path pg_catalog,pg_temp, owner moneyverse_migrator; revoke PUBLIC, app EXECUTE only, no direct app SELECT/DML.
- Benchmark additive index (user_id,created_at DESC,id DESC) WHERE user_id IS NOT NULL in isolated PG using EXPLAIN and 10,001 synthetic rows. If necessary forward-only CREATE INDEX CONCURRENTLY outside transaction with invalid-index recovery; do not edit old migrations 180/214/266. Export snapshot one READ ONLY REPEATABLE READ transaction, audit write separate.
- **P0 actor boundary:** passing p_actor UUID is NOT a DB-authenticated identity. SessionGuard/requireUserId is API protection but compromised app role can still spoof actor unless independently constrained. Prove session-to-actor binding and least-privilege before release; no false SECURITY DEFINER guarantees. Shared dev/Production-connected 5433: NO fixtures/migrations/tests/writes.
- Admin only anonymized aggregate groups >=20 members, errors/latency/abuse; no member-level export. Respect audit retention/erasure; no silent extension. SEO noindex/no-store/sitemap exclusion, analytics without IDs/raw timestamps/metadata.

### KPI, QA, rollout and rollback
- KPI p95 <300ms for 10,001 synthetic rows, zero cross-owner exposure, zero fake complete-history or partial CSV success, export preparation success and 413/429/503 rates. Isolated real PG, Nest, Next and Android tests: 100 cross-owner, 100 concurrent, 10,001 equal-time/microsecond rows, NULL owner, unknown type, tampered/filter cursor, session expiry/revocation, zero vs 503, consent/reauth/CSRF, 5,001/2MiB+1/30s, CSV formula/quote/CRLF, audit failure, privacy deletion, 320px a11y, flag-OFF and existing preview regression.
- Engineering separate branch only, flag OFF by default, additive migrations; exact integrated-main SHA full CI/security PASS, isolated DB ACL proof, authenticated official Test E2E, independent QA, backup restore and authorized GitOps before Production. Rollback flag OFF/disable new API while preserving original card/events. RELEASE HOLD.
- QA v1288 FAILED/HOLD: root 100% (~466MiB now, QA ~601MiB), frontend 1397 PASS/6 FAIL, production audit 2 High, full audit 2 Critical/10 High, bot 1 Critical/5 High, dev app direct write 235/241 tables, 3 PUBLIC bank functions, migration 266 checksum mismatch, exact-SHA Test/restore unverified. Preserve other workers' worktrees and Docker volumes.

## v2026.10.26.1306 — F675-133 Cross-Generation Receipt Provenance, Money and Cursor Integrity (Feature Improvement / PLANNING DRAFT / RELEASE HOLD)

**Cycle:** Previous Feature Addition v1304 → Current Feature Improvement v1306 → Next Feature Addition v1308 (provisional). Authoritative remote main at start/mid: 1c5b84a1c45baafcca83f375a6f99698121e9bb5; v1304 Draft #1390 is unmerged and remains the stacked documentation base. No runtime, migration, Test, Production, balance or ledger changes; keep Gemini/development A/B/C/QA changes separate. This section supersedes conflicting v1304 proposals.

### New verified evidence and corrected assumptions
- Main Migration 009's legacy shop_list_my_purchases(uuid,integer) uses **INNER JOIN shop_items and users**, checks user.status=active and caps at 100 (the current controller requests 30). It is not a complete dual-source history API. A replacement read must LEFT JOIN item metadata, preserve purchase rows with unavailable names, and distinguish inactive account rejection from genuinely empty history; never simply widen the old limit.
- Migration 071 defines Store 2.0 shop_purchases with stored quantity INTEGER and unit_price BIGINT, a transaction FK, and a purchase timestamp. Migration 165's discount-aware shop_purchase_catalog writes the **effective charged unit price** into shop_purchases.unit_price. Current shop_catalog.base_price is not historical price evidence. Legacy purchases.paid_amount is nullable, quantity is not recorded.
- Ledger postings use NUMERIC amount and lower-case **debit/credit** direction enum. Compare amounts as exact PostgreSQL NUMERIC, never JavaScript Number, and never infer settlement from a transaction FK alone. The ledger_transactions.type, actor_user_id, and actual owner cash debit/system sink credit must be checked for a stronger proof; reverses_transaction_id alone does not establish a purchase refund state.
- Fresh read-only development DB audit (2026-10-26 09:09 KST): 0 legacy receipts, 6 catalog receipts and 6 distinct catalog transactions. All six have two postings, equal debit/credit totals, exact expected catalog amount and matching transaction actor/type; this is **development-only, not Production reconciliation**, and does not prove all future receipts.
- Existing Next BFF returns HTTP 200 with empty purchases on apiOrNull failure; MyReceipts also maps non-2xx/network failures to empty. Developer A Draft #1258 already addresses legacy fail-closed behavior; coordinate interface ownership and do not overwrite it.
- PostgreSQL timestamptz may preserve microseconds while JavaScript Date serializes milliseconds. Building keyset cursor ordering keys by Date round-trip risks duplicate or skipped receipts with same millisecond. Use raw DB precision and canonical UTC microsecond strings for cursor payload and DB comparisons; do not claim stable pagination without this test.

### Member experience, trust states and API contracts
- Keep v1304 private /account/shop/purchases, /shop entry CTA, ALL/LEGACY/CATALOG filters, inclusive-from/exclusive-to UTC range (default last 90 days; max 366 days), 1–50 page size, detail, retry and explicit refresh. Server chooses actor from authenticated session, not client input. Source-qualified receipt key is (LEGACY|CATALOG, UUID), not UUID alone; names are labeled **current display name, not name at purchase**; absent item name displays neutral unavailable text without hiding the purchase.
- List returns only **recorded** purchase facts. New explicit fields: amountStatus=RECORDED|UNKNOWN_LEGACY|OUT_OF_RANGE|MISMATCH; ledgerProofStatus=NOT_CHECKED|MATCHED|MISMATCH|UNAVAILABLE. A list's default is NOT_CHECKED unless an actual account/type/actor/posting check ran. Only detail may show MATCHED after validating transaction type (SHOP_PURCHASE for legacy or SHOP_CATALOG_PURCHASE for catalog, after checking exact migration history), actor, debit of the owner's USER_CASH, credit of the SINK and expected amount. Do not claim refunded/settled/chargeback solely from a purchase FK, event or reversal pointer.
- Catalog total is computed with NUMERIC(unit_price) * NUMERIC(quantity), checked against positive BIGINT bound 9223372036854775807 before string serialization; overflow returns amountStatus=OUT_OF_RANGE and null total, not a truncated value. Legacy paid_amount NULL means UNKNOWN_LEGACY, never zero; quantity and unit price stay null. All WLD amounts are exact integer strings or null. Preserve a stable typed error response: 400 invalid filter/cursor, 401 session expired, 403 inactive/consent denied, 404 foreign/unknown detail, 410 expired cursor, 429 rate limit, 503 source unavailable. No HTTP 200 empty on failure, no partial fake totals.
- NestJS GET /api/v1/shop/purchases/history and detail; same-origin Next GET /api/shop/purchases/history and Android /app-api/v1/shop/purchases/history must preserve status, no-store and source completeness flags. No purchases or refund mutation, no idempotency key on GET. On logout/account switch clear cached rows/cursors and fence in-flight responses; on partial source failure show SOURCE_UNAVAILABLE or PARTIAL_UNVERIFIED, never VERIFIED_EMPTY.

### SQL, cursor, concurrency and security boundary
- Proposed additive versioned owner read function shop_purchase_history_v2 (do not modify applied migration or replace v1 contract). UNION ALL legacy and catalog with source rank CATALOG=2, LEGACY=1; join current item metadata LEFT, preserve original purchase rows, return source-qualified identity and DB-precision timestamp. Enforce active actor and consent in authenticated API and reviewed DB boundary; an arbitrary p_actor UUID passed by an app process is **not database-verifiable authentication**. Recheck DB role DML exposure before claiming a SECURITY DEFINER boundary.
- Keyset order (purchased_at DESC, source_rank DESC, receipt_id DESC); SQL seek tuple is strictly less than last triple for descending order. The opaque HMAC cursor binds actor, session epoch, filter canonicalization, 15-minute expiry, cutoff and full-precision last tuple; tampering and cross-account replay reject. UTC normalization must preserve PG microseconds end-to-end, not JS Date milliseconds. Fetch limit+1 to compute hasMore, with stable tie-break across source UUID collisions. No OFFSET.
- READ ONLY REPEATABLE READ makes a single multi-query request internally consistent; it does **not** preserve an MVCC snapshot across HTTP pages. A cutoff excludes newer timestamps but not late backfills with older purchased_at; disclose REFRESH_REQUIRED/CHANGED_RESULTS and test backfill. If strict frozen history is required, separately approve a durable snapshot or ingestion-sequence design before promising it. Never hold a transaction open between page requests.
- For stronger ledger proof use scoped joins to ledger_transactions, ledger_postings and accounts with exact NUMERIC sums and expected account direction; account owner must equal authenticated actor. Reversal/refund detection requires a separate reviewed policy and explicit linked transaction validation; otherwise status NOT_CHECKED. Keep list latency bounded and run expensive proof in detail or async audited reconciliation, never an unbounded per-row N+1.
- Do not grant moneyverse_app direct purchase or ledger table DML to make reads work. Revoke PUBLIC EXECUTE on new SECDEF, use fixed search_path and schema-qualified objects, actor-scoped filtering, no raw receipt/user identifiers in telemetry, k>=20 admin aggregates, noindex/nofollow/no-store private pages. Request rate 30/min/member, timeout and response size limits, strict UUID/date/enum input validation and 404 for foreign detail.
- Index candidates (user_id,created_at DESC,id DESC) on purchases and (user_id,purchased_at DESC,id DESC) on shop_purchases require isolated EXPLAIN, concurrent index/lock/storage review and forward-only migration numbering after current collision resolution. No new tables proposed. Preserve existing functions for rollback.

### P0/P1 engineering handoff, acceptance and release gates
| Priority | ID | Acceptance / owner |
| --- | --- | --- |
| P0 | ENV1306-01 | Root 100% with 608MiB available; owner-coordinated reversible capacity recovery, protected contributor worktrees, verified isolated backup restore. |
| P0 | SEC1306-02 | 235/241 app-role broad UPDATE+DELETE and 3 PUBLIC bank EXECUTE in dev; negative BOLA, actor-binding, real-PG grants, no live privilege changes in planning. |
| P0 | REL1306-03 | QA v1287 FAILED: six frontend tests, production/full/bot audit failures, migration266 historical checksum divergence; exact-SHA authenticated Test and restore HOLD. |
| P0 | SHP1306-04 | Replace false-empty BFF/UI, inactive-account false-empty, and false ledger/discount/refund assertions; coordinate Developer A #1258. |
| P1 | SHP1306-05 | Additive v2 dual-source function, LEFT JOIN preservation, owner-only ACL and EXPLAIN-backed indexes. |
| P1 | SHP1306-06 | NUMERIC amount/discount/nullable legacy proof, actor/type/account/posting matching and explicit proof states. |
| P1 | SHP1306-07 | Microsecond-safe signed cursor, source-qualified identity, cutoff/backfill honesty, 15m TTL. |
| P1 | SHP1306-08 | NestJS/Next.js/Android typed status parity, logout fencing and truthful empty/error UI. |
| P1 | SHP1306-09 | Privacy, noindex/no-store, >=20-member admin aggregation, WCAG 2.2 AA 320px/200% zoom, keyboard/touch, anonymous KPI. |
| P1 | SHP1306-10 | Isolated PG tests: 10,001 synthetic mixed receipts, 100 foreign-owner probes, same-millisecond distinct-microsecond ties, cross-source UUID collision, discount, legacy NULL, 64-bit overflow, ledger type/actor/direction mismatch, backfill, inactive account, 503-vs-empty, Android and rollback. |

**Deployment:** SHOP_UNIFIED_RECEIPTS_ENABLED OFF by default. Separate engineering branch, reviewed additive DB change, isolated real-PG permission and exact-head CI, authenticated official Test exact-SHA shop/finance regression, security audits, independent restore and approved GitOps before any Production promotion; 1%→10%→100% only after evidence, rollback feature OFF first. No runtime code, DB, WLD, ledger, feature flag, Test or Production modification in this planning run.

**External evidence gate:** Three independent publisher families reviewed (PostgreSQL transaction isolation, OWASP API object authorization, W3C WCAG 2.2); newly independently acquired, deduplicated and approved records **0**; cumulative verified corpus **UNKNOWN/10,000**, BLOCKED / IN PROGRESS. Reviewing links does not establish 10,000 evidence records.

## v2026.10.26.1304 — F675-133 Verified Cross-Generation Shop Purchase History (Feature Addition / PLANNING DRAFT / RELEASE HOLD)

**Cycle:** Previous Feature Improvement v1302 → Current Feature Addition v1304 → Next Feature Improvement v1306 (provisional). Authoritative `main` at start/mid: `1c5b84a1c45baafcca83f375a6f99698121e9bb5`; predecessor planning Draft #1384 is **unmerged**, and this is a stacked **documentation-only** proposal. Ownership stays separate from Gemini, development A/B/C, and QA.

### Verified source baseline and intent
- `frontend/src/app/shop/my-receipts.tsx` exists but is **not mounted** in `frontend/src/app/shop/page.tsx` on main. Its GET fetch silently turns upstream non-2xx/network errors into an empty receipt list. `frontend/src/app/api/shop/purchases/route.ts` likewise returns HTTP 200 `{purchases:[]}` when `apiOrNull` fails. Do not label this verified empty.
- `backend/src/shop/shop.controller.ts` GET `/api/v1/shop/purchases` reads legacy `public.shop_list_my_purchases(actor,30)` through the repository; migration 009 returns legacy `public.purchases` rows ordered `created_at,id` with `transaction_id` and BIGINT `paid_amount`. This does **not** include Store 2.0 `public.shop_purchases`.
- Migration 071 defines Store 2.0 `shop_purchases(id,user_id,catalog_id,quantity,unit_price,transaction_id,purchased_at)` and FK-linked ledger transaction; migration 072 implements catalog purchases. Catalog names are mutable, so current names are **not historical name snapshots**. Source row plus FK proves a recorded purchase link, not independent full ledger-posting reconciliation.
- Developer A's unmerged Draft #1258 already proposes fail-closed **legacy** purchase history. F675-133 extends the read model to **both** generations; do not overwrite or claim A's implementation has landed. No purchase mutation, refund, exchange, WLD transfer, inventory mutation or new synthetic receipt.

### User story, entry, states and controls
- Audience: authenticated, consented member viewing only their own purchase receipts. From `/shop`, CTA **All purchase receipts** opens private `/account/shop/purchases`. Source tabs **All / Legacy / Store 2.0**, date-range picker (default 90 days, maximum 366 days per query), **Apply filters**, **Older purchases**, **Receipt details**, **Retry**.
- Each row: source badge, canonical receipt UUID, current item display name explicitly labeled *current name*, UTC purchase instant rendered in viewer locale, quantity, exact historical unit price when available, exact recorded paid amount as decimal string, ledger transaction UUID and `recorded` provenance badge. Legacy quantity is displayed as `not recorded`, never fabricated as one. No claimed ledger settlement or refund status unless separately proven by posting reconciliation.
- States: LOADING, VERIFIED_EMPTY (successful authoritative 200 with zero rows), READY, PARTIAL_UNVERIFIED (amount/name/provenance discrepancy), SOURCE_UNAVAILABLE (upstream error/invalid payload), SESSION_EXPIRED, FILTER_INVALID, CURSOR_EXPIRED and RETRYING. No optimistic success, false empty, fake totals or synthetic history; clear sensitive client state on logout/account switch.
- Keep `/shop` public catalogue indexable; private page, BFF and Android API use `noindex` and `Cache-Control: private, no-store`. The feature flag `SHOP_UNIFIED_RECEIPTS_ENABLED` defaults OFF.

### Exact API contract and authorization
- Proposed NestJS `GET /api/v1/shop/purchases/history?source=ALL|LEGACY|CATALOG&from=ISO8601&to=ISO8601&limit=1..50&cursor=opaque`, and `GET /api/v1/shop/purchases/history/:source/:receiptId`. Both use SessionGuard + AuthenticatedGuard + ConsentGuard; actor comes only from verified session, never from query/body. Next same-origin BFF `GET /api/shop/purchases/history` and Android `GET /app-api/v1/shop/purchases/history` preserve status and private headers, do not use `apiOrNull` empty fallback. No mutation endpoint or CSRF bypass.
- Success `200 {receipts:[{source,receiptId,productId,itemNameCurrent,nameIsHistorical:false,quantity:null|integer,unitPriceWld:null|string,totalPaidWld:null|string,amountStatus:'RECORDED'|'UNVERIFIED',transactionId,purchasedAt}],nextCursor:null|string,asOf}`; every amount is an exact base-10 integer string, never JS Number. Legacy quantity/unit price null unless actually stored. If Store 2.0 `unit_price * quantity` exceeds safe ledger BIGINT range or conflicts with posting proof, show UNVERIFIED and no invented total.
- HTTP 400 invalid filter/cursor, 401 expired session, 403 consent, 404 non-owner/missing detail (indistinguishable), 410 expired cursor, 429 rate limit (30/min/user), 503 DB/upstream unavailable; never `200 []` for error. Request timeout and capped response bytes; no receipt IDs or raw cursor in telemetry.

### DB/read model, constraints, concurrency and rollout
- Add an **additive versioned SECURITY DEFINER read function** `public.shop_purchase_history_v1(p_actor uuid,p_source text,p_from timestamptz,p_to timestamptz,p_after_at timestamptz,p_after_source_rank smallint,p_after_id uuid,p_limit integer,p_cutoff timestamptz)`. Inside DB enforce actor ownership on both `public.purchases` and `public.shop_purchases`, join each product with LEFT JOIN so renamed/inactive products cannot erase genuine receipts, join ledger transaction FK for provenance, UNION ALL and sort `(purchased_at DESC,source_rank DESC,receipt_id DESC)` where rank CATALOG=2/LEGACY=1. Preserve legacy function/API unchanged. Do not allocate a migration number until concurrent 280+ migration collisions and checksum provenance are resolved.
- Existing Store 2.0 PK/FKs/quantity>0/unit_price>0 apply; legacy receipt source remains immutable. Proposed composite indexes `(user_id,purchased_at DESC,id DESC)` on each purchase table only after `EXPLAIN` and lock/space review. New tables: **none**. Revoke PUBLIC and app direct purchase SELECT/DML; grant only EXECUTE on reviewed owner read function. Session actor spoofing and broad app-role DML (#993) remain independent P0 review gates.
- A single page query uses a **READ ONLY REPEATABLE READ** transaction when multiple reads are needed; cross-page cursor is signed/HMAC-bound to actor, session epoch, filters, cutoff and last triple, 15-minute TTL. It offers a stable ordering/cutoff for append-only purchases, **not a guaranteed cross-request MVCC snapshot**. New purchases after cutoff are omitted until refresh; backfilled older rows must be explicitly covered by test/UX caveat. No locks on financial rows, no ledger writes, no idempotency key for GET.
- Admin `/admin/shop` may show only >=20-member aggregate source coverage, error rate and mismatched receipt counts; no member receipt drill-down without a separately approved privacy/role design. Security: owner-only 404, CSRF protection on any future mutation, request quotas, strict UUID/time/enum validation, injection-safe parameterized SQL, no cookies or receipt identifiers in analytics. Privacy deletion/retention must follow the canonical ledger retention policy; do not delete financial receipts as part of UI hiding.
- Web/Android parity, 320px reflow, 200% zoom, 44x44 CSS px recommended touch controls, keyboard and screen-reader status announcements, WCAG 2.2 AA. KPI: verified-empty vs unavailable correctness, cross-generation source coverage, receipt-detail error ratio, p95 latency (<800ms target in isolated Test), page cursor stability; zero sensitive identifiers in event payloads.
- Tests before code: isolated real PostgreSQL fixture for both source types, same-time/same-UUID-across-source ordering, 10,001 synthetic purchases, 100 foreign-owner probes, legacy 30-limit boundary, renames/deactivated catalogue, concurrent new purchases/backfill, 64-bit overflow, missing/incorrect ledger linkage, 503 vs true empty, malformed/tampered/expired cursor, account switch, Android contract, 320px/200% a11y. Separate exact-SHA Test authenticated E2E, all frontend/backend/DB/app-api CI, audits, backup/restore and approved GitOps required before promotion. Flag OFF or revert read-only code for rollback; any additive index/function rollback only after dependency/lock review.

| Priority | ID | Deliverable / acceptance |
| --- | --- | --- |
| P0 | ENV1304-01 | Root 100% (~737MiB free) safe capacity recovery and verified isolated DB/backup restore; no contributor cleanup. |
| P0 | SEC1304-02 | App-role least privilege, PUBLIC bank EXECUTE and authenticated actor binding negative tests; no live DB writes. |
| P0 | REL1304-03 | QA v1286 test/audit FAIL, migration 266/280 provenance, exact-SHA official Test and release hold. |
| P0 | SHP1304-04 | Replace false-empty purchase response and never claim a legacy-only list is complete; coordinate unmerged A #1258. |
| P1 | SHP1304-05 | Dual-source owner-scoped SECURITY DEFINER read, stable composite ordering, EXPLAIN-backed indexes. |
| P1 | SHP1304-06 | Strict amount/provenance schema, immutable receipt identity, overflow/mismatch handling. |
| P1 | SHP1304-07 | NestJS filters/details and signed actor/session-bound keyset cursor, read-only snapshot semantics. |
| P1 | SHP1304-08 | Next.js private route, BFF and Android parity with truthful loading/error/empty UI. |
| P1 | SHP1304-09 | Admin anonymous aggregates, privacy/SEO/a11y, rate limits and measurable KPIs. |
| P1 | SHP1304-10 | Isolated PG owner/ACL/concurrency/10,001-row regressions, exact-SHA E2E and feature-OFF rollback. |

**Evidence/release gate:** QA v1286 at 07:26 KST: main typecheck/lint/build PASS, frontend six failures, production/full/bot audits FAIL, authenticated official exact-SHA Test and isolated restore unverified; root 100%. External independent references reviewed: PostgreSQL, OWASP, W3C (3 publisher families); **new approved deduplicated records 0; cumulative approved UNKNOWN/10,000 → BLOCKED / IN PROGRESS**. No runtime, DB, ledger, WLD, Test, Production or feature-switch changes.
