## v2026.10.26.1302 — F675-132 Session Nickname Contract, Revocation-Race and Trust-Boundary Hardening (Feature Improvement / DRAFT / RELEASE HOLD)
- **Cycle:** Previous Feature Addition v1300 → Current Feature Improvement v1302 → Next Feature Addition v1304 (provisional). Authoritative main `1c5b84a1c45baafcca83f375a6f99698121e9bb5` verified at start and midpoint; predecessor Draft #1379 remains unmerged. No runtime/DB/member/WLD/ledger/Test/Production changes. This section supersedes conflicting proposed v1300 interface details; earlier entries are historical.
- **Source-proven corrections:** Migration 213 defines `account_active_sessions(uuid,uuid)` with a seven-column `RETURNS TABLE` and user-agent-derived `device_label`; PostgreSQL cannot change its return row shape with `CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION`. The NestJS repository maps the exact seven fields, and the Next.js security page derives its mobile icon from `deviceLabel` and converts `apiOrNull` failures to an empty sessions list. Existing revoke-one and revoke-others operations `UPDATE auth_sessions` directly; session revocation is not synchronized by any proven shared nickname-specific lock protocol. The app role already has `UPDATE` on `auth_sessions`; do not claim exclusive SECURITY DEFINER ownership or DB-authenticated identity merely because a function takes `p_actor`.
- **Product truth and UI:** A nickname is member-supplied display-only text, not device attestation, current-session proof, admin status or a security event. In `/account/security`, keep server-supplied current/admin badges and the original heuristic device type/icon independently of `customLabel`. Display `customLabel` separately with explicit “Not verified device identity” copy; never classify hardware by user-supplied text. Preserve existing sign-out and reauthentication actions. Distinguish VERIFIED_EMPTY (successful sessions response with zero rows), SOURCE_UNAVAILABLE (backend/DB failure), REAUTH_REQUIRED, EDITING, SAVING, CONFIRMED, UNCONFIRMED_TIMEOUT, VERSION_CONFLICT, REVOKED, OFFLINE and ACCOUNT_SWITCHED; a failed GET must never become “no devices”.
- **Backward-compatible read API:** Do **not** replace Migration 213's existing function signature/return type. Add `account_active_sessions_v2(uuid,uuid)` with old seven fields in the same order plus nullable `custom_label`, integer-string `label_version`, `label_updated_at`, or an equally narrow companion function with one consistent owner read; leave v1 executable for rollback. Extend `GET /api/v1/account/security/sessions` additively (`customLabel`, `labelVersion`, `labelUpdatedAt`), no-store, no change to existing session ordering, existing badges or revoke response. No direct app-role DML grants on new tables; validate real-PG EXECUTE/PUBLIC privileges and actor provenance. A service-supplied `p_user` UUID is not, by itself, DB-verifiable proof of session ownership; unresolved principal binding is a security gate.
- **Mutation transport correction:** v1300's proposed DELETE JSON body is brittle across proxies/app-api clients. Prefer `PUT /api/v1/account/security/sessions/:id/label` with JSON `{label}` and `DELETE` on the same URL **without a request body**; both carry `If-Match: "label-vN"` and `Idempotency-Key: <UUID>`. Version 0 is an explicit initial ETag; 428 missing precondition, 412 stale version, 409 same key/different canonical payload, 404 foreign/unknown, 410 own expired/revoked only after authenticated owner proof, 429 throttled, 503 unavailable. Return new `ETag`, committed `labelVersion`, `confirmed:true` only after DB commit; on timeout show UNCONFIRMED and refetch. This intentionally supersedes v1300's JSON expectedVersion/requestId and generic 409 version contract; test Next.js BFF and Android app-api header forwarding and no-body DELETE.
- **Normalization and receipt:** Server NFC normalization, whitespace trim, 1–40 grapheme clusters and ≤160 UTF-8 bytes; reject controls/bidi overrides/zero-width spoofing and unsafe markup; escape as text. Use canonical payload digest over actor, target session, HTTP verb, normalized label or RESET sentinel, expected version and contract version, never raw nickname in logs. Keep revision tombstone on Reset; no row means revision 0 only before first mutation. Per-actor UUID receipt uniqueness and 24h retention; identical replay returns a committed result **only after fresh session, consent, reauth, owner and active-target checks**. Key reuse with changed operation/payload fails 409. Receipt expiry never resets label revision; prevent ABA and replay across account switch.
- **Transaction and revoke races:** Lock the target `auth_sessions` row in the same transaction before alias revision/receipt changes; recheck `user_id`, `revoked_at`, `expires_at` and current actor session/reauth under the final authorization policy. Existing revoke-one and revoke-others UPDATE the same session rows, but bulk UPDATE lock order is not proven deterministic: avoid claiming a universal deadlock-free order. Review/align revoke and alias lock order on a **separate engineering branch**; on SQLSTATE 40P01/40001 use bounded retries with a fixed request key, never emit optimistic success, and surface 503/409 after exhausted retries. Revoke-before-save rejects; save-before-revoke may commit but later GET hides the revoked session. A label never extends expiry or reverses revocation. Include audit metadata and receipt in the atomic commit without raw label text.
- **Data model:** `account_session_label_states(session_id UUID PK FK auth_sessions ON DELETE CASCADE, user_id UUID FK users ON DELETE CASCADE, label TEXT NULL, version BIGINT CHECK >0, updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ)`; retained NULL tombstone and unique (user_id,request_id) receipt with 24h cleanup. Physical session DELETE cascades; setting `revoked_at` does **not** cascade, so explicit bounded retention/cleanup and account-erasure tests are required. Index by session PK; add other indexes only with isolated EXPLAIN. No direct app table read/write grants. Revalidate `auth_sessions` direct UPDATE baseline before any SECDEF security assertion.
- **Admin, abuse, SEO, Android, a11y, KPI:** no member labels in admin screens, logs, analytics, structured data or URLs; aggregate only k≥20, 10 accepted mutations/hour/member and IP/session abuse controls; noindex/nofollow/no-store. Existing security page is server-rendered: isolate edit UI as keyboard-accessible dialog with focus return, 44px touch targets, 320px/200% zoom and screen-reader status; no badge/icon spoofing. Android app-api must forward If-Match, Idempotency-Key, ETag, 412/428, no-body DELETE, CSRF and reauth; no local optimistic success. Count adoption only from committed server receipts, not CTA clicks; monitor stale-version, revoke-race, 404/410, 429, 503 and latency with no personal content.
- **P0 backlog:** ENV1302-01 root 100% (~786MiB now; QA v1285 928MiB), preserve other workers and prove isolated restore; SEC1302-02 app-role 235/241 broad DML, three PUBLIC bank EXECUTE, actor binding and BOLA; REL1302-03 QA v1285 FAILED/HOLD (frontend 1,397 pass/6 fail; prod audit 2 High, full 2 Critical/10 High, bot 1 Critical/5 High; migration 266 provenance; exact-SHA authenticated Test/restore absent); AUTH1302-04 false-empty session list, spoofed device identity and revoked-target success.
- **P1 backlog:** AUTH1302-05 additive v2 function and revision tombstone with privileges; AUTH1302-06 target-row revoke/CAS/receipt/deadlock retry; AUTH1302-07 PUT/DELETE header contract and typed errors; AUTH1302-08 Next.js/Android compatibility, accessibility and state fencing; AUTH1302-09 privacy/erasure/admin/SEO/KPI; AUTH1302-10 isolated PG 100 concurrent renames, 100 foreign probes, revoked-vs-save races, DELETE proxy/header parity, v1 rollback, Unicode/TTL, 412/428 and exact-SHA release checks.
- **Backlog IDs:** ENV1302-01 SEC1302-02 REL1302-03 AUTH1302-04 AUTH1302-05 AUTH1302-06 AUTH1302-07 AUTH1302-08 AUTH1302-09 AUTH1302-10.
- **Release and evidence:** Feature flag `account_session_nicknames_v1` OFF; additive expand, real-PG negative ACL and function-shape tests, official exact candidate SHA CI, authenticated Test web/Android/security/finance E2E, independent backup restore, security audit, 1%→10%→100% approved GitOps and flag-off rollback; never rewrite Migration 213 or applied Migration 266. Independent external reference corpus: newly approved deduplicated 0; cumulative accepted UNKNOWN / 10,000 → BLOCKED / IN PROGRESS. DRAFT / RELEASE HOLD.

- **v1300 Draft PR:** https://github.com/wtrdd1-hash/Woldeok-Moneyverse-Migration/pull/1379 (stacked on unmerged #1371; docs only, DRAFT / RELEASE HOLD).
## v2026.10.26.1300 — F675-132 Verified Member Session Nicknames (Feature Addition / DRAFT / RELEASE HOLD)
- **Cycle:** Previous Feature Improvement v1298 → Current Feature Addition v1300 → Next Feature Improvement v1302 (provisional). Source-of-truth remote main `1c5b84a1c45baafcca83f375a6f99698121e9bb5` checked at start/midpoint; prior unmerged planning Draft #1371 is the isolated documentation base. Engineering handoff: https://github.com/wtrdd1-hash/Woldeok-Moneyverse-Migration/issues/1378. No Gemini/development A/B/C/QA source edits or deployment.

### Evidence, problem, users, and product boundaries
- **Confirmed source:** `frontend/src/app/account/security/page.tsx` already renders an authenticated active-session list and security-event history. `backend/src/account/account-security.controller.ts` already implements owner-scoped GET sessions/events and CSRF+recent-reauth protected DELETE one/revoke others. `backend/src/account/account-security.repository.ts` maps `account_active_sessions` results to `deviceLabel`. Migration `213-account-session-details.sql` derives this label heuristically from recent activity's user-agent, not a verified physical-device identity. Migration `214-account-security-events.sql` exposes the most recent owner security events (capped at 50). No owner-editable session nickname endpoint or durable nickname storage was found on the inspected main paths. This is a proposed feature, **not evidence of a Production defect**.
- **Purpose and audience:** signed-in, consent-complete members managing several browser/mobile sessions need a memorable personal nickname to distinguish active sessions when deciding which existing session to revoke. The nickname is a **self-declared display label**, never evidence that a device is authenticated, trusted, or belongs to a particular hardware model. Show verbatim caution beside the field: “You chose this name; it does not verify device identity.” Korean: “내가 지정한 이름이며 기기 신원 확인을 뜻하지 않습니다.”
- **Explicit exclusions:** do not implement another session listing, new sign-out behavior, security-event provenance, IP geolocation, fingerprinting, automatic trust, passkeys, device attestation, admin authority, session-token disclosure, ledger/WLD mutation, push notifications or historical login archive. A rename must not extend session expiry, change reauthentication freshness, or reset the activity timestamp. Existing generic user-agent `deviceLabel` remains visible as an unverified hint.

### Member screen and interaction contract
- **Entry:** existing `/account/security` > active-session row > “Rename device” / “기기 이름 수정”. Show current-session and administrator-session badges based only on server fields, alongside a separate optional `customLabel`; never replace the authoritative badges. Inline edit dialog with visible label, editable text, grapheme/byte counter, Save / Reset to default / Cancel; Reset removes the alias and returns to the existing heuristic device label. Reauthentication CTA uses the existing flow; no hidden auto-submit. Only active, owner sessions are eligible.
- **States:** INITIAL_LOADING, READY_DEFAULT, READY_CUSTOM, EDITING, REAUTH_REQUIRED, SAVING, SAVED_CONFIRMED, RESET_CONFIRMED, VERSION_CONFLICT (refresh), SESSION_REVOKED_OR_EXPIRED (410), NOT_OWNED_OR_UNKNOWN (404 indistinguishable), CONSENT_REQUIRED, SESSION_EXPIRED, RATE_LIMITED (429), OFFLINE and SOURCE_UNAVAILABLE (503). On network timeout/lost response, display UNCONFIRMED and refetch owner session/version before any success toast; do not optimistically claim persistence. Disable duplicate Save while pending; preserve text on retry without exposing it to URLs/logs. Empty valid input is **not** an implicit delete; use Reset CTA.
- **Input:** server canonicalizes Unicode NFC, trims surrounding Unicode whitespace, rejects controls/newlines, bidi overrides/isolate controls, zero-width spoofing controls and HTML markup where relevant. Allow 1–40 user-perceived grapheme clusters **and** at most 160 UTF-8 bytes after normalization; `Intl.Segmenter` or equivalent pinned ICU semantics on both client/server with server authoritative. Escape output as text, never raw HTML; distinguish similar-looking/confusable names and never imply security confidence. Forbid alias equality checks as authorization.

### API, app-api, DB, locking, and idempotency
- **Existing GET extension:** `GET /api/v1/account/security/sessions` retains `sessions[]` and existing fields unchanged; add nullable `customLabel`, integer-string `labelVersion` (0 when absent), `labelUpdatedAt`; use owner/session-derived actor and `Cache-Control: private, no-store`. Preserve existing order and current-session behavior. A stale client can ignore new fields.
- **Proposed mutations:** `PUT /api/v1/account/security/sessions/:id/label` with JSON `{"label":"...","expectedVersion":"0","requestId":"UUID"}`; `DELETE /api/v1/account/security/sessions/:id/label` with `{"expectedVersion":"N","requestId":"UUID"}`. Both require SessionGuard, AuthenticatedGuard, ConsentGuard, CsrfGuard and ReauthGuard, plus strict UUID validation, rate limit 10 accepted mutations/hour/member, exact ownership and active session. Return `{"sessionId":"UUID","customLabel":"...|null","labelVersion":"N","updatedAt":"ISO-UTC","confirmed":true}` only after DB commit. 409 version conflict; 404 unknown/foreign; 410 own expired/revoked (avoid existence oracle by not exposing foreign state); 400 invalid; 429 rate-limited; 503 storage unavailable. No 200 when a mutation is uncommitted.
- **DB proposal (not executed):** additive account_session_label_states with session_id UUID PRIMARY KEY REFERENCES auth_sessions(id) ON DELETE CASCADE, user_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE, label TEXT NULL, version BIGINT NOT NULL CHECK(version>=1), updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(), CHECK(label IS NULL OR (char_length(label)>0 AND octet_length(label)<=160)). A NULL label is a retained revision tombstone: Reset increments version instead of deleting state, and missing row means version 0 only before first edit. The session_id PK supports joins; add (user_id,updated_at DESC,session_id) index only if real EXPLAIN justifies it. Add account_session_label_requests(user_id UUID, request_id UUID, session_id UUID, payload_hash BYTEA, response_version BIGINT, outcome TEXT, created_at TIMESTAMPTZ, PRIMARY KEY(user_id,request_id)) with 24-hour cleanup. Never store raw token/IP/user-agent/email. Account deletion cascades; expired/revoked cleanup has explicit retention SLA, not an immediate-delete claim.
- **SECURITY DEFINER:** new forward-only migration creates `account_session_label_set(p_actor,p_current_session,p_target_session,p_expected_version,p_request_id,p_label)` and `account_session_label_reset(...)` with `SET search_path=pg_catalog,pg_temp`, schema-qualified objects, fixed role ownership, `REVOKE ALL FROM PUBLIC`, narrow `GRANT EXECUTE TO moneyverse_app`; never grant direct SELECT/INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE on label/receipt tables. Existing account session list function extended to LEFT JOIN owner aliases without exposing foreign labels. Verify function signatures against existing migration names before implementation.
- **Atomicity:** authenticate/reauthenticate/consent and feature flag before mutation or replay; single transaction locks current actor session then target owner session FOR UPDATE, rejects revoked/expired at lock time, then state row and receipt in deterministic order. Insert state row version 1 on first edit; thereafter every Save or Reset increments version (Reset writes label=NULL, preserving tombstone), so expectedVersion=0 cannot be reused (ABA prevented). UUID receipt unique key: same actor/request/payload returns committed result only after current-session/permission revalidation; different payload yields 409. One commit contains state/revision/receipt/audit; rollback leaves none. Lost response is UNCONFIRMED until GET reconciliation; revoke race cannot return stale success.

### Administrator, security, SEO, Android, accessibility, analytics
- **Admin:** do not expose individual member labels, session IDs or aliases in administrative analytics. Only opt-in/feature aggregate adoption counts for cohorts >=20, suppress small cells and rate-limit queries; no privileged label mutation route. Existing security-event audit records may log event type, actor and target ID if policy permits, **never the nickname text**. Audit event must correspond to a committed change; no synthetic success event.
- **Privacy and abuse:** owner-only BOLA and no cross-member metadata, request-body redaction, CSRF/recent reauth, no-store/private, XSS-safe rendering, Unicode spoofing mitigation, brute-force/rate-limit and enumeration-resistant 404, account erasure, device revocation and session expiration races. Label is personal data and should not be indexed or embedded in third-party analytics.
- **SEO/Android/a11y:** existing member `/account/security` stays `noindex,nofollow`; add response `no-store` and no public structured data. Android app-api must proxy the exact owner-only fields and PUT/DELETE semantics, preserve cookie/CSRF/reauth boundaries and redact request IDs in logs; no Android OS-device trust inference. WCAG 2.2 AA keyboard dialog, visible focus restoration, 44px touch targets, screen-reader status announcement, 320px viewport and 200% zoom; support reduced motion.
- **KPI:** denominator = eligible members who viewed active sessions, numerator = server-committed unique successful label changes; 7/30-day adoption, conflict rate, 404/410 errors, accessibility task completion, median API latency. Do not count button clicks or lost responses as success; report cohort thresholds and zero-data honestly.
- **Tests/rollout:** 100 concurrent CAS renames on one session -> one version winner, 100 foreign owner probes -> zero labels, revoked/expired/current/admin sessions, 40 graphemes/160 bytes, NFC/bidi/XSS, idempotent retry and request-ID collision, tombstone ABA prevention, 24h cleanup, account erasure, Android parity, keyboard/zoom, noindex/no-store. Real isolated PostgreSQL role-negative tests verify no direct app table DML, SECDEF function grants, deterministic lock order and rollback. Feature flag `account_session_nicknames_v1` OFF by default; additive expand/read/mutate/canary; disable flag to fall back to generic device labels without changing existing revoke paths. Separate engineering branch + official exact-SHA CI, authenticated Test web/Android/security/financial E2E, independent restore, security audit and authorized GitOps before merge/Production. No runtime changes in planning cycle.

### Prioritized engineering backlog and release/evidence gates
- **P0 ENV1300-01:** recover root capacity safely (99%, ~1.0GiB) without deleting other teams' worktrees; verify backups/restore and DB environment separation.
- **P0 SEC1300-02:** close dev app-role UPDATE/DELETE 235/241 and three PUBLIC bank EXECUTE exposures through reviewed forward-only ACL; prove owner/CSRF/reauth/DB-negative tests.
- **P0 REL1300-03:** QA v1284 main 1,397 frontend PASS/6 FAIL, prod audit 2 HIGH, full audit 2 CRITICAL/10 HIGH; migration 266 historical checksum divergence, exact-SHA Test/E2E/restore/GitOps HOLD.
- **P0 AUTH1300-04:** establish truth of existing user-agent deviceLabel and existing revocation; never present user nickname as verified device security identity.
- **P1 AUTH1300-05:** schema, revision tombstones, indexes and SECDEF owner-only functions with no app direct grants.
- **P1 AUTH1300-06:** transaction/locks, CAS, UUID idempotency, TTL cleanup and revoke race handling.
- **P1 AUTH1300-07:** NestJS owner GET extension and guarded PUT/DELETE DTO/errors, rate limit and security event integrity.
- **P1 AUTH1300-08:** Next.js security-row edit/reset CTA, Android app-api parity, server-confirmed UI states and WCAG.
- **P1 AUTH1300-09:** consent/privacy/erasure, admin k>=20 aggregates, SEO/no-store, non-sensitive KPIs.
- **P1 AUTH1300-10:** isolated real-PG 100-race/100-BOLA/Unicode tests, exact-SHA regressions, canary and reversible flag-off rollback.
- **Backlog IDs:** ENV1300-01 SEC1300-02 REL1300-03 AUTH1300-04 AUTH1300-05 AUTH1300-06 AUTH1300-07 AUTH1300-08 AUTH1300-09 AUTH1300-10.
- **Independent external evidence gate:** new deduplicated, independently verified/classified references this cycle **0**; cumulative accepted count **UNKNOWN / 10,000**. Do not treat named standards, websites, or unverified links as accepted corpus entries. Gate **BLOCKED / IN PROGRESS**. QA v1284 **FAILED / RELEASE HOLD**. Documentation-only, no Test/Production deployment, DB writes, WLD/ledger or session mutation.

## v2026.10.26.1298 — F675-131 Notification Archive Export Integrity (Feature Improvement / DRAFT / RELEASE HOLD)
- **v1298 Draft PR:** https://github.com/wtrdd1-hash/Woldeok-Moneyverse-Migration/pull/1371; **engineering handoff:** https://github.com/wtrdd1-hash/Woldeok-Moneyverse-Migration/issues/1363; DRAFT / RELEASE HOLD.

### Evidence, scope, ownership and cycle
- **Cycle:** Previous Feature Addition v1296 → Current Feature Improvement v1298 → Next Feature Addition v1300 (provisional). Rechecked remote main `1c5b84a1c45baafcca83f375a6f99698121e9bb5` and canonical AGENTS.md, README.md, PROJECT_PLAN.md and Korean parity. This docs-only change stacks on unmerged planning Draft #1364; do not touch Gemini, development A/B/C or QA code/branches.
- **Source confirmed:** main `backend/src/notification/notification.service.ts` still reads `public.in_app_notifications` directly with actor-scoped `WHERE user_id=$1`, `created_at DESC LIMIT/OFFSET`, and returns `dedupe_key`; `notification.dto.ts` uses `@Type(() => Boolean)` for `unreadOnly`. Existing member mark-read/read-all SQL functions are distinct from archive export. There is **no implemented archive CSV endpoint** on main. These are source-level risks, not proof of a Production incident. Development B #1356 owns base list parsing/pagination; do not duplicate it.
- **Latest QA raw v1283 (2026-10-26 ~04:25 KST):** frontend 1,397 passed/6 failed, backend 1,313 passed/398 skipped, DB 15 passed/3 skipped; typecheck/lint/build passed, full audit 2 critical/10 high, production audit 2 high; official authenticated exact-SHA Test E2E and isolated backup restore not proven. Root disk 99% with ~1.2GiB available, separate data volume ~56GiB available. Main unchanged; Production promotion BLOCKED.

### Export safety decisions and user-visible contract
- **Purpose/target:** allow signed-in, consent-complete members to obtain a trustworthy **bounded export of actually stored owner notifications**, not fabricated UI tabs, simulated browser alerts, historical delivery proof, reward receipts or a financial ledger. Entry `/account/notifications/archive` → filter → Search → Export CSV → verified result; Android app-api uses the same owner-scoped server contract. Explicit LOADING, EMPTY_VERIFIED, EXPORTING, VERIFIED, SOURCE_UNAVAILABLE, LIMIT_EXCEEDED, CONSENT_REQUIRED, SESSION_EXPIRED and UNCONFIRMED_TRANSFER states; no success after a lost response.
- **No partial download:** enforce 5,000-row/2MiB UTF-8 encoded output/30-second caps before any 200 headers or body bytes. At this size, materialize the complete bounded buffer (or private bounded spool) before sending, check row count/encoded byte count/deadline, and return 413 for row/byte overflow, 503 for unavailable source and an explicit timeout failure for deadline. Never stream unvalidated CSV chunks then attempt a 413; never silently truncate or offer an incomplete file as success. Empty verified export is header-only with rowCount=0.
- **Exact bytes:** fixed ordered columns `id,category,title,body,link,is_read,read_at_utc,created_at_utc`, deterministic `created_at DESC,id DESC`, UTF-8 **without BOM**, CRLF record separators, RFC 4180 quote doubling and full-field quoting, ISO-8601 UTC timestamps and documented NULL encoding. Fixed ASCII download filename; `Content-Type: text/csv; charset=utf-8`, `Content-Disposition: attachment`, `Cache-Control: private, no-store`, `X-Content-Type-Options: nosniff`; server-computed SHA-256 over the exact transmitted bytes and byteLength/rowCount/schemaVersion/sanitizationVersion metadata. No user-supplied filename or public CDN caching.
- **Spreadsheet injection trade-off:** all text cells are untrusted (title/body/category/link), including embedded commas, quotes, CR/LF, tabs, leading Unicode whitespace, zero-width/BOM and full-width formula characters. Apply a versioned, per-cell spreadsheet-safe transform **before** RFC 4180 serialization and hash, then regression-test save/reopen behavior in supported spreadsheet clients. OWASP explicitly warns no universally safe CSV mitigation exists and some spreadsheet re-saves reactivate formulas. Label CSV as a transformed, human-viewable export; **do not claim byte-for-byte fidelity to raw DB text**. Record transformedCellCount and a clear in-product note; any lossless machine-readable format requires a separately approved contract. Do not claim SHA-256 is a signature or independent proof of database truth: it only verifies the response bytes against the provided digest.
- **Snapshot truth:** one dedicated PostgreSQL client and `BEGIN ISOLATION LEVEL REPEATABLE READ READ ONLY` for authorization, filtered count, bounded row collection and snapshot metadata. Use database clock and one transaction; commit/rollback before HTTP transfer. `snapshotCutoff` is descriptive and does not prove immutable multi-request pagination. Browsing remains BEST_EFFORT when read flags, retention or deletions can change between requests; only the completed single-transaction export can be labeled snapshot-consistent.
- **Authorization/privacy:** actor only from validated session and consent, CSRF for export POST, recheck session epoch and consent immediately before releasing bytes; account switch/logout aborts pending download and clears browser cache. Explicit DB role-negative tests: do not widen `moneyverse_app` direct table SELECT/DML; design narrowly scoped audited SECURITY DEFINER read function with fixed search_path and revoked PUBLIC EXECUTE. Review actor spoofing threat separately: a caller-supplied UUID alone is not a database-authenticated identity. Limit 3 exports/hour/member and bounded IP quotas; no raw title/body/link/dedupe_key in logs, analytics, admin dashboards or audit metadata. Safe same-origin links only; others exported blank. Retention/deletion/merge revokes stale cursors and download authorizations.
- **API/DB:** owner-only `GET /api/v1/notifications/archive` and CSRF-protected `POST /api/v1/notifications/archive/export` through Next.js BFF; Android equivalent. Validate strict UTC from-inclusive/to-exclusive (max 90 days), category from source-proven allowlist, read state tri-state, cursor HMAC bound to session epoch/owner/filters/version/expiry. No direct user_id request parameter. Proposed `(user_id,created_at DESC,id DESC)` index only after isolated EXPLAIN and forward-only migration review; never backfill historical events or infer missing delivery.
- **UX/SEO/admin/KPI:** private `no-store` + `noindex` and sitemap exclusion; WCAG 2.2 AA, 320px layout, 44px targets, keyboard and screen-reader result/error states, retry with identity fence. Admin sees only k>=20 aggregated attempts, 413/429/503, p95 latency and byte buckets; no personal content. Count completion only after full response and optional client digest verification, not button clicks; false success=0, foreign-row leakage=0 are hard gates.
- **Test/release:** isolated real PostgreSQL with 100 concurrent export/read races, 100 foreign-account negatives, 10,001 synthetic identical-timestamp UUID rows, 5,001-row and 2MiB+1 byte boundaries, 30-second deadline, malformed UTC, session/consent expiry, zero rows, Unicode/CRLF/CSV formula variants, spreadsheet save/reopen, exact SHA rehash, aborted network, no bytes on 413, Android parity, ACL/SECDEF negatives and existing notification regressions. Feature flag `notification_archive_export_v1` OFF; code requires separate development branch, exact candidate SHA official CI/Test authenticated E2E, security audit, isolated restore and approved GitOps 1%→10%→100%. Flag OFF rollback preserves existing inbox and DB records. No runtime or DB writes in planning.

### P0/P1 implementation handoff
- **P0 ENV1298-01:** root 99%/~1.2GiB, preserve other workers, classify/relocate only authorized stale artifacts; prove isolated restore and dev/Test/Production separation.
- **P0 SEC1298-02:** least-privilege DB role/SECDEF actor boundary, BOLA/consent/CSRF, account-switch fencing, no PII in export telemetry.
- **P0 REL1298-03:** QA v1283 frontend 6 fails and audits 2 critical/10 high, prod 2 high; migration 266/280 and exact-SHA authenticated Test/restore/GitOps HOLD.
- **P0 ARC1298-04:** prohibit fake notification and reward provenance in CSV, never report download success before full verified transfer, no partial 200 on overflow.
- **P1 ARC1298-05:** strict filter/source-category/read-state and safe-link validation; deterministic null/date/CSV schema.
- **P1 ARC1298-06:** single-client REPEATABLE READ export, truthful BEST_EFFORT multi-page keyset and stable UUID tie-breaker.
- **P1 ARC1298-07:** bounded buffer/413 before headers, exact-byte SHA and documented transformed spreadsheet-safe cells.
- **P1 ARC1298-08:** NestJS/Next.js/Android contract, session epoch, accessible private archive/export states.
- **P1 ARC1298-09:** admin k>=20 aggregates, abuse limits, noindex/no-store, verified completion KPI and privacy lifecycle.
- **P1 ARC1298-10:** isolated PG concurrency/BOLA/CSV injection/spreadsheet/limits/abort regression, exact-SHA Test, flag-off rollback.
- **Backlog IDs:** ENV1298-01 SEC1298-02 REL1298-03 ARC1298-04 ARC1298-05 ARC1298-06 ARC1298-07 ARC1298-08 ARC1298-09 ARC1298-10.
- **External independent reference gate:** newly approved/deduplicated 0; cumulative independently verified UNKNOWN of required 10,000 → BLOCKED / IN PROGRESS. RFC 4180 and OWASP CSV Injection reviewed as **candidate** primary references only, not counted toward the accepted corpus.
- **Status:** PARTIAL / DRAFT / RELEASE HOLD. Planning-only, no code, migration, member data, WLD, ledger, Test or Production changes.

## v2026.10.26.1296 — F675-131 Verified Member Notification Archive Search and CSV Export (Feature Addition / DRAFT / RELEASE HOLD)

### Provenance, target and non-goals
- **Cycle:** Previous Feature Improvement v1294 → Current Feature Addition v1296 → Next Feature Improvement v1298 (provisional). Authoritative main SHA 1c5b84a1c45baafcca83f375a6f99698121e9bb5; AGENTS.md, README.md, English PROJECT_PLAN.md canonical and Korean counterpart rechecked. Independent docs-only worktree stacked on unmerged planning Draft #1359; no A/B/C/Gemini/QA worktree changes.
- **Source-proven:** main NotificationController provides owner-authenticated GET /api/v1/notifications and unread-count, plus CSRF-protected mark-read/read-all. NotificationService uses WHERE user_id=$1 with created_at DESC, LIMIT/OFFSET; frontend /api/notifications forwards only limit/unreadOnly. Development public.in_app_notifications has UUID id, UUID user_id, category, title, body, link, dedupe_key, is_read, read_at, created_at; zero rows and indexes on (user_id,created_at DESC) and (user_id,is_read,created_at DESC) without UUID tie-breaker. This proves a product gap, not a Production incident or an actual corpus of exportable messages.
- **Purpose/target:** authenticated, consent-complete members can search their **actually persisted** in-app notification history and export a bounded, self-verifiable UTF-8 CSV. No synthetic notification tabs, localStorage market-close simulation, reward claim, WLD/ledger mutation, offline push, guaranteed historical retention, or invented category mappings. Historical rows are not proof of successful browser delivery. This addition is distinct from B PR #1356, which fixes base list filter/pagination; integrate only after exact-SHA review, do not duplicate B's source changes.

### User journey, screens, states and CTA
- Existing /account/notifications → new private /account/notifications/archive; filter panel: UTC start/end inclusive/exclusive range, server-validated real category only, read/unread/all, reset, Search, Load more, Export CSV, Retry, Open notification (safe same-origin allowlisted link only). Show UTC export cutoff and local display timezone explicitly. Default last 30 days **within available records**, never promise that 30 days of data exist; maximum selected window 90 days, server-owned upper bound.
- States: SIGN_IN_REQUIRED, CONSENT_REQUIRED, LOADING, EMPTY_VERIFIED, READY, EXPORTING, EXPORT_COMPLETE_VERIFIED, LIMIT_EXCEEDED, SNAPSHOT_EXPIRED, SOURCE_UNAVAILABLE, FORBIDDEN. Never render a success toast for a network timeout or failed digest verification; preserve prior confirmed rows only when session identity is unchanged. Account switch/logout clears private client cache and fences late responses.
- Mobile: 320px viewport, sticky export action not obstructing controls, 44x44px targets, keyboard-only and screen-reader filter labels, live result count without noisy announcements, accessible focus after error, WCAG 2.2 AA, reduced motion. No indexing, no sitemap entry, private/no-store and X-Robots-Tag noindex.

### API, query, database and security contract
- NestJS GET /api/v1/notifications/archive?from&to&category&readState&limit&cursor: 50 default/100 maximum, owner from authenticated session **never request body**, strict ISO UTC timestamps and enum parsing, 400 malformed, 401 unauthenticated, 403 consent, 404 for foreign row, 429 throttled, 503 DB unavailable; response {items,nextCursor,snapshotCutoff,filterHash}. Next.js /api/notifications/archive is a same-origin authenticated no-store proxy; Android app-api uses the same server contract and session-bound cursor. No direct table grants to moneyverse_app.
- POST /api/v1/notifications/archive/export with CSRF and typed filter, same-origin, rate limit at most 3 exports/hour/member; stream or buffer complete CSV only after full validation. Max 5,000 records, 2MiB UTF-8 output, 30 seconds; on overflow return 413 with zero partial file bytes. Response includes Content-Disposition attachment, no-store, no-sniff, SHA-256 of exact bytes, rowCount, snapshotCutoff and canonical filter hash. CSV columns id, category, title, body, link (safe allowlisted or blank), is_read, read_at_utc, created_at_utc. Do not export dedupe_key, session secrets, IPs, email or other members' data. Escape RFC4180 quotes/newlines and neutralize spreadsheet formula prefixes = + - @ and leading whitespace/control characters before CSV serialization. Excel opening is not a data integrity guarantee.
- Forward-only reviewed migration **if justified** adds index (user_id,created_at DESC,id DESC), optional category composite index only after isolated EXPLAIN; read-only owner SELECT via SECURITY DEFINER audited function with SET search_path, strict actor and grants. Do not mutate or backfill historical notification rows. Export in one PostgreSQL connection with BEGIN ISOLATION LEVEL REPEATABLE READ READ ONLY; authorize actor, count and size, collect rows ordered (created_at DESC,id DESC) under same snapshot, calculate digest after complete bytes. Read-only export needs no idempotent write receipt: re-download produces a new snapshot; never promise replay of bytes from a metadata-only receipt. Rate-limit/audit metadata may record owner, filter hash, row count, digest and timestamp, but **never** message contents or CSV; restrict to approved short retention and least privilege. Recheck session/consent before sending bytes.
- Cursor is HMAC-signed with owner, session epoch, filter hash, UTC cutoff, last (created_at,id), expiry and schema version; invalid/mismatched/expired cursor fails closed 400/410. Keyset uses tuple comparison with deterministic UUID tie-breaker and stable snapshot semantics; for multi-page retrieval, a timestamp cutoff alone cannot prove a cross-request MVCC snapshot. Require immutable source rows or a bounded server-held snapshot; otherwise label browsing BEST_EFFORT and make only the single-transaction export COMPLETE. Never claim pagination is an immutable historical snapshot when concurrent read flags change.
- Administrator sees only aggregated export attempts, success/failure and size/latency buckets with k>=20 privacy threshold; no member titles, bodies, raw downloads, links or foreign row access. Abuse protections: per-member/IP limits, no caching, no unsafe links, maximum range/rows/bytes, query timeout, CSV injection defense, telemetry redaction and audit for repeated denials.

### Acceptance, KPI and rollout
- KPIs from **verified** server events only: export attempts, completed digest-verified exports, 413/429/503, empty verified queries, p95 export latency and retry rate; never count UI clicks as completed exports. No personal notification content in analytics.
- Isolated real PostgreSQL tests: 100 concurrent exports/read updates, 100 foreign-member probes, 10,001 synthetic notifications with identical timestamps and UUID tie, DST/UTC boundaries, empty/expired snapshots, content formula injection, 5,001 row and 2MiB overflow, network abort, session switch, disabled consent, role ACL, safe link filtering, CSV digest recomputation, 413 with no partial bytes, Android parity, 320px/keyboard/screen reader and existing notification regressions. Roll back fixture transaction; **tests not executed in this planning cycle**.
- Flag notification_archive_export_v1 defaults OFF. Separate development branch + forward-only migration review; exact candidate SHA CI and full security audit, isolated role-negative PostgreSQL tests, authenticated exact-SHA official Test E2E, verified backup/restore and approved GitOps rollout at 1%→10%→100% with telemetry and kill switch. Rollback flag OFF and revert app routes only after data/contract compatibility review; no automatic Production promotion. Existing QA #1317 candidate has frontend 1404 PASS and prod audit PASS on **unmerged** SHA; latest development candidates still report six frontend failures and advisory HOLD. Root 99% (~1.4GiB free), release BLOCKED.
- **External independent reference gate:** RFC 4180, OWASP API Security Top 10 and W3C WCAG 2.2 are unapproved candidate references only. New deduplicated/classified accepted **0**, cumulative verified accepted **UNKNOWN / 10,000**; gate BLOCKED / IN PROGRESS. No claim of completion or reference corpus provenance.

### Actionable backlog
- **P0 ENV1296-01:** root 99% capacity remediation without deleting others' worktrees/volumes; isolated Test/restore evidence and environment separation.
- **P0 SEC1296-02:** least-privilege app-role audit, owner BOLA/CSRF/consent/session fencing, forbid raw notification table reads and unsafe content.
- **P0 REL1296-03:** reconcile QA/frontend six failures, advisories, migration 266/280, exact-SHA official Test, backup restore and GitOps hold.
- **P0 ARC1296-04:** remove/label synthetic tab and localStorage notifications; guarantee exports contain only DB-backed owner rows; coordinate without overwriting B #1356.
- **P1 ARC1296-05:** filter/date/category/read-state validation and real-category provenance, safe source-link contract.
- **P1 ARC1296-06:** owner keyset, UUID tie-break index, cursor/session fencing and honest cross-page consistency.
- **P1 ARC1296-07:** one-transaction bounded CSV, 413 fail-closed, formula injection defense, SHA-256/manifest.
- **P1 ARC1296-08:** NestJS/Next.js/Android parity, private UX and accessibility.
- **P1 ARC1296-09:** privacy, rate limit, aggregate admin telemetry, SEO and KPIs.
- **P1 ARC1296-10:** isolated real-DB races/ACL/10,001 synthetic rows, regressions, exact-SHA release/rollback.
- **Backlog IDs:** ENV1296-01 SEC1296-02 REL1296-03 ARC1296-04 ARC1296-05 ARC1296-06 ARC1296-07 ARC1296-08 ARC1296-09 ARC1296-10.
- **Release:** PARTIAL / DRAFT / HOLD. Planning docs only; no runtime code, migration, data, WLD, ledger, Test or Production changes.

## v2026.10.26.1292 — F675-130 Verified Member Notification Controls and Quiet Hours (FEATURE ADDITION / DRAFT / RELEASE HOLD)

### Source truth and product purpose
- **Cycle:** Previous Feature Improvement v1290 → Current Feature Addition v1292 → Next Feature Improvement v1294 (provisional). Canonical remote main 1c5b84a1c45baafcca83f375a6f99698121e9bb5 checked at start and midpoint; reviewed main AGENTS.md, README.md, English PROJECT_PLAN.md and Korean counterpart. Planning-only branch stacked on unmerged Draft #1349; A/B/C/Gemini/QA branches untouched.
- **Source evidence:** frontend/src/app/account/notifications/page.tsx currently exposes only engagement goal/NPC notifications_enabled via PUT /api/v1/engagement/preferences. notification-tabs-bar.tsx renders six hard-coded INITIAL_NOTIFICATIONS, including invented bank interest and security session events, and marks them read only in React state. stock-alert-push-engine.tsx derives localStorage scenarios and triggers a simulated daily browser Notification after a timeout, without a verified event source. NestJS NotificationService.listNotifications reads public.in_app_notifications with user_id filter, created_at DESC and LIMIT/OFFSET; no unique tie breaker. This is source-level trust risk, not proof of a Production incident. Existing notification modal and its verified read receipt remain separate; do not replace its working paths with a mock.
- **Outcome:** let authenticated, consented members choose optional *foreground browser interruptions* by category and quiet hours across devices while keeping a real, complete, owner-only in-app notification history. No promised offline push, email, Discord DM, guaranteed delivery, money issuance, rewards, or stock-price triggers. Security/system notices must remain accessible in the in-app inbox and are not suppressed by optional quiet hours.

### Member experience and precise states
- **Entry:** existing /account/notifications and bell → new /account/notifications/preferences; real inbox category filter and Load more in existing notification center. Preferences UI includes timezone selector (IANA, default Asia/Seoul), quiet-hours OFF by default, start/end HH:mm (if enabled; equal times rejected), category switches for BUSINESS/BANK/SEASON/SPACE and other source-confirmed optional categories only. SECURITY and SYSTEM cannot be opted out of *in-app recording*; never treat UI labels as authoritative DB categories until mapping is audited.
- **CTA:** Save preferences, Reset to defaults, Retry verification, Open notification, Mark read, Load more. Browser permission requested only on a user gesture; permission is per device and cannot be synchronized. Foreground notifications are OFF until explicit opt-in and browser permission granted; while page is closed, no delivery is promised. Existing engagement goal/NPC toggle is independent and must not be silently overwritten.
- **States:** LOADING, REAL_EMPTY, REAL_ITEMS, MORE_AVAILABLE, SAVING, VERIFIED_SAVED, UNCONFIRMED_TIMEOUT, STALE_REVISION, QUIET_ACTIVE, PERMISSION_DEFAULT, PERMISSION_DENIED, BROWSER_UNSUPPORTED, SESSION_EXPIRED, SOURCE_UNAVAILABLE, FEATURE_OFF. Never show invented event rows, read receipts, earned WLD, stock market-close reports, or success after a lost response. A timeout requires owner GET reconciliation before a success toast.
- **Quiet-hours semantics:** evaluate event creation timestamp in configured IANA timezone using an audited timezone library; half-open [start,end), overnight crossing midnight and DST gap/fold defined by instant-to-local conversion. Quiet hours silence only optional foreground alerts; in-app persistence and unread counts remain unchanged. Existing critical security/system in-app items remain visible, with any separate urgent interruption policy subject to security review. No backfill or burst of expired browser alerts after quiet hours; user may read in-app history.

### API, persistence, transactions and privilege boundary
- **Read contracts:** GET /api/v1/notifications/preferences returns revision, timezone, quietEnabled, quietStart, quietEnd, categoryOptIns, updatedAt and server-observed timestamp. GET /api/v1/notifications/history?category=&cursor=&limit=1..50 returns actual member rows, hasMore, nextCursor and sourceStatus; use (created_at DESC,id DESC) stable keyset with owner/session/filters/expiry-bound signed cursor, no offset. Reject unknown categories until a source-backed registry exists. Never trust client-supplied actor. Android /app-api/v1/notifications/preferences and /history mirror fields and auth/consent; Next.js same-origin BFF, no-store/noindex.
- **Write contract:** PUT /api/v1/notifications/preferences requires authenticated active session, consent, CSRF, If-Match revision and UUID idempotencyKey. Validates IANA timezone, HH:mm, optional category allowlist and boolean values; 200 only after DB transaction receipt with receiptId, revision, savedAt and canonical preferences. 400/422 invalid, 401 expired, 404 owner resource concealment, 409 stale/reused key with different body, 429 throttled, 503 source unavailable. Replays return the historical receipt but cannot assert present state after account changes.
- **Forward-only schema proposal:** public.member_notification_delivery_preferences(user_id UUID PK FK users, timezone VARCHAR(64), quiet_enabled BOOL, quiet_start TIME, quiet_end TIME, revision BIGINT CHECK >0, updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ); public.member_notification_category_optins(user_id UUID FK, category TEXT CHECK allowlist, enabled BOOL, PRIMARY KEY(user_id,category)); public.member_notification_preference_receipts(id UUID PK, user_id UUID, request_key UUID, payload_sha256 BYTEA, revision BIGINT, result_json JSONB, created_at TIMESTAMPTZ, UNIQUE(user_id,request_key)). Receipt payload excludes notification bodies, IPs and tokens. Index public.in_app_notifications(user_id,created_at DESC,id DESC), plus optional user_id/category/created_at/id only if EXPLAIN justifies. Explicit reviewed account deletion/merge purge policy; never broaden app direct DML.
- **Atomic mutation:** validate actor+consent before replay; advisory owner/request lock → compare receipt digest → lock users row → lock preference row → If-Match revision → validate category set → upsert prefs+categories, increment revision, insert immutable receipt in one transaction. Fixed-search_path SECURITY DEFINER functions owned by migrator; revoke PUBLIC EXECUTE; grant only scoped EXECUTE to moneyverse_app. Historical receipts immutable except lawful approved purge. On logout/account switch, fence pending responses by session epoch.
- **Real inbox integrity:** read owner rows via SECDEF function rather than broad table grants; verify actual in_app_notifications category inventory before mapping UI tabs. Preserve server mark-read/read-all receipts, stable total order and unread-count source; prohibit browser-only fake read state. Client-side foreground alerts may be emitted only for actual owner event IDs returned by server, with device-local dedupe and explicit opt-in; never invent dividends or a grant.

### Security, admin, UX, measurement, rollout
- **Security:** owner/BOLA negative tests (100 cross-member probes), session/consent/CSRF, 5 writes/min and 30 reads/min initial caps, replay digest, no PII in analytics, private noindex/sitemap exclusion, no shared caching. Browser notification title/body must be sanitized and never include sensitive finance/security detail on lock screens; secure defaults avoid permission prompts without gesture. Account deletion/merge invalidates revisions/cursors and erases preferences according to approved policy.
- **Admin:** aggregate opt-in/quiet-hour adoption and source failures only with k>=20 cohorts; no individual schedules, devices, inbox bodies or security notices. SEO public help explains in-service virtual notifications, never guaranteed push or financial payout. Mobile 44px controls, semantic switches, visible keyboard focus, reduced motion, live-region save errors, timezone accessible labels, WCAG 2.2 AA.
- **KPI:** verified preference-save rate, false-success=0, fake-inbox-items=0, foreign-data-leak=0, stale-409, 429/503, optional foreground alert eligibility/suppression, in-app retention, p95 GET/PUT and opt-out reversals; adoption is a hypothesis, not observed impact.
- **Rollout:** notification_controls_v1 default OFF; docs-only this cycle. Development creates independent code/migration branch; RED/GREEN tests and isolated real PostgreSQL privilege, concurrency and rollback verification. Official Test deploy only exact code SHA with authenticated backend/API/DB/user-flow QA, frontend regression, security audits, backup restore, GitOps approvals; 1%→10%→100% after gates. Flag OFF reverts new UI/delivery only, retains existing inbox and old engagement toggle; no destructive migration or Production mutation from planning.

## v2026.10.26.1290 — F675-129 Shop Wishlist Price and Availability Integrity (FEATURE IMPROVEMENT / DRAFT / RELEASE HOLD)

### Authority and verified source behavior
- **Cycle:** Previous Feature Addition v1288 → Current Feature Improvement v1290 → Next Feature Addition v1292 (provisional). Remote main 1c5b84a1c45baafcca83f375a6f99698121e9bb5 checked at start/mid. Read main AGENTS.md, README.md, canonical PROJECT_PLAN.md and Korean counterpart. Documentation-only, stacked on unmerged planning Draft #1345, separate engineering #1344. No changes to Gemini, Development A/B/C, QA, runtime or migrations.
- **Actor-dependent price, confirmed in migration 165:** shop_effective_unit_price(p_actor,p_catalog) checks early_event_claims for the market_sale event on the current Asia/Seoul calendar day and membership in starter_tools. Eligible members receive GREATEST(1,(base_price*9)/10) integer WLD; public p_actor=NULL sees base price. Both shop_catalog_list_v2(p_actor) and shop_purchase_catalog call this function. The wishlist must show the authenticated actor's *effective* current price, never reuse a public catalog base price. The discount can expire at Seoul midnight or change before checkout. This is source analysis, not proof of a Production incident.
- **Browse versus buy:** migration 165 shop_catalog_list_v2 filters active items and inventory starts_at/ends_at, but does not exclude quantity=0 or check sale_ends_at. The purchase function separately checks quantity, active member/account, and shop_assert_purchase_limit, but does not visibly enforce sale_ends_at or required_progression_code. Escalate policy mismatch for separate engineering review; do not silently invent purchase restrictions or label catalog visibility as purchase eligibility. Existing BIGINT v_price * p_quantity calculation (quantity up to 100) requires overflow boundary testing.

### Product journey, UI and precise states
- **Unchanged goal:** active authenticated consented member saves up to 30 catalog UUIDs via /shop Save/Remove and revisits /account/shop-wishlist via View in shop, Remove, Retry. No purchase, reservation, price lock, automatic alert, reward, WLD or ledger effect. Wishlist has **no enabled Buy CTA**. Logged-out visitors may browse the public shop but must authenticate to save.
- **Price response:** owner-only read uses shop_effective_unit_price(actor,catalogId) and returns effectiveUnitPriceWld as a canonical positive decimal *integer string*, priceObservedAt ISO timestamp, priceStatus CURRENT|UNAVAILABLE. Never store member price in wishlist/receipt, never use JS Number, never show a fallback or guaranteed 10% discount. Show "price at refresh; checkout confirms final amount". Unknown source means neutral price unavailable, not zero.
- **Availability response:** BROWSEABLE means catalog-visible only; OUT_OF_STOCK if quantity=0, INACTIVE_OR_OUT_OF_WINDOW if confirmed inactive/window mismatch, CHECK_AT_CHECKOUT if member purchase limits, account, progression or sale-end policy cannot be proven, SOURCE_UNAVAILABLE on read error, REMOVED after hard purge. Do not emit purchasable=true based only on a catalog row or sale_ends_at. An expired sale_ends_at without purchase enforcement is a policy discrepancy, not an automatic claim that checkout rejects.
- **UI states:** LOADING, EMPTY, SAVED, ALREADY_SAVED, REMOVED, LIMIT_REACHED, UNAVAILABLE_SAVED, CHECK_AT_CHECKOUT, UNCONFIRMED, STALE_REVISION, SESSION_EXPIRED, SOURCE_UNAVAILABLE, FEATURE_OFF. Session epoch fences old responses after logout/account switch. Timeout/lost response stays UNCONFIRMED until owner read, never a false saved toast. Saving inactive/unknown catalog is 404; **removing a previously saved inactive or expired item must succeed without catalog sale eligibility**.

### API, DB, transactions and account lifecycle
- **Owner API:** GET /api/v1/shop/wishlist?cursor=&limit=30 returns items with catalogId,revision,savedAt,effectiveUnitPriceWld,priceObservedAt,priceStatus,availabilityState plus nextCursor,wishlistGeneration. POST /api/v1/shop/wishlist accepts catalogId,idempotencyKey UUID; DELETE /api/v1/shop/wishlist/:catalogId accepts idempotencyKey UUID,expectedRevision positive integer. Immutable mutation receipt has receiptId,operation,resultRevision,replayed; a replay is historical, **not a guarantee of current saved state**. Session-derived actor only, active status/consent/CSRF writes, same-origin Next.js and Android /app-api/v1 parity. 400/422 invalid, 401 expired, 404 foreign/absent, 409 stale or reused key with different payload, 429 abuse/quota, 503 source outage. Private no-store/noindex, no cross-user cache.
- **Forward-only schema handoff:** preserve member_shop_wishlist UNIQUE(user_id,catalog_id), removed_at tombstone, positive BIGINT revision, active (user_id,saved_at DESC,id DESC) index and 30 active quota. Receipt UNIQUE(user_id,request_key), SHA-256 canonical payload digest including operation/catalog/expectedRevision, original result/revision/timestamp; ordinary UPDATE/DELETE denied by privilege and immutable trigger, with separately approved lawful erasure path. Never copy price, description, IP or token into receipts. Live wishlist FK ON DELETE CASCADE on hard catalog purge means historical receipt replay must not resurrect the item. Soft account deletion does not trigger FK cleanup.
- **Atomic order:** authenticate/revalidate active member+consent *before idempotent replay* → actor+request-key advisory lock → immutable receipt digest comparison → owner users row FOR UPDATE → existing wishlist row FOR UPDATE → for SAVE only catalog FOR KEY SHARE and allowed-to-save check → 30 quota, CAS, UNIQUE, row mutation, immutable receipt and wishlistGeneration increment in one transaction. REMOVE has no current catalog eligibility prerequisite. Align lock order with migration 163 account merge; advisory hash collision is not uniqueness. Hard-purge FK cascade must invalidate cursor generation through reviewed trigger or force single-page refresh; do not claim stable multi-page snapshot otherwise.
- **Cursor:** HMAC actor, session epoch, wishlist generation, saved_at+UUID, key version and 15-minute expiry; mutation, purge, logout and merge invalidate. Production max is 30 active items, while 10,001 synthetic rows are isolated test data, not a raised quota. Prices/stock may change even within a read; not a checkout snapshot.
- **Privacy/admin/security/mobile/KPI:** block merges involving wishlist/receipts until approved migration and erasure policy; revoke sessions/cursors on lifecycle change. Fixed search_path SECURITY DEFINER owner checks, revoke PUBLIC EXECUTE, no broad app DML, 100 foreign-owner negatives, initial 5 writes/min and 30 reads/min. Admin k>=20 aggregate only, never member lists or discount claims. Private noindex/sitemap exclusion, 44px mobile controls, keyboard/focus/live regions/reduced motion, WCAG 2.2 AA. Measure verified save/remove, price-unknown rate, midnight mismatch, p95, 409/429/503, false-success=0 and BOLA leaks=0; revenue uplift remains hypothesis.

### P0/P1 engineering backlog — exact acceptance
| Priority | ID | Deliverable and acceptance |
| --- | --- | --- |
| P0 | ENV1290-01 | Root 98% (~2.0GiB free), isolated DB/restore and capacity alerts; preserve worker worktrees/volumes. |
| P0 | SEC1290-02 | Re-measure dev app direct UPDATE/DELETE 235/241 and PUBLIC bank EXECUTE now 0 in QA v1279; independently certify SECDEF owner/consent and 100 BOLA negatives. |
| P0 | REL1290-03 | QA v1279 frontend 1,397 pass/6 fail, route-ledger invocation error and audits; migration 266 checksum/280 provenance, exact-SHA Test finance E2E, isolated restore and rollback. |
| P0 | SHP1290-04 | Reconcile actor-specific v165 price, zero stock, sale_ends_at/progression policy and BIGINT quantity overflow with authoritative purchase gates; no false purchasable or guaranteed discount. |
| P1 | SHP1290-05 | Immutable owner wishlist/receipts, purge semantics, exact string effective price, no price snapshot, 30 cap. |
| P1 | SHP1290-06 | Auth-before-replay, actor/key digest, owner→wishlist→catalog lock order, inactive REMOVE, CAS, generation invalidation and 100 concurrent mutations. |
| P1 | SHP1290-07 | Actor-aware DB read, BROWSEABLE/OUT_OF_STOCK/CHECK_AT_CHECKOUT, Seoul midnight price observation and HMAC cursor. |

## v2026.10.26.1288 — F675-129 Verified Member Shop Wishlist (FEATURE ADDITION / DRAFT / RELEASE HOLD)

### Authority, scope and source evidence
- **Cycle:** Previous Feature Improvement v1286 → Current Feature Addition v1288 → Next Feature Improvement v1290 (provisional). Fresh main `1c5b84a1c45baafcca83f375a6f99698121e9bb5` checked at start and mid; authority: main `AGENTS.md`, `README.md`, canonical `PROJECT_PLAN.md`, Korean counterpart. This planning branch stacks on unmerged Draft #1340, with separate engineering [#1344](https://github.com/wtrdd1-hash/Woldeok-Moneyverse-Migration/issues/1344). No edits to Gemini/Development A/B/C/QA or runtime.
- **Verified current source, not an observed Production incident:** `frontend/src/app/shop/page.tsx` loads actual member/public catalog; `shop-store-view.tsx` renders `catalog_id`, `code`, string `price`, ownership and stock-related fields. `backend/src/shop/shop.controller.ts` exposes `/shop/public-catalog`, authenticated `/shop/catalog`, `/shop/holdings`, `/shop/catalog/:id/purchases`; no owner wishlist CRUD was identified on inspected main. Migration `071-shop-catalog-schema.sql` defines `shop_catalog.id UUID`, unique `code`, `base_price BIGINT`, `active`, `shop_inventory` quantity/start/end; migration 075's `shop_catalog_list` is an active/time-window catalog read model. Current catalog `price` is an integer-WLD string, **not** the marketplace NUMERIC(20,4) price. Public claims about stock or sink cannot substitute for a verified purchase/ledger receipt.
- **Product purpose:** members save up to **30 distinct catalog items** to revisit without purchasing. This is private, non-financial intent storage: **no stock reservation, price lock, purchase, wallet/ledger transfer, automatic alert, or WLD reward**. A wishlist is never proof of ownership or purchase intent consent.

### Member journeys, state machine and exact contracts
- **Audience/entry:** authenticated, consented active member; existing `/shop` catalog item card/preview → **Save to wishlist** or **Remove from wishlist** (must be explicit and screen-reader named) → confirmed server receipt → **View wishlist**. Account entry `/account/shop-wishlist`; each item has **View in shop**, **Remove**; `Buy` is enabled only on the authoritative live shop page after existing purchase guards, never from stale wishlist data. Logged-out visitor may browse public shop but must sign in before save.
- **States:** `LOADING`, `EMPTY`, `SAVED`, `ALREADY_SAVED`, `REMOVED`, `LIMIT_REACHED`, `UNAVAILABLE_SAVED`, `SESSION_EXPIRED`, `STALE_REVISION`, `SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE`, `FEATURE_OFF`. For disabled/sold-out/expired/hard-deleted catalog items, display a neutral unavailable placeholder or omit after hard purge, not a stale price/Buy CTA. Server error or lost response yields `UNCONFIRMED` until receipt/status re-read; never optimistically claim persisted success.
- **Canonical identifiers:** `catalogId` UUID is the persistent key; `code` is a unique catalog identifier but never a user-owned ID or authorization token. Current catalog join supplies `name`, `category`, `priceWld` as exact decimal integer string, `availableNow` and `availabilityReason`; no cached price or user-specific inventory claim in wishlist rows. Quantity and timing may change after GET; show “current at refresh”, no guaranteed availability. `priceWld` must round-trip BIGINT as a string without JS Number conversion, while purchase settlement remains an independent authoritative operation.
- **API (NestJS, mirrored via same-origin Next.js and Android `/app-api/v1`):** `GET /api/v1/shop/wishlist?cursor=&limit=30` → `{items:[{catalogId,revision,savedAt,currentCatalog:{name,category,priceWld,availableNow}|null}],nextCursor}`; `POST /api/v1/shop/wishlist` body `{catalogId:UUID,idempotencyKey:UUID}` → `{catalogId,revision,receiptId,state:'SAVED'|'ALREADY_SAVED'}`; `DELETE /api/v1/shop/wishlist/:catalogId` body `{idempotencyKey:UUID,expectedRevision:positiveInteger}` → immutable removal receipt. Actor derived from session, never supplied userId. Auth/session/consent and CSRF for writes. Errors 400/422 malformed, 401 missing session, 404 unavailable/foreign (indistinguishable), 409 stale/key-payload mismatch, 429 quota/rate, 503 unavailable. No direct browser DB, no side effects on shop purchase endpoints.

### DB schema, atomicity, lifecycle and abuse prevention
- **Proposed forward-only migration; number owned by Development:** `member_shop_wishlist(id uuid PK,user_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id),catalog_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES shop_catalog(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,revision bigint NOT NULL DEFAULT 1 CHECK(revision>0),saved_at timestamptz NOT NULL,updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL,removed_at timestamptz NULL,UNIQUE(user_id,catalog_id))`; index `(user_id,saved_at DESC,id DESC) WHERE removed_at IS NULL`; active quota 30, with re-save of removed item incrementing revision. `member_shop_wishlist_receipts(id uuid PK,user_id uuid,request_key uuid,payload_digest bytea CHECK(octet_length=32),catalog_id uuid,operation text CHECK(operation IN ('SAVE','REMOVE')),result_revision bigint,created_at timestamptz,UNIQUE(user_id,request_key))`. Audit/retention and FK deletion must be reviewed; receipts must not copy price, token, IP or catalog descriptions.
- **Single PostgreSQL transaction:** validate membership/consent and live catalog UUID → acquire transaction-scoped member+request-key advisory lock → check immutable receipt and canonical digest (same-key same payload returns original, changed payload 409) → lock member row `FOR UPDATE` for 30-active quota → lock existing wishlist row if any → enforce revision CAS/uniqueness → mutate row and append receipt atomically. Lock ordering consistent with account lifecycle; advisory hash collision is not the correctness boundary, UNIQUE constraints and row locks are. Database SECURITY DEFINER functions use fixed `search_path`, explicit actor authorization, minimal `EXECUTE`, revoke PUBLIC and broad `moneyverse_app` DML. Reads enforce owner inside DB; foreign probes must not reveal catalog preference.
- **Current availability:** read current `shop_catalog.active`, inventory quantity, `starts_at`/`ends_at`, `sale_ends_at` and any other reviewed eligibility constraints; inactive/expired/out-of-stock/permission-ineligible items remain `UNAVAILABLE_SAVED` without purchase CTA. Never assume `shop_catalog_list` alone explains every failure reason or gives a frozen snapshot; test DB join and edge windows. Hard-purged catalog FK cascades wishlist relation, no synthetic resurrection.
- **Account lifecycle:** migration 010 soft-deletes users (FK cascade alone does not erase rows), migration 163 merges identities; until a reviewed merge/purge policy exists, reject account merge involving wishlist/receipts and revoke related cursors on account state change. Implement explicit privacy retention, erasure/anonymization and receipt minimization. User data `Cache-Control: private, no-store`, `robots noindex,nofollow`, sitemap exclusion; public `/shop` SEO remains unchanged. Admin sees only suppressed aggregate counts (`k>=20`), never private lists. Limit 5 mutations/min/user, 30 reads/min/user (initial TEST TARGET), max 30 active items, cursor HMAC owner/session/last savedAt+UUID/expiry/key version, 15-minute expiry; no cross-user cache or price tracking alerts.
- **Mobile/a11y/analytics:** responsive single-column wishlist on narrow screens, keyboard/focus, 44px targets, explicit errors and live-region confirmations, reduced motion, WCAG 2.2 AA; Android parity for error/receipt/string price. KPI from verified server receipts only: save rate, 7/30-day reopen, unavailable saved rate, removal, latency p95, 409/429/503, BOLA leaks=0, false saved success=0. Conversion to purchase only after separate authenticated ledger-backed purchase receipt; any retention/revenue lift is `HYPOTHESIS`, not observed.

## v2026.10.25.1286 — F675-128 Marketplace Saved-Search Semantic and Precision Integrity (FEATURE IMPROVEMENT / DRAFT / RELEASE HOLD)
- **GitHub planning review:** [Draft PR #1340](https://github.com/wtrdd1-hash/Woldeok-Moneyverse-Migration/pull/1340) (stacked on #1335, unmerged); [development handoff #1334](https://github.com/wtrdd1-hash/Woldeok-Moneyverse-Migration/issues/1334).

### Authority, cycle and source evidence
- **Cycle:** Previous Feature Addition v1284 → Current Feature Improvement v1286 → Next Feature Addition v1288 (provisional). Verified remote `main=1c5b84a1c45baafcca83f375a6f99698121e9bb5` at start and mid; checked `AGENTS.md`, `README.md`, English canonical `PROJECT_PLAN.md` and Korean counterpart. Stacked only on unmerged planning Draft [#1335](https://github.com/wtrdd1-hash/Woldeok-Moneyverse-Migration/pull/1335), engineering [#1334](https://github.com/wtrdd1-hash/Woldeok-Moneyverse-Migration/issues/1334); no Development A/B/C, Gemini or QA runtime branch changes.
- **Verified in main (potential incompatibilities, NOT observed production incidents):** migration 232 defines `marketplace_listings.price_wld NUMERIC(20,4)`; `market-listings-view.tsx` sorts using `Number(BigInt(a.priceWld)-BigInt(b.priceWld))` and checks affordability with `BigInt(priceWld)`. A decimal string such as `1.2500` throws in `BigInt`, and conversion of large differences to JS Number loses precision. `marketplace.service.ts` filters with `ILIKE '%' || input || '%'` without escaping literal `%`/`_`; `MarketplaceQueryDto` has no max lengths for category/search. UI matches sellerName, itemName and description; actual SQL matches item_name and description only and has no seller-name projection. SQL orders `created_at DESC` with `LIMIT/OFFSET` and no unique tie-breaker. Existing mock listings, timeout purchase-success and 1000-WLD fallback remain P0 truth blockers.

### Corrected filter contract and numeric semantics
- **Scope:** existing F675-128 only, signed-in active consented member, fixed-price `ACTIVE` listings; saving criteria does not reserve price/stock, settle a purchase, notify or transfer WLD. Keep `/marketplace` → `Save this search` → `/account/marketplace-searches` → `Run / Rename / Update / Delete`. Preserve `LOADING/EMPTY/SAVED/DUPLICATE/LIMIT_REACHED/STALE_REVISION/SESSION_EXPIRED/DATA_UNAVAILABLE/FEATURE_OFF`; add `INVALID_FILTER`, `PRICE_UNAVAILABLE` and `RESULTS_CHANGED` with a retry/reset CTA. Never render mock listings or fake purchase receipts as verified results.
- **Canonical filter schema v2:** `{query,category,maxPriceWld,sort}`. Query: Unicode NFKC, trim, maximum 80 Unicode code points and 320 UTF-8 bytes, control/bidi-control rejection, exact literal substring semantics (not SQL pattern syntax); apply the *same* canonical value to fingerprint, API validation and SQL. For SQL `ILIKE` escape backslash, `%` and `_` with explicit `ESCAPE '\\'` and bound parameters; limit query execution time and use query-plan-tested indexes. Category: live reviewed category allowlist, 1–60 code points, no client-only phantom category. Sort: `newest|price_asc|price_desc`. **Do not advertise seller-name search** until a reviewed privacy-safe seller display-name join and indexed predicate exist; UI placeholder and API contract must agree.
- **Exact WLD:** optional maxPriceWld is a positive canonical decimal string with at most 16 integer digits and 4 fractional digits (NUMERIC(20,4)), no exponent, comma, sign, Infinity or Number coercion; reject excess precision/overflow with 422 rather than round. Compare/sort in PostgreSQL NUMERIC; JSON and Android carry strings. UI price/affordability uses exact decimal scaling (10^4 integer units) or a reviewed decimal library; no `BigInt('1.2500')` and no `Number(BigInt delta)` comparator. Show `PRICE_UNAVAILABLE` if wallet truth is missing, never infer balance. Preserve actual settlement/receipt as separate authority.

### Stable live result query, persistence and authorization
- **DB read:** fixed-price results predicate `status='ACTIVE'` AND category AND literal keyword AND `price_wld<=maxPrice` is evaluated **before** LIMIT. Newest sort tuple `(created_at DESC,id DESC)`; price asc `(price_wld ASC,created_at DESC,id DESC)` and price desc `(price_wld DESC,created_at DESC,id DESC)`, with lexicographic continuation predicates matching each direction and a unique UUID final tie-breaker. Indexes only after real PostgreSQL `EXPLAIN (ANALYZE, BUFFERS)` on isolated representative data; avoid unapproved extension/index migration. Live listings may disappear or change between pages; report `RESULTS_CHANGED` and refresh, **never promise a complete frozen snapshot**.
- **Cursor:** versioned signed opaque HMAC token containing member/session binding, saved-search id, revision, normalized filter digest, exact decimal last-price string, full timestamp/id tuple, sort, key version and expiry (15 min). 401 for session loss, 404 indistinguishable foreign id, 409 for revision/digest mismatch, 422 for invalid filter/cursor, 429 for budget exceeded, 503 for unavailable DB. Recheck owner, consent, active membership and saved-search tombstone on every page; no cursor reuse after update/delete/account merge.
- **Mutation and storage:** retain 20-active-per-member cap, partial unique `(user_id,filter_fingerprint) WHERE deleted_at IS NULL`, owner+updated_at+id index and immutable receipts `UNIQUE(user_id,request_key)`. Document that identical normalized filters under two different labels are a **duplicate 409** by policy; rename uses PATCH, not a second row. Lock order: request-key advisory lock → receipt replay digest check → owner row FOR UPDATE → saved-search row FOR UPDATE; quota, revision CAS, duplicate constraint and receipt commit atomically in a SECURITY DEFINER function with pinned search_path, explicit actor/consent check, PUBLIC EXECUTE revoked and no broad app-role DML. Verify request-key lock scoped to member+UUID; uniqueness is final collision guard.
- **Lifecycle:** migration 010 soft-delete and migration 163 account merge do not imply FK cascading cleanup. Until an explicit privacy-preserving merge/erase policy is implemented and tested, reject merge involving active saved searches or retained receipts; revoke sessions/cursors on account state change, delete or anonymize member search terms and request metadata under approved retention policy. Admin sees only k>=20 aggregated counts, never member queries. Private account page/API `no-store`, `noindex`, authenticated CSRF on mutations, Android `/app-api/v1` parity, keyboard/focus/44px/reduced motion and WCAG 2.2 AA.

### P0/P1 engineering backlog — exact acceptance
| Priority | ID | Deliverable / acceptance |
| --- | --- | --- |
| P0 | ENV1286-01 | Root disk 98% (~2.2GiB free), isolated DB/restore and 24h capacity guard; preserve all other worktrees/volumes. |
| P0 | SEC1286-02 | Fix app-role direct DML and PUBLIC function exposure; owner-bound SECDEF, 100 foreign-user probes, lifecycle checks. |
| P0 | REL1286-03 | QA v1276 FAILED/HOLD, newer v1277 evidence still failing; migration 266 checksum / 280 collision, dependency audits, route inventory, exact-SHA Test and isolated restore gates. |
| P0 | MKT1286-04 | Fail-closed mock listings, 1000-WLD fallback, timeout purchase-success; test real source/settlement receipts separately from search save. |
| P1 | MKT1286-05 | Filter v2 validation, literal wildcard escaping, seller search parity and exact NUMERIC(20,4) decimal UI/API. |
| P1 | MKT1286-06 | 20-user quota, canonical fingerprint/duplicate policy, atomic CAS/receipt/replay and merge/deletion policy. |
| P1 | MKT1286-07 | Server-side filtered ACTIVE keyset query, total order, HMAC cursor, index plan and change/restart states. |
| P1 | MKT1286-08 | NestJS/Next.js/Android error contract, consent/session/CSRF, no-store, exact string amounts. |
| P1 | MKT1286-09 | Owner UI and administrator privacy k>=20, SEO noindex, mobile WCAG AA, KPI from verified events only. |
| P1 | MKT1286-10 | Isolated PostgreSQL: 100 concurrent writes, 100 BOLA negatives, 10,001 equal-price/time rows, '%'/'_' literals, decimal 1.2500/max precision, cursor expiry/revision/merge, rollback. |

- **QA evidence:** latest previously completed v1276 FAILED/HOLD; newer v1277 local evidence (22:23 KST) shows 1,397 frontend passes/6 failures, typecheck/lint/build exit 0, tests/route-check/full/prod/bot audits exit 1. No QA final signoff observed. Root 98%, ~2.2GiB free; Test finance exact-SHA and isolated restore remain unverified; Production **BLOCKED**.
- **Rollout:** docs-only planning. Engineering branch separate, default `marketplace_saved_searches_v1=OFF`, forward-only reviewed migration, isolate DB/privilege/decimal and route-ledger regression, exact SHA CI → authenticated Test end-to-end → QA approval → gradual 1%/10%/100% → smoke; rollback disables feature without mutating listings, escrow, WLD or ledger.
- **External 10,000 reference gate:** newly accepted independently sourced, deduplicated, evidence-classified references **0**; cumulative accepted count **UNKNOWN**, target **10,000**, status **BLOCKED / IN PROGRESS**.
