## v2026.10.25.1284 — F675-128 Verified Marketplace Saved Searches (FEATURE ADDITION / DRAFT / RELEASE HOLD)

### Authority, scope and evidence
- **Cycle:** Previous Feature Improvement v1282 → Current Feature Addition v1284 → Next Feature Improvement v1286 (provisional). Start main `1c5b84a1c45baafcca83f375a6f99698121e9bb5`; authority: `AGENTS.md`, `README.md`, canonical `docs/planning/PROJECT_PLAN.md` and Korean counterpart. Stack on unmerged planning Draft #1329, engineering handoff [#1334](https://github.com/wtrdd1-hash/Woldeok-Moneyverse-Migration/issues/1334); Development A/B/C, Gemini, QA and their migration allocations remain separate. **Planning documents only.**
- **Source-confirmed gaps, not observed Production incidents:** `frontend/src/app/marketplace/marketplace-tabs.tsx` seeds `INITIAL_MARKET_LISTINGS` and locally mutates holdings/balance after buying; `market-listings-view.tsx` shows success after `setTimeout` without server settlement; `/marketplace/page.tsx` substitutes `1000` WLD on missing wallet API. Existing `GET /api/v1/marketplace/listings` queries actual `marketplace_listings` but orders `created_at DESC LIMIT/OFFSET` without a unique tie-breaker. Migration 232 defines `price_wld NUMERIC(20,4)`; do not parse through JS Number or assume integer WLD. No member saved-search persistence or CRUD API identified on checked main. Marketplace P2P trade issue #1314 is a separate Development C dependency, not this feature.

### Purpose, audience, permission and user journey
- **Purpose:** let signed-in, consented members save *validated search criteria* for real ACTIVE fixed-price marketplace listings and reopen them across devices. This is a **read-only discovery feature**, not a price lock, notification subscription, escrow, purchase, inventory or WLD mutation. No auto alerts, fake listings or inferred purchases. Initial capacity **20 active searches/member**; a search contains an optional user label (1–80 chars) and versioned filter `{query,category,maxPriceWld,sort}`. Server validates categories against a reviewed live allowlist (do not trust the hard-coded UI list); `query` NFKC-trimmed <=80 chars, no unescaped LIKE wildcards; `maxPriceWld` canonical positive decimal string fitting NUMERIC(20,4); `sort` allowlisted `newest|price_asc|price_desc`.
- **Entry:** `/marketplace` fixed-price tab → filter inputs → **Save this search** CTA (auth/consent required) → confirm name → server receipt → **Saved** with link to `/account/marketplace-searches`. The account page offers **Run search / Rename / Update filters / Delete** with explicit confirmation; clicking a saved search opens `/marketplace?savedSearch=<owner-bound opaque id>`, with server-owned filters and real listing results. Show `LOADING / EMPTY / SAVED / DUPLICATE / LIMIT_REACHED / STALE_REVISION / SESSION_EXPIRED / DATA_UNAVAILABLE / FEATURE_OFF`. Failure must never show a saved or purchase-success toast; no fallback balances. A deleted/inactive listing displays `NO_LONGER_AVAILABLE`, never a buy CTA. User may filter without saving.
- **Scope:** only fixed-price ACTIVE listings, no auction, P2P or black-market offers; search criteria and result list are private to the member. A saved query is **not** a snapshot: subsequent results may change as listings are sold/cancelled. Distinguish search-save receipt from any future purchase-settlement receipt.

### API, DB, transactions and pagination
- **NestJS authenticated+consent+CSRF mutations:** `GET /api/v1/marketplace/saved-searches?cursor=&limit=20`; `POST /api/v1/marketplace/saved-searches` with `idempotencyKey:UUID,label,filters,filterSchemaVersion=1`; `PATCH /api/v1/marketplace/saved-searches/:id` with `expectedRevision` and key; `DELETE /api/v1/marketplace/saved-searches/:id` with expected revision/key; `GET /api/v1/marketplace/saved-searches/:id/results?cursor=&limit=50`. Responses include `id,revision,normalizedFilters,receiptId,updatedAt` or bounded `items,nextCursor,liveAsOf`; 400 validation, 401 auth, 403 authorization, 404 non-owner/not found (indistinguishable externally), 409 duplicate/stale/key reuse, 429 quota/rate, 503 source unavailable. Use existing session and consent guards, CSRF on mutations, server-side actor from session only. Next.js same-origin proxy and `/app-api/v1/*` Android parity must preserve cookie/session/CSRF and error semantics.
- **Proposed migration (number reserved only by Development):** `member_market_saved_searches(id uuid PK,user_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id),label text NOT NULL,filter_schema_version smallint NOT NULL CHECK(=1),normalized_filters jsonb NOT NULL CHECK(jsonb_typeof='object'),filter_fingerprint bytea NOT NULL,revision bigint NOT NULL DEFAULT 1 CHECK(revision>0),deleted_at timestamptz NULL,created_at timestamptz,updated_at timestamptz)`; partial UNIQUE `(user_id,filter_fingerprint) WHERE deleted_at IS NULL`; index `(user_id,updated_at DESC,id DESC) WHERE deleted_at IS NULL`; `member_market_saved_search_receipts(id uuid PK,user_id,search_id,request_key uuid,payload_digest bytea,result_revision bigint,result_kind text,created_at)`, UNIQUE `(user_id,request_key)`. Review FK/retention/soft-deletion against account lifecycle and GDPR before migration. No stored listing snapshots or PII seller profiles.
- **Atomicity and lock order (AGENTS.md migration 045):** validate inputs → acquire transaction-scoped advisory lock on the idempotency UUID **before** receipt lookup → compare original payload digest and owner → lock `users` owner row `FOR UPDATE` to serialize the 20-active quota → lock saved-search row for edits, revalidate owner/revision, and persist canonical filter fingerprint, duplicate UNIQUE guard and immutable receipt in the same transaction. Same-key exact replay returns original receipt; same-key different payload returns 409; concurrency must never exceed 20. A deleted search has tombstone; re-create requires a fresh idempotency key. DB SECURITY DEFINER functions fix `search_path`, revoke PUBLIC EXECUTE and avoid broad `moneyverse_app` direct DML. No modification of existing `marketplace_listings`, WLD, escrow, ledger or inventory.
- **Result query:** apply `status='ACTIVE'`, validated category/text/price predicates in SQL *before* limit; exact decimal comparison in PostgreSQL NUMERIC. For newest `ORDER BY created_at DESC,id DESC`; price sorts `price_wld ASC|DESC,created_at DESC,id DESC`; composite index per observed EXPLAIN plans (e.g. `status,created_at DESC,id DESC` and conditional `status,category,price_wld,id`) after cardinality review, no speculative index claim. Opaque HMAC cursor binds actor, savedSearchId, normalized filter digest, sort, last full sort tuple, expiry (15 min), key version and source generation. If criteria/revision changes → 409 restart. This is a **live query**, so concurrent listing state changes can alter later pages; no completeness/snapshot guarantee unless a separately proven immutable manifest is introduced. Tests explicitly cover equal timestamps/prices and 10,001 rows.

### Administrator, security, SEO, mobile and analytics
- **Admin:** read-only aggregate active-search counts, quota failures and search-result error rates with k>=20 suppression; no private keywords, member IDs or raw result histories in admin telemetry. No admin editing of member searches. Deletion/account merge/retention are explicit and auditable, not dependent solely on FK cascade.
- **Abuse/privacy:** owner BOLA checks at DB and API; no user_id in request, prepared parameters, LIKE escape, query cost limits, <=20 saved, 5 mutations/min/user and 30 search requests/min/user (test targets, tune after measurement), max page 50, no free-text logs, CSRF and rate controls, no XSS from labels, no SSRF/external fetch, no purchase/reward endpoints in this feature. Protect member searches with `Cache-Control: private, no-store`, robots `noindex,nofollow`, sitemap exclusion; public listing SEO unaffected. Do not expose seller private fields.
- **Responsive/a11y:** keyboard-operable filter and saved-search dialogs, 44px targets, visible focus, field error summary, loading/empty/live-status announcements, reduced motion and mobile reflow to one-column rows; WCAG 2.2 AA. Android App API contract uses the same revision, cursor, decimal strings and 409/429/503 behavior. KPI: verified save rate, reopen rate D7/D30, query latency p95, zero fake success, quota/409/503, saved-search deletion rate, discovery→**verified** settlement conversion only after independent ledger receipts; retention/revenue uplift remain HYPOTHESIS.
- **Release/rollback:** `marketplace_saved_searches_v1` default OFF; P0 mock-marketplace truth gate must pass before member exposure. Forward-only reviewed migration in separately owned development branch; isolated DB role tests, actual fixed-price source and WLD/escrow regression, full CI/audits, exact-SHA Test authenticated finance E2E, independent restore and 1%→10%→100% gated rollout. On error flag OFF and hide CTA/results; preserve legitimate owner metadata under approved retention and do not touch marketplace/ledger. No runtime or Production changes in planning.

## v2026.10.25.1282 — F675-127 Support Transcript Snapshot and Status Transition Integrity (FEATURE IMPROVEMENT / DRAFT / RELEASE HOLD)

### Authority and scope
- **Cycle:** Previous Feature Addition v1280 → Current Feature Improvement v1282 → Next Feature Addition v1284 (provisional). Main `1c5b84a1c45baafcca83f375a6f99698121e9bb5` verified at start and mid; AGENTS.md, README.md, English canonical PROJECT_PLAN.md and Korean counterpart consulted. This docs-only work is stacked on unmerged Draft #1324 and coordinated with [development issue #1323](https://github.com/wtrdd1-hash/Woldeok-Moneyverse-Migration/issues/1323); QA/Development A/B/C/Gemini PRs and runtime work remain untouched.
- **Source-verified findings (migration 196, NestJS support repository/controller, db.ts):** `support_add_message` and `admin_support_reply` insert a message then unconditionally set `support_threads.status` and `last_message_at`; both check idempotency before insert, so simultaneous same-key calls can hit unique constraint `23505` rather than returning the original receipt. `admin_support_set_status(p_actor,p_thread,p_status)` has no request key and audits the requested new status only, even for no-op writes. Existing message timestamps are `clock_timestamp()` assigned at INSERT before acquiring the thread row lock; a blocked earlier writer can subsequently overwrite `last_message_at` with an older timestamp. These are **source-level race risks**, not proven production incidents. `support_thread_messages` orders ascending and caps at 500 in SQL (repository requests 200), so current UI can omit later messages. `SupportRepository` uses a generic `Queryable` (pool or client); a series of its default queries does NOT itself establish one repeatable-read snapshot.

### Corrected state and transaction model
- **Single serialization point:** for all future support writes, acquire the owned thread row `FOR UPDATE` before assigning the authoritative event sequence and effective time. Recheck actor permission, active account, consent, request-key payload and current status **inside the lock**. Append the message, apply status transition if and only if status changes, append audit and update `last_message_at=GREATEST(existing, committed_write_time)` in the same PostgreSQL transaction. Never rely on `clock_timestamp()` taken before the lock as ordering authority. A thread-local monotonically increasing `event_seq` is incremented under the same lock; event ordering is by `event_seq`, not wall clock or UUID. The message's display `created_at` remains historical metadata and is not rewritten.
- **Existing status values are exactly `open`, `waiting_user`, `resolved`**. `admin_support_set_status` is currently a three-argument boolean function; an idempotent status-change request therefore requires a **new versioned function/API contract**, not a silent signature/return-type change. New `p_request_key` UUID + canonical payload digest bind actor/thread/from/to; exact replay returns original receipt, conflicting reuse returns a deterministic 409, and no-op status requests return `unchanged` without a fabricated transition. Preserve the existing controller's status response contract until versioned clients migrate. The three existing write functions (member reply, admin reply, admin status) must converge on the same locking/transition implementation; creation records a clearly labeled initial observation, never an invented old status.
- **Proposed durable rows:** `support_thread_event_clock(thread_id PK FK, next_seq bigint CHECK(next_seq>0))` or a locked column on support_threads, chosen after migration ownership review; `support_status_events(id UUID PK, thread_id FK, seq BIGINT, actor_id UUID NULL, old_status TEXT NULL, new_status TEXT NOT NULL, origin TEXT, request_key UUID NULL, payload_digest BYTEA NULL, changed_at timestamptz)`. CHECK `origin='initial_observation' => old_status IS NULL AND request_key IS NULL`; `origin='recorded' => old_status IS NOT NULL AND request_key IS NOT NULL`; CHECK both non-null status values in allowed enum; UNIQUE(thread_id,seq) and partial UNIQUE(thread_id,request_key) WHERE request_key IS NOT NULL; index (thread_id,seq,id). No broad direct DML to `moneyverse_app`; SECURITY DEFINER functions fix search_path, revoke PUBLIC EXECUTE and verify actor ownership/admin role in DB. FK deletion/retention policy must be reviewed; 'append-only' does not mean retaining deleted personal data forever.

### Complete snapshot and cursor semantics
- **Transaction-bound export:** current `queryRows` calls on a pool are independent transactions. Implement a dedicated read-only `REPEATABLE READ` export using a pinned `PoolClient` via `connect()`, explicit `BEGIN ISOLATION LEVEL REPEATABLE READ READ ONLY`, bounded statement timeout, all owner checks/counts/rows/status events/digest generation on that SAME client, `COMMIT/ROLLBACK` and `release()` in finally. A generic `Queryable` without `connect()` must fail closed; do not borrow a financial transaction helper with a default READ COMMITTED isolation and call it snapshot-safe. Do not hold an open DB transaction across HTTP pagination requests.
- **Cross-request pagination:** `snapshotAt` and `created_at<=snapshotAt` alone do NOT prove a stable snapshot: a transaction may insert with an earlier timestamp and commit later. For truly stable multi-request export, materialize an immutable, owner-scoped bounded snapshot manifest (snapshot_id UUID, actor, thread, source revision/recorded count, canonical bytes digest, created/expires, redaction generation), or use a separately proven commit-order watermark; until one exists, each request is explicitly a fresh page, NOT certified as a complete stable cross-request transcript. Cursor HMAC binds actor/thread/snapshot_id/filter/last tuple/expiry/key version and is invalid after redaction, deletion, permission loss or expiry; reject with 409 and restart. Never silently skip/duplicate when a concurrent reply arrives.
- **Limits and failure truth:** cap 10,000 messages, 2MiB UTF-8 output and 30s, verify **both row count and byte count before HTTP 200 or streamed headers**; on breach 413 without partial file, offer owner-only paged viewer. UTF-8 TXT canonicalization includes version, thread ID, UTC ISO time, stable sequence, escaped controls, explicit `HISTORY_PARTIAL` baseline and redaction markers; SHA-256 only proves returned bytes, not independent completeness. Repeated download of unchanged immutable snapshot must return identical bytes/digest. Do not log content, send email, share URLs or cache privately identifiable data in CDN. Export API and Android App API must agree on `snapshotId`, digest, completeness, 401/404/409/413/429/503, and owner-only download. Admin aggregate k>=20; no bulk export.

### Product, accessibility, abuse controls and rollout
- **Entry/UX:** existing `/support` → `/support/transcripts/[threadId]` shows `LOADING/READY/HISTORY_PARTIAL/EXPORT_TOO_LARGE/REDACTED/STALE_SNAPSHOT/UNAVAILABLE`; CTA `Refresh snapshot` after 409, `Download TXT` only after verified server readiness; no fake success. Keep original member/admin reply flows and status semantics unchanged. Mobile and keyboard navigation, visible focus, live regions, contrast/touch targets and WCAG 2.2 AA. Private noindex/nofollow/no-store and no sitemap. Rate-limit member export (target 5/hour) and service-wide (target 20/min), detect enumeration and excessive snapshot creation; require session, consent, CSRF on mutations, and DB-enforced BOLA/BFLA.
- **KPI:** snapshot generation success and p95 latency, 409 refresh rate, 413 limit rate, history-partial rate, duplicate/reordered event count (target zero), unauthorized probe leaks (target zero), redaction invalidation latency, and stale `last_message_at` regression count (target zero); aggregate k>=20 and no message bodies or personal IDs in telemetry.
- **Test gate:** 100 concurrent same-key member/admin replies and 100 concurrent status writes → one persisted receipt/event per committed semantic change, no raw 23505/duplicate events; adversarial older-writer/newer-writer interleaving → nondecreasing `last_message_at`; 100 cross-owner probes → zero leaks; 10,001 messages/2MiB+ → explicit 413, no partial 200; late commit with earlier `created_at`, concurrent reply between pages, deletion/redaction/revoked session → stable snapshot or explicit 409, no silent gaps; 100 repeated snapshot downloads byte-for-byte/digest identical; actual PostgreSQL role/ACL negatives, Android/Next contract parity, rollback feature-OFF regression. **Not executed in planning.**
- **Deployment:** default `support_transcript_export_v1=OFF`; migration-number owner coordination and forward-only schema, shadow events after isolated DB restore and security proof; official exact-SHA CI, complete audits, real PostgreSQL, authenticated finance Test E2E, QA and rollback evidence before staged 1%→10%→100%. On regression flag OFF, retain audited append-only events subject to retention, do not roll back by deleting history. Runtime/DB/WLD/ledger/Test/Production unchanged in this planning cycle.

### P0/P1 development handoff
| Priority | ID | Concrete acceptance |
| --- | --- | --- |
| P0 | ENV1282-01 | Root 98% capacity risk, separate data volume, isolated Test/DB and verified restore; preserve all worker worktrees. |
| P0 | SEC1282-02 | Owner/admin SECURITY DEFINER checks, PUBLIC revoke, app-role least privilege, deletion/redaction authorization. |
| P0 | REL1282-03 | Latest exact-main QA v1275 FAILED/HOLD, migration 266 checksum, 280 conflict, full audits, signed-in Test finance E2E and rollback. |
| P0 | SUP1282-04 | Prove SQL status/last_message_at races, no-op audit and idempotency 23505; design versioned API with compatibility. |
| P1 | SUP1282-05 | Pinned-client read-only repeatable-read export and immutable snapshot or honest per-page freshness, explicit limits. |
| P1 | SUP1282-06 | Thread-locked sequence, atomic status transitions for all 3 write paths, replay receipt and nondecreasing timestamp. |
| P1 | SUP1282-07 | Versioned NestJS, Next same-origin, Android owner API, cursor/409/413/error parity and private headers. |
| P1 | SUP1282-08 | Member timeline/snapshot CTA, mobile WCAG AA, stale/partial/redacted/large states, noindex. |
| P1 | SUP1282-09 | Privacy/retention/account merge, admin k>=20, abuse limits, redaction invalidation, KPI. |
| P1 | SUP1282-10 | Isolated PostgreSQL concurrency, late commit, snapshot digest, BOLA, 10,001 rows, exact-SHA Test and rollback. |

- **Fresh QA:** v1275 (2026-10-25 20:24 KST) FAILED/HOLD: exact-main build/typecheck/lint PASS, frontend tests 1397 PASS/6 FAIL, full/prod/bot audits FAIL, route-ledger integration missing inventory, dev DB ACL/migration 266 remain; public smoke 200, root 98% (~2.5GiB), Test/Production SHA not latest main. Previous QA #1317 candidate PASS is not full release certification.
- **Independent reference gate:** 10,000 independent published external sources require deduplication, provenance and applicability classification; newly approved this run **0**, cumulative verified **UNKNOWN**, target 10,000, **BLOCKED / IN PROGRESS**. No internal document counts as an external source.


## v2026.10.25.1280 — F675-127 Verified Private Support Transcript and Status Timeline (FEATURE ADDITION / DRAFT / RELEASE HOLD)

### Authority, boundaries and source-confirmed gap
- **Cycle:** Previous Feature Improvement v1278 → Current Feature Addition v1280 → Next Feature Improvement v1282 (provisional). Latest `main` `1c5b84a1c45baafcca83f375a6f99698121e9bb5` rechecked at start and mid; `AGENTS.md`, `README.md`, English canonical `PROJECT_PLAN.md` and Korean counterpart compared. Predecessor Draft PR #1316 is unmerged. This is a **planning-only** branch; do not alter or deploy runtime/DB/feature switches/WLD/ledger, merge, or promote Test/Production.
- **Evidence:** `frontend/src/app/support/page.tsx` already provides member/admin support threads, one selected conversation and reply; `backend/src/support/support.repository.ts` defaults to 50 member threads and 200 member messages (500 admin messages); `packages/database/migrations/196-user-admin-support-chat.sql` orders/limits without keyset cursors. `admin_support_set_status` overwrites current status and emits a generic admin audit event, but does not provide a member-readable old/new status transition ledger. No owner transcript export endpoint appears in current `support.controller.ts`. Do not claim all historic messages/status changes can be reconstructed.
- **Scope:** owner-only human-readable UTF-8 TXT transcript and paged message/status viewer. Existing support communication and moderator decisions remain authoritative; no new reward, money, transfer, public share, email, attachment or notification delivery. Existing support/admin API paths remain backward compatible. Coordination: QA Draft #1317, development A/B/C, Gemini and other open PRs are **separate**.

### Purpose, personas, permissions and navigation
- **Purpose:** let a member preserve the support messages that actually exist and inspect *recorded* administrative status transitions without confusing capped UI lists with complete history. Target: signed-in active members with accepted consent; staff may see authorized cases via existing admin console, but this feature does **not** introduce bulk admin transcript export.
- **Entry:** existing `/support` thread row adds `View full history` and `Download transcript` CTA, both authenticated; new `/support/transcripts/[threadId]` shows the thread title, timestamp, current status, recorded status timeline, message pagination and TXT download. Empty/no thread, loading, unauthorized, deleted, >export cap, service unavailable, status-history incomplete and success states have distinct accessible copy. Only a response from the authenticated server with correct owner/thread ID can show `DOWNLOAD_READY`.
- **State machine:** `LOADING → READY | EMPTY | HISTORY_PARTIAL | EXPORT_TOO_LARGE | ACCESS_DENIED | UNAVAILABLE`; download `IDLE → GENERATING → READY | FAILED`; a status baseline captured when the feature first ships is `HISTORY_PARTIAL`, not proof of the original opening status. No client-local success or synthetic historical events.

### Exact API and application integration contract
- Keep existing `/api/v1/support/threads`, `POST /threads`, `/threads/:id/messages`, admin reply/status paths untouched for backward compatibility. Add owner-only `GET /api/v1/support/threads/history?limit=20&cursor=...`, `GET /api/v1/support/threads/:id/transcript?limit=100&cursor=...` (messages + status events, independent signed cursors and historyCompleteness), and `GET /api/v1/support/threads/:id/transcript/export` returning `text/plain; charset=utf-8` with `Content-Disposition: attachment`, `Cache-Control: private, no-store`, `X-Content-Type-Options: nosniff` and `ETag`/digest scoped to a single verified snapshot. An owner with no permission receives indistinguishable 404; unauthenticated 401, stale cursor 409, oversized 413, throttled 429, infrastructure 503. Validate UUIDs, query bounds and cursor signatures; no raw SQL client predicates.
- Next.js server-only `frontend/src/lib/api.ts` remains the only browser-to-NestJS hop. New Next route handler for same-origin authenticated download must not expose `INTERNAL_API_TOKEN`; no static caching, public CDN caching, cross-origin redirect or bearer token in URL. Android App API contract must provide equivalent owner-only metadata/pagination/download semantics with platform-appropriate streamed file response; contract fixtures include all errors and partial-history flags.
- Keyset cursors bind actor, thread, filter, last `(created_at,id)`, snapshot boundary, expiry and HMAC key version; validate ownership on **every** page. Sort `(created_at ASC,id ASC)` for messages and `(seq ASC,id ASC)` for status events. A fresh export uses one read-only `REPEATABLE READ` transaction and records `snapshotAt`, exact row counts and SHA-256 of canonical output; the digest proves byte integrity only, **not** independent certification or pre-feature historical completeness. Explicit hard limit **10,000 messages / 2 MiB / 30 seconds**; exceeding it returns 413 and points to paged viewing, never silently truncates. Escape control characters, normalize CRLF to LF, preserve Korean/Unicode, and prefix metadata that identifies incomplete status history. No CSV or HTML export to avoid formula injection/XSS.

### DB, invariants, transactions and admin behavior
- Proposed `support_status_events(id uuid PK, thread_id uuid FK, actor_id uuid FK, seq bigint NOT NULL, old_status text NULL, new_status text NOT NULL, changed_at timestamptz NOT NULL, request_key uuid NOT NULL, origin text NOT NULL CHECK(origin IN ('recorded','initial_observation')))`; constraints: allowed status values (`open`, `waiting_user`, `resolved`), `UNIQUE(thread_id,seq)`, `UNIQUE(request_key)`, immutable append-only events, index `(thread_id,seq,id)` and partial member-thread index `(user_id,last_message_at DESC,id DESC)` reuse existing index. Do not write a fictitious `old_status` for pre-feature transitions: baseline `initial_observation` stores observed current status and `historyCompleteness=partial`, with explicit null old status if a baseline event is used; schema must allow that case and enforce old_status only for recorded transitions. No table DML grants to `moneyverse_app`; expose owner-checked `SECURITY DEFINER` read functions, with fixed `search_path` and explicit `REVOKE FROM PUBLIC`/`GRANT EXECUTE` only.
- In future runtime migration, replace `admin_support_set_status` **transactionally**: lock the thread row `FOR UPDATE`, authorize admin in DB, reject invalid transition, read previous status, change status and append the event + admin audit within the same transaction; request key/idempotency for retried admin actions; duplicate identical request returns existing receipt; key reused with changed payload → 409. Member/admin replies that implicitly change status (`support_add_message`, `admin_support_reply`) must also emit status transitions in the same transaction when state changes, or timeline cannot claim completeness. Serialize concurrent replies/status changes and enforce monotonic `seq`; zero changes should not emit fictitious transitions. Do not retrofit old status history from generic audit logs unless independently proven complete.
- Existing support bodies may contain sensitive information; no new body logging, search indexing, external object storage or staff bulk export. Approved account deletion/retention policy governs redaction; when redacted, show `REDACTED` and never reconstruct text from logs. Account merge must fail closed until owner transfer policy and immutable history handling are approved. Admin sees only non-identifying aggregate export failure/success and latency at cohort size `k>=20`; staff case access remains existing admin role-checked functions.

### Security, mobile, accessibility, SEO and measurable acceptance
- Security: enforce active member + consent, BOLA/BFLA checks inside DB functions, session/CSRF for any future mutation, export rate limit **5/member/hour** and **20/minute service-wide** (tunable), uniform unauthorized errors, strict MIME/no-store/CSP, anti-enumeration, no permanent share URLs. Admin writes require existing AdminGuard/AdminSessionGuard and DB admin role check. Enforce least privilege and audit sensitive export events without message bodies or user identifiers in aggregate analytics.
- UX: responsive mobile layout, focus management, explicit keyboard-accessible `Download TXT`, polite live-region progress and failure, visible partial-history warning, localized dates with UTC ISO source, WCAG 2.2 AA focus/contrast/touch target. Private pages `robots: noindex,nofollow`, no sitemap/canonical public URLs, private responses `no-store`.
- KPIs (no content/PII): authenticated export completion rate, percent of transcripts with `HISTORY_PARTIAL`, p95 export time, 413 rate, 404 owner-scope probes, admin status-event gap count (target zero post-activation); suppress cohorts <20, never treat a download as a support resolution.
- Test: 100 foreign-owner probes → 0 message/status/metadata leakage; 100 concurrent admin status/reply mutations → exactly one ordered event per committed status transition; 10,001 synthetic messages → explicit 413 or complete keyset pagination without silent truncation; UTF-8 Korean, CRLF/control escaping and 2 MiB limit; 100 repeat downloads of unchanged snapshot → same digest, a newly committed message → new snapshot/digest; member deletion/merge/role revocation, expired cursor, admin spoofing, race at pagination boundary, 401/404/409/413/429/503; real PostgreSQL `moneyverse_app` privilege-negative tests, Next/Android contract and regression tests. **No runtime tests performed by planning.**
- Deployment: feature flag `support_transcript_export_v1` OFF by default; migration/permissions and shadow status recording only after isolated Test and verified backup restore; exact-SHA official CI, full dependency audit, Test finance E2E, DB ACL and migration parity, security review, QA approval, then staged 1%→10%→100% with error/latency thresholds. Roll back via flag OFF and previous API routes; do not delete append-only events or downgrade schema until audited. Production remains RELEASE HOLD.

### Prioritized actionable backlog and evidence gate
| Priority | ID | Developer acceptance |
| --- | --- | --- |
| P0 | ENV1280-01 | Root capacity budget, isolated DB/Test, backup restore evidence before writes. |
| P0 | SEC1280-02 | DB least privilege, actor-scoped SECURITY DEFINER, admin role enforcement and no PUBLIC grants. |
| P0 | REL1280-03 | Exact-SHA official CI/security/Test E2E, migration 266/280 resolution and rollback gate. |
| P0 | SUP1280-04 | Prove current 50/200/500 caps, status provenance and explicit HISTORY_PARTIAL; prevent misleading completeness claims. |
| P1 | SUP1280-05 | Owner-only keyset pagination, snapshot-bound 10k/2MiB TXT export and digest. |
| P1 | SUP1280-06 | Atomic append-only status transitions for admin changes and implicit reply transitions. |
| P1 | SUP1280-07 | NestJS owner API and Android App API parity, error/stream contracts. |
| P1 | SUP1280-08 | Next.js support/history/download CTAs, states, responsive/a11y. |
| P1 | SUP1280-09 | Privacy, retention, admin k>=20, noindex/no-store and KPI. |
| P1 | SUP1280-10 | Real-DB concurrency, BOLA, 10,001-message, Unicode, exact-SHA Test and rollback regression. |

- **Operational gate:** last completed QA v1273 FAILED/HOLD; QA Draft #1317 local frontend 1404/1404 PASS and production-dependency audit PASS on candidate SHA, but full dev audit still 2 Critical/8 High and official CI/exact-SHA Test/DB restore not verified. Root disk at run start 98% (~2.6 GiB free), dev app-role grants and migration 266/280 unresolved. Do not call release PASS.
- **External independent reference gate:** 10,000 distinct externally published sources with deduplication, provenance, classification and verified applicability remain required; **new approved = 0; cumulative approved = UNKNOWN; target 10,000; status BLOCKED/IN PROGRESS**. Do not fabricate or count this internal specification as an independent source.

## v2026.10.25.1278 — F675-126 Calculator Scenario Provenance, Replay and Promotion Integrity (FEATURE IMPROVEMENT / DRAFT / RELEASE HOLD)

### Cycle, authority and reproduced implementation mismatch
- **Previous** Feature Addition v1276 → **Current** Feature Improvement v1278 → **Next** Feature Addition v1280 (provisional). Read latest main `1c5b84a1c45baafcca83f375a6f99698121e9bb5`, AGENTS.md, README.md, English canonical PROJECT_PLAN.md and Korean comparison at start/mid. Stack this docs-only candidate on unmerged Draft #1310; engineering issue [#1309](https://github.com/wtrdd1-hash/Woldeok-Moneyverse-Migration/issues/1309). QA v1273 FAILED/P0 HOLD. No code, DB, WLD, ledger, Test or Production changes.
- **Reproduced:** `CalculatorScenarioData.type` only accepts stock/retirement/pension/isa/tax, while `compound-calculator-client.tsx` and `loan-calculator-client.tsx` both pass `type: 'stock'`; thus `type` and `ticker` are **not authoritative calculator identities**. Loan `details` contains formatted principal/rate/term/totalRepayment but omits `repaymentType`; compound `details` are currency-formatted strings; retirement `details` mix inputs and derived outputs. Existing results are not lossless replayable input payloads.
- **Reproduced:** `calculator-save-action.tsx` identifies saved items by title OR stock ticker; writes `wdmv_saved_scenarios` under a shared key and deduplicates by stockName/title, silently discarding distinct scenarios with identical titles. Its exception path still sets saved=true; browser cookie/localStorage guesses authentication; fire-and-forget `/api/portfolio/watchlist` has no matching main Next route. `watchlist-promotion-engine.tsx` sets `wdmv_scenarios_promoted=true` and success even on null/non-2xx fetch or exception; `calculator-retention-funnel.tsx` reports alert setup and a 10,000 WLD booking using local state only. Do not treat these claims or flags as server truth.
- **Boundary:** F675-126 is a private scenario-input vault, not a stock watchlist, trade, reward, notification, calculator tax certification or cross-account migration. Coordinate with QA calculator/link PR #1306 and Development C watchlist PR #1308, plus Gemini/A/B ownership; avoid overlapping runtime files.

### Canonical provenance, UX and replay contract
- Add an **explicit adapter registry** keyed by stable `calculatorId`, `inputSchemaVersion`, `formulaVersion`, `locale` and `currencyCode`, not by legacy type/title/ticker or displayed result. Start with compound-interest, loan-interest and retirement **only after** each adapter extracts every independent input and units from the actual form and round-trips them through the current calculation engine. For loans, require repayment method, principal, rate, term and frequency; no inference from total repayment. Store typed decimal strings and enums; keep derived metrics as optional display-only, never as input or proof of a historical quote.
- `UNSUPPORTED_LEGACY` for incomplete/mislabeled `type:'stock'` drafts; `REPLAY_UNAVAILABLE` for missing loan repayment method or noninvertible formatted values; `FORMULA_CHANGED` for calculation rule changes; `LOCAL_ONLY`, `AUTH_REQUIRED`, `SAVING`, `SYNCED_VERIFIED`, `CONFLICT`, `OFFLINE_UNCONFIRMED`, `DELETED` remain distinct. Never convert these to verified server saves by changing labels. Recalculate only after typed-input validation; display historical assumptions, formula version and estimated/not-advice disclaimer.
- Entry: existing /tools save actions; owner-only /account/calculator-scenarios list and /account/calculator-scenarios/[scenarioId] detail. CTA: Preview local draft, Save to account, Retry with same key, View receipt, Recalculate, Rename, Delete, Import selected. A failed network write must remain UNCONFIRMED; never show success before matching server receipt plus owner-scoped read-back. Clear prior user state on logout/account switch; stale tab responses cannot overwrite current identity.
- Separate local-only saved calculator inputs from `wdmv_calculator_watchlist` and legacy `wdmv_saved_scenarios` promotion records. Import is **opt-in, preview-first and per-item**, never automatic on login; dedupe by canonical typed-input digest and calculator/schema, not title/ticker; distinct same-title inputs remain distinct. Reject malformed, oversized, stale, forged, non-finite or noncanonical inputs and any PII. Preserve originals until receipt/read-back; a failed import cannot set `wdmv_scenarios_promoted` or silently erase local drafts. Old promotion flags never count as imported. No GET side effects.

### API, DB invariants, concurrency and security
- Keep owner-only NestJS GET list/detail and POST/PATCH/DELETE `/api/v1/account/calculator-scenarios`, plus Android `/app-api/v1` parity. Server session supplies actor; CSRF/consent for browser writes, app-token scope for Android; no browser cookie substring heuristics. Payload: `calculatorId` allowlist, `inputSchemaVersion`, `formulaVersion`, `currencyCode`, canonical `inputs`, title, `idempotencyKey`; updates/deletes require `expectedRevision`. Response includes immutable receiptId, scenarioId, owner-bound revision, canonical digest, committedAt and explicit verified state. No raw sensitive inputs in receipts/logs/analytics.
- Define deterministic JSON canonicalization and decimal normalization (no JS floating-point roundtrip for money), fixed units/currency, formula fingerprint, field ordering and explicit missing/null handling; reject unknown keys and out-of-range values. Include calculatorId, versions, actor, operation, target ID, expectedRevision and canonical inputs in request fingerprint. Same actor/key/fingerprint replays the **original committed result**, including delete; same key with different fingerprint is 409. A retry after commit-before-response must read original receipt, not create a second scenario.
- Forward-only collision-free additive tables/indexes from v1276, append-only receipt trigger, `UNIQUE(user_id,request_key)`, `UNIQUE(user_id,id)` owner lookup, active quota 100 under serialized owner lock, `(user_id,deleted_at,updated_at DESC,id DESC)` keyset index. DB `SECURITY DEFINER` functions with fixed `search_path`, authenticated actor binding, REVOKE direct app table DML and PUBLIC EXECUTE, narrowly GRANT EXECUTE; never rely on TypeScript WHERE for BOLA. Lock order member→scenario→receipt; account merge and soft-delete must preflight both source and target, never silently transfer historical ownership or leak local data.
- Signed keyset cursor binds owner/session, sort/filter, visibility generation and expiry; membership change or deletion invalidates cursor; 10,001 synthetic records must paginate without gaps/duplicates. Distinguish 401 auth, 403 consent/CSRF, 404 other-owner/missing, 409 revision/idempotency conflict, 413 >8KiB, 422 unsupported adapter/formula/inputs, 429 quota/rate, 503 DB failure. Fail closed and no success receipts on rollback.
- Manager may see only suppressed aggregate counts (k>=20) of verified saves, rejected legacy imports, replay conflicts, formula drift and deletion latency; no scenario input, title, URL, identity or personal export. Private routes noindex/no-store/sitemap excluded; public calculator canonical stays indexable. Mobile 320px, 44px targets, WCAG 2.2 AA, screen reader error/receipt live regions, keyboard-only import/conflict, reduced motion, localized copy in all shipped locales (at least the five-locale QA gate). Do not claim permanent sync/alerts/10,000 WLD without separate verified contracts.
- KPI denominators are server receipt-confirmed saves, successful cross-device replay, local-import reject rate, verified read-back latency, stale-formula rate, conflict/503, 7-day revisit and privacy deletion SLA. Do not count click, localStorage flag, modal, optimistic toast or attempted POST as verified conversion.

### P0/P1 engineering backlog, tests and release
- **P0** `ENV1278-01`: preserve worker worktrees, root capacity alarm at 98% (~2.8GiB free), isolate dev/Production DB and prove independent restore. **P0** `SEC1278-02`: revoke broad dev app-role DML (235/241), PUBLIC bank EXECUTE (3), enforce owner-only SECURITY DEFINER and negative BOLA tests; Production ACL still unknown. **P0** `REL1278-03`: unblock QA v1273 audits (full Critical2/High10; prod High2), migration266 checksum/280 numbering, complete CI, exact-SHA authenticated Test finance E2E and restore before release. **P0** `CAL1278-04`: eliminate false saved/promoted/alert/bonus UI states and cross-account stale-session leaks without overlapping QA/stock-watchlist code ownership.
- **P1** `CAL1278-05`: typed calculator adapter/schema/formula registry and legacy `stock` mislabel detection; **P1** `CAL1278-06`: canonical digest, serialized quota, immutable idempotent receipts and read-back; **P1** `CAL1278-07`: NestJS+Android owner API, signed cursor, error taxonomy; **P1** `CAL1278-08`: Next.js list/detail, explicit local import, safe logout/retry and accessible copy; **P1** `CAL1278-09`: privacy, admin suppression, SEO, mobile/a11y and evidence-only KPIs; **P1** `CAL1278-10`: real-PG negative ACL, crash/retry, regression, phased rollout and rollback.
- **Acceptance proposed, not executed:** compound and loan `type:'stock'` drafts cannot be mis-imported as equities; missing loan repaymentType never produces a verified replay; same-title/different-input drafts survive; malformed/PII/NaN/locale-formatted values rejected or explicitly normalized; 100 identical concurrent writes → exactly one receipt; 100 foreign-owner probes → zero disclosure; 10,001 synthetic scenarios → no cursor gaps; quota races and account merge/deletion fail closed; failure after DB commit before HTTP response returns same receipt; 401/409/422/503 and offline/cross-tab/logout/five-locale/320px/screen-reader cases tested. No WLD/ledger/stock-watchlist/notification writes.
- **Release:** planning only; feature flag default OFF; migration additive and separately reviewed; exact-SHA official CI, security, real-PG ACL, authenticated finance Test E2E, independent restore and GitOps approval before <1%→5%→25%→100% staged rollout. Rollback disables UI/writes and preserves immutable receipts/data; no destructive down-migration. Production **RELEASE HOLD**.
- **External independent reference gate:** 10,000 independently published, canonical-URL deduplicated, actually reviewed and classified sources required. New approved **0**, cumulative approved **UNKNOWN**, **BLOCKED / IN PROGRESS**; standards or unreviewed candidates are not counted. Integrate only into existing bilingual master/review/project-plan documents; do not create fragmented planning files.

Show SYNCED_VERIFIED only after server receipt matches actor, scenario ID, revision and canonical input digest. Network uncertainty stays UNCONFIRMED with safe retry; no optimistic success. Reject malformed/oversized JSON, unknown calculator/schema, out-of-range units, injection, sensitive identity data and duplicate keys. No automatic upload on login. Unsupported adapters remain explicitly local-only.

### API, PostgreSQL, transactions and security
- NestJS owner API: GET /api/v1/account/calculator-scenarios?cursor&limit (1..50), GET /api/v1/account/calculator-scenarios/:id, POST /api/v1/account/calculator-scenarios, PATCH /api/v1/account/calculator-scenarios/:id, DELETE /api/v1/account/calculator-scenarios/:id. Body: calculatorType enum, schemaVersion positive integer, title 1..80, allowlisted inputs, idempotencyKey UUID; update/delete require expectedRevision. Receipt: id, owner-bound revision, canonicalInputDigest, receiptId, savedAt, state. Android App API parity required; actor always from authenticated server session, Consent and CSRF guards for writes.
- Errors: 401 no auth; 403 consent/CSRF/role; 404 nonowner/missing; 409 version/key-body conflict; 413 >8KiB; 422 invalid/obsolete schema; 429 quota; 503 DB unavailable. Signed keyset cursor bound to owner/session/filter/revision/expiry; (updated_at DESC,id DESC) and no fabricated counts.
- Proposed collision-free additive migration: calculator_scenarios(id UUID PK, user_id UUID FK, calculator_type allowlist CHECK, schema_version int>0, title varchar(80), inputs jsonb validated/<=8KiB, revision bigint>0, input_digest bytea, created_at, updated_at, deleted_at); indexes (user_id,deleted_at,updated_at DESC,id DESC), (user_id,id). calculator_scenario_receipts(id UUID PK, user_id FK, scenario_id FK, request_key UUID, request_fingerprint bytea, operation enum, revision bigint, canonical_digest bytea, created_at, UNIQUE(user_id,request_key)). Append-only receipts without raw input copies. 100 active scenarios/member, import max 30/batch.
- SECURITY DEFINER owner-only functions; revoke app-role direct table DML and PUBLIC EXECUTE. Deterministic owner quota lock → scenario lock; CAS expectedRevision; canonical request fingerprint; same key/body returns original receipt, same key/different body 409. Mutation and receipt commit atomically, failures have no success receipt. Account merge and soft-delete preflight, no assumed FK cascade.
- Allowlist numeric ranges, units, precision and versioned formula inputs; never accept account numbers, addresses, identifiers, secrets, salary slips or freeform PII. Treat financial-looking inputs as personal data; approved encryption/retention/deletion, no body logs/analytics. Recompute using current calculator engine with formula version/assumption date/estimate disclaimer; stale schema is STALE_CALCULATOR_VERSION, not silently migrated or presented as a current quote.

## v2026.10.25.1274 — F675-125 Monthly Statement Source/Precision/Closure Integrity (FEATURE IMPROVEMENT / PLANNING DRAFT / RELEASE HOLD)

### Cycle, authority, verified discrepancy, and boundaries
- Previous **Feature Addition v1272** → Current **Feature Improvement v1274** → Next **Feature Addition v1276** (provisional). Main SHA **1c5b84a1c45baafcca83f375a6f99698121e9bb5** rechecked at start/mid; AGENTS.md, README.md, English canonical PROJECT_PLAN.md and Korean comparison read. Stacked on unmerged planning Draft PR #1296, development issue #1295; no runtime/DB/Test/Production deployment or ledger writes.
- **P0 design correction (verified, not hypothetical):** migration 194-wide-wld-numeric-storage.sql changes ledger_postings.amount and account_balances.available_amount to unconstrained PostgreSQL NUMERIC. The development DB information_schema independently confirms both are NUMERIC. The v1272 draft used BIGINT for opening/closing and debit/credit/delta archive fields; that draft schema would overflow on legitimate wide-WLD amounts. This v1274 specification **supersedes v1272 BIGINT proposals**, without rewriting historical planning notes.
- **Further source facts:** init/001 and wallet.repository.ts use debit as positive account delta and credit as negative; migration 083 implements correction as a new reversal transaction linked by reverses_transaction_id; migration 163 retains historical source-account ownership and creates a real ACCOUNT_MERGE_TRANSFER transaction; ledger_transactions.created_at and ledger_postings.created_at are distinct timestamps. These are source facts, not proof of any Production statement defect. Dev schema is not asserted to equal Production.
- Keep F675-125 strictly read-only to economy; never mutate WLD, postings, balances, rewards, or legacy wallet APIs. Other owners A/B/C, Gemini and QA own runtime work; overlapping A recent CSV #1213 and C P2P #1286 remain separate. This is documentation-only.

### Corrected numeric and provenance schema contract (engineering proposal, NOT IMPLEMENTED)
- Proposed generation columns opening_cash, opening_bank, closing_cash, closing_bank and totals: **NUMERIC with CHECK(value = trunc(value))** where non-null; proposed row debit_amount, credit_amount, delta_amount: **NUMERIC** with nonnegative debit/credit and delta = debit-credit. No BIGINT casts, JS Number conversion, 64-bit DTO coercion or floating point anywhere on monetary read/export. Database returns canonical base-10 integer strings; validate /^-?(0|[1-9][0-9]*)$/ with no exponents or decimals; compute with SQL NUMERIC or JS BigInt only after canonical validation, never serialize JS BigInt directly. Negative deltas allowed, negative debit/credit not allowed. Use CHECK(opening_cash IS NULL OR opening_cash=trunc(opening_cash)) etc; all four amounts must be NULL when UNVERIFIABLE; no fabricated zero or partial VERIFIED.
- Preserve the existing proposed four-table generation/head/row/receipt structure, with unique(owner_user_id,month_start,generation), immutable generation FK, unique(generation_id,transaction_id,account_id), owner/month head pointer and source row count. Each row stores source ledger_transaction_id, account_id, transaction_created_at (canonical period key), account_type, source posting count and exact NUMERIC sums; optionally source posting-id digest for deterministic proof. Group repeated postings of one transaction/account before inserting a row; account_id remains an immutable source key, not a transferable owner alias. Add NOT NULL source_generation_digest, checkpoint_version, source_seal_id and HMAC key-version metadata where VERIFIED; never expose keys. Enforce all amount and status invariants in DB, not only DTOs.
- **Period semantics:** filter on ledger_transactions.created_at in [Seoul first-day 00:00, next-month first-day 00:00), converted to UTC instants; never use posting.created_at as the membership key or mix a transaction across months. Preserve posting.created_at as audit metadata, and fail verification if posting rows are missing or incompatible with the transaction. Sort source proof deterministically by transaction_created_at, transaction_id, account_id, posting_id; signed cursor uses owner/session/month/generation and that immutable sort tuple, expiry and key version. Snapshot source query must not apply LIMIT before aggregation.
- **Sign and reversal semantics:** account delta is SUM(debit amount) - SUM(credit amount), matching economy_post_transaction and wallet.repository.ts; cash↔bank internal transfers cancel in combined net, but appear as separate pocket rows. An ADMIN_ADJUSTMENT reversal is a new posted transaction in its own period; link its reverses_transaction_id for context, never retroactively erase or move the original month. Reconcile entire source transaction debit/credit parity across all accounts separately from owner pocket deltas; flag broken parity/unknown accounts rather than publishing VERIFIED.

### Completion barrier, ownership and publication
- **Do not confuse snapshot isolation with a durable close.** PostgreSQL REPEATABLE READ plus MAX(created_at), MAX(id), a row count, or a 24-hour wait does not prove no in-flight transaction with an earlier transaction timestamp can commit later. VERIFIED requires an independently auditable, monotonic ledger writer-coordinated source seal / commit-order watermark covering the whole closed period, all relevant writer paths (including corrections, administrative writes, account merge and legacy direct-balance adjustments), plus reconciliation of postings and independent opening/closing anchors. If no such protocol is implemented or any writer bypasses it, mark UNVERIFIABLE with machine-readable reason SOURCE_SEAL_UNAVAILABLE; do not claim historical completeness.
- Publication job must acquire owner/month namespaced lock, obtain a consistent snapshot, validate source-seal identity and all writers' coverage, verify owner source account IDs and active status, calculate NUMERIC amounts and posting parity, then insert one complete immutable generation and HMAC receipt and advance one head atomically. Recheck seal and account status before commit; if seal changed or late source arrives, abort/retry and append a new **complete** revision generation only after a new verified seal. No partial replacement, no in-place row rewrite, no blind replay; identical owner/month/seal/digest returns original receipt. Reader pins head generation once per request and validates signed cursor; changed head returns 409 restart.
- Migration 163 account merge keeps source wallet accounts under the merged UUID while moving funds through ACCOUNT_MERGE_TRANSFER; **do not automatically treat the survivor's historical ledger as containing the merged account's earlier postings**. Before merge, block when either source or target has any statement generation/head/receipt until a separately approved ownership and privacy policy exists. A legitimate merge transfer appears in the month it was posted, not as an invented prior-month opening balance. Migration 010 soft-delete revokes access but does not physically delete member rows; require erasure/retention workflow for statement rows, HMAC receipts, cached CSV and backups. Private owner-only SECURITY DEFINER functions with fixed search_path, no app direct table privileges or PUBLIC EXECUTE; negative owner/cross-session tests required.
- Fail states: PENDING_SOURCE (open month), NOT_ARCHIVED (no record), UNVERIFIABLE with SOURCE_SEAL_UNAVAILABLE / MISSING_OPENING_ANCHOR / FRACTIONAL_SOURCE / UNBALANCED_SOURCE / OWNER_AMBIGUOUS / CHECKPOINT_CHANGED / SOURCE_GAP; VERIFIED and REVISED only with complete proof. Never turn a DB error into EMPTY_VERIFIED. VERIFIED CSV only, no-store/noindex, sanitized filename and spreadsheet-formula neutralization, privacy-safe admin aggregate k>=20. Accessible 320px/200% zoom, keyboard focus, live status, Android app-api parity, no counterpart UUID leaks; KPI: zero false VERIFIED, zero precision truncation, zero cross-owner rows, zero extra generations on idempotent close.

### Updated executable engineering backlog and gates
- **P0** ENV1274-01: 95%-used root (~4.8GiB free at 17:08 KST), isolate DB/Test/Production and prove backup restore. SEC1274-02: remediate dev app direct UPDATE+DELETE on 235/241 tables and 3 PUBLIC bank EXECUTE grants, verify owner isolation. REL1274-03: resolve migration 266 checksum provenance, competing 280 IDs, frontend 5 failures (QA v1271), dependency audit Critical2/High10, exact-SHA authenticated Test finance E2E and independent QA PASS. WAL1274-04: source seal / writer inventory / complete-period watermark and independent historical anchors, fail closed if impossible.
