## v2026.10.25.1272 — F675-125 Verified Owner-Only Monthly WLD Statements (FEATURE ADDITION / PLANNING DRAFT / RELEASE HOLD)
- **GitHub:** [Draft PR #1296](https://github.com/wtrdd1-hash/Woldeok-Moneyverse-Migration/pull/1296) OPEN/HOLD, [development issue #1295](https://github.com/wtrdd1-hash/Woldeok-Moneyverse-Migration/issues/1295) OPEN. Unmerged stacked documentation-only; runtime/DB/Test/Production untouched.

### Authority, source audit and scope
- **Cycle:** Previous Feature Improvement v1270 → Current Feature Addition v1272 → Next Feature Improvement v1274 (provisional). Authoritative main `1c5b84a1c45baafcca83f375a6f99698121e9bb5` checked at start and midpoint, AGENTS.md/README.md and canonical EN/KO PROJECT_PLAN consulted. Predecessor Draft #1289 remains unmerged; new handoff [#1295](https://github.com/wtrdd1-hash/Woldeok-Moneyverse-Migration/issues/1295). Documentation only; no runtime/DB/WLD/ledger/Test/Production changes.
- **Verified code gap:** NestJS `GET /wallet?recent=1..50` is bounded in `wallet.controller.ts` and `wallet.repository.ts`; `/wallet/activity` fetches 50 entries and client `export-ledger-csv.tsx` exports only the rows already received. Source joins `ledger_transactions` and `ledger_postings` to `accounts.owner_user_id`, with USER_CASH and USER_BANK deltas and BIGINT amounts. No complete monthly statement, historical opening-balance authority, or month archive identified in inspected main. This is a **missing member workflow**, not a proven live ledger discrepancy.
- **Product intent:** let a consenting active member review their own closed-month virtual WLD flows and download a source-backed statement without mistaking a recent-50 CSV for a complete month. The statement is a read-only derived record; never posts money, alters cash/bank, adjusts rewards, or changes existing wallet/history. WLD is in-service virtual currency, not a bank deposit or regulated financial statement. Prefer an on-demand, source-verified month read with immutable generation publication over an unverified client aggregation; historical months with missing anchor are explicitly UNVERIFIABLE.

### Users, journeys, permissions and interface
- Active signed-in consent-current members only; anonymous → SIGNED_OUT/login; restricted/deleted accounts → 403. Existing `/wallet` and `/wallet/activity` gain a secondary **Monthly statements** link. `/wallet/statements` lists closed Asia/Seoul months with status, date range, cash/bank/combined delta only when verified, last generation and **View statement** CTA; `/wallet/statements/YYYY-MM` displays opening/closing balances per pocket only when independently proven, debit/credit transaction rows, source-completeness timestamp, revision badge, CSV action and return navigation. Future month 422, current open month PENDING (not VERIFIED), missing historical anchor UNVERIFIABLE, no archived generation NOT_ARCHIVED. Status is never silently replaced by zero.
- State machine: SIGNED_OUT, LOADING, PENDING_SOURCE, VERIFIED, NOT_ARCHIVED, UNVERIFIABLE, REVISED, EMPTY_VERIFIED, PAGE_CONFLICT, DOWNLOAD_READY, RATE_LIMITED, FEATURE_UNAVAILABLE, ERROR. A zero-transaction month may be EMPTY_VERIFIED **only** if opening/closing anchor and contiguous source coverage are proven. Distinguish unavailable backend from genuine empty month. On account switch/logout clear server/client owner caches, signed cursors and downloads. Screen-reader status live region, table headings/captions, keyboard focus return, 320px layout, 200% zoom, WCAG 2.2 AA; no invented chart values or generic KPI tiles.

### Proposed API and contract (NOT IMPLEMENTED)
- `GET /api/v1/wallet/statements?limit=12&cursor=SIGNED` (1..24); `GET /api/v1/wallet/statements/:month` where month is strict `YYYY-MM`; `GET /api/v1/wallet/statements/:month/entries?limit=50&cursor=SIGNED` (1..100); `GET /api/v1/wallet/statements/:month/export.csv` only if generation VERIFIED; `GET /api/v1/wallet/statements/:month/receipt` returns immutable generation metadata and proof status, not signing keys. Response `{month,timezone:'Asia/Seoul',status,generation,asOf,sourceCheckpoint,opening:{cash,bank,total}|null,closing:{cash,bank,total}|null,deltas:{cash,bank,total}|null,entryCount,revisionReason?}`; WLD values are canonical decimal integer **strings**, never JS floating point. Rows `{transactionId,accountType,occurredAt,type,debit,credit,delta}`; do not leak counterpart owner/account IDs or sensitive memo fields.
- Server derives owner exclusively from authenticated session; SessionGuard→AuthenticatedGuard→ConsentGuard, no public read, no bearerless export. Android `/app-api/v1/wallet/statements/*` must preserve JSON/auth and support CSV response or explicit safe download flow after gateway audit; do not infer parity merely from route prefix. Errors: 401 unauthenticated, 403 inactive/consent, 404 foreign or absent private resource (indistinguishable), 409 generation cursor mismatch, 422 malformed/future month, 429 bounded requests, 503 source/DB unavailable; never fabricate success or return empty array on failure. `Cache-Control: private, no-store`, `X-Robots-Tag: noindex,nofollow`, no public sitemap/SEO/canonical leak, Content-Disposition safe filename, `text/csv;charset=utf-8` with BOM and spreadsheet formula-injection neutralization for every untrusted string, rate limit 30 reads/min and 3 exports/min per owner. GETs must not create financial transactions.

### Proposed schema, source proof and atomicity
- After resolving migration266 checksum provenance and competing unmerged 280 numbers, propose additive `wallet_monthly_statement_generations(id UUID PK,owner_user_id UUID,month_start DATE,month_end_exclusive TIMESTAMPTZ,generation BIGINT,status TEXT CHECK(status IN ('VERIFIED','UNVERIFIABLE')),opening_cash BIGINT NULL,opening_bank BIGINT NULL,closing_cash BIGINT NULL,closing_bank BIGINT NULL,source_checkpoint JSONB NOT NULL,source_digest TEXT NOT NULL,created_at,UNIQUE(owner_user_id,month_start,generation))`; `wallet_monthly_statement_heads(owner_user_id,month_start,generation_id UUID,version BIGINT,PRIMARY KEY(owner_user_id,month_start))`; `wallet_monthly_statement_rows(generation_id,transaction_id,account_id,created_at,account_type,debit_amount BIGINT,credit_amount BIGINT,delta_amount BIGINT,PRIMARY KEY(generation_id,transaction_id,account_id))`; `wallet_monthly_statement_receipts(generation_id PK,proof_hmac,source_row_count,created_at)`. FKs and `CHECK(debit>=0 AND credit>=0)`, exact month boundaries, generation monotonicity; index (owner_user_id,month_start DESC), (generation_id,created_at DESC,transaction_id DESC,account_id DESC), and ledger source indexes (account_id,created_at,id) / (created_at,id) **only after EXPLAIN**. Avoid duplicate aggregation if one transaction has multiple postings to the same account: normalize grouped posting rows before unique storage.
- Close only a **completed** Seoul month using `[month_start, next_month_start)` converted to UTC timestamptz, never a rolling 30-day interval. Prove all source streams are contiguous through a recorded checkpoint, no pending/reversed/incomplete posting, transaction/posting counts match and every included account is historically owner-bound; check debit-credit parity for whole transactions separately from owner pocket net (cash↔bank moves net zero). Opening and closing must reconcile from an independently trusted ledger genesis or a prior VERIFIED generation plus all intervening postings; current `account_balances` alone cannot attest an arbitrary past month, and direct-balance-write legacy flows make unsupported historic reconstruction unsafe. If any anchor or source completeness fails, publish UNVERIFIABLE with reason **without fabricated amounts**; do not label a 24-hour grace period as proof. If no archived month exists, NOT_ARCHIVED is not equivalent to zero transactions.
- Publication uses a bounded background job on isolated/authorized runtime (not this planning run): acquire namespaced advisory lock (owner,month), recheck account status and source checkpoint under REPEATABLE READ or stronger with documented phantom policy, derive all per-pocket rows, verify balance/source proof, insert immutable generation/rows/HMAC receipt, atomically advance one head pointer; idempotent re-run of identical source digest returns original generation. Late correction appends a **complete replacement generation**, never mutates old rows or silently mixes generations; old downloads remain labeled superseded and new head is authoritative. Cursor binds owner/session/month/generation/last (created_at,transaction_id,account_id)/expiry 15m/signing-key-version with HMAC; changed generation => 409 restart. No direct app-role SELECT/INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE on private statement tables, no PUBLIC EXECUTE; SECURITY DEFINER fixed search_path, least-privilege owner verification and negative tests.
- Account merge (migration163) and soft-delete (migration010) require explicit privacy policy: preflight fail closed for accounts with published statements until authorized ownership-transfer rules exist; revoke reads immediately on deletion, redact account-linked identifiers, define retention/erasure for immutable rows, receipts, exports and backups without claiming soft-delete physically purges data. Operators see aggregate job health only (k>=20 suppression), no individual statement or raw CSV without separately approved elevated case access. HMAC keys remain in server secrets, never in DB rows or response.

### Backlog, metrics, regressions and release
- **P0** `ENV1272-01` root disk headroom, independent dev/Test/Production DB identity and encrypted restore; `SEC1272-02` app role least privilege (235/241 direct DML, 3 PUBLIC bank EXECUTE) and owner-only read negatives; `REL1272-03` migration266/280 remediation, full CI/dependency/security and exact-head Test attestation; `WAL1272-04` source checkpoint, ledger genesis/closing anchor, historical ownership and reconciliation proof.
- **P1** `WAL1272-05` additive immutable schema/indexes/HMAC receipt; `WAL1272-06` idempotent atomic close/revision generation; `WAL1272-07` NestJS owner listing/detail/cursor/export/receipt; `WAL1272-08` Next.js member UI/Android parity; `WAL1272-09` admin/privacy/SEO/WCAG/KPI; `WAL1272-10` isolated real-PG 100-way concurrent close/retry, 100 foreign probes, 10,001 synthetic postings, same-transaction multi-posting, missing/late postings, partial months, DST/Seoul boundaries, bigint precision, CSV injection, soft-delete/merge, backup restore, rollback and full wallet regression.
- KPI targets **only after implementation**: zero cross-owner data, zero fabricated VERIFIED, zero unbalanced verified statements, duplicate close creates zero extra generations, 99.9% available verified read, p95 list <300ms for 10,001 synthetic postings, no critical WCAG defects. No user identifiers or amounts in analytics; opt-in consent policy applies. QA v1270 FAILED/HOLD: frontend 6 failures, dependency audit Critical2/High10, app DML 235/241, PUBLIC bank EXECUTE 3, migration266 checksum and 280 collision, exact-SHA authenticated Test finance E2E and isolated restore unproven; root 93%/7.4G free at 16:05 KST. Feature OFF. Engineering must use independent branch, full isolated PG+CI/security, exact-SHA Test, QA PASS, authorized zero-downtime canary and documented rollback; rollback disables new reads/jobs but never deletes archived proof or touches WLD/ledger. **No Production promotion.**
- External independent references: target **10,000** distinct independently sourced, deduplicated, classified and approved. Newly approved **0**, cumulative approved **UNKNOWN**, gate **BLOCKED / IN PROGRESS**; PostgreSQL/OWASP/W3C design guidance is not counted as approved corpus entries.

## v2026.10.25.1270 — F675-124 Board Author Mute Identity, Merge and Cursor Integrity (FEATURE IMPROVEMENT / PLANNING DRAFT / RELEASE HOLD)
- Draft PR [#1289](https://github.com/wtrdd1-hash/Woldeok-Moneyverse-Migration/pull/1289) OPEN/HOLD; engineering issue [#1280](https://github.com/wtrdd1-hash/Woldeok-Moneyverse-Migration/issues/1280) updated; unmerged, no runtime/DB/Test/Production changes.

### Authority, cycle and verified source
- Previous **Feature Addition v1268** → Current **Feature Improvement v1270** → Next **Feature Addition v1272** (provisional). Authoritative remote main **1c5b84a1c45baafcca83f375a6f99698121e9bb5** verified at start and midpoint, with AGENTS.md, README.md and canonical English/Korean PROJECT_PLAN consulted. Predecessor Draft PR #1281 is OPEN/unmerged; this is a documentation-only successor. Latest completed QA v1269 FAILED/HOLD; do not equate successful local selected tests with release approval.
- Main evidence: migration 175 member_board_public_list selects the top 50 before any member mute logic and returns no author UUID; migration 259 exposes author_user_id on post detail. Migration 181 member_board_public_list_with_stock joins stock context to the already limited public list; backend stock-community.service applies the stock symbol filter **after** that top-50 cap. Migration 163 admin_account_merge preflight blocks authored posts/comments but knows nothing about proposed mute ownership, target links or request receipts. Migration 010 account_soft_delete retains user rows, changes status and revokes sessions; ON DELETE CASCADE alone cannot purge these new personal records. Existing public /board is cached 60s and indexable. These are source-level design gaps, **not demonstrated production incidents**.
- Trade-offs: do not client-filter the cached public top 50 (biased pages); do not reuse stock-tagged public read model (stock filter after LIMIT); do not reassign owner/target UUIDs on account merge (cross-account privacy and receipt collisions). Choose a new owner-only SECURITY DEFINER read model with author and optional stock predicates before keyset LIMIT, immutable author UUID, explicit merge fail-closed and durable replay receipts. Keep public /board, /board/[postId], existing stock filters, SEO, admin moderation, DM, notifications, other members and WLD/ledger untouched.

### Purpose, roles, journeys and states
- Target: active signed-in consent-current member. Guests may read public board but cannot see personal feed/mute state. On a currently visible non-self post detail, secondary **Hide this author in My Feed** CTA uses postId, never a client-supplied author UUID. Display explanatory copy: only private feed changes; public posts, replies, messages and notifications remain accessible. Never show CTA for deleted/unavailable author, unresolved identity, self post or auth error.
- Private /board/personal offers All/Stock-filtered view, cards with verified title, current public author name, timestamp, comment count and optional stock badge; CTA Open post, Manage hidden authors, Public board and Refresh. /account/board-muted-authors lists owner records, including redacted "Unavailable member" and Unmute even when the original post is deleted. A hidden author may still appear in the public board, public comments and direct messages; this is not a safety block or report. Existing public stock-filter behavior is not silently repaired by this planning change.
- States: SIGNED_OUT, LOADING, READY, EMPTY, MUTING, MUTED_CONFIRMED, UNMUTING, UNMUTED_CONFIRMED, AUTHOR_UNAVAILABLE, CONFLICT_REFRESH, CURSOR_EXPIRED, UNKNOWN_COMMIT, RATE_LIMITED, FEATURE_UNAVAILABLE, ERROR. Do not convert API/DB failure into EMPTY or toast success before an original committed receipt. On logout/account switch discard in-flight results, clear cursors and owner-specific caches; screen-reader live region announces result/error, focus returns to CTA; 320px/200% zoom, keyboard, WCAG 2.2 AA.

### Proposed API and privacy contract (not implemented)
- GET /api/v1/board/personal?limit=20&stock=SYMBOL&cursor=TOKEN (1..50; optional normalized uppercase stock symbol 1..16 ASCII allowed chars). Response {posts,nextCursor,muteGeneration,asOf,filter}; use no-store/private. Stock filter must join the authoritative post stock context **before** LIMIT, not filter member_board_public_list_with_stock or public top 50 in TypeScript.
- GET /api/v1/board/mutes?limit=20&cursor=TOKEN, GET /api/v1/board/mutes/state?postId=UUID, POST /api/v1/board/mutes {postId,requestId,expectedVersion}, DELETE /api/v1/board/mutes/:muteId {requestId,expectedVersion}, GET /api/v1/board/mutes/receipts/:requestId. POST target derived from currently visible source post in SQL; DELETE by opaque muteId with owner check works after post deletion. Return original 200 JSON receipt {requestId,muteId,version,muteGeneration,muted,replayed}; no false 204 success for unknown commit. Same requestId and different normalized payload returns 409. Foreign/nonexistent resource indistinguishable 404; 401/403/409/422/429/503 explicit, with Retry-After for 429.
- SessionGuard, AuthenticatedGuard, ConsentGuard and browser CsrfGuard/same-origin; Android app-api board group already exists and DELETE JSON bodies are proxied, but If-Match and Idempotency-Key headers are not forwarded. Use body expectedVersion/requestId and verify official Android session and server authorization, not User-Agent as authentication. All owner responses noindex/nofollow, Cache-Control private,no-store, Vary: Cookie where applicable; no newly exposed author identifiers or mute relations in public HTML, sitemap, metadata, structured data, CDN cache or telemetry; existing public post-detail authorUserId behavior is not changed.

### Proposed schema, transactions and identity lifecycle
- Additive, collision-free migration only after migration266 provenance and competing 280 migration numbers are resolved: member_board_author_mutes(mute_id UUID PK,owner_user_id UUID NOT NULL,author_user_id UUID NOT NULL,muted BOOLEAN NOT NULL,version BIGINT NOT NULL CHECK(version>=1),created_at,updated_at, UNIQUE(owner_user_id,author_user_id),CHECK(owner_user_id<>author_user_id)); member_board_mute_states(owner_user_id PK,generation BIGINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK(generation>=0)); member_board_mute_receipts(owner_user_id,request_id,operation,target_mute_id,request_hmac,original_response_json,created_at, UNIQUE(owner_user_id,request_id)). Keep tombstones/versions; receipts contain no raw post body, profile name, message, IP or token. Index active mutes (owner_user_id,author_user_id) WHERE muted; owner list (owner_user_id,updated_at DESC,mute_id DESC); post feed (created_at DESC,id DESC) WHERE deleted_at IS NULL; stock context index (stock_id,post_id) after EXPLAIN. No app direct table SELECT/INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE, no PUBLIC EXECUTE; SECURITY DEFINER with fixed search_path, actor ownership checks and bounded return fields.
- Mutation lock order: validate bounded input, acquire a namespaced transaction advisory lock on (owner,requestId) **before receipt lookup** (per AGENTS.md/migration045), reject inactive owner and compare stored payload HMAC for exact replay; only on a new request does POST first resolve the immutable author UUID from a currently visible post as an untrusted non-locking hint; then locks actor and target users in ascending UUID order FOR UPDATE, locks the post FOR SHARE and rechecks author UUID, visibility and both active statuses, then owner state FOR UPDATE and mute row. DELETE likewise acquires request-key advisory lock before receipt lookup, then locks only actor user, owner state and mute row. This matches migration163 sorted user locking and prevents actor/target inversion deadlocks; never trust author UUID from browser. Create missing owner state via INSERT ... ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING before locking; serialize the 200-active-mutes cap on owner state; enforce expectedVersion CAS including absent row version 0, update durable tombstone, increment generation only on actual transition, persist immutable HMAC payload-bound original receipt atomically. Repeated exact request returns original receipt without changing generation, mismatched replay 409, stale expectedVersion 409, timeout UNKNOWN_COMMIT triggers receipt GET then state GET. Never create an extra mute on duplicate request.
- Account merge: migration163 currently locks both users in ID order and blocks authored posts/comments but not future mute records. Before enabling, add reviewed preflight that **rejects merges when either source owner has mute state/tombstones/receipts or merged identity is referenced as mute target**; do not silently transfer, rewrite receipt ownership or delete rows. Maintain globally consistent user lock order with merge and deletion; lock analysis and 100-way merge-vs-mute races are release gates. Explicitly document and obtain privacy approval before any later transfer policy.
- Soft deletion: migration010 does not physically delete users; deny owner reads/writes immediately after status change, invalidate session-bound cursors, redact target names and suppress stale personal feed results. Approved retention/erasure procedure must cover mute rows, tombstones, HMAC receipts, backups and privacy requests; do not claim FK cascade performs erasure. Author UUID remains immutable on historic posts; a deleted target is redacted, and Unmute still works by muteId.
- Personal read: one transaction-consistent SECURITY DEFINER statement/transaction binds owner active status, muteGeneration, current visible post and optional stock filter, author NOT EXISTS active mute, keyset (created_at,id), and LIMIT+1. First page captures a high-water tuple; HMAC cursor binds owner, authenticated session, generation, normalized stock filter, limit, high-water tuple, last tuple, expiry (15 minutes) and key version. Reject changed generation/session/filter with 409 restart, malformed/forged token with 422, expired token with 409. New posts after high-water are not mixed into the same traversal; deleted/moderated posts may disappear and **historical snapshot retention is not promised**. Never use OFFSET or sort by editable updated_at.

### Admin, metrics, security and verification
- Admin may see only aggregated adoption, mutation failures, stale-cursor rate and p95 query latency; suppress cells with <20 members and never expose a person's mute graph. KPI: personal-feed success >=99.9%, foreign owner disclosure 0, duplicate replay extra writes 0, cursor inconsistency 0, p95 query <300ms at 10,001 synthetic posts, 200-mute cap strict, a11y critical 0. Measure only with opt-in/approved analytics and no raw author IDs.
- P0/P1 backlog (10 testable handoffs): **P0** ENV1270-01 root capacity/DB isolation/restore, SEC1270-02 app least privilege and PUBLIC bank function closure, REL1270-03 full CI/security/migration266/280/exact-SHA Test gates, BOARD1270-04 source identity/merge/deletion authorization; **P1** BOARD1270-05 additive schema/tombstones/HMAC receipts, BOARD1270-06 actor-first atomic mute/unmute/CAS/cap, BOARD1270-07 stock-aware pre-LIMIT keyset/HMAC cursor, BOARD1270-08 NestJS/Next.js/Android/session UI, BOARD1270-09 privacy/admin/SEO/a11y/KPI, BOARD1270-10 isolated real-PG 100-way concurrency, 100 foreign probes, 10,001 synthetic posts, deletion/merge races, regression and rollback.
- Test exact-SHA isolated PostgreSQL negative app-role grants, real session/consent/CSRF/Android, 100 identical and conflicting replay requests, 100 simultaneous cap attempts, 100 merge-vs-mute/deletion races, 100 foreign-owner probes, 10,001 synthetic posts with stock filtering before LIMIT, signed cursor tamper/expiry/generation change, public 60s cache and SEO unchanged, no WLD/ledger writes. New engineering branch, feature OFF by default, schema forward-only with old reader compatibility, approved isolated backup restore, full dependency audit, GitHub CI and independent QA PASS, official Test exact-SHA authenticated E2E, staged canary/observability, human-authorized GitOps Production promotion and feature OFF rollback. Do not merge/promote while P0 open.
- Latest QA v1269 FAILED/HOLD: 6 frontend failures (1397 pass), audit Critical 2/High 10, prod audit High 2, dev app UPDATE+DELETE on 235/241 public tables and 3 PUBLIC bank functions, migration266 checksum mismatch, migration280 collision, no official exact-SHA Test financial E2E or isolated restore. Root disk recovered from 100% but was ~92% at v1270 start; do not attribute recovery. External independent evidence gate: 10,000 target, **0 newly approved**, cumulative deduplicated approved **UNKNOWN**, **BLOCKED / IN PROGRESS**. Existing OWASP/PostgreSQL/W3C publisher families are design references only, not 10,000 accepted records. No runtime, DB, WLD, ledger, Test or Production changes.


## v2026.10.25.1268 — F675-124 Member-controlled Board Author Mutes and Private Feed (FEATURE ADDITION / PLANNING DRAFT / RELEASE HOLD)
- Draft PR [#1281](https://github.com/wtrdd1-hash/Woldeok-Moneyverse-Migration/pull/1281) OPEN/HOLD; engineering issue #1280 OPEN; main unmerged, runtime/Test/Production unchanged.
- **Engineering handoff:** [GitHub issue #1280](https://github.com/wtrdd1-hash/Woldeok-Moneyverse-Migration/issues/1280) OPEN/HOLD; predecessor planning Draft #1274 unmerged. This is not a runtime change.

### Cycle, intent, alternatives and source truth
- **Previous Feature Improvement v1266 → Current Feature Addition v1268 → Next Feature Improvement v1270 (provisional).** Authoritative remote main eb27f5590e80bb71462e3888d052106a068e661a checked at start and midpoint, including AGENTS.md, README.md, English PROJECT_PLAN.md and Korean counterpart. Prior planning Draft #1274 remains OPEN/unmerged; this successor is docs-only, not runtime authorization.
- **Purpose/target:** give signed-in, active, consent-current members control over which public-board authors appear in a private personalized feed, without changing public visibility, moderation, other members' experience, messages, notification delivery or money. Guests retain the ordinary public board. Alternative A, client-side filtering of 50 cached public posts, is rejected because it produces empty/biased pages and exposes personalized state in a public cache. Alternative B, globally hiding or blocking authors, is rejected because it changes public SEO, moderation and DM semantics. **Selected:** isolated owner-only feed with SQL filtering before LIMIT, explicit author mute/unmute and a private management screen.
- **Observed main:** frontend/src/app/board/page.tsx is canonical, indexable, publicApi-cached for 60 seconds; backend/src/board/public-board.service.ts and migration 175 public list return authorName but not authorUserId, capped at 50; frontend/src/app/board/[postId]/page.tsx and migration 259 detail expose authorUserId; migration 044/048 list visible posts with deleted_at IS NULL and stable created_at/id ordering. No owner mute read/write relation found. App API frontend/src/app/app-api/v1/[...path]/route.ts supports the board group and JSON DELETE bodies. Do not assume existing public feed can be safely personalized or that post IDs alone prove ownership.
- **Collision boundary:** F675-109 reading list, F675-110 private drafts, F675-116 post revision, F675-121 follow/reply alerts and developer C's board/notification work are separate. No new notification delivery, direct-message block, harassment enforcement, post takedown, crawler-specific content, reward, WLD, stock or ledger mutation. No separate planning spec file; this integrated master is the design authority.

### Member journeys, rights and state machine
1. **Entry:** public /board and public /board/[postId] remain unchanged for guests and search engines. On an authenticated post detail with a verified non-self authorUserId, show secondary CTA **Hide this author in My Feed**, explaining "Only your personal feed changes; public posts remain accessible." Add a signed-in link to /board/personal and owner-only /account/board-muted-authors. Never render mute controls for an unavailable, self-authored or unresolved author.
2. **Private feed:** /board/personal is authenticated, noindex/nofollow, private/no-store, server-fetched from an owner-authorized read model. Cards show verified post title, author display name, createdAt, reply count, and Open post. CTA Manage hidden authors, Show public board, Refresh. The feed excludes actively muted authors **before keyset LIMIT**; do not fetch public top 50 and then filter. Public canonical /board and public post detail always remain readable, subject to existing visibility/moderation rules.
3. **Mute management:** /account/board-muted-authors lists owner mute records only, with redacted "Unavailable member" where an author is deleted or no longer publicly nameable; Unmute remains possible. The member cannot mute themself, mute arbitrary UUIDs, see another member's mute list, or mute on behalf of others. Admin moderation, legal takedown and platform safety take precedence; mutes are a personal display preference, not a report/block mechanism.
4. **States:** SIGNED_OUT, LOADING, READY, EMPTY_FEED, EMPTY_MUTES, MUTING, MUTED_CONFIRMED, UNMUTING, UNMUTED_CONFIRMED, AUTHOR_UNAVAILABLE, CONFLICT_REFRESH, UNKNOWN_COMMIT, RATE_LIMITED, ERROR, FEATURE_UNAVAILABLE. Do not toast success until a persisted receipt is returned; unknown commit → receipt lookup then current-state refresh. Account switch/logout discards stale responses and resets client state. Error is never rendered as "no posts".

### Proposed NestJS / Next.js / Android contracts
5. **Owner endpoints (new, proposed):** GET /api/v1/board/personal?limit=20&cursor= (max 50; posts,nextCursor,muteGeneration,asOf); GET /api/v1/board/mutes?limit=20&cursor= (max 50); GET /api/v1/board/mutes/state?postId=UUID (owner-only current mute/version for CTA, 404 inaccessible post); POST /api/v1/board/mutes JSON {postId:UUID,requestId:UUID,expectedVersion:nonnegativeInteger}; DELETE /api/v1/board/mutes/:muteId JSON {requestId:UUID,expectedVersion:positiveInteger}; GET /api/v1/board/mutes/receipts/:requestId. POST resolves target author from an existing currently visible post **server-side**; never accept target user UUID from the client. DELETE resolves opaque muteId and owner in SQL, foreign and nonexistent both 404. Existing public endpoints and cached /board route stay byte-for-byte semantically public.
6. **Authorization/errors:** SessionGuard + AuthenticatedGuard + ConsentGuard for all owner reads/writes, CsrfGuard and same-origin checks on browser mutations, bounded JSON schema, rate limit 12 writes/min/member and 60 reads/min/member, request body <=4KiB, fixed 20 default/50 max. 401 unauthenticated, 403 suspended or consent-ineligible, 404 inaccessible post/mute (indistinguishable from missing), 409 stale version, generation or conflicting replay, 422 self-mute/invalid target, 429 quota/rate, 503 DB/feature source unavailable; no optimistic "saved" on transport timeout. Error envelopes follow existing contract and never include foreign author/mute metadata.
7. **Android parity:** JSON list/mute/unmute/receipt via /app-api/v1/board/... only after app-gateway route-map contract and minimum-version checks. Existing gateway forwards POST/DELETE bodies; use body requestId/expectedVersion rather than relying on unforwarded custom headers. Test auth cookie/token, CSRF policy appropriate to app transport, account switch and 401/403/409/503 behavior. No mobile-only shadow state.

### PostgreSQL schema, ownership, locks and consistency
8. **Proposed forward-only migration, number UNASSIGNED until 266/280 provenance gate clears:** member_board_author_mutes(id UUID PK, owner_user_id UUID FK users, muted_author_user_id UUID FK users, active BOOL, version BIGINT >=1, created_at, updated_at, UNIQUE(owner_user_id,muted_author_user_id), CHECK owner<>muted); member_board_mute_states(owner_user_id UUID PK FK users, generation BIGINT >=0); member_board_author_mute_receipts(owner_user_id,request_id UUID, operation enum, request_hmac BYTEA, mute_id, result_version, resulting_generation, committed_at, PRIMARY KEY(owner_user_id,request_id)). All raw tables REVOKE PUBLIC and moneyverse_app DML/SELECT; only narrow actor-validated SECURITY DEFINER functions receive EXECUTE. Do not expose unrestricted author-ID lookup.
9. **Indexes/quotas:** partial idx_mutes_owner_active_created on (owner_user_id,created_at DESC,id DESC) WHERE active; unique owner+author prevents duplicates across tombstones; receipt PK owner+request; existing visible posts index (created_at DESC,id DESC) and additional author/visibility index only if EXPLAIN on isolated fixture proves needed. Max **200 active mutes/member**, serialized under owner user/state row lock; never cap the number of synthetic feed posts used for tests. Durable tombstones retain monotonically increasing version; unmute and remute do not reset version.
10. **Atomic mutation:** begin transaction; lock active owner users row, then owner mute state, then target visible post FOR SHARE (for mute), then existing mute row in stable UUID order; validate target user active and not self, check quota, expectedVersion, unique replay key; write active/tombstone and increment mute version + owner generation; insert immutable receipt; commit once. Same owner+requestId+canonical operation/post-or-muteId/expectedVersion HMAC returns original receipt on replay; reused key with different canonical payload → 409. A concurrent post deletion, account deletion or mute/unmute must fail closed/recheck; no stale-author success. HMAC secret version rotation and retention documented; no author names/post text in receipt.
11. **Feed read:** DB-owned member_board_personal_list(actor,limit,cursor) joins visible posts to active owner mutes with NOT EXISTS **before LIMIT**, order (created_at DESC,post_id DESC), returns post summary plus authorUserId for authenticated use only. Cursor signed with HMAC binds owner, session identity digest, filters, owner mute generation, upper-watermark, last (created_at,postId), expiry <=15 minutes; tamper/other account/expired cursor → 409 or 400 without leakage. On generation change force Refresh rather than mix filtered pages. New posts after watermark appear on refresh; deleted posts may disappear and no full historical snapshot is promised. Avoid OFFSET and cache mixing. Public sitemap/structured data continue from existing public read model, not this feed.
12. **Lifecycle:** user soft deletion immediately denies reads and writes; privacy policy must approve retention/deletion of owner mutes, state and receipts, including backups. Because users are soft-deleted, ON DELETE CASCADE alone is insufficient. Account merge must fail closed when mute state exists until an explicit reviewed migration policy is implemented; do not silently merge owner preferences or expose muted author identities. Redact removed author names in management. Admin has no per-owner mute browser/edit path; aggregate only with cohort k>=20 and differencing suppression.

### Security, quality, measurement and release gates
13. **Security/abuse:** prevent BOLA/BFLA, CSRF, cross-account receipt/cursor replay, UUID enumeration, IDOR on DELETE, self-mute, XSS from display names, request floods, unbounded pagination and cache leaks. Validate auth/consent inside DB function as well as Nest guard; no broad table grants. No punitive moderation action, invisible shadowban, direct-message block, notifications or social graph publication is implied. Audit actor/action/outcome without names, post body, token, cookie or HMAC key.
14. **Accessibility/SEO/mobile:** WCAG 2.2 AA keyboard, focus return after mute, aria-live for pending/confirmed/error, accessible confirm/unmute, 320px and 200% zoom, reduced motion and 44px primary touch targets (product target, not WCAG AA normative minimum). Personal feed/settings noindex/nofollow, no sitemap, private no-store and Vary session/cookie as appropriate. Public /board stays indexable, revalidate=60, canonical unchanged. Never serve personalized HTML under public ISR cache or use bot-detection as authorization.
15. **Analytics/KPI:** privacy-safe counts only: mute_intent→persisted_mute_receipt conversion, unmute rate, personal-feed repeat D7, post open-through, feed empty/error/409 rate, source unavailable rate, p95 feed latency. **Zero foreign disclosure, zero public listing suppression, zero ledger/WLD mutation, zero false success, zero unverified owner state** are release invariants, not measured results. Cohort k>=20; no author-target identity analytics. Targets and business uplift remain HYPOTHESIS until observed.
16. **Acceptance / regression:** disposable real PostgreSQL with 100 simultaneous same-request replays, 100 conflicting-key payloads, 100 foreign-owner reads/deletes, 100 quota races around 200, 10,001 synthetic visible posts for keyset paging, author post delete vs mute, self-mute, soft-delete/merge, account switch, public SEO/cache unchanged, empty feed and partial outage, signed cursor generation invalidation, XSS names, Android POST/DELETE parity, full keyboard/320px/screen-reader. Check immutable receipts, no duplicate active mute, exact order/no duplicates, no grants, no WLD/ledger mutation. Fail any unauthorized or fabricated success.
17. **Rollout/rollback:** feature switch OFF by default; first resolve P0 ENV/SEC/REL and migration 266/280; separate developer branch and human review of written spec/implementation plan; isolated PG RED/GREEN, lint/typecheck/unit/integration/app-api/Android/security/SEO/a11y; **exact final SHA** official authenticated Test (API, DB, session, user flow, regression), independent QA PASS, backup restore, human GitOps approval, 1%→10%→100% canary with rollback. Rollback turns flag OFF and restores prior routes; keep inert tables/tombstones/receipts, no destructive migration or retroactive public content filtering. No Production promotion from this planning PR.
18. **External evidence gate:** reviewed official OWASP API Top 10 (https://api-security.owasp.org/editions/2023/en/0x11-t10/), W3C WCAG 2.2 (https://www.w3.org/TR/wcag/) and PostgreSQL SELECT locking docs (https://www.postgresql.org/docs/18/sql-select.html) as contextual design references, three publisher families. Newly **approved** independent, deduplicated, source-classified records **0**; cumulative verified count **UNKNOWN / 10,000**. Gate **BLOCKED / IN PROGRESS**, not achieved.

### P0/P1 engineering handoff — 10 independently testable items
- **P0 ENV1268-01** — Live miniPC root 89%/11G free at 14:06 KST (improved since QA v1268's historical 100%/177MiB); owner-safe headroom monitoring, independent Production/dev/Test DB and encrypted isolated restore proof; preserve worker worktrees.
- **P0 SEC1268-02** — Dev moneyverse_app direct UPDATE/DELETE on 235/241 public tables and three PUBLIC bank EXECUTE functions; least-privilege forward-only SQL with owner/consent negative tests, no Production ad-hoc privilege changes.
- **P0 REL1268-03** — QA v1268 FAILED/HOLD, migration266 checksum mismatch, four migration280 candidates, six frontend CI failures, root audit Critical2/High10, SWF/auction ledger risk, exact-SHA Test finance E2E, GitOps and restore blocked.
- **P0 BOARD1268-04** — Verify public/member post author identity, deleted_at and moderation semantics; private-only filter boundary and no public ISR/SEO behavior change, including post deletion race.
- **P1 BOARD1268-05** — Schema with owner/target unique tombstone, 200-cap, monotonic generation, receipt HMAC, index/ACL and account lifecycle policy on isolated PG.
- **P1 BOARD1268-06** — Atomic post-derived mute/unmute with actor-first locks, version CAS, same-key replay and different-payload 409, 100-way races and receipt readback.
- **P1 BOARD1268-07** — SQL-side filtered personal keyset feed, owner/session/generation-bound signed cursor, 10,001-post synthetic pagination, no post-limit filtering.
- **P1 BOARD1268-08** — NestJS guarded API, Next.js /board/personal + /account/board-muted-authors, authenticated post CTA and Android app-api contract parity.
- **P1 BOARD1268-09** — Account deletion/merge, redaction, admin k>=20 aggregation, noindex/no-store, WCAG 2.2 AA, safe telemetry and abuse throttling.
- **P1 BOARD1268-10** — Exact-SHA isolated DB/security/CI/Android/SEO/a11y/QA, zero-downtime feature-flag rollout and reversible flag-off rollback; no runtime promotion before all gates PASS.
