## v2026.10.25.1266 — F675-123 Calendar Source Revision, Expiry and Private ICS Truth (FEATURE IMPROVEMENT / PLANNING DRAFT / RELEASE HOLD)
- Draft PR [#1274](https://github.com/wtrdd1-hash/Woldeok-Moneyverse-Migration/pull/1274) OPEN/HOLD; development issue [#1267](https://github.com/wtrdd1-hash/Woldeok-Moneyverse-Migration/issues/1267) updated; no main merge or runtime promotion.

### Cycle and verified source boundary
- **Previous Feature Addition v1264 → Current Feature Improvement v1266 → Next Feature Addition v1268 (provisional).** Remote main c9a868b1e622341e2daa8b936ddf0988eda9988e verified at start and midpoint; main AGENTS.md, README.md, canonical English PROJECT_PLAN.md and Korean counterpart inspected. Unmerged planning Draft #1268 remains the parent; development issue #1267 remains the engineering handoff. This is a documentation-only correction, not a live implementation.
- **Concrete main findings:** Migration 037 season_active_events() returns **season.ends_at**, not an event-specific deadline; migration 038 season_event_admin_update() can rename or deactivate an event **without updated_at or revision**. Migration 165 shop_catalog_list_v2() exposes **catalogue.sale_ends_at** while separately filtering **inventory.starts_at/ends_at** and catalogue.active. frontend/src/app/calendar/shop-deadlines.ts accepts any parseable non-null sale_ends_at, **including already elapsed timestamps**. The member calendar is a live listing, not a durable change/cancellation log. Do not treat an old list row as an authoritative future appointment.
- **Product scope:** member-initiated Watch/Unwatch from /calendar; owner-only /account/calendar-watchlist with **Open source**, **Refresh verification**, **Remove**, **Download verified .ics**. No push/email/subscription feed, no inferred weekly/daily event, no WLD/ledger/reward changes. Guest → login; suspended/consent-expired → no owner data. Source status, not watch existence, decides whether an event may be exported.

### Source identity, revision and time integrity (P0 contract)
1. **Authoritative resolver:** a new narrowly granted SECURITY DEFINER function returns for (actor,kind,sourceId) a stable typed source key, source revision, UTC deadline, current source availability and reason. SEASON_END requires event.active AND season.active AND season.starts_at<=verifiedNow<season.ends_at; its deadline is explicitly the **season end** and must not be presented as an independently scheduled event. SHOP_SALE_END requires catalogue.active, inventory availability, non-null catalogue.sale_ends_at > verifiedNow and valid inventory window; if inventory ends earlier, mark **UNAVAILABLE** instead of inventing an earlier sale end. Expired, disabled, removed, sold-out or unverifiable source cannot be exported as a live deadline. Distinguish SOLD_OUT from CANCELLED when inventory.quantity=0; it may recover.
2. **Monotonic revision required:** current main does not provide trustworthy source revisions. Proposed forward-only migration (number TBD; never reuse conflicting 280) adds revision/changed_at or an append-only source-change log for season event + parent season, shop catalogue + inventory. Every admin/stock/time-window mutation increments the relevant revision in the same transaction; an opaque composite sourceRevision binds all participating rows. Hash of current values alone cannot detect change→revert (ABA), so **do not declare VERIFIED until this contract and isolated regression tests exist**. No app-role direct table grants; revoke PUBLIC on resolver.
3. **Temporal snapshot and locks:** watch mutation serializes actor row → watch row → source event/season or catalogue/inventory rows in deterministic UUID order; source reads use a single statement/transaction snapshot and DB-side verifiedNow, not browser time. Source mutation must respect compatible row locks; if it races, return CONFLICT/RECHECK_REQUIRED or retry a bounded serialization failure, never confirm a stale deadline. A saved source revision differs from current revision → CHANGED_RECONFIRM; source missing → UNAVAILABLE_SAVED (redacted, owner may remove). Account switch/logout fences pending results.
4. **Durable watch and replay:** proposed member_calendar_watches unique (user_id,source_kind,source_id), monotonic watch.version and watch.generation, active tombstone; member_calendar_watch_receipts unique (user_id,request_id) with HMAC-bound actor+operation+kind+sourceId+expectedVersion+sourceRevision. Same payload replay returns the original committed receipt; different payload with same key → 409. Lock actor before counting active watches (max 100) so 100-way quota races cannot overbook. Deletion increments version without deleting receipt; rewatch cannot reset version or opaque calendar UID. Soft deletion and account merge require explicit purge/hold review; ON DELETE CASCADE alone does not clean soft-deleted users.

### UX, API, ICS and cross-platform acceptance
5. **Member states:** LOADING, AVAILABLE, WATCHING, SAVED_CONFIRMED, CHANGED_RECONFIRM, EXPIRED, SOLD_OUT, UNAVAILABLE_SAVED, UNVERIFIABLE, CONFLICT, UNKNOWN_COMMIT, ERROR, SIGNED_OUT. Display verifiedAt/sourceRevision, source-kind label and actual status. A changed deadline is **not silently accepted**: CTA **Review changed deadline** refreshes source and records acknowledgement, while **Remove** remains available when the source is gone. Never show a stale timestamp as current; errors are not empty success. 320px/200% zoom, keyboard and screen-reader announcements, no color-only meaning, WCAG 2.2 AA.
6. **Nest owner API (proposed):** GET /api/v1/calendar/watchlist?limit=20&cursor= (HMAC actor+session+filter+snapshot, keyset created_at,id; limit max 50); PUT/DELETE /api/v1/calendar/watchlist/:kind/:sourceId JSON {requestId,expectedVersion,expectedSourceRevision}; GET /api/v1/calendar/watchlist/receipts/:requestId; GET /api/v1/calendar/watchlist/export-preview returns verifiedCount, omittedCount, verifiedAt, sourceRevisions (never raw other-member data). 401 guest, 403 inactive/consent, 404 foreign/unknown indistinguishable, 409 stale revision/version/replay conflict, 429 rate, 503 source unavailable. CSRF/session/consent and no-store on all owner routes. Android app-api only after route allowlist and JSON parity, using body requestId/expectedVersion because gateway does not forward arbitrary idempotency headers; binary ICS stays web-only.
7. **Single-source export:** authenticated same-origin GET /account/calendar-watchlist/export.ics is an on-demand **snapshot**, never a permanent shareable feed. Resolve all active watched sources **in one consistent DB read transaction** with a captured verifiedAt and composite revisions, then render only confirmed future entries from that materialized result. Do not interleave individual HTTP catalogue calls, cache a prior preview, or generate on partial DB failure. Explicit preview and post-download omission count; preview may differ if sources change between requests, so the download response is authoritative. Exports with zero eligible rows are clearly explained, not a misleading valid schedule.
8. **RFC 5545 and injection gate:** each VEVENT uses stable opaque UID per durable watch (no user UUID/email), DTSTAMP UTC, DTSTART UTC at the real deadline, SUMMARY escaped as TEXT; no fabricated duration, organizer, attendee, alarm, RRULE or cancellation update. Avoid SEQUENCE/LAST-MODIFIED claims unless actual monotonic source revision/timestamp is available; a new downloaded snapshot does **not** retroactively update prior imports. CRLF endings, TEXT escaping (backslash, comma, semicolon, newline), 75-**octet** UTF-8 folding without splitting multibyte sequences, strict control-character rejection, 100 events and 256KiB bound, parser round-trip. Response Content-Type text/calendar; charset=utf-8, Content-Disposition attachment with fixed safe filename, Cache-Control private,no-store, X-Content-Type-Options nosniff; never expose owner IDs in URL, filenames, UID, logs or analytics.
9. **Admin/privacy/metrics:** admin aggregate-only status and export failures with cohort k>=20 and differencing suppression; no member watchlist browser or edits. Owner deletion/merge policy and retention windows require privacy signoff; logs exclude raw ICS and titles. Noindex/nofollow/no-store private pages, no sitemap/structured member events. Rate limit 12 writes/min, 6 exports/hour, bounded cursor and strict UUID/enum/date parsing. KPI: **zero stale or unverified exported deadlines**, zero foreign watch/receipt disclosures, zero WLD/ledger mutations; source revision lag p95, CHANGED_RECONFIRM rate, omission reason counts, ICS parser pass rate, 429 and 503 rate.
10. **Isolated QA and rollout:** tests on disposable PG only: 100 identical/different-key retries, 100 foreign actor probes, 100 quota races, 10,001 synthetic cursor rows (production watch cap 100), season disable/rename/season-end edit, shop sale date shift, inventory early end/sold-out/replenish, source change→revert ABA, export concurrent with admin cancellation, DST-independent Seoul rendering, Unicode 75-octet fold and CRLF/injection/parser round-trip, logout/merge/soft-delete and partial source outage. Separate engineering branch, feature flag OFF, forward-only migration after 266 checksum and 280 collision remediation, exact-final-SHA official Test finance E2E + isolated encrypted restore + security/ACL + rollback/canary evidence before any approved GitOps promotion. Flag rollback hides entry points and stops export, preserves watch/receipt history under retention policy; no runtime deployment by planning.

### P0/P1 handoff — ten independently testable items
- **P0 ENV1266-01** — Owner-safe root disk headroom (13:02 KST root 100%, 181MiB free; data volume 91GiB), independent Production/dev/Test DB and encrypted restore proof; do not delete other workers.
- **P0 SEC1266-02** — Revoke excessive dev app-role direct UPDATE/DELETE (235/241 public tables) and three PUBLIC bank EXECUTE grants, enforce SECURITY DEFINER owner/consent and BOLA/BFLA negatives.
- **P0 REL1266-03** — QA v1267 FAILED/HOLD: migration266 checksum drift, four migration280 candidates, six frontend failures, prior audit Critical2/High10, exact-SHA Test/restore/CI security and GitOps release gates.
- **P0 CAL1266-04** — Authoritative event-kind resolver with season-parent/shop-inventory effective eligibility, monotonic composite revision and no fabricated source deadlines.
- **P1 CAL1266-05** — Forward-only revision/change-log and watch tombstone/UID/receipt schema, UNIQUE/CHECK/indexes, retention/merge policy; migration number only after conflicts resolved.
- **P1 CAL1266-06** — Actor-first transaction locks, revision CAS, 100-watch quota, HMAC replay and 409, admin-source change/revert concurrency safety.
- **P1 CAL1266-07** — Owner-only Nest list/preview/receipt and single-snapshot BFF ICS with 503 fail-closed, safe headers, verified omission accounting.
- **P1 CAL1266-08** — Next /calendar and /account/calendar-watchlist changed-source acknowledgement, accessibility, Android JSON gateway parity and session fencing.
- **P1 CAL1266-09** — Privacy soft-delete/merge/retention, k-anonymous admin aggregates, noindex/no-store, abuse throttles and provenance/KPI.
- **P1 CAL1266-10** — Disposable PG 100-way/10,001-synthetic/ABA/source-cancel/ICS parser regressions, exact-final-SHA Test and canary/rollback acceptance.

**Evidence gate and safety:** RFC Editor RFC5545, OWASP API1:2023, PostgreSQL locking and W3C WCAG 2.2 used as standards families, **new independently deduplicated and approved evidence records 0; cumulative approved UNKNOWN / 10,000 → BLOCKED / IN PROGRESS**. QA v1267 FAIL/HOLD; no Production release. Planning changes only: no runtime, DB, WLD, ledger, feature flag, Test or Production mutation.

## v2026.10.25.1264 — F675-123 Verified Member Calendar Watchlist and Private ICS Export (FEATURE ADDITION / PLANNING DRAFT / RELEASE HOLD)
- Draft PR [#1268](https://github.com/wtrdd1-hash/Woldeok-Moneyverse-Migration/pull/1268) and engineering issue [#1267](https://github.com/wtrdd1-hash/Woldeok-Moneyverse-Migration/issues/1267) OPEN/HOLD; stacked on unmerged documentation Draft #1261.

### Cycle, actual source and boundaries
- **Previous Feature Improvement v1262 → Current Feature Addition v1264 → Next Feature Improvement v1266 (provisional).** Remote main `c9a868b1e622341e2daa8b936ddf0988eda9988e` checked at start/mid; authoritative main `AGENTS.md`, `README.md`, English `PROJECT_PLAN.md` and Korean counterpart reviewed. Documentation-only successor to unmerged Draft #1261, no feature deployed. Development issue [#1267](https://github.com/wtrdd1-hash/Woldeok-Moneyverse-Migration/issues/1267). Latest completed QA **v1265 FAILED/HOLD**; v1266 audit artifacts exist but no complete release PASS was established.
- **Code-backed discovery:** main `frontend/src/app/calendar/page.tsx` displays season events from `GET /api/v1/seasons/events` (stable `event_id`, `ends_at`), shop catalogue `GET /api/v1/shop/catalog` (`catalog_id`, nullable `sale_ends_at`) and derived weekly reset from `/api/v1/engagement/early-game`. `calendar/shop-deadlines.ts` only sorts non-null parseable timestamps; `/api/v1/early-game/today` is today's personalized event, not a future scheduled event. No owner watchlist or authenticated ICS export was identified in inspected main. This is a **proposed** capability, not an existing runtime feature. Do not infer that an advertised sale deadline guarantees a purchase or a reward.
- **Target and value:** logged-in members who want to revisit real, currently announced seasonal/shop deadlines without copying dates manually. Deliberately exclude push/email reminders, auto-subscribe URLs, calendar scraping, fabricated recurring events and WLD/ledger writes; avoid C's notification inbox and A/B treasury/finance scopes. Existing useful calendar and owner-only patterns are incorporated here; no new scattered planning spec.

### Member experience, authorization and exact states
1. Entry `/calendar` offers **Watch deadline** for only source-confirmed future season-event end or shop sale-end; `/account/calendar-watchlist` lists saved items and **Unwatch**, **Open source**, **Refresh status**, **Export verified .ics**. Guest → login gate; owner-only private list. Per member maximum **100 active** watched sources; one source has one saved row, including tombstone. Save after deadline or canceled/unpublished source refuses 409 `SOURCE_NOT_WATCHABLE`.
2. States: `LOADING`, `AVAILABLE`, `WATCHING`, `SAVED_CONFIRMED`, `CHANGED`, `EXPIRED`, `UNAVAILABLE_SAVED`, `CONFLICT`, `UNKNOWN_COMMIT`, `ERROR`, `SIGNED_OUT`. A removed/hidden source becomes a **redacted, removable** saved entry; never show a stale timestamp as current. A changed timestamp requires explicit **Review changed date** and displays old-vs-current only to its owner; an export always uses **fresh authoritative source time**, not saved copies. Source lookup failure is `UNVERIFIABLE`, not zero or an invented schedule.
3. Daily personalized event has no future occurrence contract, and weekly reset is calculated rather than a durable event row; both are **informational only in v1** and not watchable/exportable. Locale/timezone: store UTC instants, display Asia/Seoul with explicit timezone; if no parseable source time, show `DATE_UNAVAILABLE`. Never use an arbitrary redirect or external URL; source deep links use an allowlisted internal path.

### API, DB, transactions and abuse controls (proposed, not implemented)
4. Nest owner-only endpoints `GET /api/v1/calendar/watchlist?cursor=`, `PUT /api/v1/calendar/watchlist/:kind/:sourceId`, `DELETE /api/v1/calendar/watchlist/:kind/:sourceId`, `GET /api/v1/calendar/watchlist/receipts/:requestId`; writes carry JSON `{requestId:UUID,expectedVersion:integer}`, authenticated CSRF/session/consent guards and per-owner rate limit **12 writes/min**. List returns `items[],nextCursor,sourceCheckedAt` with signed actor/session-bound `(created_at,source_kind,source_id)` keyset cursor and 409 restart on snapshot/version change; avoid OFFSET. `PUT`/DELETE return immutable `{receiptId,requestId,committedVersion,active,sourceStatus}`; same key+payload replays first receipt; same key with different payload returns 409 `IDEMPOTENCY_CONFLICT`; version mismatch returns 409 `VERSION_CONFLICT`. 404 must not reveal foreign receipt/source identity, 503 for unverified authoritative source, 429 for abusive writes. All read/write functions enforce actor ownership inside SECURITY DEFINER; no direct app-role table grants.
5. New tables (migration **number TBD** until applied 266 checksum and four-way 280 collision resolved): `member_calendar_watches(user_id UUID,source_kind enum/check IN('SEASON_END','SHOP_SALE_END'),source_id UUID,active boolean,version bigint CHECK(version>0),created_at timestamptz,updated_at timestamptz,PRIMARY KEY(user_id,source_kind,source_id))`; `member_calendar_watch_receipts(user_id,request_id UUID,source_kind,source_id,operation,request_hmac,key_version,committed_version,result_active,created_at,PRIMARY KEY(user_id,request_id))`. Index watches `(user_id,active,created_at DESC,source_kind,source_id)` and receipts `(user_id,created_at DESC)`; CHECK known operation, key_version>0 and positive versions; immutable receipts, no member name, raw event description or original text in receipt. No FK that prevents legitimate source purge; on missing source keep only opaque tombstone metadata until privacy retention expiry.
6. **Atomic operation:** DB function resolves actor active/consent, locks actor row first to serialize 100-item quota, then existing watch row, validates source from authoritative season/shop source functions under a consistent transaction snapshot, checks version and HMAC-bound idempotency key, writes watch + immutable receipt atomically; repeated concurrent requests cannot exceed cap or resurrect a newer unwatch. Tombstone retains monotonically increasing version (never reset to zero); unknown commit reconciles receipt then current GET. Source cancellation between check and commit is revalidated under documented source lock/snapshot or fails closed. Account soft-delete blocks reads/writes immediately; explicit purge/merge preflight must include watches/receipts (soft-delete is not FK CASCADE), no automatic cross-account transfer; retention and backup erasure need privacy approval.

### On-demand RFC 5545 export and cross-platform boundaries
7. Authenticated same-origin Next BFF `GET /account/calendar-watchlist/export.ics` re-resolves every active saved source immediately against authoritative data, omits expired/canceled/unverifiable events with **separate omission count** (UI preflight status, not invented ICS entries). Maximum **100 VEVENT**; no public permanent feed, signed URL, unauthenticated subscription endpoint, user ID, session token, balances, raw notes or external links. For each verified deadline use stable non-personal `UID=SHA256(kind|sourceId)@easy-scraping.com`, `DTSTAMP` UTC, `DTSTART` UTC at deadline with `DURATION:PT1M` to mark the deadline instant (not a claimed event start); `SUMMARY` clearly says 'deadline'. RFC 5545 escaping of TEXT backslash/semicolon/comma/newline, CRLF and 75-octet UTF-8-safe line folding; bounded UTF-8 title. Headers `Content-Type:text/calendar; charset=utf-8`, `Content-Disposition:attachment`, `Cache-Control:private,no-store`, `X-Content-Type-Options:nosniff`. Return 409 JSON if no verified events, 503 on full source outage; never silently export cached or stale dates. Calendar import is a **snapshot** and will not auto-update when the platform changes an event.
8. Android app-api: JSON watch/list/remove/receipt parity only after allowlist/contract validation; current `frontend/src/app/app-api/v1/[...path]/route.ts` forwards JSON body and selected headers, so version/idempotency fields are in JSON. Binary ICS is **web-only in v1** pending explicit gateway content-type and attachment review. Session/logout/account switch discards in-flight owner data. Private routes `noindex,nofollow`, excluded from sitemap, `Cache-Control:private,no-store`; WCAG 2.2 AA keyboard operation, status live region, focus management, 320px viewport and 200% zoom.
9. Admin only k≥20 privacy-preserving aggregates with differencing suppression: active watches by source kind, source-changed/unavailable share, export error rate and latency; no raw owner lists, filenames, watch identifiers or manual editing. KPI targets **0 unverified exported timestamps, 0 foreign reads, 0 WLD/ledger mutations**, plus 7-day watch-to-return, stale-source rate, successful export rate and p95 export latency. Admin metrics never drive a member's source truth.

### P0/P1 handoff — ten independently testable items
- **P0 ENV1264-01** — Root storage recovery without touching other workers (observed 100%, ~349MiB free), independent Production/dev/Test DB, encrypted backup restore.
- **P0 SEC1264-02** — Remove excessive dev app-role DML (235/241) and three PUBLIC bank EXECUTE grants; owner-only SECURITY DEFINER functions, BOLA/BFLA negative tests.
- **P0 REL1264-03** — Resolve migration266 checksum/280 collision, frontend six failures, audits (v1266 full Critical2/High10), exact-final-SHA Test financial E2E and signed promotion HOLD.
- **P0 CAL1264-04** — Define stable source identity, authoritative season/shop deadline status, cancellation/revision and snapshot/lock semantics; reject inferred weekly/daily events.
- **P1 CAL1264-05** — Watch/tombstone/receipt schema, constraints/indexes, quota and retention/merge contract; forward-only migration only after number conflict resolution.
- **P1 CAL1264-06** — Actor-first locking, source validation, version CAS, 100-cap and HMAC-bound replay/409 with crash-safe receipts.
- **P1 CAL1264-07** — Nest owner list/write/receipt, same-origin BFF RFC5545 export, fresh source checks, safe headers and typed errors.
- **P1 CAL1264-08** — Next calendar CTA/watchlist/status and Android JSON parity, session fencing and WCAG 2.2 AA.
- **P1 CAL1264-09** — Admin aggregates, soft-delete/merge/privacy retention, SEO/no-store, abuse limits and KPIs.
- **P1 CAL1264-10** — Isolated real-PG tests: 100 concurrent same-key requests, 100 foreign probes, 10,001 synthetic keyset rows (production cap stays 100), 100-cap races, source cancel/date change during export, 75-octet UTF-8 folding and injection/parser round-trip, stale versions, logout/soft-delete/merge, backup restore, Android gateway. Feature flag OFF, Test exact-SHA and authorized canary/GitOps only; rollback disables UI/write/export while preserving audit receipts, never mutates WLD or source records.

### Release and evidence ledger
- QA v1265 FAILED/HOLD; v1266 audit artifacts observed at 11:21–11:23 KST (full **Critical2/High10**, production dependencies **High2**, bot test **8/8**) but no overall v1266 release PASS. Root 100% (~349MiB); DB grants/migration/CI/Test/restore blockers persist. This is a draft **not a runtime fix**.
- External source families reviewed: RFC Editor [RFC 5545](https://www.rfc-editor.org/rfc/rfc5545.html), [OWASP API1:2023](https://api-security.owasp.org/editions/2023/en/0xa1-broken-object-level-authorization/), [W3C WCAG 2.2](https://www.w3.org/TR/WCAG22/). **New independently deduplicated/approved evidence 0; cumulative approved UNKNOWN / 10,000 → BLOCKED / IN PROGRESS.** These three standards are design references, not counted toward the 10,000 gate without approved registry evidence.
- Planning automation does not change runtime code, DB, feature flags, WLD, ledger, Test or Production. Separate developer branch, independent QA exact-SHA Test and approved production promotion are required.

## v2026.10.25.1262 — F675-122 Coherent Weekly Archive Publication and Source Completeness (FEATURE IMPROVEMENT / PLANNING DRAFT / RELEASE HOLD)
- Draft PR #1261 (https://github.com/wtrdd1-hash/Woldeok-Moneyverse-Migration/pull/1261), development issue #1256 (https://github.com/wtrdd1-hash/Woldeok-Moneyverse-Migration/issues/1256); both remain OPEN/HOLD.

### Cycle and verified boundaries
- Previous **Feature Addition v1260** → Current **Feature Improvement v1262** → Next **Feature Addition v1264** (provisional). Remote main `c9a868b1e622341e2daa8b936ddf0988eda9988e` rechecked at start/mid; main `AGENTS.md`, `README.md`, canonical English `PROJECT_PLAN.md` and Korean counterpart reviewed. This is a documentation-only successor to unmerged Draft #1257, not an implemented feature. Latest completed QA **v1265 FAILED/HOLD** (2026-10-25 10:20 KST).
- Exact-main migration `101-early-game-progress.sql` computes current-week goals with `created_at >= v_from` but **no exclusive upper bound**, chooses the **current mutable** highest-level job, and includes a separate lifetime/self-reported NPC metric. Do not reuse this live function as a historical archive finalizer. Its receipt and ledger counts must be independently reproduced for a bounded historical week with frozen catalog/job evidence.
- **New design risks in the unimplemented v1260 proposal:** a per-goal `archive_heads` pointer can expose a mixed week (one corrected goal, two older goals) and falsely aggregate VERIFIED; an elapsed 24-hour grace is not a proof that receipt and ledger source ingestion is complete. No runtime incident or historic loss is claimed.

### Coherent publication and source-completeness contract (proposed)
1. **One authoritative week generation.** Introduce append-only `member_weekly_goal_archive_generations(user_id,week_start,generation,published_at,source_cutoff,policy_version,completeness_status,prior_generation,reason_code,provenance_hmac,key_version)`, append-only `member_weekly_goal_archive_goals(user_id,week_start,generation,goal_code,target,progress,source_family,evidence_status,source_count,...)`, and one mutable `member_weekly_goal_archive_week_heads(user_id,week_start,generation)`. PKs `(user_id,week_start,generation)` and `(user_id,week_start,generation,goal_code)`; FK goal→generation and head→generation, plus unique `(user_id,week_start)` head. CHECK positive generation/target, nonnegative progress, exact Seoul Monday and seven-day interval, known goal codes/statuses, and eligible `window_kind='week' AND self_reported=false`. Index `(user_id,week_start DESC)` for history and `(user_id,week_start,generation,goal_code)` for detail. Do not allocate migration number until 266/280 resolved.
2. **Atomic full-week publish.** Finalizer takes a deterministic per-(member,week) advisory lock then week-head row lock, reads a stable source snapshot, inserts **all three** eligible goal rows and a generation manifest, verifies count/code-set and completeness, then atomically switches the single week head and writes an audit receipt in the same transaction. Never publish a partially populated week. On correction, publish an entire **new full-week generation** even when only one goal changed; old generation and its evidence remain immutable. Exact duplicate replay returns the original generation; different evidence requires an audited correction, not a silent rewrite. Crash before commit exposes no new head.
3. **Completeness before VERIFIED.** For each required source family (work reward receipts, relevant ledger transactions/postings, frozen job/catalog attribution), persist a trustworthy durable source checkpoint/sequence and its captured upper bound. A wall-clock `now()-24h` or maximum `created_at` alone is **not** a completeness watermark. Publish VERIFIED only when every required source family has a documented contiguous checkpoint through the Seoul week-end, an ingestion/late-arrival policy with reconciliation, and ≥24h grace; if the existing pipeline cannot prove this, publish `PENDING_FINALIZATION`/`UNVERIFIABLE` with no verified numerator. Backdated, late or reversed postings trigger review and a new full-week correction generation, never mutation of old evidence. Bank savings retain the actual net deposit-minus-withdrawal rule; sums use exact bigint/decimal strings and an exclusive upper week boundary.
4. **Reader snapshot and privacy.** Every list/detail/receipt read resolves a **single published week generation** once per SQL statement or stable read transaction; all goals and totals join on that generation. Extend the owner-only API responses with `generation`, `completenessStatus`, `sourceFamilyStatus`, `publishedAt`, `correctedFromGeneration`. The signed, actor/session-bound cursor pins a publication-as-of cutoff or returns 409 `ARCHIVE_CHANGED` with a restart token rather than mixing generations across pages. 404 hides foreign receipts; 503 never produces fabricated VERIFIED/zero; private no-store/noindex and session-switch fencing remain mandatory. Source fingerprints use keyed HMAC with key version and retention/rotation policy; no raw member IDs, ledger rows or reversible/guessable source identifiers in receipts or logs.
5. **User/admin states and cross-platform parity.** On `/quests/history` and detail, show `PENDING_SOURCE`, `PENDING_FINALIZATION`, `VERIFIED`, `CORRECTED`, `UNVERIFIABLE`, `NOT_ARCHIVED` and `ERROR` with accessible text and an explanation CTA. A correction banner links to non-sensitive revision history; do not offer Claim/Complete or change WLD. Android app-api returns the same owner-scoped generation/status contract, never cached across account switches. Admin receives only k≥20 aggregated missing-checkpoint, mixed-generation-prevention, correction and lag metrics (suppress differencing), no raw member data or manual goal mutation. SEO: authenticated routes excluded from sitemap, `noindex,nofollow`, `Cache-Control: private,no-store`; WCAG 2.2 AA, keyboard and 320px/200% zoom.
6. **KPI, isolated tests and release gate.** Targets: **zero mixed-generation reads**, **zero false VERIFIED**, **zero foreign disclosures**, **zero ledger/WLD changes**; report checkpoint lag p95, pending share, correction latency, archive coverage and source-family failure rates. Future isolated real-PG tests: 100 concurrent finalizer retries and correction races; 100 foreign probes; 10,001 synthetic cursor rows while a correction publishes; crash between goal rows/head switch; missing/reordered/backdated receipt; ledger reversal; week upper boundary and Seoul Monday; mutable job ranking and target; bigint >2^53; HMAC key rotation; soft-delete/merge; Android/logout; full security/backup restore. Feature OFF until disk, DB ACL, migration integrity, CI/security, exact-final-SHA authenticated Test finance E2E, approved canary/GitOps and rollback gates pass. Rollback disables finalizer/read UI without deleting immutable evidence.

### P0/P1 engineering handoff — 10 distinct items
- **P0 ENV1262-01** — Owner-safe root storage recovery (QA v1265: 100%, ~368MiB), Production/dev/Test DB isolation, encrypted backup restore; preserve all other workers.
- **P0 SEC1262-02** — Least-privilege DB grants (development app UPDATE/DELETE 235/241; 3 PUBLIC bank functions), owner-only SECURITY DEFINER, negative BOLA/BFLA and HMAC privacy.
- **P0 REL1262-03** — Resolve applied migration 266 checksum drift, four proposed migration 280 collisions, six frontend CI failures, security audit, exact-SHA Test and signed promotion HOLD.
- **P0 QUEST1262-04** — Establish independently auditable per-source contiguous completeness checkpoints, bounded-week semantics and historical catalog/job evidence; refuse VERIFIED without proof.
- **P1 QUEST1262-05** — Forward-only immutable week generation/goals, single head FK/unique/indexes and privacy-safe source attestations.
- **P1 QUEST1262-06** — Atomic full-week finalizer, source checkpoint gate, deterministic lock ordering, replay receipts, full-generation correction and crash rollback.
- **P1 QUEST1262-07** — NestJS owner-only generation-consistent list/detail/provenance APIs, 409 cursor restart, 404/503, decimal-string bigint.
- **P1 QUEST1262-08** — Next.js explanation/correction UI, Android app-api parity, session fencing and WCAG 2.2 AA.
- **P1 QUEST1262-09** — Soft-delete/merge retention, aggregate-only admin, SEO/no-store, HMAC rotation and source-lag KPI.
- **P1 QUEST1262-10** — Isolated real-PG concurrency/foreign/cursor/late-source/fault-injection regression, exact-final-SHA Test/restore/canary/rollback.

**QA and evidence truth:** v1265 FAILED/HOLD; main CI success had runtime-check SKIPPED, PR #1255 runtime-check FAILED (6 frontend tests); root 100%, dev role grants excessive, migration266 drift and 280 collision; Production and Test runtime SHAs differ from main. External independent 10,000-source deduplicated, evidence-classified gate: newly accepted **0**, cumulative accepted **UNKNOWN**, **BLOCKED / IN PROGRESS**. This planning run changes no runtime code, DB, WLD, ledger, Test or Production. No other worker's changes or reservations touched.


## v2026.10.25.1260 — F675-122 Verified Weekly Quest Goal Archive and Provenance (FEATURE ADDITION / PLANNING DRAFT / RELEASE HOLD)

### Cycle, source of truth, product boundary
- Previous **Feature Improvement v1258** → Current **Feature Addition v1260** → Next **Feature Improvement v1262** (provisional). Latest remote **main** `c9a868b1e622341e2daa8b936ddf0988eda9988e` checked at start; recheck at midpoint and finish. Canonical main `AGENTS.md`, `README.md`, `docs/planning/PROJECT_PLAN.md` (English) and `PROJECT_PLAN.ko.md` consulted; this is stacked on **unmerged** planning Draft #1248, not an implemented feature. Latest completed QA **v1264 FAILED/HOLD** (2026-10-25 09:56 KST).
- Code evidence: `frontend/src/app/quests/page.tsx` shows the current week's goals from `GET /api/v1/engagement/early-game`; `backend/src/engagement/engagement.controller.ts` and migration `101-early-game-progress.sql` call `early_game_weekly_goals(p_actor)` with **current Seoul Monday** `v_week`, **only a lower timestamp bound**, and no historical archive API or persisted close-of-week snapshot. Three `window_kind='week'` metrics are computed from work reward receipts and ledger postings; the fourth NPC goal is **lifetime and self-reported**. Current `user_job_progress` job ranking is mutable, so naive historical recomputation would rewrite old job-task counts. This is a design gap, **not evidence of past data loss**.
- Need: members want to review **previous completed weeks**, see which recorded activities counted and distinguish verified completion from missing/uncertain evidence. Audience: authenticated, active, consent-current members; no leaderboard, no public member history, no new WLD reward or ledger mutation. Current `/quests` stays the source of current-week progress.

### Member journey, states, authority
- Entry: `/quests` under the existing weekly-goals cards → **View previous weeks**. Destination: private `/quests/history` with a list of archived Seoul Monday–Sunday weeks; `/quests/history/[weekStart]` details, **Back to current week**, **View evidence explanation**, and **Retry** for transient failures. Guest → existing login gate with validated same-origin return; do not leak history in pre-auth HTML.
- Archive cards show immutable `weekStart`, `weekEndExclusive`, frozen goal label/target/unit, `progress` as decimal string, `sourceCutoff`, `revision`, `capturedAt`, `evidenceStatus` and `selfReported`. Explicit states: `NOT_ARCHIVED` (historical data not captured, **never zero**), `PENDING_FINALIZATION`, `VERIFIED`, `CORRECTED`, `UNVERIFIABLE`, `UNAVAILABLE`, `ERROR`. `VERIFIED` only after closed-week, trusted-source finalization; `CORRECTED` displays revision history and reason without changing old receipts. Current week stays `IN_PROGRESS` on `/quests`, not a final archive.
- **Only** the three catalog `window_kind='week'`, `self_reported=false` goals qualify for verified weekly completion. `weekly_npc_regular` is lifetime/self-reported and must not appear in the weekly completion numerator or be presented as weekly historical fact. Preserve the label `self-reported` wherever that metric is separately referenced. Freeze the applicable catalog/policy version; if an old target/job assignment cannot be proven, mark `UNVERIFIABLE`, never backfill invented counts. No user-facing `Complete`, `Claim`, `Adjust` or manual archive-write CTA.
- Accessibility: keyboard and screen-reader ordered headings, table alternatives, visible state text independent of color, accessible correction timeline, 44px touch targets, 320px/200% zoom, TalkBack/VoiceOver parity, WCAG 2.2 AA; avoid animation-driven success signals.

### Proposed API and Android contract (NOT implemented)
- `GET /api/v1/engagement/weekly-archive?limit=8&cursor=...` → `{items:[{weekStart,weekEndExclusive,status,verifiedGoals,eligibleGoals,revision,sourceCutoff}],nextCursor}`; max 12 weeks/page, signed HMAC cursor binds actor/session, immutable week boundary, last week and filter; owner-only, `Cache-Control: private, no-store`.
- `GET /api/v1/engagement/weekly-archive/:weekStart` → `{weekStart,weekEndExclusive,status,goals:[{code,label,target,progress,unit,completed,sourceKind,evidenceStatus,selfReported,revision,receiptId}],capturedAt,sourceCutoff,correctionAvailable}`. Only a valid `YYYY-MM-DD` Monday in Asia/Seoul, prior to current week; invalid 400, missing 404, transient authoritative failure 503. All WLD/bigint counts remain **decimal strings**, not JavaScript Number.
- `GET /api/v1/engagement/weekly-archive/receipts/:receiptId` → owner-only provenance metadata `{weekStart,goalCode,revision,sourceCutoff,policyVersion,sourceFamily,finalizedAt,status}`, never raw postings, other-member details, personal identifiers or confidential request logs. No member mutation endpoints. Next.js server-only API boundary and session/consent checks; Android `/app-api/v1/engagement/weekly-archive/... ` must proxy the same read contract, no forged offline completion and no cross-account cached responses.
- HTTP: 401 guest, 403 suspended/consent, 404 foreign or nonexistent receipt indistinguishable, 400 malformed week/cursor, 429 abuse, 503 DB/provenance unavailable. Logout/account switch invalidates in-flight results and cursor; no 200 with fabricated empty `VERIFIED` rows on failure.

### Proposed PostgreSQL snapshot, concurrency and source integrity
- Forward-only migration **number not allocated** until 266 checksum and 280 collisions resolved. Add `member_weekly_goal_archive` immutable revision rows keyed `(user_id uuid,week_start date,goal_code text,revision integer)` with `week_end_exclusive date`, `catalog_version`, `source_cutoff timestamptz`, `captured_at timestamptz`, `source_family`, `source_digest`, `source_count bigint`, `target bigint`, `progress bigint`, `unit text`, `completed boolean`, `evidence_status`, `correction_reason_code`, `job_context_version`; CHECK Monday/7-day interval, positive target, nonnegative progress/revision, known source family/status, `self_reported=false`. Composite index `(user_id,week_start DESC,goal_code,revision DESC)` plus unique `(user_id,week_start,goal_code,revision)`. A separate `member_weekly_goal_archive_heads` atomically points to the latest approved revision; do not UPDATE historical rows.
- A privileged, narrowly scoped finalizer (not a member/API write) closes a Seoul week only after **source ingestion watermark and explicit 24-hour minimum grace**; runs under job advisory lock + row-level `FOR UPDATE` on archive head, snapshot transaction with stable cutoff, and deterministic idempotent `(weekStart,goalCode,revision,sourceDigest)`. First commit inserts rows + head + audit/outbox together. Repeated same digest returns the original receipt; changed digest needs **new audited correction revision**, never overwrite or silently change completion. Job crash/rollback creates no partial week marked VERIFIED.
- Reuse 101's actual transaction-type allowlist and net bank deposit-minus-withdrawal semantics; use **both lower and exclusive upper bounds** for a past week, trusted receipt timestamps, exact bigint arithmetic. Current-job `user_job_progress` is not a historical job identity: require a versioned at-week job attribution or provenance snapshot before marking `weekly_job_tasks` VERIFIED. A missing historical catalog/attribution, late backdated posting, source correction or ambiguous bank posting is `UNVERIFIABLE` pending reviewed correction, **not zero**. Do not retrospectively fabricate weeks before feature activation.
- `moneyverse_app` and PUBLIC have **no direct table DML/SELECT**; grant narrowly scoped EXECUTE on `SECURITY DEFINER` owner-checked read functions; finalizer uses a dedicated least-privilege principal and explicit function grants. Validate user active status inside DB function, not only controller. Do not grant member write functions. Account soft-delete/merge requires explicit archive access fencing, retention/erasure and no implicit reassignment; historical immutable receipt retention subject to approved privacy policy. No WLD, postings, bank balance, rewards or NPC affinity changed.

### Admin, abuse, SEO, metrics and acceptance gates
- Admin: read-only operational `/admin/engagement/weekly-archive` showing last successful week, finalizer lag, source-watermark gap, missing snapshots, correction count, dead-letter and anonymized cohort aggregates (k≥20; suppress differencing). No member names, raw ledger, ability to change counts, or direct manual reward grant. Correction is separately reviewed/audited privileged job only.

SEO: private pages `noindex,nofollow`, `no-store`, no public sitemap/JSON-LD of member data; public `/quests` marketing metadata must not expose authenticated state.
- KPI: archive eligible-week coverage, `VERIFIED` share, `UNVERIFIABLE` share, finalization p95 latency, correction count and time-to-correction, 0 false VERIFIED rows, 0 foreign receipt exposures, 0 ledger mutations. Count a verified week only when all eligible goals have approved evidence; display incomplete weeks separately.
- Future **isolated real-PG** tests: 100 concurrent finalizer replays yield one head/revision; 100 foreign member/receipt probes reveal nothing; 10,001 synthetic archive rows cursor without duplicate/missing week; Seoul Monday boundary, DST-independent KST, policy/target changes, job ranking drift, late backdated ledger correction, failure halfway through week, missing source, account merge/soft delete, bigint >2^53, Android/session switch and 320px accessibility. Assert ledger/balances/rewards bit-for-bit unchanged. These are **planned**, not run.
- Rollout: OFF by default; separately owned engineering branch; reconcile migration numbering and DB privilege defects first; backfill disabled until provenance audit; staged shadow computation on isolated Test, compare against 101 current-week totals where comparable, authenticated exact-final-SHA Test E2E, security, restore, canary, GitOps approval. Rollback disables read UI/finalizer and preserves immutable archive/audit, never deletes history or touches finance. QA v1264 **FAILED/HOLD**: root 100% (~375MiB), app UPDATE/DELETE 235/241 public tables, 3 PUBLIC bank functions, migration 266 checksum drift, migration 280 collisions, prior frontend 6 failures and audit Critical2/High10, bot Critical1/High5; exact-SHA Test and isolated restore not passed.

Do not count synthetic fixtures, duplicate links or publisher families as accepted references.
