## v2026.10.25.1258 — F675-121 Board Reply Follow Replay, Deletion and Delivery Integrity (FEATURE IMPROVEMENT / DRAFT / RELEASE HOLD)

### Cycle and authoritative evidence
- Previous **Feature Addition v1256** → Current **Feature Improvement v1258** → Next **Feature Addition v1260** (provisional). Remote main `c9a868b1e622341e2daa8b936ddf0988eda9988e` rechecked at start and mid; main AGENTS.md, README.md, PROJECT_PLAN.md (English canonical) and PROJECT_PLAN.ko.md reviewed. This is a **documentation-only** successor to unmerged Draft #1241 and issue #1240; no code, migrations, Test or Production changes.
- Exact-main source inspected: `packages/database/migrations/048-member-board-threads.sql` creates `member_board_comments` with globally unique `idempotency_key`. Its `member_board_comment_create(p_actor,p_post,p_body,p_key)` replays a prior key after checking **only author**, not original post/body; same-author key reuse with different content/post can return the original comment as apparent success. Concurrent first requests can race at the unique key. Its post existence check is not locked against deletion. `member_board_comment_delete(p_actor,p_comment,p_key)` **does not use p_key** and returns a boolean; `backend/src/board/board.controller.ts` DELETE `:id/comments/:commentId` ignores the route post ID. These are **code-backed design risks**, not proven exploitation or observed member loss.
- Migration 259 public/member comment reads exclude soft-deleted comments and posts. Existing comment create does not produce a committed reply outbox event. Development C's real inbox Draft #1206 remains unmerged; do not build a competing notification store or assert that inbox exists in main.
- QA v1261 evidence from isolated exact-main audit: frontend Vitest **6 failed / 1,397 passed**, pnpm audit **2 critical / 10 high**, bot audit **1 critical / 5 high**, root filesystem **100% (~444 MiB free at start)**. Existing live/dev database boundary, excessive app DML/PUBLIC bank EXECUTE, migration 266 checksum and 280 collisions, official exact-SHA authenticated Test finance E2E and isolated restore remain release gates; QA is **FAILED/HOLD**.

### Corrected functional and data contracts (proposal, NOT implemented)
1. **Comment-create replay truth:** A forward-only reviewed replacement must lock the post row, validate actor and visibility, and enforce an immutable per-actor request receipt bound to `(actor, postId, canonicalBody, requestId, operation)`. Identical retry returns the **original committed comment/receipt**, with `newlyInserted=false`; reuse of the same key with different post/body/operation returns deterministic 409 without any new comment or event. Never treat an existing row as a new comment. Race on first insert must resolve through unique-key conflict lookup and payload comparison, not an unhandled 500 or duplicate alert. Do not rewrite applied migration 048.
2. **Post/delete ordering:** For new writes use deterministic `post FOR UPDATE → comment row → follow/receipt/outbox` lock ordering; post delete obtains the same post lock. A comment committed before post deletion may create an event, but the worker must suppress it after deletion. A comment attempted after deletion fails without event. Historical already-visible notifications cannot be retroactively un-seen; if deleted later, **retract/redact the inbox item or gate source detail at read time**, and never claim guaranteed non-exposure after earlier delivery.
3. **Delete route integrity:** Validate `:postId` against the comment's actual post under lock; reject cross-post URL mismatch uniformly without deleting another thread's comment. Preserve owner authorization. Bind delete `requestId` to `(actor, postId, commentId, DELETE)`; repeated identical delete returns original receipt, conflicting reuse 409, not silent boolean ambiguity. Keep legacy HTTP 204 compatibility until web/Android clients migrate; new versioned receipt endpoint returns 200 JSON, not a body on 204.
4. **Follow generation and delivery:** `(user_id,post_id)` durable tombstone with monotonically increasing `version` and `generation`. Subscribe/mute/unfollow and reply creation share post-first serialization. Only **newly inserted** comments emit one transactional outbox event with unique `comment_id`, stable `(post_id,event_seq)`; retry never emits again. Worker delivery key `(recipient,commentId,generation)` is unique, and delivery rechecks member active/consent, follower generation, mute, self-author, comment/post visibility and feature switch before insert into the **existing verified inbox contract after #1206 is accepted**. If state changes after delivery, source-detail read and retraction policy must handle stale notices.
5. **Privacy and UX:** `/board/[postId]` Follow/Mute/Unfollow and `/account/board-follows` remain owner-only; show PENDING/UNKNOWN_COMMIT/CONFLICT/UNAVAILABLE/OFFLINE explicitly and confirm only from committed receipts. Owner/session/filter-bound signed cursor, 20/page, 200 active follows, 12 mutations/min, safe noindex/no-store, same-origin links, Android app-api JSON `requestId/expectedVersion`, keyboard/TalkBack/VoiceOver WCAG 2.2 AA. Admin sees only k-anonymized queue/retraction metrics (k≥20; suppress differencing), not identities or comment text. Account soft delete/merge requires forward-only preflight and privacy-approved retention/erasure; FK cascade alone is insufficient.

### Implementation handoff and acceptance (separate engineering branch)
- **P0 ENV1258-01** — Owner-approved disk recovery without deleting workers; Production/dev/Test DB isolation and encrypted restore evidence.
- **P0 SEC1258-02** — Forward-only app least-privilege/PUBLIC EXECUTE hardening and cross-user/post authorization negative tests.
- **P0 REL1258-03** — Resolve QA v1261 failures/audits, migration 266/280, exact-final-SHA official Test, security and GitOps gates.
- **P0 BOARD1258-04** — Specify and verify migration-048 create replay payload binding, unique-key races, post lock ordering, delete URL post binding, C #1206 inbox ownership before implementation.
- **P1 BOARD1258-05** — Versioned comment-create/delete receipts and conflict mappings with immutable request identity; forward-only schema/function, no app DML grants.
- **P1 BOARD1258-06** — Post-locked new-comment event/transactional outbox, unique sequence, generation-fenced worker, stale-notice retraction/read gating.
- **P1 BOARD1258-07** — NestJS/Next.js versioned receipt and 409/412/404/503 semantics, legacy 204 compatibility, Android JSON parity.
- **P1 BOARD1258-08** — Follow list and thread CTA state/unknown-commit reconciliation, 20-row signed cursor, accessibility and account switch fencing.
- **P1 BOARD1258-09** — Admin aggregate/retraction, privacy deletion/merge, rate/queue controls, noindex/no-store and privacy-safe KPI.
- **P1 BOARD1258-10** — Isolated real-PG tests: 100 same-key same/different-body races; cross-post delete 100; comment-create vs post-delete and unfollow/refollow; 10,001 synthetic cursor; event rollback/worker replay; exact-SHA authenticated Test E2E, canary and rollback.
- Release: flag **OFF**, no WLD/ledger mutation, no migrations allocated before 266/280 reconciliation, no production promotion without all P0 and QA/security/Test/restore gates. External independent reference target **10,000 deduplicated, source-classified accepted records**: publisher families reviewed PostgreSQL/OWASP/W3C (3), newly accepted **0**, cumulative accepted **UNKNOWN**, gate **BLOCKED / IN PROGRESS**.


## v2026.10.25.1256 — F675-121 Verified Board Thread Follows and Reply Alerts (FEATURE ADDITION / DRAFT / RELEASE HOLD)

### Cycle, evidence, boundary
- Previous **Feature Improvement v1254**; Current **Feature Addition v1256**; Next **Feature Improvement v1258** (provisional). Source of truth: remote main `c9a868b1e622341e2daa8b936ddf0988eda9988e` at start and mid; `AGENTS.md`, `README.md`, English `PROJECT_PLAN.md` canonical and Korean parity; latest QA **v1259 FAILED/HOLD**. This branch stacks on unmerged documentation PR #1235; **no feature is implemented or released**.
- Observed on main: `frontend/src/app/board/page.tsx` exposes public posts; `board/[postId]` displays comments; `backend/src/board/board.controller.ts` and migration 048 provide guarded post/comment writes; migration 259 provides public comment reads and author IDs. `member_board_comment_create` persists new comments with an idempotency key, but no verified per-thread follow/alert flow was found. `backend/src/notification/notification.controller.ts` already exposes authenticated in-app list/read routes. Development C's unmerged #1206 proposes a real notification inbox: **integrate, do not duplicate or assume merged**. Prior F675-109 reading list, F675-110 board drafts and F675-116 revision history are different scopes.
- User need: readers want to follow a specific discussion and learn about **new, committed, still-visible replies** without polling. This is an explicit opt-in, **in-app only** feature. It is not a financial notification, reply guarantee, push/email subscription, public follower count, or WLD reward.

### Member experience and permissions
- Entry: public `/board/[postId]` offers **Follow replies**; logged-in active, consent-current member may follow. Guests see **Sign in to follow** with safe return path, never an implied subscription. Owner may follow own post but receives **no alert for their own comment**. Only public, not-deleted/not-moderated posts qualify.
- Destination: private `/account/board-follows` with `Following`, `Muted`, `Unavailable` views, 20-row signed keyset pagination, per-row **Open discussion**, **Mute/Unmute**, **Unfollow**, and unavailable-post **Remove** without exposing hidden title/body. Existing `/account/notifications` displays server-verified board reply entries **only after** the C inbox contract is merged and exact-SHA tested. No new independent inbox or claim-all endpoint.
- CTA states: `NOT_FOLLOWING`, `FOLLOWING`, `MUTED`, `PENDING`, `CONFLICT`, `OFFLINE`, `UNAVAILABLE`, `ERROR`; server receipt alone confirms success. Offline never shows a confirmed follow or reply. Show retry using the **same requestId** and current-version reload on 412. Screen reader live status, keyboard focus return, 44px targets, 320px layout and 200% zoom per WCAG 2.2 AA.
- Authorization: owner-only follow/list/receipt; admins see aggregate queue health only, not follower identities or content. Session+Authenticated+Consent on reads; CSRF on mutations. Actor always derived from verified session; reject supplied userId. Deleted/suspended members, hidden posts and removed comments are ineligible. Feature disabled: read-only existing state allowed for cleanup/unfollow; **no new follows or notifications**.

### Proposed API (not implemented)
- `GET /api/v1/board/follows?status=following|muted|all&limit=20&cursor=...` → `{items:[{postId,status,version,updatedAt,available,href?}],nextCursor}`; title/href only after fresh public visibility check. Cursor HMAC binds actor, session, filter, snapshot bound, last `(created_at,post_id)`; no raw owner IDs in URL; immutable created_at order is not an exact snapshot if follow status changes during pagination; `Cache-Control: private, no-store`.
- `PUT /api/v1/board/posts/:postId/follow` body `{requestId:UUID,expectedVersion:nonnegative integer,muted:boolean}`; initial create expects version 0, returns `{status,version,receiptId,committedAt}`. `DELETE /api/v1/board/posts/:postId/follow` same requestId/version body, tombstones row. `GET /api/v1/board/follows/receipts/:requestId` owner-only immutable original result metadata; no hidden post title/comment body. A muted follow stays active but generates no new alerts.
- Errors: 400 invalid fields, 401 login, 403 consent/CSRF, 404 nonexistent/foreign/unavailable post without existence oracle, 409 same key with different canonical request HMAC, 412 stale version, 429 throttled, 503 dependency failure; never translate 503 into success. For Android use existing `/app-api/v1/board/...` proxy with JSON `requestId/expectedVersion` because `frontend/src/lib/app-gateway.ts` does not forward `If-Match`/`Idempotency-Key` headers. Enforce cookie/CSRF/version contract and session-switch response fencing.

### PostgreSQL, ordering and delivery invariants
- Forward-only migration **number to be allocated after migration 280 conflict reconciliation**, not reserved here. Proposed `member_board_thread_follows`: `(user_id uuid,post_id uuid)` PK, `version bigint NOT NULL CHECK(version>=1)`, `generation bigint NOT NULL`, `active boolean`, `muted boolean`, `start_event_seq bigint`, `created_at timestamptz`, `updated_at timestamptz`; unique owner/post, active cap **200 per member**, index `(user_id,active,created_at DESC,post_id DESC)`. Follow FK to posts must not block authorized hard purge; tombstone and receipts must not resurrect after deletion.
- Proposed `member_board_reply_sequences(post_id PK,last_seq bigint)`, `member_board_reply_events(comment_id uuid UNIQUE,post_id,event_seq bigint,committed_at)` with unique `(post_id,event_seq)`, and `member_board_reply_deliveries(user_id,comment_id,generation,notification_id,delivered_at)` with unique `(user_id,comment_id)`. `member_board_follow_receipts(user_id,request_id PK per owner,request_hmac,key_version,result_json,created_at)` retains **metadata only**, no title/body, no user-supplied URL; HMAC key rotation and retention require privacy sign-off. Worker outbox has retry/backoff/dead-letter and metrics; no browser-originated reply event is authoritative.
- Atomicity: both follow mutations and **new** comment commits acquire the same post-row lock in deterministic order, allocate a monotonically increasing `event_seq` only for a genuinely inserted comment (not idempotent replay), and commit event/outbox in the comment transaction. Follow captures `start_event_seq` under that lock; only `event_seq > start_event_seq` and the **same active generation** qualify. Subscriber row `FOR UPDATE` + version CAS prevents follow/unfollow/mute ABA; a replay returns its first immutable receipt, a changed payload with same requestId is 409. If transaction rolls back, **no event, notification or receipt**.
- Delivery worker rechecks member active/consent, subscription generation+mute, post public visibility, comment existence/deleted status and commenter != recipient immediately before inserting into existing verified notification store. Unique delivery key prevents duplicates across retries; on deletion/moderation or unfollow, suppress queued and pending alerts. Never copy comment body, financial amount or hidden title into durable notification payload; render safe title only after visibility recheck. If C #1206 contract is absent, **keep alerts disabled** rather than implement a parallel inbox.
- Account soft-delete and account merge (migration 010/163) do not guarantee FK cascade: add forward-only owner data cleanup/merge preflight; do not silently move follows or grant old identity access. Separate approved retention/erasure and backup purge policy. Revoke all new table DML from PUBLIC and `moneyverse_app`; owner-checked `SECURITY DEFINER` functions only; exact grants/ACL negative tests.

### Operations, analytics, QA and release
- Rate limit **12 follow writes/min/member**, 200 active follows; bounded fanout, per-post hot-thread queue throttling, no external push/email, abuse/report controls. Admin can see delayed/failed queue counts, retry age, disabled switch and anonymized aggregates only. Metrics: follow opt-in rate, first verified reply alert latency p95, duplicate delivery rate target 0, post-delete leakage target 0, notification open rate (no personal tracking), queue age and 429 counts; no sensitive log payloads.
- Public post canonical SEO unchanged; private follow and receipt endpoints `noindex`/`no-store`; safe same-origin links; Android/TalkBack, VoiceOver, keyboard and screen reader status parity.
- Future isolated tests: 100 same-key concurrent follows → one receipt; 100 different payload same-key → 409; 100 foreign-member reads → no leak; follow-vs-comment insert ordering; comment replay, delete/moderation, unfollow/refollow generation, mute, account merge/deletion, worker crash/retry, queue outage, rollback, and 10,001 **synthetic** delivery rows for cursor tests (200 active-follows cap remains in real service). Check DB negative grants, real-PG migration/rollback, Nest/Next/App API and full finance regression; **not executed in this planning run**.
- Release gate: default OFF; isolated development branch and code owner separate from Gemini/A/B/C/QA; P0 disk headroom, live/dev DB separation, least privilege, migration 266/280 reconciliation, full security/CI, isolated encrypted backup restore and authenticated **exact-head-SHA official Test** frontend/backend/API/DB/mobile/a11y/finance E2E; approved GitOps canary then gradual enable with observable queue and instant flag-off. Rollback stops new follows/alerts, drains/discards unsafe queued events, preserves monotonic tombstones/receipts and never rewrites WLD or ledger. **No planning-time runtime, DB, Test or Production mutation.**

### P0/P1 development backlog (10 items)
- **P0 ENV1256-01** — Owner-verified root disk recovery, Production/dev/Test DB isolation, backup restore readiness.
- **P0 SEC1256-02** — Revoke unsafe app direct DML and PUBLIC bank EXECUTE via reviewed forward-only isolated-PG migration; owner ACL/BOLA.
- **P0 REL1256-03** — Resolve QA v1259 audits, six frontend CI failures, migration 266/280 and exact-SHA Test/GitOps gate.
- **P0 BOARD1256-04** — Audit real comment insert/replay, post visibility/moderation, C notification inbox #1206 integration and lock ordering before implementation.
- **P1 BOARD1256-05** — Owner-only follow/tombstone/version/receipt schema, grants, indexes, 200 cap.
- **P1 BOARD1256-06** — Atomic per-post reply event sequence, transactional outbox, worker dedup and generation fencing.
- **P1 BOARD1256-07** — NestJS routes, owner validation, 404/409/412/429/503 and app-api JSON contract.
- **P1 BOARD1256-08** — Next.js post CTA, account list, Android parity, keyboard/TalkBack and offline states.
- **P1 BOARD1256-09** — Admin anonymous metrics, privacy erasure/merge, abuse limits, SEO/KPI.
- **P1 BOARD1256-10** — Isolated 100-way concurrency, 10,001 synthetic cursor, exact-SHA full regression, canary and rollback drills.
- External independent-reference gate: reviewed **PostgreSQL, OWASP, W3C** official publisher families (3); **new independently approved deduplicated records 0**, cumulative approved **UNKNOWN / 10,000**, **BLOCKED / IN PROGRESS**. Source guidance: https://www.postgresql.org/docs/17/explicit-locking.html ; https://api-security.owasp.org/editions/2023/en/0x11-t10/ ; https://www.w3.org/TR/WCAG22/. Publisher families are **not** 10,000 individual evidence items.


## v2026.10.25.1254 — F675-120 Wallet Annotation Merge, Erasure and Cursor Integrity (FEATURE IMPROVEMENT / DRAFT / RELEASE HOLD)

### Cycle, canonical authority and source-verified defect class
- **Previous:** Feature Addition v1252; **Current:** Feature Improvement v1254; **Next:** Feature Addition v1256 (provisional). Remote main c9a868b1e622341e2daa8b936ddf0988eda9988e verified at start and mid; main AGENTS.md, README.md, PROJECT_PLAN.md (English canonical), PROJECT_PLAN.ko.md checked. Latest QA v1257 FAILED/HOLD. Stacked docs-only child of unmerged Draft #1229, not an implemented feature or production incident.
- **Actual conflict:** migration 163 admin_merge_forked_member_account moves identities and sessions from p_merge to p_keep, but intentionally leaves the old accounts and historical postings owned by p_merge, then soft-deletes p_merge. Its existing refusal list does not mention proposed annotation/receipt tables. Migration 010 account_soft_delete likewise retains users. Wallet repository reads postings via accounts.owner_user_id, not ledger_transactions.actor_user_id. Therefore silently transferring annotations or exposing merged-user history to p_keep would violate the existing ownership proof; unguarded merge after annotation rollout could strand private data. This is a future integration hazard, not an observed live annotation loss.
- **Prior design correction:** v1252 cursor proposed mutable updated_at ordering and a vague snapshot fallback: editing a not-yet-read item moves it before the cursor and can skip it, while editing an already-read item can repeat it. A watermark alone does not freeze membership. Prior receipt original_result/payload_sha256 wording did not expressly prevent sensitive note content or offline dictionary attacks. Resolve these before engineering begins.

### Exact lifecycle and authorization contract (proposed; no live SQL change)
- The authoritative owner of a historical transaction is the active actor's own USER_CASH or USER_BANK posting joined through immutable accounts.owner_user_id. Keep account status out of historical posting eligibility; check actor status, consent and current session independently. Do not infer ownership from transaction.actor_user_id, identity merge audit alone, or a signed client transaction ID. Foreign, system-only and absent transactions return identical 404 without counterparty metadata.
- **Account merge gate is P0:** in a separately reviewed forward-only replacement of migration 163's admin_merge_forked_member_account, lock both users in stable UUID order before any transfer/identity movement, then refuse the entire merge with a controlled operator error (SQLSTATE 55000) if p_merge has ANY annotation row (including tombstones) or idempotency receipt. The check must serialize with annotation writes that lock their actor user row; no partial merge, no reassignment of account ownership, no auto-copy of notes, no WLD adjustment beyond existing approved merge semantics. Existing p_keep annotations remain untouched. A later migration policy requires explicit verified same-person authority, privacy approval, ledger provenance and separate tests; it is NOT approved here.
- Even when the source has no annotations, surviving p_keep cannot browse/annotate historical p_merge postings under the existing owner predicate; show only a generic unavailable state if a former transaction reference is supplied. Do not silently broaden the wallet read model, imply merged transaction portability, or leak old-user note metadata. Account soft-delete/administrative closure must revoke annotation reads/writes immediately; FK ON DELETE CASCADE is not a soft-delete erasure mechanism.
- Lock order for writes: actor users row FOR UPDATE -> verify owner postings and transaction -> annotation row -> receipt insert/read. Admin merge locks both user rows in deterministic order and performs preflight before economic transfers. Lock timeout/deadlock must fail closed; use only bounded retries with the SAME requestId and payload. Save/remove/merge races require serializable final outcomes.

### Private data model, removal and immutable idempotency receipts
- Keep (user_id,transaction_id) durable PK, monotonic bigint version and tombstone to prevent ABA. Enforce CHECK: active rows have allowlisted category and NFC-normalized 1–240 code-point note; removed rows MUST have category IS NULL AND note IS NULL and is_deleted=true. Remove clears private text in the same transaction as version increment and receipt creation. Retain only minimum owner/transaction/version/timestamps for tombstone until approved retention/erasure; do not promise physical erasure from WAL, MVCC pages or backups immediately.
- Store only keyed HMAC-SHA256 canonical-request fingerprints with key_version in private receipts, never plain SHA256 of short personal notes. Keep prior HMAC keys protected for the approved receipt TTL so replay can be checked after rotation; never persist secret keys in DB or logs. Immutable original_result contains only operation, transactionId, committedVersion, confirmedAt and status, **no note, category, original request body or raw ledger counterparty**. Same owner+requestId+same canonical payload replays original metadata receipt before CAS; different payload is 409; stale version is 412. A receipt cannot resurrect erased content or prove the current note still exists.
- After uncertain response: receipt lookup then owner GET; show CONFIRMED only on authoritative commit, UNKNOWN_COMMIT otherwise. Upon Remove, purge client draft and private caches, invalidate in-flight stale UI responses, retain version and privacy-safe receipt. On soft-delete, immediately deny owner API and queue policy-approved data erasure with backup/retention handling. Privacy owner and DPO must approve retention (proposed receipt 30 days is NOT a settled policy). Do not expose note contents in error messages, traces, telemetry, administrator tools, CSV, sitemap or SEO.

### Stable list, web/Android, security and test acceptance
- Replace mutable updated_at cursor with immutable annotation.created_at DESC, transaction_id DESC (stable unique tie-breaker); updated_at remains display metadata only. Use HMAC-signed owner+session-generation+filters+issuedAt+asOf+lastCreatedAt+lastTransactionId cursor, max limit 50, short TTL, and DB index (user_id,created_at DESC,transaction_id DESC) WHERE is_deleted=false. Require server-assigned immutable created_at and constrain created_at <= asOf to exclude new rows. Update must not move list position; deletion may shorten later pages, and tombstone reactivation can alter visibility, so label this **stable ordering, not an exact historical snapshot**. For strict snapshot semantics a separate bounded materialized snapshot would be required; do not claim it implemented.
- Keep existing /wallet/activity recent-50 CTA and /wallet/annotations saved-list route; do not advertise a complete historical transaction browser. Use the existing owner-only NestJS GET/PUT/DELETE/receipt endpoints from v1252; Android app-api forwards PUT/DELETE body, but only allowlisted headers, so expectedVersion/requestId remain in validated JSON body. Protect cookie+CSRF+consent, no-store/noindex, account-switch response fencing, 320px/200% zoom, keyboard/TalkBack focus and explicit Removed/Conflict/Unknown/Unavailable UI. Never change WLD, account balances, ledger, or other users' data.
- Admin receives only k>=20 aggregate metrics with anti-differencing; merge-block counts contain no member IDs or note text. Rate 12 writes/min/member, 500 active notes/member under actor-row lock, request body <=2KiB, canonical Unicode validation; 404 equalized for foreign/nonexistent IDs; no public note sharing.
- **Isolated real-PG acceptance (not executed):** 100 save-versus-admin-merge interleavings, merge with active/tombstone/receipt-only source all rollback before WLD movement, merge with no annotation follows existing function unchanged, p_keep cannot read p_merge history, 100 foreign probes, 100 same-key replays and changed-payload conflicts, 100 save/remove races, soft-delete while writing, HMAC key rotation/replay and content non-recovery, 10,001 cross-owner list records with concurrent edit/delete/re-add and no update-induced reorder, session switch, Android request forwarding, keyboard/mobile/noindex/no-store, exact ledger/balance invariance.
- Rollout remains OFF by default; allocate no migration number until 266 checksum and four competing 280 migrations are reconciled. Separate development branch and approved forward-only SQL; exact final SHA full CI/security, isolated ACL negative tests, official Test authenticated finance+Android E2E, encrypted backup restore, rollback rehearsal, human-authorized GitOps before Production. OFF must retain tombstones/receipts and owner reconciliation without exposing text. No runtime or deployment changes in this cycle.

### P0/P1 actionable backlog and external evidence
- **P0 ENV1254-01:** root filesystem still 100% (~589MiB free at start), separate data disk ~101GiB; isolate Production/dev/Test DB and 5433 bind, preserve worker artifacts, prove isolated encrypted restore before any DB writes.
- **P0 SEC1254-02:** dev app role has UPDATE/DELETE on 235/241 tables and three PUBLIC bank EXECUTE functions; forward-only least-privilege repair and negative tests; no assertion about Production role ACL.
- **P0 REL1254-03:** QA v1257 FAILED/HOLD, six frontend failures, dependency audit last known 2 Critical/10 High, bot 1 Critical/5 High, main/PR runtime-check SKIPPED, migration 266 checksum/280 collision, exact-SHA Test and restore missing; no build-only approval.
- **P0 WAL1254-04:** forward-only account merge preflight and owner-history non-portability contract; refuse merges with annotation/tombstone/receipt until approved policy; merge-vs-write 100-way tests.
- **P1 WAL1254-05:** CHECK/nullable erased tombstone, content-free original receipt, version and protected HMAC fingerprint/key rotation, privacy-approved retention.
- **P1 WAL1254-06:** actor→owner posting→annotation→receipt lock discipline, CAS and same-key replay, bounded deadlock retries and 500-cap race.
- **P1 WAL1254-07:** immutable created_at keyset index, signed owner/session/asOf cursor, explicit non-snapshot semantics and 10,001-item stress.
- **P1 WAL1254-08:** NestJS/Next.js/Android body contract, unknown-commit and removed-content UI, account-switch/merge/soft-delete fencing, a11y.
- **P1 WAL1254-09:** no-store/noindex, DPO-approved deletion/backup policy, anonymous admin, HMAC secrets, abuse and privacy-safe KPI.
- **P1 WAL1254-10:** isolated PostgreSQL negative ACL, merge/save/erase/cursor/concurrency regression, exact SHA full CI, staged OFF/rollback and authorized GitOps handoff.
- **External independent references gate:** target 10,000 URL/provenance/date/licence verified, deduplicated, classified records. Official PostgreSQL locking and OWASP authorization guidance consulted as publisher families, NOT counted as accepted records. Newly accepted **0**; cumulative verified **UNKNOWN/10,000** => BLOCKED / IN PROGRESS. No new scattered planning documents.


## v2026.10.25.1252 — F675-120 Owner-Private Wallet Transaction Annotations (FEATURE ADDITION / DRAFT / RELEASE HOLD)

### Cycle, authoritative source and non-overlap
- **Previous:** Feature Improvement v1250 (F675-119 dashboard shortcuts, unmerged Draft #1224). **Current:** Feature Addition v1252. **Next:** Feature Improvement v1254 (provisional). Start/mid main `c9a868b1e622341e2daa8b936ddf0988eda9988e`; authoritative main `AGENTS.md`, `README.md`, `docs/planning/PROJECT_PLAN.md` (English canonical), `PROJECT_PLAN.ko.md` (comparison). QA v1255 **FAILED/HOLD**. Independent documentation branch, not a runtime feature or approved deployment.
- **Verified code:** `frontend/src/app/wallet/activity/page.tsx` uses `GET /api/v1/wallet?recent=50`, displaying at most 50 recent owner-scoped entries; `wallet/export-ledger-csv.tsx` exports only its supplied recent rows, not a full ledger. `backend/src/wallet/wallet.controller.ts` has wallet overview/transfers but no transaction-annotation contract on inspected main. `wallet.repository.ts` derives transactions from `ledger_transactions` → `ledger_postings` → `accounts.owner_user_id`, not merely `actor_user_id`. The immutable ledger schema in `packages/database/init/001-economy-core.sql` has no transaction status column: an existing transaction and owner posting are the committed evidence. `frontend/src/lib/app-gateway.ts` allowlists the wallet prefix; Android path/method/body/header compatibility must be verified before use. No claim that all open PRs lack analogous work.
- **Scope separation:** This is a member-only organizational overlay, **not** ledger mutation, dispute adjudication, refund, balance recalculation, automatic categorization, public financial proof, or complete-history CSV. Coordinate with wallet dispute planning #1048 and wallet read-model/CSV work, and avoid Gemini/developer A/B/C/QA branch edits.

### Purpose, actors, screens and truthful UX
- **Purpose:** Let signed-in members add a private note and personal category to an actual WLD ledger transaction for recall and organization. Target: members with repeated shop, work, bank and transfer activity. Default feature OFF; no annotation exists until confirmed server commit. Actors: active consenting owner; operators see only thresholded anonymous health metrics, never note text or per-member transaction links.
- **Entry:** existing `/wallet/activity` recent-50 row gets a clearly labelled **“개인 메모” / “Private note”** CTA, plus `/wallet/annotations` for saved annotations (keyset pagination). A transaction older than 50 may be annotated through an owner-verified transaction reference from an independently approved future history UI; do **not** invent an unbounded transaction list or promise that all historical transactions are browsable today.
- **Editor:** `category` one of `PERSONAL|WORK|SHOP|TRANSFER|BANK|OTHER` (personal labels only, never change ledger type); `note` 1–240 Unicode code points, NFC-normalized, no HTML interpretation; **Save / Cancel / Remove note / Retry / View original receipt**. Category and note are private. Preserve original WLD amount, signed pocket deltas, type, date and transaction ID from ledger without modification. User-entered notes must never be presented as bank-certified facts.
- **States:** NO_NOTE (virtual version 0), EDITING, SAVING, SAVED_CONFIRMED, REMOVED_CONFIRMED (durable tombstone), STALE_VERSION (412), KEY_REUSE_CONFLICT (409), UNKNOWN_COMMIT (receipt lookup, then current GET), NOT_OWNED_OR_NOT_FOUND (uniform 404), OFFLINE, UNAVAILABLE (503), SIGNED_OUT. Distinguish a confirmed server receipt from an optimistic local draft. Fallback on API outage must not fabricate a saved note. Cancel does not mutate server state. On account switch, consent revocation, soft deletion or session rotation discard local drafts/caches and fence in-flight responses.

### Proposed API and database (implementation requires separate reviewed branch)
- **NestJS owner-only:** `GET /api/v1/wallet/transactions/:transactionId/annotation`; `PUT` same path with `{category,note,expectedVersion,requestId}`; `DELETE` same path with `{expectedVersion,requestId}`; `GET /api/v1/wallet/transaction-annotations?limit=20&cursor=...` (max 50); `GET /api/v1/wallet/transaction-annotations/receipts/:requestId`. Use session/authentication/consent and CSRF for mutations; actor ID derived server-side, never from request body. Require UUID transactionId/requestId, 2 KiB body max, canonical payload fingerprint. Response includes annotation version, confirmedAt, currentState, original immutable receipt and `replayed` flag; no counterparty identity or unrelated ledger details. 400 invalid, 401/403 auth/consent/CSRF, 404 absent/foreign transaction, 409 key reused with different payload, 412 stale CAS, 429 rate limited, 503 uncertain DB; `Cache-Control: private, no-store` for every owner endpoint.
- **DB proposal:** `member_wallet_transaction_annotations(user_id uuid REFERENCES users(id),transaction_id uuid REFERENCES ledger_transactions(id),category text CHECK IN(...),note text,version bigint NOT NULL CHECK(version>=1),is_deleted boolean NOT NULL,created_at timestamptz,updated_at timestamptz,PRIMARY KEY(user_id,transaction_id))`; `member_wallet_annotation_receipts(user_id,request_id,transaction_id,operation,payload_sha256,committed_version,committed_at,original_result,PRIMARY KEY(user_id,request_id))` with bounded approved retention. Unique immutable owner/request receipts and `(user_id,updated_at DESC,transaction_id DESC)` index for keyset. Add CHECK note length, NFC validation in application and DB guard. Preserve tombstone after Remove: never reset version to 0 (ABA protection). Account soft-delete changes status but does not physically delete `users`; FK CASCADE alone does **not** purge notes. Obtain approved retention/erasure policy before migration.
- **Trust boundary:** only reviewed `SECURITY DEFINER` functions with fixed `search_path`, schema-qualified relations, owner/active-status checks, and explicit function EXECUTE for `moneyverse_app` (never PUBLIC). Revoke direct app DML/SELECT on new private tables; test negative grants. For every read and write, verify `EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM ledger_postings p JOIN accounts a ON a.id=p.account_id WHERE p.transaction_id=:tx AND a.owner_user_id=:actor AND a.account_type IN ('USER_CASH','USER_BANK'))`. Do not use `ledger_transactions.actor_user_id` as sole ownership proof; counterparties may legitimately own a posting. Do not require current account status active for a historical owner posting. Never expose other participants or system accounts.
- **Atomicity:** lock active actor row, verify ledger ownership, lock/insert annotation row and receipt in deterministic order; look up `(user_id,requestId)` first under owner lock. Identical payload replay returns original committed receipt **before** CAS; different payload 409. First save requires expectedVersion 0; each save/removal increments durable version, including removal; stale version 412. Commit annotation, tombstone and receipt in **one transaction**; do not mutate `ledger_transactions`, `ledger_postings`, `accounts`, `account_balances`, `outbox_events`, or WLD. Receipt expiry (proposed 30 days pending privacy review) means client reconciles by current GET, not by claiming unlimited replay. Cursor signed to actor and immutable `(updated_at,transaction_id)` snapshot/watermark; exclude tombstones from normal listing and avoid duplicates/skips under concurrent updates; if snapshot stability cannot be proven, use a server-issued snapshot token with bounded lifetime.

### Admin, abuse, mobile, SEO, analytics and acceptance
- **Security/abuse:** reject foreign transaction with indistinguishable 404, avoid existence/timing oracle, 12 writes/min/member, max 500 active annotations/member, 240 code points/note, escape text on display, strip control/bidi override characters, protect logs/telemetry from note content, deny mass enumeration, apply CSP and CSRF, audit only minimal non-content metadata. Delete/soft-delete and retention rules require privacy review; administrator cannot rewrite private notes.
- **Android and accessibility:** verify `/app-api/v1/wallet/*` passthrough supports PUT/DELETE bodies, CSRF, request ID and session cookies; use body `expectedVersion`/`requestId` if gateway drops If-Match/Idempotency-Key headers. Native TalkBack/keyboard, visible focus, 44px touch controls, 320px/200% zoom, live status, translated KO/EN copy; screen `/wallet/annotations` and owner data noindex, no-store. Private note content never enters SEO, structured data or public sitemap.
- **Admin/KPI:** anonymous count of confirmed saves/removals, 409/412/503, retry recovery, median save latency, 30-day retained usage; cohort >=20 and anti-differencing thresholds, no note body, user ID or transaction ID in analytics. Proposed success: zero foreign-owner leaks, zero unauthorized ledger mutations, >=99.5% confirmed mutation success under healthy isolated Test, p95 read <=500ms and write <=800ms (targets, not measured).
- **Tests:** isolated real PostgreSQL SECURITY DEFINER owner/foreign/system-only posting checks, missing owner/expired session, app-role direct DML denial, PUBLIC EXECUTE denial, soft-delete, CAS ABA, 100 identical concurrent saves, 100 same-key/different-payload attempts, save-vs-remove and owner-switch races, missing-response receipt reconciliation, 500-cap boundary, 10,001 synthetic cross-member records and keyset stress, Unicode/CSV injection, Android parity, keyboard/TalkBack, noindex/no-store, ledger hash/balance invariance. No test is claimed executed in this planning run.
- **Rollout:** separate engineering branch; migration number assigned only after #1105/#1117/#1139/#1143 280 collision and migration266 checksum are reconciled; additive forward-only schema; OFF by default, backfill **none**, dark-read then 1→5→25→100% opt-in after exact candidate SHA CI/security, isolated real-PG ACL tests, official exact-SHA Test authenticated wallet+finance and Android E2E, verified encrypted backup restore, authorized GitOps. OFF disables new edits/CTA, retains version/tombstone/receipts and provides authenticated reconciliation for uncertain commits. No destructive rollback, Production promotion or Test mutation from planning.

### P0/P1 implementation backlog and gates
- **P0 ENV1252-01:** root disk 100% (~19MiB free this run), production/development/Test DB separation, 5433 binding, owned artifact preservation and isolated restore.
- **P0 SEC1252-02:** repair observed dev 235/241 direct app UPDATE/DELETE and three PUBLIC bank EXECUTE grants via reviewed forward-only migration and negative role tests; avoid inferring Production ACLs.
- **P0 REL1252-03:** QA v1255 FAILED/HOLD; dependency audit 2 Critical/10 High, bot 1 Critical/5 High, six frontend assertions, skipped CI runtime, migration266 checksum and 280 collision, official exact-SHA Test and restore unverified.

- **P1 WAL1252-05:** schema/indexes, SECURITY DEFINER owner-only read/write, tombstone and receipt fingerprint.
- **P1 WAL1252-06:** deterministic locking, version/CAS, same-key replay, unknown commit recovery and signed snapshot cursor.
- **P1 WAL1252-07:** NestJS owner API, Next recent-50 row CTA/editor and saved annotation listing.
- **P1 WAL1252-08:** Android app-api contract, session fencing, keyboard/TalkBack/320px/200% accessibility.
- **P1 WAL1252-09:** privacy erasure/retention, anonymous admin, noindex/no-store, abuse controls and KPI.
- **P1 WAL1252-10:** isolated real-PG 100-way concurrency, 100 foreign-owner probes, 10,001 synthetic cross-member keyset, full regression, staged OFF/rollback and exact-SHA release evidence.
- **Independent external evidence gate:** target **10,000** independent reference records with URL/provenance/licence/date, dedup and verified classification. Newly accepted **0**; cumulative accepted **UNKNOWN**. Official publisher families are not 10,000 independent records. **BLOCKED / IN PROGRESS**. No runtime/DB/ledger/WLD/Test/Production changes in this documentation-only cycle.



## v2026.10.24.1210 — F675-109 Board Reading List Hardening: Deletion, Idempotency, Cursor and Resume (FEATURE IMPROVEMENT / DRAFT / RELEASE HOLD)

### Cycle, verified baseline and explicit corrections
- Previous **Feature Addition v1208**; Current **Feature Improvement v1210**; Next **Feature Addition v1212** (provisional). Authoritative remote main `54d5140ddb49dd3e2193a2f82b71a6a0a57cfede` and canonical main EN/KO plans rechecked start/mid; v1208 Draft PR #1087 is an **unmerged documentation dependency**, not deployed source. Latest QA v1213 (2026-10-24 08:25 KST) FAILED / HOLD; no runtime, production-linked DB, WLD, feature switch, Test, Production, or other worker changes.
- Source-backed: `044-member-board.sql` creates `member_board_posts(id,deleted_at)` and `member_board_delete` soft-deletes; `175-member-board-public-read.sql` and `259-board-author-user-id-and-chat-search.sql` define `member_board_public_get` returning only `deleted_at IS NULL` posts. The actual post page's comment heading has `id="comments-title"`, not `id="comments"`. No reading-list table/API was found in inspected main. Prior v1208 `ON DELETE RESTRICT` FK would prevent future hard-purge of posts while bookmarked; prior generic 404 mapping could prevent an owner from removing a now-unavailable saved post. These are **design defects to resolve before implementation**, not observed production incidents.
- Product remains voluntary member-only Save/Remove and explicit TOP/COMMENTS resume, no passive scroll history, automated interest inference, advertisements, notification, or WLD transfer. Static multilingual blog remains excluded pending an authoritative publication/visibility contract. Reuse existing public board and account UX; no overlap with A/B/C/Gemini/QA branches.

### Precise member, web and Android state contracts
- Entry: `/board/[postId]` Save/Remove and `/account/reading-list` owner list. States GUEST, LOADING, NOT_SAVED, SAVING, SAVED_CONFIRMED, UNAVAILABLE_SAVED, REMOVING, CONFLICT, UNKNOWN_COMMIT, ERROR, SIGNED_OUT. Buttons: Save, Remove, Open post, Resume at comments, Mark TOP, Mark COMMENTS, Refresh saved status, Retry after sign-in. A deleted/unpublished item remains removable by its owner **without public visibility**; owner GET/status may return `available:false` and redacted metadata, while Save of a deleted post returns indistinguishable 404. Unowned or never-saved status and DELETE are 404, not a post-existence oracle. REMOVE returns committed tombstone receipt even when source is unavailable; replay of same key returns original receipt, not current state.
- Actual `/board/[postId]` has `h2#comments-title`; the proposed resume URL is exactly `/board/<canonical-UUID>#comments-title` after fresh visibility check. No arbitrary fragments, query-supplied redirects or untrusted DOM selectors. Heading must be focusable for programmatic keyboard navigation (`tabIndex=-1` or equivalent) with a visible focus indicator and scroll margin below sticky navigation; TOP uses plain canonical URL. If source is unavailable, show redacted card with Remove, not a broken deep link. Reduced-motion preference must not be overridden. Android uses native navigation to comment section, same explicit-only marker and no background reading analytics.
- Auth/session generation fences: on logout, account switch, consent revocation or session expiry, discard in-flight GET/write response and cached title/list data; private BFF/API/edge responses `Cache-Control: private, no-store` and private pages `noindex,nofollow`. A historical idempotency receipt cannot make UI claim SAVED_CONFIRMED after a later removal; reconcile with owner GET after replay/unknown. Never persist another account's list in browser-wide localStorage.

### API, SQL authority, referential integrity and race resolution (PROPOSED ONLY)
- Preserve v1208 owner-only `GET /api/v1/board/reading-list`, `GET .../:postId`, `POST`, `PUT .../:postId/marker`, `DELETE .../:postId` and proposed Android `/app-api/v1/board/reading-list/*`. Session actor, consent and CSRF are server-derived; request body cannot select user. POST/PUT/DELETE require UUID `Idempotency-Key`; PUT/DELETE require `If-Match: "vN"` (return strong ETag and committedVersion). Error mapping: 400/422 malformed, 401 unauthenticated, 403 denied consent/CSRF, 404 not-owned/nonexistent or unavailable-to-SAVE, 409 version/key-digest conflict or cap, 429 throttle, 503 unknown commit; no false 2xx on timeout. DELETE of an owner-saved but unavailable source is **allowed**, with redacted 200 receipt.
- Proposed forward-only table `member_board_reading_list`: `(user_id,post_id)` PK, `user_id REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE`, `post_id REFERENCES member_board_posts(id) ON DELETE CASCADE` **instead of RESTRICT**; `saved_at`, `updated_at`, `removed_at`, `marker CHECK IN ('TOP','COMMENTS')`, positive monotonic `version`. Soft-deleted posts keep rows as UNAVAILABLE_SAVED; a separately authorized **hard purge** cascades active bookmarks (no stale title/body remains). The migration must test existing deletion/account-erasure behavior and avoid unapproved hard deletion. A hard purge may make the old receipt non-resolvable; it never recreates the post. `member_board_reading_requests` and `member_board_reading_events` require explicit `user_id REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE` or separately reviewed anonymized security retention, UUID `post_id` without blocking post purge, 32-byte request digest CHECK, unique `(user_id,idempotency_key)`, immutable receiptId/operation/resultVersion; retention/tombstone policy subject to privacy review (90 days is a proposal, not authorization). No copied post title/body, token, IP or passive reading trail.
- Transaction lock order is **actor users row → source post row FOR SHARE → owner reading-list row → idempotency insert/lookup** consistently for SAVE/MARK/REMOVE when source exists; unavailable REMOVE must still lock actor+saved row and not require visible source. For SAVE, acquire source `FOR SHARE`, re-evaluate `deleted_at IS NULL` after lock, serialize owner cap count (max 500) under actor lock, CAS row version, commit receipt/event atomically; concurrent post soft-delete UPDATE must wait on the row lock. Do not claim an already-started read can retract data after a later deletion commits; enforce no metadata disclosure for reads **started after** the deletion commit. For same-key concurrent requests use unique conflict-safe insert/re-read under the same transaction (not SELECT-then-INSERT without conflict handling); identical digest replays exact original receipt, mismatched digest 409. Re-SAVE after REMOVE needs a **new key plus current tombstone version**; a stale SAVE/MARK cannot resurrect.
- Cursor: HMAC-authenticated opaque payload with owner/session-generation scope, stable `saved_at,post_id` ordering, filter/version and short expiry; reject cross-user, tampered, expired or filter-mismatched cursor (400/422, never another member's data). Explicit initial `asOf` boundary stabilizes paging against new saves; concurrent deletes may reduce results and must not leak unavailable metadata. The 10,001 same-timestamp fixture is a **dedicated isolated DB synthetic test with cap overridden for the fixture only**; production 500-item cap remains enforced. No raw bookmark history in telemetry.

### P0/P1 executable backlog, QA and release gate
- **P0 ENV1210-01** — Isolate shared Production/dev `woldeok_moneyverse_dev` and constrain 5433 wildcard network exposure; verify exact connection identities and encrypted isolated restore before any write/test fixture. Root disk 93% requires capacity guard; never remove another agent's worktree.
- **P0 SEC1210-02** — Replace app-role UPDATE/DELETE grants (235/241 tables) and three PUBLIC bank EXECUTE grants with reviewed forward-only least privilege; isolated real-PostgreSQL negative tests, no ad hoc live REVOKE.
- **P0 REL1210-03** — QA v1213 full lint fail (wallet CSV), frontend Saturday blog test fail, pnpm audit Critical4/High10 and bot Critical1/High4, migration266 checksum mismatch, route-ledger missing inventory, isolated restore and exact-SHA Test all HOLD. **New P0 #1088:** auto-integrate build-only Test Candidate success cannot authorize ready-PR merge; require independently attested exact-head deployed Test backend/API/DB + authenticated login/finance E2E and security PASS before merge/branch deletion, with negative workflow tests.
- **P0 BOARD1210-04** — Resolve public visibility/delete/save race and redacted unavailable REMOVE; test 100 SAVE-vs-soft-delete interleavings, 100 foreign-owner probes, no post metadata in reads begun after deletion commit, no title leakage in private list/SEO.
- **P1 BOARD1210-05** — Forward-only FK CASCADE and account/post erasure parity, owner-scoped SECURITY DEFINER, deterministic actor→post→list lock order, request digest and 100-way original receipt; hard-purge/account-delete fixtures.
- **P1 BOARD1210-06** — HMAC owner-bound expiring snapshot cursor, 500 cap, 10,001 synthetic same-timestamp fixture, concurrent insert/delete pagination and key-rotation invalidation.
- **P1 BOARD1210-07** — Source-accurate `#comments-title` resume, explicit marker CAS, focus/scroll-offset/reduced-motion/320px/200%-zoom and Android native deep-link regression.
- **P1 BOARD1210-08** — Account switch/session invalidation, private no-store/noindex, aggregate-only admin with small-cell suppression, CSRF/BOLA/rate-limit, privacy/erasure and consent withdrawal.
- **P1 BOARD1210-09** — Web+Android confirmed receipt/unknown-commit/unavailable saved/remove UX, negative authorization, conflict retry, KPI success vs false-success, no passive tracking.
- **P1 BOARD1210-10** — Real isolated PostgreSQL TDD, 100-way concurrent SAVE/MARK/REMOVE and deletion, immutable migration/checksum parity, flag OFF and rollback preserving tombstones, receipts, privacy and existing board access.
- Release: `board_reading_list` defaults OFF. Separate engineering branch only; exact-head full CI/security, isolated PostgreSQL ACL+backup restore, official Test authenticated web/Android/financial regression, independently attested deployment and approved GitOps canary before Production. Build Test Candidate SUCCESS alone is not a release/merge gate. No runtime deployment from planning. No main merge, Test/Production change, or other worker branch changes in this cycle.
- Independent reference gate: consulted PostgreSQL explicit locking (https://www.postgresql.org/docs/17/explicit-locking.html), OWASP authorization (https://cheatsheetseries.owasp.org/cheatsheets/Authorization_Cheat_Sheet.html), W3C focus visibility (https://www.w3.org/WAI/WCAG22/Understanding/focus-not-obscured-minimum). **Three publisher families reviewed, zero newly approved/deduplicated records, cumulative approved UNKNOWN / 10,000; BLOCKED / IN PROGRESS.** Reviewing pages is not collecting 10,000 independent evidence records.


## v2026.10.24.1208 — F675-109 Verified Member Board Reading List and Manual Resume Marker (FEATURE ADDITION / DRAFT / RELEASE HOLD)

### Cycle, authority, evidence, and product boundary
- Previous Feature Improvement v1206; Current Feature Addition v1208; Next Feature Improvement v1210 (provisional). Source-of-truth main `54d5140ddb49dd3e2193a2f82b71a6a0a57cfede` rechecked start/mid; v1206 Draft #1082 is unmerged and is the stacked documentation base, not main. Latest QA v1211 (2026-10-24 07:28 KST) FAILED / Production HOLD. No runtime, DB, WLD, Test, Production, feature switch, or other worker branch changes.
- Confirmed current code: `frontend/src/app/board/[postId]/page.tsx` renders public and member board posts; `backend/src/board/public-board.controller.ts` and `public-board.service.ts` call `member_board_public_get` for public visibility; `packages/database/migrations/044-member-board.sql` has UUID post IDs, `deleted_at`, and a visible-post index. There is no verified owner reading-list API or bookmark table in the inspected main migration/source paths. `frontend/src/app/blog/blog-page-server.tsx` serves locale-specific articles from frontend data, without a verified NestJS public-content authority: blog saving is OUT OF SCOPE until a reviewed publication manifest or canonical backend visibility contract exists. Do not reuse collectible `collections` favorites as article bookmarks or duplicate C's reading-scale UI.
- **Purpose/personas:** Allow authenticated members to manually save visible public board posts, revisit them across devices, and explicitly mark whether to resume at post top or comments; never infer scroll tracking, reading completion, or user interests. Guests can read public posts but must sign in to save. Member, Android app member, aggregate-only content operator, and authorized security/privacy reviewer are the actors. No reward, WLD credit, trade, promotion, algorithmic profiling, or automatic email/push send.

### Navigation, screen contract, and state machine
- Entry: `/board/[postId]` action **Save to reading list** / **Remove from reading list**; account shortcut and board list link to `/account/reading-list`. A saved item shows **Open post**, **Resume at comments** (only when explicitly selected), **Mark start/comments**, **Remove**, and **Refresh**. Anonymous Save opens login with safe same-origin return path, never creates a server record.
- States `guest|loading|not_saved|saving|saved|removing|unavailable|conflict|error|unknown`; no optimistic success or URL-query success flag. A confirmed DB receipt alone shows `saved`; network uncertainty shows **Check saved status**, not **Saved**. Empty list has a link to `/board`; deleted/moderated/inaccessible posts show only **Post unavailable** and **Remove**, never a cached title, author, body or screenshot. Unavailable rows may be counted in the owner's private list but are never externally exposed.
- No automatic scroll capture. Resume marker enum `TOP|COMMENTS` is saved only on explicit button activation. Do not accept arbitrary CSS selectors, DOM paths, URLs or free-text anchors. Opening a saved item rechecks current public visibility and resolves only the canonical `/board/<UUID>` link.

### Proposed API, auth and mobile contract — not implemented
- `GET /api/v1/board/reading-list?cursor=&limit=20` member session+consent; max 100, opaque signed keyset cursor `(saved_at DESC,post_id DESC)`; return `items:[{postId,savedAt,marker,version,available,publicTitle?}]`, `nextCursor`, never private body or foreign post metadata. `GET /api/v1/board/reading-list/:postId` owner-only status, 404 for not saved/foreign. `POST /api/v1/board/reading-list` authenticated+CSRF+consent with `{postId:UUID}`, `Idempotency-Key:UUID`; `PUT /api/v1/board/reading-list/:postId/marker` with `{marker:TOP|COMMENTS}`, `If-Match:version` and idempotency header; `DELETE /api/v1/board/reading-list/:postId` same guards and idempotency header. Successful writes return committed `{receiptId,postId,version,state,savedAt?}`, not an inferred client state.
- Server actor derived from session, never userId in body; 400 malformed UUID/marker, 401 missing login, 403 invalid CSRF/consent, 404 unavailable or not-owned, 409 stale version/key reused with different payload or max saved items, 429 throttled, 503 uncertain persistence (offer status recheck). No membership or existence oracle for another member's saved item. Next server-only BFF forwards session and CSRF without exposing internal API token. Android `/app-api/v1/board/reading-list/*` is a proposed contract requiring route-map/allowlist parity and authenticated device-session checks; it is NOT currently implemented. Private responses always `Cache-Control: private, no-store`.

### Proposed schema, SQL authority, atomicity and retention
- New forward-only reviewed migration (number assigned by engineering only after reconciling unmerged 280/281 and main migration history): `member_board_reading_list(user_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,post_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES member_board_posts(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,saved_at timestamptz NOT NULL,updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL,removed_at timestamptz,marker text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'TOP' CHECK(marker IN ('TOP','COMMENTS')),version bigint NOT NULL DEFAULT 1 CHECK(version>0),PRIMARY KEY(user_id,post_id))`; partial owner list index `(user_id,saved_at DESC,post_id DESC) WHERE removed_at IS NULL`; avoid copying private post title/body into list storage. Post deletion remains authoritative and never cascades into a public cache.
- `member_board_reading_requests(user_id uuid NOT NULL,idempotency_key uuid NOT NULL,request_hash bytea NOT NULL,receipt_id uuid NOT NULL UNIQUE,post_id uuid NOT NULL,operation text CHECK IN ('SAVE','MARK','REMOVE'),result_version bigint NOT NULL,created_at timestamptz NOT NULL,PRIMARY KEY(user_id,idempotency_key))` plus append-only `member_board_reading_events(id uuid PK,user_id uuid,post_id uuid,event_type text,version bigint,created_at timestamptz)`; index `(user_id,created_at DESC,id DESC)` for redacted audit. Never store raw session tokens, IPs, browsing histories, reading time or arbitrary anchors. Retention of removed-row tombstones and audit: initial 90-day proposal pending legal/privacy review; no silent destruction of mandatory security records.
- Owner-scoped `SECURITY DEFINER` functions with fixed `search_path=pg_catalog,pg_temp`, explicit actor/active-session validation, reviewed `EXECUTE` allowlist, `PUBLIC EXECUTE` revoked and no direct `moneyverse_app` table DML. SAVE transaction checks public post visibility using the same authoritative predicate as `member_board_public_get`, locks post visibility against concurrent deletion/moderation, serializes per-owner cap checks by locking the actor users row (SELECT ... FOR UPDATE) before counting active items; enforces max 500 active saves atomically, locks/updates existing owner+post row, persists original idempotency receipt and event together. Same key+same fingerprint returns exact original historical receipt (not a promise that the item is still saved); client separately rechecks current status; same key+different request 409. REMOVE records a versioned tombstone; concurrent stale SAVE/MARK must not resurrect removed rows without a new explicit key and version. Read rechecks public visibility at response time; no stale title/author leakage if post was removed. A transaction must not issue WLD/ledger writes.

### Admin, security, SEO, a11y, telemetry and abuse controls
- No member reading list in ordinary admin screens. `GET /api/v1/admin/board/reading-list/metrics` returns only sufficiently aggregated totals (saved/removed, stale-source suppression, API 409/429/5xx, p95 read latency), with small-cell suppression; no per-member titles or identity joins. Emergency privacy investigation requires separate authorized role, recent reauthentication, reason and immutable audit; bulk export prohibited by default. Abuse budgets proposed 30 writes/member/minute and 500 active saved items/member, server-enforced and adjustable after observed baseline; input length caps, rate limits, CSRF, BOLA, replay, ID enumeration and permission-negative tests required.
- Private list and member save state `noindex,nofollow`, no sitemap entry, no public OpenGraph or structured data derived from saved state, `private,no-store`. Public board canonical/SEO unchanged; a removed/unpublished post is never resurrected via reading list. At 320px and 200% zoom use full-width controls, 44px touch targets, semantic buttons, visible focus, `aria-pressed` for saved toggle, `role=status` only for confirmed receipt and `role=alert` for failures, keyboard navigation and screen-reader state. Android native UI uses equivalent labels, no background reading telemetry or notification permission inference.
- KPIs: confirmed save success / eligible CTA impressions, 7-day explicit return-to-saved-item (aggregated), unavailable-source suppression, conflict/unknown receipt rate, median list load and p95, privacy complaints, wrong-owner access failures; no raw browsing events or member IDs in analytics. Targets require real baseline; safety thresholds: 0/100 foreign owner exposures, 0 leaked removed-post titles, 0 false-success receipts.

### P0/P1 executable backlog, test and release hold
- **P0 ENV1208-01** — Separate Production/dev DB currently sharing `woldeok_moneyverse_dev`, contain PostgreSQL 5433 wildcard exposure with approved network policy; prove separate exact connection identities without touching live balances.
- **P0 SEC1208-02** — Resolve broad app-role DML (QA: 235/241 tables), three `PUBLIC` bank EXECUTE grants; reviewed forward-only least privilege, real-PG negative ACL tests; no ad hoc production revoke.
- **P0 REL1208-03** — QA v1211 full lint/frontend failure, main pnpm audit 4 Critical/10 High and bot 1 Critical/4 High, migration266 checksum provenance, exact-SHA CI/Test, backup isolated restore and approved GitOps release gate. QA #1076 CI success is not official Test/Production authorization.
- **P0 BOARD1208-04** — Prove canonical public board visibility/deletion/moderation race and zero stale metadata in saved-list reads, including 100 save-vs-delete races and foreign-post oracle tests.
- **P1 BOARD1208-05** — Forward-only schema, owner-scoped SECURITY DEFINER functions, atomic 100-way same-key receipt, 100 conflicting fingerprints, versioned tombstones and 500-item cap.
- **P1 BOARD1208-06** — Member web Save/Remove/list/empty/unavailable/unknown UX with true committed receipts and stable keyset cursor for 10,001 same-timestamp saves.
- **P1 BOARD1208-07** — Explicit TOP/COMMENTS marker, CAS/If-Match, stale-session isolation, safe canonical resume and no automatic tracking.
- **P1 BOARD1208-08** — Android App API contract and authorized aggregate-only admin diagnostics, role/CSRF/reauth negative tests.
- **P1 BOARD1208-09** — Privacy retention/erasure policy, noindex/no-store/public SEO isolation, WCAG 2.2 AA 320px/200% zoom and privacy-safe KPIs.
- **P1 BOARD1208-10** — RED/GREEN unit/real PostgreSQL/contract/web+Android/QA regression, migration parity, concurrency, idempotency, 404/409/429/503, deleted-post race, 10,001 cursor, flag-off rollback.
- Release: new feature switch `board_reading_list` default OFF, reviewed separate development branch and migrations only after source/PR-stack reconciliation. Exact commit SHA CI full lint/typecheck/build/test/security audits, isolated real-PG grants and backup restore, authenticated official Test web/Android/finance regression and controlled canary must pass before authorized GitOps zero-downtime Production promotion. Flag OFF halts new saves/updates without deleting private records; rollback keeps tombstones, receipts and old clients from resurrecting saves. Never deploy from planning; do not merge #1082 or other agents' drafts without independent review.
- **Independent reference gate:** OWASP API1 BOLA (https://api-security.owasp.org/editions/2023/en/0xa1-broken-object-level-authorization/), PostgreSQL ON CONFLICT (https://www.postgresql.org/docs/17/sql-insert.html), W3C WCAG 2.2 (https://www.w3.org/TR/WCAG22/). Three independent publisher families consulted; this is NOT 3 approved reference records. Newly accepted/deduplicated = **0**, cumulative accepted = **UNKNOWN**, target **10,000**, gate **BLOCKED / IN PROGRESS**. No fabricated count.


## v2026.10.24.1206 — F675-108 Accessibility Preference Hardening: Identity, Prepaint and Durable Sync (FEATURE IMPROVEMENT / DRAFT / RELEASE HOLD)

### Cycle, verified source and purpose
- Previous Feature Addition v1204; Current Feature Improvement v1206; Next Feature Addition v1208 (provisional). Latest canonical main 54d5140ddb49dd3e2193a2f82b71a6a0a57cfede unchanged at start/mid; v1204 is an unmerged Draft, not main. QA v1209 (2026-10-24 06:45 KST) FAILED/HOLD. Documentation only; no runtime, DB, WLD, Test, Production, feature-switch or other-agent branch changes.
- frontend/src/app/layout.tsx runs POINT_PREFERENCE_SCRIPT before SiteShell and uses next-themes ThemeProvider; frontend/src/lib/theme.ts owns wdmv.theme/wdmv.point; globals.css and cosmetics.css already honor OS reduced motion. Account page has no accessibility link. backend/src/account/account.controller.ts uses SessionGuard/AuthenticatedGuard/ConsentGuard and mutation CsrfGuard. No new accessibility preference API or schema was found. Preserve existing theme, point, session/device center and native OS ownership.
- Refined hazards, not confirmed incidents: an in-flight member response could overwrite a new account's settings; server values could leak into shared localStorage; unversioned DELETE could allow stale re-enable; site high contrast or motion could override OS forced-colors/reduced-motion.

### Entry, CTA, states and precedence
- Guest /accessibility and member /account/accessibility; shortcuts from account and common utilities. Text scale 100/125/150/175/200%, contrast standard/high, motion device/reduce. CTA Preview, Save on this device, Sync across my devices (explicit opt-in default OFF), Compare versions, Use this device, Use other device, Retry, Reset device, Stop syncing. Do not block login, WLD transaction confirmations or security alerts.
- Distinguish PREVIEW_UNSAVED (in-memory), LOCAL_SAVED (device only), SERVER_CONFIRMED (durable receipt), SYNC_PENDING, CONFLICT, OFFLINE, SAVE_FAILED and SYNC_DISABLED. aria-live announces actual persistence, not merely a sent request. Offline edits remain local; reconnection never silently queues sync.
- OS forced-colors overrides app high contrast and point palette; OS reduced-motion overrides any app motion choice. App may only further reduce motion. Use paired system colors Canvas/CanvasText and ButtonFace/ButtonText, visible focus/borders, no blanket forced-color-adjust:none. Browser zoom and Android native font scaling are not capped; preserve next-themes class and existing point-color storage. Test OS forced-colors plus prefers-contrast and light/dark combinations.
- Prepaint only allowlisted root attributes data-a11y-scale (100|125|150|175|200), data-a11y-contrast (standard|high), data-a11y-motion (device|reduce). Parse wdmv.accessibility.v1 JSON only when UTF-8 <=512 bytes, version=1, exact keys/enums; reject arbitrary CSS/HTML/scripts. Run before interactive shell, do not modify wdmv.theme/point; storage disabled/invalid JSON falls back to OS-safe defaults. SSR and first client render match; controls are unselected until mounted; no-JS baseline remains readable.
- Device-local preference can survive account switch, but server-synced values/account identifiers NEVER persist in browser-wide localStorage. Server snapshot is session-memory only. On logout, account change, consent withdrawal or expiry, increment auth-generation, discard pending GET/PUT/DELETE responses, remove server-derived root attributes and reapply only explicit device-local values. Cross-tab signals carry no IDs/values/receipts. If local and server values differ on login, require explicit comparison; never silently overwrite.
- Conflict UI shows local preview and server version/time. Reset device does not revoke server sync; Stop syncing is a versioned server operation and preserves local preferences. Error and retry paths remain keyboard and screen-reader accessible.

### API, DB, concurrency, privacy (PROPOSED; NOT IMPLEMENTED)
- GET /api/v1/account/accessibility-preferences: authenticated owner only, 200 {syncEnabled,textScalePct,contrastMode,motionMode,version,updatedAt}, strong ETag "vN"; absent row -> 200 {syncEnabled:false,values:null,version:0}, ETag "v0". Cache-Control private,no-store across API/BFF/edge. Never trust body/query userId. Web session/consent + mutation CSRF, Android native-equivalent auth and owner checks.
- PUT requires If-Match:"vN", Idempotency-Key UUID and exact allowlisted {textScalePct:150,contrastMode:"high",motionMode:"reduce",syncEnabled:true}; no implicit opt-in. Durable response {receiptId,operation:"UPSERT",committedVersion,serverConfirmedAt,replayed}. DELETE with If-Match/UUID means Stop syncing, returns same receipt fields with operation:"DISABLE"; no physical row deletion/version reset. Replay same key+same digest returns ORIGINAL receipt even if later version exists; it does not claim current state.
- Errors: 401 missing session; 403 denied/consent revoked; 409 VERSION_CONFLICT with currentVersion only (GET to compare) or IDEMPOTENCY_KEY_REUSED for different payload/operation; 422 invalid ETag/key/enum/scale; 429 per-user write throttle; 503 unavailable. No server write on preview/local save; no “synced” label before receipt.
- Additive member_accessibility_preferences: user_id UUID PK FK users(id), text_scale_pct SMALLINT CHECK IN(100,125,150,175,200), contrast_mode CHECK standard/high, motion_mode CHECK device/reduce, sync_enabled BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE, version BIGINT NOT NULL CHECK>0, updated_at timestamptz, disabled_at nullable. Separate receipts(id UUID PK,user_id UUID FK,idempotency_key UUID,operation UPSERT/DISABLE,request_digest bytea length32,committed_version bigint,created_at timestamptz,UNIQUE(user_id,idempotency_key)). PK covers owner reads; no redundant user_id index. No raw preferences in receipts/logs/analytics.
- SECURITY DEFINER function SET search_path=pg_catalog,pg_temp with qualified public objects, authenticated actor and consent, no PUBLIC EXECUTE or app direct DML. Serialize first-write via user lock/advisory lock, then row lock and version CAS; validate canonical digest, update preference and receipt atomically. DISABLE increments version and persists a tombstone(sync_enabled=false, disabled_at) to fence stale clients. 100 same-key concurrent PUT -> one mutation/receipt; 100 distinct stale-version PUT -> at most one advancement; 100 PUT/DISABLE races -> monotonic version and zero stale resurrection. Real isolated PostgreSQL role-negative tests required.
- No disability inference, fingerprinting, ad targeting, user-level admin access or account-linked client analytics. Retention of tombstone/receipt and account-deletion cascade need legal/privacy approval; no unreviewed indefinite retention. Never affect WLD balances or financial permissions.

### Admin, mobile, SEO, a11y, rollout and backlog
- Admin /admin/experience/accessibility shows only aggregated opt-in, errors and regression rates with k>=10 per cohort and differencing-resistant suppression; never member settings/mutations. ## v2026.10.24.1204 — F675-108 Member Accessibility Reading & Interaction Preferences (FEATURE ADDITION / DRAFT / RELEASE HOLD)

### Cycle, source of truth and verified gaps
- Previous: Feature Improvement v1202; Current: Feature Addition v1204; Next: Feature Improvement v1206 (provisional). Start/mid canonical main 54d5140ddb49dd3e2193a2f82b71a6a0a57cfede, canonical EN/KO SHA256 unchanged. QA v1205 FAILED/HOLD; no code, DB, Test, Production or WLD changes.
- Source-backed gap: frontend/src/app/account/page.tsx links profile, security, notifications and privacy but no accessibility-preference screen. frontend/src/lib/theme.ts already owns browser-local theme/point choices, and globals.css/cosmetics.css already respect OS prefers-reduced-motion; do NOT reimplement or override those contracts. frontend/src/app/layout.tsx permits browser zoom (maximumScale 5). The account security page already manages active sessions, so device revocation is explicitly OUT of scope.
- Purpose: give readers, including low-vision, motor and vestibular-sensitive members, an optional accessible way to control text scale, stronger contrast and reduced nonessential motion without affecting virtual balances or accessibility defaults. Target: guests (this browser only), authenticated members (local first, explicit cross-device sync), Android clients (native accessibility precedence), administrators (redacted aggregate health only). No disability inference, diagnosis or profiling.

### Entry, user flow, screen and state contract
- Entry: /account -> /account/accessibility; persistent accessibility shortcut in site utilities and sign-in page; guest /accessibility reachable without sign-in. Controls: Preview, Text 100/125/150/175/200%, Contrast Standard/High, Motion Follow device/Reduce, Save on this device, Sync across my devices (explicit opt-in), Reset, Restore device settings. Existing light/dark/point preferences stay independent and local.
- Preview updates immediately but does not claim server save; status LOCAL_ONLY, SYNC_PENDING, SYNCED(version), CONFLICT, OFFLINE, SAVE_FAILED. Distinguish unsaved preview from saved browser value and confirmed server receipt; error preserves local readable presentation and offers Retry/Keep only here. Never block login, payment review or critical alerts behind the settings page.
- On first visit, OS/browser reduced-motion and forced-colors/contrast are respected. OS Reduce ALWAYS wins over an app preference to allow motion; app may only further reduce. High contrast uses audited semantic palettes rather than arbitrary foreground/background inputs; never disable forced-colors. Text scale is additive to browser zoom and must not cap browser zoom. Existing theme palette and browser zoom remain independently controllable.
- Guests have local-only choices; account switching/logout removes the prior account's synchronized snapshot, without erasing deliberately local-only settings. Cross-device sync is opt-in and off by default. Offline/conflict fallback retains usable local preferences; authenticated server preferences do not silently overwrite explicit local choices. Conflict screen offers Use this device / Use other device with clear timestamps and version.
- Mobile 320px to 1280px, keyboard/screen reader/large text; visible focus, labeled sliders/radio groups, minimum 44 CSS px touch targets where practicable, no color-only statuses, accessible live confirmation, reduced-motion preview without moving animation. Protect login, WLD amount/sign, order-book and admin confirmation layout at 200% text + browser zoom; no truncation of transaction meaning.

### Proposed API, data model and consistency (NOT IMPLEMENTED)
- Member GET /api/v1/account/accessibility-preferences returns only the caller's allowlisted values, version and updatedAt; PUT with If-Match version and Idempotency-Key UUID updates explicit sync preference; DELETE with If-Match resets synchronized values and revokes server sync. Unauthenticated guest uses localStorage only; no guest API. Errors 401 unauthenticated, 403 forbidden, 409 stale version/different replay payload, 422 invalid enum/scale, 429 throttled, 503 unavailable. GET is private/no-store. API must authenticate from server session and consent, never trust userId from request body; mutation CSRF for web and equivalent native authorization.
- Proposed forward-only table member_accessibility_preferences: user_id UUID PRIMARY KEY FK users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE, text_scale_pct SMALLINT CHECK IN (100,125,150,175,200), contrast_mode CHECK IN ('standard','high'), motion_mode CHECK IN ('device','reduce'), sync_enabled BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE, version BIGINT NOT NULL CHECK(version>0), updated_at timestamptz. Separate member_accessibility_preference_receipts with UNIQUE(user_id,idempotency_key), request_digest bytea(32), committed_version, created_at; index on (user_id,updated_at DESC) only if needed by measured read plans. No free-form CSS, arbitrary URLs, device fingerprints or inferred disability attributes.
- One transaction in SECURITY DEFINER function with fixed search_path, session/owner check and row lock/CAS: validate enum/range, compare expected version, bind idempotency key to canonical request digest, update one row, insert receipt and commit atomically. Same key/same digest returns confirmed receipt; same key/different digest -> 409; 100-way stale-version race allows one version advancement, all others stable 409 or confirmed replay. No app direct table INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE and no PUBLIC EXECUTE; negative permission tests must prove boundary on isolated PostgreSQL.
- Guest localStorage key wdmv.accessibility.v1, strict JSON schema/size bound and no scripts or arbitrary CSS; prepaint allowlisted root data attributes to avoid flash. Storage failure is nonfatal. Server preference is never a credential, never used to authorize finance and never logged with account identity. On account deletion, cascade and retention review; audit only redacted state/version/operation, not sensitive inferred traits.

### Admin, SEO, security, analysis and release
- Admin /admin/experience/accessibility shows only aggregate opt-in, save error and regression counters, no per-member settings and no admin mutation of member preferences. Aggregate disclosure threshold >=10; no cross-tenant analytics, fingerprinting or raw values. Metrics: setting reach, local preview success, opt-in sync adoption, confirmed-save rate, conflict rate, WCAG test failure count, 200%-zoom task completion, p95 UI hydration shift; baselines UNKNOWN, target unauthorized disclosure 0.
- Private settings endpoints and member/admin pages: robots noindex/nofollow, no sitemap, Cache-Control private,no-store and no CDN/shared cache. No security claims from CSS alone; use W3C WCAG 2.2 AA contrast 4.5:1 regular text/3:1 large, resize 200%, reflow at 320 CSS px, focus visibility and reduced motion. W3C WAI-Adapt is exploratory guidance, not a claim that the product implements the specification.
- Android app-api parity: native font scale/OS Reduce/forced contrast always take precedence; allow explicit sync without webview localStorage dependency, screen-reader labels and TalkBack regression. No automatic advertising personalization from accessibility preferences.
