- Rollout OFF by default; existing UI unaffected. Block any DB migration while Production and development share 5433 and wildcard binding (#1053/#1060); never run fixtures on Production-connected DB. Before integration: resolve #993 broad app DML/PUBLIC bank EXECUTE, #1067 Next.js Critical2/High1, main lint/frontend failure, migration266 checksum, #1066 legacy backup, isolated restore and exact-SHA official Test authenticated checks. Additive reviewed migrations only; Test exact SHA with 100-way races, owner negatives, screen reader, forced-colors, dark/light, OS motion, 200% zoom and Android; then authorized canary and reversible flag. Rollback turns off server sync and new CSS attributes, preserves safe local OS behavior and durable receipts; no WLD/ledger mutation.

### Prioritized development backlog and acceptance
- P0 ENV1204-01 — #1053/#1060: separate Production/dev/Test identities and close 5433 wildcard network exposure via authorized change; prove off-host deny, authorized API and encrypted backup health, isolated restore.
- P0 SEC1204-02 — #993: revoke unsafe app table DML and PUBLIC bank function EXECUTE with reviewed forward-only security changes; owner-negative real-PG and existing finance regression tests.
- P0 REL1204-03 — #1067/#1066 plus main lint/test, migration266 and exact-SHA CI/Test/backup/rollback release blockers; no release claim before green evidence.
- P0 A11Y1204-04 — establish contrast/reflow/keyboard/OS-precedence baseline for account, wallet, stock order, support and admin-critical flows before rollout; fail closed on inaccessible critical confirmations.
- P1 A11Y1204-05 — guest/member local settings screen, accessible preview, reset, strict localStorage and prepaint safe defaults.
- P1 A11Y1204-06 — owner-only sync API, SECURITY DEFINER schema, version CAS, 100-way idempotency receipts and 409 conflicts; opt-in only.
- P1 A11Y1204-07 — semantic CSS tokens, OS/forced-colors precedence, high-contrast audited palette and 200% resize without changing theme storage ownership.
- P1 A11Y1204-08 — Android App API/native parity and redacted thresholded admin aggregate.
- P1 A11Y1204-09 — CSRF/owner/privacy/SEO/cache/abuse and KPI instrumentation without disability profiling.
- P1 A11Y1204-10 — 100 simultaneous PUT replay/stale conflicts, 100 cross-owner denies, 320px/200%/400% browser zoom, forced-colors, screen-reader, mobile, offline, account-switch, exact-SHA Test and flag rollback regression.
- Ownership: planning docs-only branch stacked after Draft #1065; engineering A/B/C, Gemini and QA branches unchanged. New code requires separate engineering branch and Test exact-SHA gate. 10,000 external independent reference gate remains BLOCKED: reviewed W3C and MDN publisher families (2), newly approved deduplicated evidence 0, cumulative approved UNKNOWN; no invented count.
- Reference guidance (reviewed, NOT registered as approved evidence): https://www.w3.org/TR/WCAG22/ ; https://www.w3.org/WAI/adapt/ ; https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/CSS/Guides/Media_queries/Using_for_accessibility .


## v2026.10.24.1202 — F675-107 Private Data Access Package: Receipt, Isolation and Disclosure Hardening (FEATURE IMPROVEMENT / DRAFT / RELEASE HOLD)

### Cycle and verified evidence
- Previous: Feature Addition v1200. Current: Feature Improvement v1202. Next: Feature Addition v1204 (provisional). Main at start and midpoint: 54d5140ddb49dd3e2193a2f82b71a6a0a57cfede. Read canonical main AGENTS.md, README.md, PROJECT_PLAN.md and Korean comparator, stacked v1200 integrated plans, latest QA v1203 (04:39 KST), GitHub open PRs and actual privacy/controller/repository/migration 015 source.
- Existing privacy/requests GET+POST is an intake and receipt workflow, not a generated data copy. In migration 015, RETURNS TABLE includes created_at while INSERT RETURNING id, created_at is unqualified. Development C Draft PR #1064 independently reproduced SQLSTATE 42702 on isolated PostgreSQL 17.11 and proposes additive migration 280 plus stable form idempotency keys; this work is NOT in main and MUST NOT be duplicated or treated as released. The v1202 export design depends on verified, merged, exact-SHA-tested intake behavior.
- QA v1203: Production API and development backend share 5433/woldeok_moneyverse_dev (#1053); Docker binds the Production-connected PostgreSQL port to 0.0.0.0 and [::] (#1060), but internet reachability is unverified. App role can UPDATE/DELETE 235/241 public tables; three bank SECURITY DEFINER functions permit PUBLIC EXECUTE (#993). Migration 266 checksum mismatch; main lint/frontend test and Next.js security audit fail; Test DB schema differs; full exact-SHA Test and restore unverified. No write-capable testing, export worker or migration on shared 5433.
- Decision: preserve v1200 F675-107 scope, but add an explicit legal/technical release barrier, server-issued request receipts, source-specific disclosure contracts, artifact atomicity, credential fencing and post-release revocation. This is DOCUMENTATION ONLY; no runtime change, actual privacy export, legal approval, DB write or release.

### Product behavior, permissions, states and response truth
- Member entry remains /account/privacy -> /account/privacy/packages; accessible CTA: Request copy, Verify identity, Check status, Review included/excluded categories, Download, Report a problem. Show three distinct milestones: ACCESS_REQUEST_RECEIVED (existing intake), PACKAGE_QUEUED (new job committed), PACKAGE_READY (object verified + authorized release); NEVER show downloaded/fulfilled merely because a request row exists or a URL includes success.
- Intake dependency: on retry reuse a stable per-form UUID for unchanged normalized type/detail, verify server receipt ID, owner, request type, status and idempotency key before displaying success; unknown outcomes show Check request history and safe retry. Different payload under same key -> 409; 42702/500 -> failure, never success. Coordinate with C #1064 and forward-only migration 280; do not cherry-pick its runtime changes into planning.
- Package states: VERIFY_REQUIRED -> QUEUED -> SNAPSHOTTING -> POLICY_REVIEW -> APPROVED -> ENCRYPTING -> READY -> DOWNLOADING -> DOWNLOADED or EXPIRED; terminal REJECTED_WITH_REASON, CANCELLED; recoverable RETRY_PENDING, REVOKED, OBJECT_UNAVAILABLE. APPROVED is NOT READY; READY is invalid if object missing, hash mismatched, review revoked or encryption key disabled. Do not promise download completion if connection breaks after a one-use grant is consumed.
- Owner session + recent step-up (proposed <=10 min) for request and each download; separate privacy.review and privacy.release admin capabilities, distinct approvers for sensitive disclosure, recent admin reauth and CSRF. Account closure/restriction uses legally reviewed alternate rights channel. No default identity-document upload. Show category-by-category inclusion, omission reason, observed-at, expiry, redacted receipt; no private counterparty data in UI, logs, telemetry or manifest.

### Source, API, DB, transaction and concurrency contracts
- Field allowlist contract per source version: own profile; consent/request history; authored public content; own WLD ledger projection as exact decimal strings (not Number); notification preferences. Default-deny DMs, support evidence, OAuth tokens, bank secrets, admin notes, other-member identifiers, deleted/redacted data. Explicit legal-review overrides require case reference and audit; no implicit all-tables dump or SELECT *.
- Proposed APIs (not implemented): POST /api/v1/privacy/access-packages {privacyRequestId, idempotencyKey, requestedCategories, disclosurePolicyVersion}; 202 only after durable owner-scoped job/event/outbox commit. GET /:id owner-only, GET list signed keyset (created_at,id), POST /:id/download-intent with fresh step-up+CSRF, GET /:id/download with atomic single-use grant. Admin /api/v1/admin/privacy/access-packages/:id/review and /release require expectedVersion, reason, reviewer identity and separation-of-duties. Error mapping: 401 unauthenticated, 403 failed authorization, 404 unknown or foreign-owned object indistinguishable, 409 digest/version collision, 410 expired/revoked, 423 review hold, 503 unavailable; no raw object path in any response.
- Additive proposed tables only after migration-tip review: packages UNIQUE(owner_user_id,idempotency_key), request_digest bytea(32), source_policy_version, state_version, owner+privacy_request composite FK; events append-only UNIQUE(package_id,version); artifacts UNIQUE(package_id,object_id) with SHA256, encrypted key version, size and verified_at; grants UNIQUE(grant_hash) with consumed_at, expires_at, session_fingerprint and revoked_at. Index (owner_user_id,created_at DESC,id DESC), partial (state,lease_until) for worker claims and (expires_at) for purge. Enforce owner/request identity inside SECURITY DEFINER fixed search_path, no app direct table DML or PUBLIC EXECUTE.
- Transaction: request+event+outbox atomic; lease/epoch fencing for workers; bounded consistent-snapshot reads with source-level authorization; encrypt and upload privately, verify bytes/hash/manifest/policy version and reviewer decision, then compare-and-swap READY with artifact+event. Crash between object upload and DB CAS -> orphan cleanup, not READY. Re-review/revoke increments version and invalidates all grants before any new byte stream. A download grant is atomically consumed before streaming; retry requires reauth and a new grant. Already delivered bytes cannot be clawed back; UI must not claim recall.
- Export hardening: JSON UTF-8 primary, bounded CSV with formula neutralization, fixed-path ZIP without symlinks/traversal, compressed/uncompressed quotas, anti-zip-bomb checks, no EXIF/secret leakage, object encrypted at rest with versioned KMS keys, HTTPS transport, private/no-store/nosniff/no-referrer; exclude data from browser analytics and shared caches. Set artifact TTL only after legal/retention review; log deletion evidence without claiming backup erasure.

### Operations, mobile, accessibility, SEO, analytics and rollout
- Admin shows redacted counts, source policy version, case SLA age, audit of approve/release/revoke and object verification; not raw package contents by default. Kill switch blocks new jobs and grants while preserving requests and audit. Android app-api mirrors owner/step-up/CSRF-equivalent security and uses OS-private storage. 320px, WCAG 2.2 AA, keyboard/focus and live statuses; noindex/nofollow, no sitemap, private/no-store on API/BFF/edge/object; never log PII.
- KPI: intake receipt correctness, package READY integrity, time-to-review p50/p95, denied cross-owner attempts, stale policy versions, grant replay count, expired object deletion lag, unauthorized disclosures (target zero). Baselines UNKNOWN; legal-response deadlines require privacy officer sign-off, not invented SLAs.
- Rollout OFF by default; environment identity proof host/port/db/user and isolation before any real PG fixture, then reviewed forward-only migrations, owner-negative tests, full security audit, exact-SHA CI, official isolated Test authenticated five-pass, encrypted backup restore, canary and reversible flag. Never modify applied migration 015/266. On rollback disable jobs/grants, revoke capabilities, preserve immutable audit and pending rights cases, reconcile orphans without wallet writes. A/B/C/QA/Gemini code and PR ownership untouched.

### Prioritized engineering backlog and acceptance
- P0 ENV1202-01: #1053 and #1060 separate live/dev DB and close wildcard 5433 exposure with authorized network change; prove off-host deny, authorized API/backup health, and Test identity. No live changes in this cycle.
- P0 PRIV1202-02: integrate C Draft #1064 only after review: isolated PG reproduction of migration 015 SQLSTATE 42702, additive 280 receipt repair, stable idempotency/verified receipt and unchanged-key replay; no duplicate migration.
- P0 SEC1202-03: #993 app role direct DML and PUBLIC bank EXECUTE removal, per-object owner access and privacy.review/release RBAC, isolated real-PG negative tests.
- P0 REL1202-04: QA v1203 Next.js Critical2/High1, lint/blog, migration 266 provenance, exact-SHA Test parity and backup restore; HOLD all releases.
- P1 PRIV1202-05: owner/request composite constraints, versioned disclosure policy, source allowlist and immutable manifest; human review for third-party/legal exceptions.
- P1 PRIV1202-06: lease-fenced bounded export, object+DB READY CAS, corruption/revocation checks and orphan cleanup.
- P1 PRIV1202-07: one-use session-bound download, revoked/stale capability rejection, truthful retry receipt and no-cache semantics.
- P1 PRIV1202-08: member/admin/Android UX, WCAG/320px, SEO privacy, redacted audit and KPI.
- P1 PRIV1202-09: regression gate: 100 same-key parallel submits => one job/receipt; 100 changed-digest replays => 409; 100 cross-owner requests => no data; 100 parallel grant uses => <=1 consume; 10,001 equal-timestamp keyset entries => no loss; missing/corrupt object => zero READY; policy revoke racing download => no NEW stream after revocation; 38-digit WLD strings exact; CSV/ZIP payloads fail closed; restore/flag rollback/owner-negative isolated Test pass.
- External evidence gate: independently reviewed publishers this cycle PostgreSQL documentation, OWASP, EDPB (3 families), newly approved independently classified/deduplicated evidence records 0; cumulative accepted UNKNOWN/10,000 => BLOCKED / IN PROGRESS. Design references: https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/plpgsql-implementation.html ; https://cheatsheetseries.owasp.org/cheatsheets/Authorization_Cheat_Sheet.html ; https://www.edpb.europa.eu/documents/guideline/guidelines-012022-on-data-subject-rights-right-of-access_en . References are guidance, not legal advice or 10,000 approved records.


## v2026.10.24.1200 — F675-107 Verified Private Data Access Package (FEATURE ADDITION / DRAFT / RELEASE HOLD)

### Cycle, authoritative baseline and decision
- Previous Cycle: Feature Improvement v1198. Current Cycle: Feature Addition v1200. Next Cycle: Feature Improvement v1202 (provisional). Main at start/mid: 54d5140ddb49dd3e2193a2f82b71a6a0a57cfede; canonical main AGENTS.md, README.md, docs/planning/PROJECT_PLAN.md (English authority), PROJECT_PLAN.ko.md checked. Planning-only successor of unmerged Draft PR #1052; not an implemented feature, approved legal interpretation, Test PASS or production change.
- Verified code: frontend/src/app/account/privacy/page.tsx lists data-subject requests and permits an access request but does not offer a completed downloadable data copy. backend/src/privacy/privacy.controller.ts has GET/POST privacy/requests, session+authenticated+consent guards and CSRF on POST. backend/src/privacy/privacy.repository.ts supports access/correction/restriction/withdrawal/deletion, with only status received; migration 015 explicitly has no delivery address, export destination, file or automatic account mutation. Existing wallet CSV export is not a personal-data package. Do not present an access-request receipt as a completed export.
- Critical QA correction (v1201, 2026-10-24 03:22 KST): public Production API at port 3343 and development backend port 3000 both connect to 127.0.0.1:5433/woldeok_moneyverse_dev. The name "dev" is NOT a safe environment designation. Official public Test API at 3363 connects separately to 5585/wdmv_v761_test_acl. All writes, migrations, fixtures, privilege experiments and export jobs against the shared 5433 database are forbidden in this planning cycle; #1053 requires approved topology separation before integration testing. The prior "Production DB unverified" description in v1198 must be read in light of this newer evidence: 877 transactions/1,784 postings/0 imbalance were measured read-only on the DB connected to Production API, but authenticated Production finance workflows remain unverified.
- Product decision: reuse the existing privacy access-request intake, then provide an explicitly requested, reviewable and verifiable package containing ONLY the requester's permitted personal data. This is a user-facing access-copy feature, not a claim that all GDPR portability rules automatically apply to a Korean service. Human/privacy officer review decides legally restricted or third-party information; never export private counterparty records, passwords, access tokens, internal administrator notes or raw private support attachments by default. No WLD mutation.

### Purpose, roles, entry, UX and state contract
- Member goal: obtain a comprehensible copy of their own account information without emailing identity documents or sending raw data to an arbitrary address. Entry /account/privacy -> View access requests -> Request private copy -> /account/privacy/packages. The existing privacy request form and its idempotent receipt remain unchanged until reviewed migration/API changes; the package is a separate, explicit action linked to an authenticated owner request of type access.
- Member roles: active authenticated owner with current consent and recent step-up reauthentication (target freshness <=10 minutes) can request/download; inactive/deleted account is routed to the reviewed legal request channel, not silently denied with a fabricated success. Administrator roles privacy.review and privacy.release are distinct; fresh elevated session, CSRF and reason-coded audit required. No admin can bulk-download by using a member's link or approve their own privileged request without dual review for sensitive categories.
- Screens and CTAs: Request copy, Verify identity, Check status, View included categories, Download once, Re-request, Ask for review. States NOT_REQUESTED -> VERIFY_REQUIRED -> QUEUED -> COLLECTING -> REVIEW_REQUIRED -> READY -> DOWNLOADED / EXPIRED; exceptional REJECTED_WITH_REASON, FAILED_RETRYABLE, CANCELLED, UNAVAILABLE. The page distinguishes request received from package ready; only server-signed committed receipt and verified object availability can show READY. No optimistic URL query-parameter success, no invented delivery SLA. Present excluded categories and human appeal/contact route.
- Export categories: basic profile and identity-provider names (no provider tokens); recorded consents and privacy request history; the owner's authored public posts/comments where still available; owner-only WLD ledger posting projections and balances as VIRTUAL WLD strings, excluding counterparties, hidden memos and internal account IDs; own notification preferences where safely scoped. Private messages, support evidence, other users' personal data, moderation intelligence and security secrets require separate itemized privacy/legal review and default exclusion. Include per-category source, observation timestamp, record count, field allowlist and exclusions in manifest. CSV optional only for approved flat data, spreadsheet-formula neutralization mandatory; JSON UTF-8 primary, ZIP with fixed safe paths and size bounds (proposed <=50MiB, subject to real measurements).

### API, data constraints, transaction, object lifecycle
- Proposed GET /api/v1/privacy/access-packages?cursor&limit=1..25: authenticated owner-only list, signed keyset (created_at DESC,id DESC), no-store. POST /api/v1/privacy/access-packages: {privacyRequestId, idempotencyKey UUID, categories enum[]} with CSRF+recent reauth; server resolves owner, request type access, active status, canonical digest; 202 {packageId,state:QUEUED,receiptId,createdAt} only after atomic commit. Same owner+key+digest returns same receipt; changed payload 409. GET /api/v1/privacy/access-packages/:id: owner-only status/manifest redacted. POST /api/v1/privacy/access-packages/:id/download-intent: fresh step-up, CSRF and short-lived one-use session-bound download capability (never an open object URL); GET /api/v1/privacy/access-packages/:id/download with server-side consumed capability, no token in query string, Cache-Control private,no-store, Content-Disposition attachment, nosniff. Admin GET /api/v1/admin/privacy/access-packages?state&cursor and POST /:id/review or /release with elevated permission, expectedVersion and reason. Android App API mirrors statuses and authorization; mobile save must use OS private storage.
- Proposed additive forward-only schema (not migrated): privacy_access_packages(id uuid PK, owner_user_id FK users, privacy_request_id FK privacy_data_subject_requests, state enum/check, version bigint>0, request_key uuid, request_digest bytea length 32, categories jsonb constrained allowlist, requested_at, expires_at, reviewed_at, released_at); UNIQUE(owner_user_id,request_key); index (owner_user_id,requested_at DESC,id DESC), (state,requested_at,id); composite owner/request FK or SECURITY DEFINER cross-check so a request from another user cannot be attached. privacy_access_events append-only with UNIQUE(package_id,version) and actor/reason. privacy_access_artifacts(package_id UNIQUE FK, private_object_id UNIQUE, sha256 bytea length32, manifest_hash, byte_length CHECK <=52428800, encryption_key_version, ready_at, expires_at); no public bucket, raw object key or signed object URL in client response. An optional one-use download grant stores a hash and consumed_at; grants expire within minutes and are tied to session + owner + package.
- Transaction boundaries: (1) create job+event+outbox intent atomically under owner/active-account lock and idempotency key; (2) worker claims job by lease/version, collects bounded actor-scoped reads from approved source functions using a consistent read snapshot, never performs wallet writes, and streams encrypted artifact outside a long-held SQL write transaction; (3) verify SHA256, size, manifest, ownership and review, then CAS commit READY + event; object-store upload alone is NOT success; (4) timeout/crash leaves retryable state and orphan cleanup; one-time download grant is consumed atomically before streaming and failure can issue a fresh grant after step-up. TTL proposal: ready artifact 24h, delete object within 48h after expiry, retain minimal event metadata per approved retention policy, never promise physical deletion of legally retained backup copies.
- DB role boundary: no direct app SELECT/INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE on sensitive tables; actor-bound fixed-search_path SECURITY DEFINER functions with least EXECUTE, revoke PUBLIC; privileged reviewer path independently checks capability, session age and version. Do not treat the live shared 5433 DB as disposable. No arbitrary URL, external email recipient, path traversal, ZIP symlink, cross-tenant read, SQL interpolation, CSV injection or unbounded collection. Throttle requests (proposed one active package and <=2 per seven days per owner; exception process for statutory rights), byte/time budget and worker concurrency; limits must not unlawfully block access rights and need legal review.

### Admin, security, SEO, mobile, analytics and acceptance
- Admin privacy queue displays state, age, redacted category counts and reason-coded release decisions, not raw exported contents by default; privileged preview is separately authorized and audited. Incident kill switch disables new generation/download without deleting request/event history; notify affected owners through verified channels only after review.
- Private pages /account/privacy/packages and /admin/privacy/access-packages are noindex/nofollow, absent from sitemap; BFF, API, edge, object responses no-store, Referrer-Policy no-referrer, no analytics payloads with PII. Accessible at 320px, keyboard, visible focus, labelled progress/status live region, safe failure messages, WCAG 2.2 AA; Android uses the same owner/step-up semantics, does not trust spoofable client headers.
- KPI: valid request -> committed receipt, median/p95 queue and review age, approved-category coverage, download completion, artifact expiry/delete lag, false READY count target 0, cross-owner disclosures target 0, failed step-up, staff overrides, byte budget; baselines UNKNOWN until measured. Do not optimize for download count at expense of privacy.
- Regression acceptance: 100 concurrent same-key submits -> exactly one package/event/outbox and one receipt; 100 same-key different digests -> 409; 100 other-owner package/request/download attempts -> no disclosure; stale/revoked sessions and expired reauth -> 401/403; one-use grant consumed at most once under 100 concurrent downloads; 10,001 same-timestamp jobs paginate without omissions; crash before artifact CAS never READY; corruption/missing object never READY; 38-digit WLD strings preserved without floating-point conversion; CSV cells beginning =,+,-,@ or control-prefix cannot execute as formulas; ZIP bomb/path traversal and >=50MiB fail closed; legal exclusion/appeal and data retention/expiry verified; Android and WCAG/SEO/no-store tests; isolated encrypted-backup restore and exact-SHA five-pass Test with synthetic accounts only after #1053 environment separation.
- Deployment: OFF by default; do not deploy runtime from planning. Development A owns wallet data projection and receipts; B owns admin privilege and ACL, C owns account UI where assigned; QA owns environment topology and exact-SHA gate; Gemini and existing PRs untouched. New code requires independent feature branch, reviewed migration after current tip (never rewrite 266), Test database isolation verified by hostname/port/db/user/credential fingerprint, CI security/lint/test/audit, encrypted restore drill, canary + feature flag + rollback proof. On rollback stop jobs/downloads, retain immutable requests/audit, expire objects safely, no WLD changes. P0 #1053, #993, Next.js critical/high, lint/frontend test, migration 266 and backup/release identity remain BLOCKED.

### P0/P1 engineering backlog and external evidence gate
- P0 ENV1200-01: #1053 prove Production/Test/dev DB topology and isolate all write-capable fixtures; verify credentials, port, actual backend runtime and immutable connection identity before any integration test.
- P0 SEC1200-02: #993 revoke broad app DML and PUBLIC privileged EXECUTE via reviewed forward-only migration; negative real-PostgreSQL tests in isolated Test.
- P0 PRIV1200-03: design per-field owner/third-party disclosure matrix, recent step-up and human exception review; zero unauthorized raw export or misleading READY.
- P0 REL1200-04: unblock current main lint, frontend test, Next.js 16.3.8 Critical 2/High 1, migration266 checksum, backup restore, exact-SHA Test/Production gates; no promotion while failing.
- P1 PRIV1200-05: additive package/event/artifact/grant schema, immutable audit, UNIQUE/partial indexes and owner-scoped SECURITY DEFINER API.
- P1 PRIV1200-06: bounded snapshot worker, encrypted object store, SHA256 manifest, TTL deletion, fail-closed CAS and durable receipts.
- P1 PRIV1200-07: member privacy center, admin release review, Android App API, 320px/WCAG 2.2 AA, private/noindex/no-store UX.
- P1 PRIV1200-08: 100-way concurrency, 10,001 keyset, legal exclusion, CSV/ZIP abuse, step-up, restore, staged flag rollback regression.
- External independent references reviewed: Korean PIPC privacy access portal, KLRI Korean Personal Information Protection Act, EDPB access/portability guidance, OWASP Cheat Sheets (4 publisher families, URLs below). These are comparative design sources, NOT four accepted independently deduplicated evidence records. Newly approved = 0; cumulative verified approved = UNKNOWN; 10,000 gate = BLOCKED / IN PROGRESS. https://www.privacy.go.kr/front/wcp/dcl/per/persnalInfo.do ; https://elaw.klri.re.kr/eng_service/lawTwoView.do?hseq=62389 ; https://www.edpb.europa.eu/system/files/2023-04/edpb_guidelines_202201_data_subject_rights_access_v2_en.pdf ; https://cheatsheetseries.owasp.org/


## v2026.10.24.1198 — F675-106 Wallet Dispute Evidence and Review Integrity (FEATURE IMPROVEMENT / DRAFT / RELEASE HOLD)

### Cycle, source authority and boundaries
- **Previous:** Feature Addition v1196; **Current:** Feature Improvement v1198; **Next:** Feature Addition v1200 (provisional). Start/mid origin/main 54d5140ddb49dd3e2193a2f82b71a6a0a57cfede; reviewed AGENTS.md, README.md, canonical PROJECT_PLAN.md and Korean counterpart. Latest independent QA v1199 (2026-10-24 02:25 KST) FAILED/HOLD. This is a planning-only child of unmerged Draft #1048 (which itself depends on #1043), not an implementation, financial incident, or Test certification.
- **Verified implementation:** backend/src/wallet/wallet.repository.ts recentTransactionsForUser reads owner-owned USER_CASH/USER_BANK postings, groups by ledger transaction, orders (created_at DESC,id DESC) and limits to 50; frontend/src/app/wallet/activity/page.tsx renders the same 50 and no dispute action. backend/src/wallet/wallet.controller.ts exposes GET wallet, not a dispute endpoint. backend/src/support/support.repository.ts uses support_create_thread, support_add_message and admin_support_set_status with generic threads. App API proxy exists. QA v1199 DEV read-only ledger 877 transactions/1,784 postings and 0 imbalance, but **Production ledger and authenticated exact-SHA user flows are unverified**. Draft #1049 proposes a private receipt for the *latest 50*; it is unmerged, so older-transaction eligibility must not be silently excluded or treated as implemented. No new dispute schema is claimed deployed.
- **Risk decision:** member-facing dispute creation must re-verify committed ledger ownership inside the database transaction, not rely on a UI receipt, notification, transaction actor, browser-supplied WLD amount, or a recent-50 list. A member with postings on an older transaction may still be eligible through a bounded, separately authorized lookup. A zero-net cash↔bank movement may still be disputed; the signed cash/bank components and ledger posting proof, not net alone, establish the receipt. Never change WLD in the dispute path.

### Hardened product and API contracts
- **Entry and states:** /wallet/activity and a future owner-scoped transaction history or receipt (Draft #1049 is a dependency, not a release assumption) → Review transaction → /wallet/disputes/new?transactionId=UUID. If the transaction is outside the recent-50 window, show “Not in recent activity” and a secure lookup/history path; never show “transaction not found” solely because of the window. Member CTAs: Submit dispute, View receipt, Add information, Request re-review. Operator: /admin/support/transaction-disputes, Assign, Request information, Record decision, View redacted audit. UI states DRAFT, SUBMITTING, SUBMITTED, TRIAGED, NEEDS_MEMBER_INFO, UNDER_REVIEW, RESOLVED_NO_CHANGE, RESOLVED_CORRECTION_REFERENCED, REJECTED, CLOSED, UNAVAILABLE and RETRY_UNKNOWN. A timeout is not proof of failure or success: query the receipt by the same idempotency key.
- **Owner lookup:** GET /api/v1/wallet/transactions/:transactionId/dispute-eligibility checks current authenticated actor against at least one committed USER_CASH/USER_BANK posting via accounts.owner_user_id, regardless of recent-50 position. 404 for missing/other owner, uniform no-store/error timing where feasible; 503 for source unavailable. Response is minimal {transactionId,eligible,existingOpenDisputeId?,receiptAvailable,observedAt}; do not expose counterparty IDs, private memo or internal ledger metadata. A new bounded history cursor may be proposed separately after A/#1049 ownership review; no unbounded transaction scan.
- **Submission:** POST /api/v1/wallet/disputes requires authenticated session, consent, CSRF and UUID idempotency key; body includes transactionId, category and normalized summary only. Atomic function recomputes posting ownership and stores canonical request digest. Same actor/key/digest returns the same committed receipt (200); same key/different digest is 409; concurrent second open dispute for same owner+transaction is 409 or an explicitly owner-scoped existing-case redirect, never another case. Non-owner and nonexistent transaction both 404. Return 201 only after dispute+support thread+first event+outbox intent commit; unknown commit outcome returns RETRY_UNKNOWN with safe same-key receipt lookup. No debit/credit/freeze/refund/reversal.
- **Review:** POST /api/v1/admin/wallet/disputes/:id/decisions requires elevated support.dispute.resolve, recent reauthentication, CSRF, mandatory reasonCode, expectedVersion and idempotencyKey. Under row lock verify state/version, write immutable decision event and version once. Staff can never approve their own disputed transaction or directly invoke financial posting through this API. A correction reference is accepted only if a separately authorized finance decision and committed, balanced ledger transaction are independently proven; allow multiple affected owners to reference the same correction, but disclose only their own posting projection. F675-104 owns re-review lifecycle; F675-105 owns private evidence; no second escalation or public image store.

### Data, security, operations and acceptance
- **Schema contract (additive, forward-only):** retain v1196 wallet_disputes, wallet_dispute_events, wallet_dispute_resolution and owner-scoped partial unique open-case index. Store immutable ledger transaction UUID, owner, support thread, request digest, decision reason, event version, timestamps and receipt ID. Add an owner-scoped receipt lookup index (owner_user_id,request_key), history keyset (owner_user_id,created_at DESC,id DESC) and reviewer queue (state,updated_at,id). Do not use a global UNIQUE on ledger_transaction_id or correction_transaction_id. Re-review must lock the existing case, increment version and preserve history rather than create a competing open case. Prevent orphan support threads/outbox events on rollback; outbox dispatch is an intent, not evidence of delivery. DB app role gets EXECUTE on actor-bound fixed-search_path SECURITY DEFINER functions only, no broad finance/support DML or PUBLIC EXECUTE.
- **Cross-agent dependencies:** A wallet receipts #1049 and P2P replay #1025; B elevated-admin and finance grants (#1050), C onboarding/status changes (#1051/#1046), QA #1199, Gemini and F675-104/105 Drafts remain separate owners. Do not modify their runtime files or assume their Draft PRs merged. QA v1199 measured app UPDATE/DELETE grants on 235/241 DEV tables, three bank functions with PUBLIC EXECUTE, Next.js 16.3.8 audit 2 Critical/1 High, Migration 266 checksum mismatch, main lint failure and one frontend assertion failure. No production exploitability or ledger incident has been proven. Release remains HOLD until reviewed forward-only privileges, migration reconciliation, dependency patch, complete CI, backup restore and authenticated official Test exact-SHA gate.
- **Privacy, mobile, SEO, analytics:** member/admin routes and receipt endpoints noindex/nofollow, private/no-store at backend, Next BFF, App API and edge; stable 404 without identity leak. Android parity for authorization, CSRF/session binding, offline retry with same key, minimum version and 409/503 messaging. Keyboard, 320px layout, status live region and WCAG 2.2 AA. Audit privileged reads, exports and decisions without logging raw WLD memo, counterparty, evidence URLs or member identifiers in analytics. Track real eligible→submit conversion, first-response p95, unresolved age, idempotent replay count, correction-reference verification failures, non-owner denial count and false-success rate (target 0); targets for latency require measured baseline.
- **Regression matrix:** (1) 100 same-key simultaneous submits → one case/thread/event/outbox and one receipt; (2) 100 same-key different payloads → 409; (3) 100 cross-owner IDs including shared P2P transaction → only owner projections, no counterparty leak; (4) 51st/older real owner transaction eligible, while Draft #1049 recent-50 receipt boundary remains truthful; (5) internal cash↔bank transfer with net 0 retains exact signed components, 38-digit amount strings never float; (6) 100 concurrent reviewer updates → version conflicts, no lost decisions; (7) 10,001 same-timestamp events keyset complete; (8) crash between support/case/event/outbox rolls all back; (9) timed-out submit followed by same-key retry returns existing receipt; (10) unrelated/unbalanced/unapproved correction rejected; (11) app-role direct DML, PUBLIC EXECUTE, cross-owner admin and cache negative tests; (12) Android/web/a11y/SEO, encrypted backup isolated restore, rollback and 5-pass authenticated exact-SHA Test.
- **Rollout and rollback:** flag wallet_transaction_disputes OFF. Additive schema + functions behind reviewed privilege migration, shadow-read owner proof on restored Test with synthetic users, exact-SHA CI and authenticated Test/QA sign-off, allowlisted canary and reversible flag. On failure disable new submissions and privileged decisions, preserve cases/events/support evidence and read-only receipts; never rewrite applied Migration 266, delete financial history or post compensating WLD automatically. **No runtime, DB, Test, Production or flag change in this planning cycle.**

### P0/P1 engineering backlog and evidence gate
- **P0 WAL1198-01:** prove committed posting ownership beyond recent-50 and preserve signed cash/bank receipt truth; coordinate with #1049; tests older owner/foreign owner/net-zero/38-digit.
- **P0 WAL1198-02:** isolate P2P #1025 replay metadata/notification from ledger proof and reject uncommitted evidence; 100 retries no duplicate side effects.
- **P0 SEC1198-03:** forward-only app-role least privilege, no PUBLIC bank EXECUTE without reviewed actor checks, elevated reviewer session/CSRF/reauth; real-PG negative tests.
- **P0 REL1198-04:** QA v1199 lint/frontend/audit/migration266/branch preservation/backup restore/exact-SHA CI+Test release gates; no Production promotion.
- **P1 WAL1198-05:** atomic owner-bound case+thread+event+outbox with canonical digest, same-key replay, open-case uniqueness, versioned re-review.
- **P1 WAL1198-06:** verified separately approved correction reference and immutable decision, no balance-changing support API.
- **P1 WAL1198-07:** older-history discovery, truthful receipt CTA/states, Android App API, noindex/no-store, WCAG/mobile.
- **P1 WAL1198-08:** F675-104 re-review, F675-105 evidence linkage, reviewer audit/retention/legal hold without data leakage.
- **P1 WAL1198-09:** rate limits, privacy-safe KPI and operator workload/aging indicators, zero false-success.
- **P1 WAL1198-10:** 12-scenario concurrency/security/rollback matrix, exact-SHA 5-pass Test, staged canary and rollback proof.
- **Independent reference gate:** OWASP API1 BOLA, PostgreSQL 17 explicit locking, W3C WCAG 2.2 status messages reviewed (3 publishers, **0 newly approved records**, cumulative independently deduplicated approved count **UNKNOWN**, target **10,000**, gate **BLOCKED / IN PROGRESS**). URLs: https://api-security.owasp.org/editions/2023/en/0xa1-broken-object-level-authorization/ ; https://www.postgresql.org/docs/17/explicit-locking.html ; https://www.w3.org/WAI/WCAG22/Understanding/status-messages.html . Do not claim 10,000 achieved.


## v2026.10.24.1196 — F675-106 Verified Wallet Transaction Disputes & Resolution Receipts (FEATURE ADDITION / DRAFT / RELEASE HOLD)

### Cycle, authority, observed baseline and non-goals
- **Previous:** Feature Improvement v1194. **Current:** Feature Addition v1196. **Next:** Feature Improvement v1198 (provisional). Canonical origin/main at start/mid: `54d5140ddb49dd3e2193a2f82b71a6a0a57cfede`; AGENTS.md, README.md, PROJECT_PLAN.md (English canonical), PROJECT_PLAN.ko.md reviewed. This proposal stacks on unmerged planning Draft PR #1043; it is NOT merged, implemented, tested, deployed or a finding of an actual Production financial incident.
- **Source-verified gap:** `frontend/src/app/wallet/activity/page.tsx` displays at most 50 recent entries from `GET /api/v1/wallet?recent=50`, without a transaction-specific dispute action. `backend/src/wallet/wallet.repository.ts::recentTransactionsForUser` derives transaction IDs and cash/bank deltas from owner-owned postings. `backend/src/support/support.controller.ts` offers general member/admin support threads and messages but no transaction-bound dispute, reviewer decision receipt or finance-specific queue. Android uses `frontend/src/app/app-api/v1/[...path]/route.ts`. The admin support inbox exists at `/admin/support`. DEV read-only inspection: 877 ledger transactions, 1,784 postings, 3 support threads, 6 support messages, **0 tables matching *disput***. `moneyverse_app` has direct UPDATE/DELETE on `account_balances` and support tables; ledger_transactions/postings have SELECT but no UPDATE/DELETE in the inspected DEV snapshot. QA v1197 (2026-10-24 01:25 KST) FAILED/HOLD, and Production ledger is not certified.
- **Important dependency:** GitHub #1025 documents P2P metadata/notification replay after an idempotent ledger posting. The dispute feature MUST use only committed, owner-visible ledger transaction IDs, not notification text, browser-supplied amounts, or unverified P2P metadata. A submitted dispute must NEVER debit, credit, freeze, reverse, refund or change WLD; any eventual correction requires an independently authorized finance workflow with a new balanced transaction and exact-SHA gates. Existing F675-104 case history, F675-105 private evidence, and wallet A/B/C/QA candidates are dependencies, not code owned by this planning cycle.

- **GitHub planning tracking:** Draft PR #1048 (parent Draft #1043), engineering issue #1047. One independently approved correction transaction may resolve multiple affected-member cases; do not impose a global UNIQUE constraint on its reference here. Financial posting uniqueness belongs to the independently authorized ledger boundary.

### Purpose, personas, authorization and navigation
- **Member:** report an unexplained/mismatched WLD transaction, track human review, attach optional private evidence only after F675-105 is safe, and receive a verifiable resolution receipt. Entry from a real `/wallet/activity` row via **Review this transaction** → `/wallet/disputes/new?transactionId=<uuid>` (the ID is only a hint, never proof of ownership). History `/wallet/disputes`; detail `/wallet/disputes/:disputeId`; existing `/support` shows linked case without exposing private counterparty metadata. No guest, deleted account or unrelated member access.
- **Support reviewer:** `/admin/support/transaction-disputes` with scoped `support.dispute.read` and `support.dispute.triage`; a separate elevated `support.dispute.resolve` permission, fresh admin step-up, CSRF and mandatory reason for final decisions. Admins cannot modify ledger balances via dispute APIs; sensitive transaction identity and evidence fields are minimized and audited. A finance correction reference can be attached only after separate finance approval/ledger verification, never minted from the support screen.
- **UX state machine:** client-only DRAFT → SUBMITTED (only after committed server receipt) → TRIAGED / NEEDS_MEMBER_INFO / UNDER_REVIEW → RESOLVED_NO_CHANGE or RESOLVED_CORRECTION_REFERENCED or REJECTED → CLOSED. Invalid transitions return 409; missing or inaccessible IDs return the same non-enumerating 404. Member-facing status distinguishes pending, unavailable, rejected and final; do not promise reimbursement, SLA or a credit before an independently verified ledger receipt. CTA: **Report issue**, **Attach private evidence**, **Send additional details**, **View review history**, **Request re-review** (reuse F675-104, do not create a second escalation engine). Use server-confirmed receipt ID, timestamp and state for success; never URL query-string-only success.

### Proposed API contract (not implemented)
- `GET /api/v1/wallet/transactions/:transactionId/dispute-eligibility`: authenticated, owner-verified transaction from `ledger_postings JOIN accounts`; response `{transactionId, eligible, allowedCategories, existingOpenDisputeId?, observedAt}`, no counterparty identities, no caller-provided amount trust; 404 for non-owner/unknown. Eligibility is advisory and rechecked atomically at create.
- `POST /api/v1/wallet/disputes`: session+consent+CSRF, body `{transactionId, category: missing|incorrect_amount|duplicate|unrecognized|other, summary: 10..2000 chars, idempotencyKey: UUID}`. 201 `{disputeId, supportThreadId, state:'SUBMITTED', version:1, receiptId, createdAt}`; exact same actor+key+canonical request returns 200 same receipt; same key/different request or already-open case for the same actor+transaction returns 409 without leaking any other user's case. No implicit ledger write.
- `GET /api/v1/wallet/disputes?cursor&limit=1..50` and `GET /api/v1/wallet/disputes/:disputeId`: owner-scoped keyset `(created_at DESC,id DESC)`, signed/validated cursor, bounded projection, `Cache-Control: private, no-store`, stable `nextCursor`. `GET /api/v1/admin/wallet/disputes?state&cursor`: elevated role, reason-coded audit and bounded queue; `POST /api/v1/admin/wallet/disputes/:id/decisions` requires `{expectedVersion, action, reasonCode, explanation, idempotencyKey, verifiedCorrectionTransactionId?}`, re-verifies actor, state and correction reference. App API `/app-api/v1/... ` must mirror contract, errors, CSRF/session and minimum-version gate; no header-only Android identity assumption.
- Error taxonomy: 400 invalid input; 401 unauthenticated; 403 insufficient reviewer privilege; 404 missing/not owned; 409 stale version or idempotency conflict; 422 invalid state/correction proof; 429 rate-limited; 503 source ledger/DB unavailable. No raw SQL, owner UUID, counterparty, storage key, internal metadata or finance secrets in responses/logs.

### Proposed database, constraints, transaction and privacy
- Additive forward-only migration after current migration tip, **never rewrite applied migration 266**. `wallet_disputes(id uuid PK, owner_user_id FK users, ledger_transaction_id FK ledger_transactions, support_thread_id FK support_threads UNIQUE, category CHECK, state CHECK, version bigint CHECK(version>0), request_key uuid, request_digest bytea, created_at timestamptz, updated_at timestamptz)`; `UNIQUE(owner_user_id,request_key)`; partial unique `(owner_user_id,ledger_transaction_id) WHERE state IN ('SUBMITTED','TRIAGED','NEEDS_MEMBER_INFO','UNDER_REVIEW')`; index `(owner_user_id,created_at DESC,id DESC)` and `(state,updated_at,id)`. The uniqueness scope is per owner; a counterparty may submit a separate case only for their own postings.
- `wallet_dispute_events(id uuid PK, dispute_id FK, version bigint, actor_user_id FK, action, from_state, to_state, reason_code, occurred_at, request_key uuid, request_digest bytea, UNIQUE(dispute_id,version), UNIQUE(dispute_id,request_key))`; append-only with denied app UPDATE/DELETE. Optional `wallet_dispute_resolution(dispute_id PK FK, correction_transaction_id NULL FK ledger_transactions, verified_by, verified_at, decision_reason)`; if linked, require explicit independently authorized finance decision, balanced ledger proof, and no cross-owner detail disclosure. `support_thread_id` and any F675-105 evidence reference must be checked against the same owner/case.
- Actor-bound `SECURITY DEFINER` functions (restricted EXECUTE, fixed search_path, authenticated actor bound by backend session) check posting ownership using `accounts.owner_user_id = actor` inside the transaction; never authorize solely by `ledger_transactions.actor_user_id` or by knowing a UUID. Create dispute, linked support case, first immutable event and deduplicated outbox receipt in **one database transaction**. On concurrent same-key requests use unique constraint + conflict readback after ownership AND canonical request-digest equality; on same-transaction open-case collision return 409. Admin state transitions `SELECT ... FOR UPDATE`, compare `expectedVersion`, insert one event and increment version in same transaction. Any notification is an outbox intent, not a delivered-message claim. Existing support thread idempotency/atomicity gaps in v1190 must be addressed before wiring the new path.
- No raw posting payload, private notes, support screenshots, identity-provider IDs or recipient names in analytics. Private evidence is F675-105 gated; if not available, text-only disputes remain possible. Set retention and legal-hold policy before release, encrypted backups, audit every privileged view/export, and ensure exports are access-controlled. Nonpublic member/admin pages `noindex,nofollow` and private/no-store on web, BFF, app-api, CDN and download. Abuse controls: per-account/IP burst limits, category/length validation, duplicate-open-case gate, reviewer separation of duties, no auto-refund, no case-ID enumeration.

### Acceptance, analytics, release and rollback
- **P0 WAL1196-01** owner-verified ledger truth, non-enumerating 404, no fabricated submission success; source snapshot must be fresh enough to prove committed transaction. **P0 WAL1196-02** P2P replay/metadata side-effect fix #1025 before relying on P2P notification evidence. **P0 SEC1196-03** revoke DEV/Production app-role direct finance/support DML via separately reviewed forward-only migration, preserve SECURITY DEFINER/actor-bound boundary and admin step-up. **P0 REL1196-04** restore CI/lint/security (2 Critical/1 High), migration266 checksum, branch-preservation evidence, verified backup+isolated restore, exact-SHA authenticated Test and finance promotion HOLD.
- **P1 WAL1196-05** schema, atomic create and immutable receipt/event/outbox; **WAL1196-06** owner/admin APIs, App API parity and version/error contract; **WAL1196-07** member wallet entry/history and admin triage/review UX; **WAL1196-08** separate finance correction reference verification, private evidence and F675-104 re-review integration; **WAL1196-09** privacy, mobile WCAG 2.2 AA, noindex/no-store and KPIs; **WAL1196-10** concurrency, regression, release and rollback drills.
- Required QA: 100 concurrent same-key submissions → 1 case+1 thread+1 event+1 outbox intent; 100 different-payload same-key requests → deterministic 409; 100 non-owner transaction/dispute probes → zero disclosure; 100 concurrent admin version conflicts → no lost update or duplicate resolution; 10,001 same-timestamp cases → keyset no skips/duplicates; exact bigint cash/bank delta parity; replay after response timeout returns same receipt; failure between case/thread/event/outbox rolls back all; correction reference rejects unbalanced, unrelated or unapproved ledger; Android/web/accessibility/security/SEO regression and signed-cursor tampering tests.
- KPI only from real committed receipts: eligible→submitted conversion, p95 create latency, p95 reviewer first-response/resolve time, reopen rate, unauthorized access denials, duplicate suppression count, false success 0, unsupported credit promises 0. Set baseline/targets only after measured data; do not claim current production values.
- Rollout: feature flag `wallet_transaction_disputes` **OFF**, additive migration with audited grants/constraints and dry-run on restored Test, Test exact SHA and authenticated member/admin/Android 5-pass E2E, staged allowlist, zero-downtime deploy and reversible flag disable. Rollback hides entry/halts new writes but preserves dispute records, linked support cases and audit; never roll back applied migration, rewrite historical ledger, delete evidence, or reverse a financial transaction. Production promotion remains BLOCKED until independent QA signs exact SHA and restore/finance gates.
- External independent sources reviewed: OWASP API1:2023 BOLA, PostgreSQL 17 concurrency control, W3C WCAG 2.2 status messages (3 independent publishers; **0 newly approved reference records**; cumulative deduplicated approved **UNKNOWN**; required **10,000** evidence gate **BLOCKED / IN PROGRESS**, never claimed satisfied). References: https://github.com/OWASP/API-Security/blob/master/editions/2023/en/0xa1-broken-object-level-authorization.md ; https://www.postgresql.org/docs/17/mvcc.html ; https://www.w3.org/WAI/WCAG22/Understanding/status-messages.html .

## v2026.10.24.1194 — F675-105 Private Support Evidence Vault: authorization, legacy containment and durable receipts (FEATURE IMPROVEMENT / DRAFT / RELEASE HOLD)

- **Cycle and authority:** Previous Feature Addition v1192; Current Feature Improvement v1194; Next Feature Addition v1196 (provisional). Canonical origin/main 54d5140ddb49dd3e2193a2f82b71a6a0a57cfede, AGENTS.md/README.md/PROJECT_PLAN.md and Korean counterpart rechecked at start and mid-run; predecessor unmerged Draft #1038 and support security issue #1037. QA v1195 (2026-10-24 00:25 KST) FAILED/HOLD. This is a docs-only stacked proposal; no claim of implementation, Test acceptance, public exploitation, or Production leakage.
- **Verified implementation and DEV evidence:** backend/src/content/chat-upload.controller.ts: upload is guarded by Session/Authenticated/Consent/CSRF but returns BOTH url and raw storageKey; GET content/chat/media/:key has no explicit owner/session guard and sets public, max-age=86400, immutable. backend/src/content/chat-image-storage.ts writes the original scanned buffer with mode 0600; the scanner check is not the same as image re-encoding or EXIF removal. frontend/src/components/floating-support-chat-widget.tsx appends the media URL to inquiry/support reply/direct-message text and shows an image-attached toast after upload, before a support message is committed. frontend/src/app/app-api/v1/[...path]/route.ts buffers request.arrayBuffer and has a 15-second upstream timeout; frontend/src/lib/app-gateway.ts forwards Cache-Control. DEV read-only aggregates: 3 support threads, 6 messages, **1 support message containing a legacy /api/v1/content/chat/media/ URL**, support_evidence table absent; moneyverse_app can DELETE support_messages and UPDATE support_threads. These are DEV/source findings, not proof of any Production incident; no raw user message or URL was exported.
- **Design choice and trade-offs:** Preserve the current generic DM/lobby media contract until its consumers and authorization are audited; do not globally disable chat images or blindly revoke every random URL. Private support evidence must use opaque evidenceId + authenticated case-bound download and never expose storageKey or original-content SHA to browsers, Android, URLs, analytics, or error messages. The existing generic upload response returning storageKey is a separate P0 compatibility/security review: eliminate it from the support path immediately when implemented, and remove it from generic responses only after downstream clients are inventoried. Unguessable keys are not authorization.
- **Personas, rights, entry points and UX:** Case owner uses /support or /support/cases/:threadId: Select screenshot → Inspect privacy notice → Upload → Scanning → Ready to send → Send with message → Confirmed attachment receipt → View/revoke eligible unlinked evidence. Support operator at /admin/support uses dedicated support.evidence.read, support.evidence.quarantine, support.evidence.audit permissions with elevated admin session and mandatory reason; generic admin role alone is insufficient. No guest, other member, DM participant or automated bot gets support bytes. UI must distinguish selected/uploaded/scan-pending/READY-but-unlinked/ATTACHED/rejected/quarantined/revoked/expired; never say “attached” until the message+link commit receipt exists. Existing text-only support works when flag OFF; upload alone never changes WLD/ledger.
- **Member API and response contract (proposed, not implemented):** POST /api/v1/support/threads/:threadId/evidence: multipart one PNG/JPEG/WebP, <=8 MiB, CSRF, actor/session, UUID Idempotency-Key, content digest bound to actor+thread+bytes+declared operation; returns 202 with opaque evidenceId/receiptId and state=PENDING when scanning, or 201 with state=READY after durable verified object+DB commit. Never return raw storageKey, storage path, EXIF, full content hash or public media URL. GET .../evidence/:evidenceId/receipt: only case owner/operator, returns state/receipt/version without bytes; POST .../messages accepts 0..3 evidenceIds and an idempotency key, returns immutable messageId/link receipt after a single transaction. GET .../evidence/:evidenceId: per-request owner/operator check, READY+ATTACHED or authorized own READY preview, then stream sanitized bytes; DELETE only own unlinked READY with CAS, not silently delete attached evidence. Operator GET and POST quarantine endpoints require elevated support permission, reason and audit. Error contracts: 400 malformed, 401 unauthenticated, 404 unknown/foreign indistinguishable, 409 replay-fingerprint mismatch or stale version, 413 size, 415 type, 429 quota, 503 scanner/object-store/commit unknown; clients resolve ambiguous retries via receipt GET, not new keys.
- **Download/cache and privacy boundary:** For every private evidence GET/HEAD and error (including 304/206 if ever supported), enforce no shared or browser cache: Cache-Control: no-store, private; Pragma: no-cache for legacy; X-Content-Type-Options: nosniff; Referrer-Policy: no-referrer; X-Robots-Tag: noindex, nofollow, noarchive; Content-Disposition: attachment unless a sanitized raster preview is explicitly authorized; strict CSP for any inline preview. Disable conditional 304 and byte Range/206 initially rather than leak metadata or bypass fresh ownership; HEAD returns no bytes and no unauthorized size, ETag or Last-Modified. The Next BFF must override any upstream public cache header on private support paths, not merely forward it. No query-string bearer tokens, raw object keys or unprotected redirects; download link itself is never proof of permission.
- **DB constraints and access (reviewed forward-only migration candidate):** Retain v1192 support_evidence, support_message_evidence, support_evidence_events concept, add scan_version, normalized_sha256 (internal only), object_generation, scanned_at and monotonically increasing version; DB READY requires nonnull scan_version/object_generation/scanned_at, and no client-supplied owner/object_key. UNIQUE (actor_id,thread_id,idempotency_key,operation_kind) in a private receipt table with stored canonical request digest; same key+same digest returns same receipt, different digest 409. UNIQUE(evidence_id) in message link, composite UNIQUE(thread_id,id) and composite FKs on message/evidence to reject cross-case linking; owner, READY, expiry and <=3 links checked by a SECURITY DEFINER function under sorted row locks. Index (thread_id,created_at DESC,id DESC) and (state,expires_at,id) for owner history and orphan cleanup; use keyset pagination for 10,001+ records. Audit table append-only. Revoke moneyverse_app direct SELECT/INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE/TRUNCATE on support evidence and affected support tables; expose only owner-checked EXECUTE with fixed search_path, no PUBLIC execute. Coordinate with DB developer B; never rewrite applied migration 266.
- **Durability, transactions and scanner:** Filesystem/object storage and PostgreSQL cannot be one ACID transaction: define explicit PENDING → READY or QUARANTINED/REVOKED/DELETED state with an outbox/reconciler, not a fictitious cross-system atomic commit. Persist idempotency reservation and PENDING receipt first, store a quarantined object, scan with bounded CPU/memory/pixels, normalize/re-encode image and strip EXIF/GPS, persist immutable private object generation, then CAS-commit READY with scan attestation. Serve only after BOTH DB READY and matching object generation/normalized digest are verified. If DB commit fails, object remains quarantined and sweeper deletes it after a documented retention window; if object write fails, receipt stays non-READY with retryable failure. Do not hold DB locks during scanner/filesystem/network calls. Attach uses thread lock → sorted evidence locks → ownership/state/expiry/count check → message+links+receipt atomic commit; revoke vs attach resolves via version CAS. Fail closed on scanner timeout, corruption or mismatched object generation; never report false READY.
- **Legacy transition / incident discipline:** Authorized aggregate inventory shows 1 DEV support message with a legacy media URL; Production count, CDN cache population and third-party copies are UNKNOWN. Security/QA must use synthetic Test accounts to verify anonymous/foreign-owner GET/HEAD and edge/CDN behavior without retrieving real member media. Categorize support vs DM/lobby references, map eligible support records to case-bound evidence after owner and retention review, preserve old text and chain of custody. New support UI must stop emitting public media URLs only after private path works; retain generic DM functionality. Purge reachable shared caches and revoke/rotate supported legacy links where feasible, but do not claim previously downloaded copies are recalled. If confirmed Production exposure, follow incident-response and notification procedures, not a docs-only declaration.
- **Gateway, mobile, abuse and accessibility:** Current BFF buffers multipart and has 15s timeout: design bounded streaming or a controlled async upload route with explicit max body 8 MiB per file / 24 MiB per message, concurrent worker cap, backpressure and 202 receipt; do not increase global proxy limits blindly. Android App API must have equivalent actor/thread/CSRF or documented mobile session protection, retry identity, offline resume and content-URI expiry handling; app version compatibility gate. Per-user/IP rate and bytes/day quotas, decompression/pixel limits, secure temp cleanup, no client-provided remote URL. 320px mobile, keyboard file selection/removal, focus on errors, progress/status live regions, alt-text instructions, WCAG 2.2 AA; support/admin pages noindex and absent from sitemap.
- **Admin, observability, KPI:** Operator may review/quarantine only under support-specific RBAC, elevated session and immutable redacted reason; never display raw object_key, EXIF, file content or customer text in general logs/metrics. Retention and legal-hold decisions are explicit, with time-bounded unlinked-object garbage collection and auditable erasure. Metrics: verified upload-to-message attachment rate, scanner failure/fail-closed count, forbidden read attempts (aggregated), legacy support URL migration coverage, orphan recovery p95, same-key dedup rate, stale-client rate and 0 false READY receipts; analytics event fields exclude member IDs and hashes.
- **P0 backlog:** SUP1194-01 inventory/contain legacy support URLs and prevent storageKey/public-URL return on private support path; SEC1194-02 enforce per-request owner/operator authorization plus no-store at backend/BFF/edge for GET/HEAD/error; SEC1194-03 forward-only DB least-privilege and SECURITY DEFINER tests; REL1194-04 unblock QA v1195 lint, critical/high audit, migration266, backup/restore, branch preservation, exact-SHA Test/Production provenance. Owners: security/support developer + DB B + QA; keep A/B/C/Gemini code branches separate.
- **P1 backlog:** SUP1194-05 durable scan/object/receipt state machine and orphan reconciler; SUP1194-06 atomic case attachment and replay fingerprint/CAS; SUP1194-07 bounded streaming/async BFF upload and 15s timeout contract; SUP1194-08 member web/Android/SEO/a11y and truthful CTAs; SUP1194-09 support operator audit, retention, metrics; SUP1194-10 negative, concurrency, cache, scanner, mobile and rollback regression suite. Each ticket must name exact source/migration paths and TDD RED/GREEN before implementation.
- **Acceptance / release and rollback:** 100 anonymous/foreign-member GET+HEAD+Range attempts reveal 0 bytes/metadata; 100 same-key concurrent uploads/attaches yield exactly one verified receipt/link; 100 key reuse with changed bytes yield 409; 100 scanner failures yield 0 READY; 100 revoke/attach races yield no detached-but-readable object; 10,001 owner history rows paginate without gaps/duplicates; 8 MiB/3-file/24 MiB caps, 15s async receipt, Android parity, zero raw storageKey in private response and zero public cache at edge all verified on controlled Test. Keep support_private_evidence OFF by default, stage Test exact-SHA full lint/typecheck/build/audit/real-PG/owner E2E, verify restore drill and migration parity, then controlled canary with monitored error budget; Production remains BLOCKED until independent QA approval. Rollback disables new uploads, preserves authorized read/retention for committed evidence and immutable audit, never drops tables or deletes files blindly; a new code path requires separate developer branch and release promotion.
- **External evidence gate:** Independently inspected OWASP File Upload Cheat Sheet, Mozilla MDN Cache-Control, PostgreSQL constraints docs (3 publishers); 0 newly accepted/validated independent evidence records, cumulative deduplicated accepted total UNKNOWN, required 10,000 BLOCKED. These sources are design references, not 10,000 verified individual records.


## v2026.10.24.1192 — F675-105 Private Support Evidence Vault (FEATURE ADDITION / DRAFT / RELEASE HOLD)
- **Cycle / authority:** Previous Feature Improvement v1190; Current Feature Addition v1192; Next Feature Improvement v1194 (provisional). At start/mid main was `54d5140ddb49dd3e2193a2f82b71a6a0a57cfede`; canonical main AGENTS/README/PROJECT_PLAN EN+KO read, and prior F675-104 proposals in unmerged Draft #1028/#1033 inspected. Latest QA v1193 FAILED/HOLD. This docs-only stacked design does not imply implemented endpoints, actual Production leakage, passing tests or permission to deploy.
- **Problem / verified code:** `frontend/src/components/floating-support-chat-widget.tsx` uploads support images through `/app-api/v1/content/chat/upload`, then concatenates `capturedImg.url` into the plain-text support reply body. `backend/src/content/chat-upload.controller.ts` uses authenticated+consent+CSRF upload and a scanner-backed storage class, but its `GET content/chat/media/:key` has no explicit auth/owner guard and sends `Cache-Control: public, max-age=86400, immutable`. `ChatImageStorage` uses random UUID keys and private filesystem permissions; guessing a key is not the same as obtaining a URL. The Next app-api gateway forwards backend Cache-Control and allows the content group, while next.config rewrites /api/v1/* to /app-api/v1/*; whether the deployed edge actually exposes a given media URL and whether a specific support URL was disclosed still require controlled routing and access-log evidence. No Production exposure is asserted. Existing `/support` page supports only text and `support_messages.body` is a 2,000-character text field; no `support_evidence` table exists in DEV (3 threads, 6 messages). This new feature must reuse the existing scanner and chat, not introduce a parallel arbitrary public upload path.
- **Purpose / personas / boundaries:** Give signed-in case owners and specifically authorized support operators a private, verifiable way to exchange image evidence (screenshots of errors/receipts) without public media URLs, while preserving plain-text support, admin oversight and optional F675-104 case tracking. Scope is support-only image evidence, NOT direct-message/lobby public media, financial proof of WLD movements, generic document sharing, arbitrary URL fetching or a new public image host. User-visible evidence is not an automatic adjudication of a claim. Default `support_private_evidence` OFF; no new WLD/ledger action.
- **UX / entry / CTA / states:** On `/support` and proposed `/support/cases/:id`, show `Attach screenshot`, `Review`, `Remove before send`, `Send with message`, `View evidence`, `Report incorrect evidence`; operator `/admin/support` gets `View authorized evidence`, `Quarantine`, `Record decision`. Accepted formats proposal PNG/JPEG/WebP, max 8 MiB per file, 3 files per message (24 MiB aggregate), no GIF/SVG/PDF initially. State machine `selected -> uploading -> scanning -> ready -> attached`; failures `rejected_type|rejected_size|scan_unavailable|quarantined|upload_failed|link_conflict|expired|revoked|unavailable`. Only a committed server receipt shows `Attached`; an uploaded-but-unlinked file is `ready_pending_send`, not a sent message. Preview never loads a public object URL. Guest and wrong-owner see no file bytes or metadata.
- **Proposed API (not implemented):** Authenticated member `POST /api/v1/support/threads/:threadId/evidence` multipart single file with CSRF, UUID `Idempotency-Key`, request digest and owner-bound thread; response `202 SCANNING` with durable `evidenceId,receiptId` for asynchronous scans or `201 READY` with `evidenceId,receiptId,sha256,size,mimeType,expiresAt` only after safe scan and persisted metadata. `GET .../evidence/:evidenceId/receipt` is pollable; the existing app-api gateway buffers the request and has a 15-second upstream timeout, so bounded body/memory and scan latency require an explicit streaming/async gateway review before implementation. Extend existing `POST /api/v1/support/threads/:threadId/messages` to accept optional `evidenceIds:uuid[]` (0..3), stable UUID key and same thread/owner binding; persist message+attachments+receipt in one DB transaction, and retain the existing text-only contract when flag OFF. `GET /api/v1/support/threads/:threadId/evidence/:evidenceId` streams bytes after fresh owner check, `DELETE .../evidence/:evidenceId` revokes only an unlinked owner upload, and `GET .../evidence/:evidenceId/receipt` resolves uncertain retries without bytes. Admin equivalents `GET /api/v1/admin/support/threads/:threadId/evidence/:evidenceId` and `POST .../quarantine` require support-specific RBAC, elevated session, reason and immutable audit. Next BFF uses server-only internal API credential; Android `/app-api/v1/support/threads/:threadId/evidence/*` must map to identical owner, CSRF/session, MIME and receipt semantics (actual route-map to be reviewed). Errors: 400 invalid, 401 unauthenticated, 403 operator denied, 404 foreign/unknown, 409 key-fingerprint or wrong-thread, 413 size, 415 type, 429 quota, 503 scanner/storage/commit unknown. Never accept a client-supplied owner, filesystem key or remote URL.
- **DB and storage (proposed reviewed forward migration):** `support_evidence(id uuid PK,thread_id uuid NOT NULL FK support_threads(id),uploader_id uuid NOT NULL FK users(id),object_key text UNIQUE NOT NULL,content_sha256 bytea NOT NULL CHECK octet_length=32,mime_type text NOT NULL CHECK IN (image/png,image/jpeg,image/webp),size_bytes bigint CHECK BETWEEN 1 AND 8388608,state text CHECK IN (PENDING,READY,QUARANTINED,REVOKED,DELETED),scan_version text,created_at timestamptz,expires_at timestamptz,version bigint CHECK >0)`. `support_message_evidence(thread_id uuid,message_id uuid,evidence_id uuid,PRIMARY KEY(message_id,evidence_id),UNIQUE(evidence_id))`; add reviewed composite UNIQUE `(thread_id,id)` on both source tables and composite FKs `(thread_id,message_id)` and `(thread_id,evidence_id)` to enforce same-thread binding. Index `support_evidence(thread_id,created_at DESC,id DESC)`, `support_evidence(state,expires_at,id)`; limit 3 attachments/message in locked SECURITY DEFINER function, not an unenforceable CHECK. Evidence audit `support_evidence_events(id PK,evidence_id FK,actor_id,event_type,redacted_reason,created_at)` append-only. Private non-webroot object directory/bucket with encryption and backups; random storage key never in response, DB audit, URLs, analytics or log. No app-role direct table DML/SELECT; only owner-checked function EXECUTE with fixed search_path and support RBAC. Coordinate with DB owner B on existing P0 privilege remediation and migration 266; never edit applied migrations.
- **Atomicity / malware / abuse:** Reuse `AntivirusScannerService` and `ChatImageStorage` scanning primitives, but create a distinct private support storage namespace and authenticated streaming handler. Server validates decoded filename, actual magic bytes, dimensions/pixel cap, decompression ratio, content type and strict allowlist; re-encode image to strip EXIF/GPS and active content, with sandboxed resource limits. Scanner unavailable/timeout => fail closed, never mark READY. Upload bytes to isolated temporary object, scan/re-encode, durably store the private object first, then atomically commit READY metadata and immutable receipt; serve only when BOTH the object exists and the owner-checked DB row is READY. A failure leaves a nonservable orphan cleaned by bounded sweeper, never a false READY receipt. Message attach locks thread then evidence rows sorted by ID, checks owner/thread/READY/expiry/remaining quota, inserts message + mapping + operation receipt atomically; retry same key/fingerprint returns same receipt, different payload 409. Never hold a DB lock across scanner, disk, CDN or network calls. A delete/revoke races safely with attach through row lock/CAS; attached evidence cannot be silently deleted by uploader and requires audited operator retention decision. Enforce per-member/IP upload rate, daily byte quota, content hash abuse detection, bounded concurrency and antivirus alert redaction. No raw support text, image bytes, EXIF, user IDs or object keys in analytics.
- **Legacy URL transition / privacy gate:** Inventory legacy support messages containing `/api/v1/content/chat/media/` URLs using authorized read-only aggregate queries, separate from direct-message/lobby references. Before exposing a private feature, verify external route reachability, existing caches/CDN rules, retention obligations and whether legacy support URLs can be mapped to an owner-scoped proxy; do not blindly break direct-chat media or delete historical evidence. Stop NEW support replies from embedding public media URLs only after compatible private path is verified. If a legacy URL was shared or cached, changing headers alone cannot revoke copies already obtained; coordinate safe invalidation/notice/remediation with privacy owner. Until verified, `legacy_unverified` is displayed, not `private`. Do not claim historical data was secured retroactively.
- **SEO / mobile / accessibility / KPI:** Member/admin evidence and receipts `Cache-Control: private, no-store`, `Content-Disposition: attachment` unless safe raster preview, `X-Content-Type-Options: nosniff`, strict CSP, noindex/nofollow, no sitemap, no referrer leak. 320px responsive, keyboard-operable picker/remove, clear 8MiB/3-file limits, progress + `role=status`, focus on errors, alternative text for screenshots, WCAG 2.2 AA; Android content URI/permission loss, background resume and retry preserve idempotency. KPIs: authorized upload-to-attach rate, scanner-fail-closed count, owner denial, orphan cleanup age p95, revoked-link access attempts, legacy URL inventory coverage, duplicate receipt rate, upload bytes and false-success count; aggregate/redacted only. Initial safety gates: 0 wrong-owner bytes, 0 public cache for protected evidence, 0 unscanned READY and 0 false attached receipts; engagement baselines must be measured, not invented.
- **Tests / deploy / rollback:** RED/GREEN real-PG+HTTP tests: 100 wrong-owner/guest/admin-role requests -> 0 bytes/metadata, 100 same-key concurrent uploads/attaches -> one receipt/object link, 100 key/different-content -> 409, 100 revoke-vs-attach -> no linked deleted bytes, 10,001 same-time evidence events -> cursor no duplicates, 8MiB boundary and 8MiB+1 rejection, polyglot/SVG/GIF, EXIF stripping, decompression bombs, scanner down, orphan sweeper, XSS in filename, range/HEAD/cache bypass, Android/web parity, mobile/a11y, flag-OFF legacy text flow. Test private media through actual edge with anonymous browser, authorized member, foreign member, and shared-cache simulation. Exact-SHA CI/lint/typecheck/build, real-PG negative app-role privilege checks, migration266 provenance, Next security audit, encrypted backup+isolated restore, authenticated Test E2E, deployment artifact SHA and approved GitOps release/rollback required. Flag OFF blocks new uploads and preserves historical support messages/immutable receipts; private objects stay access-controlled and quarantined bytes never become public. Production RELEASE HOLD; planning cannot deploy.
- **Concrete backlog:** P0 `SUP1192-01` inventory and close support public-media path before enabling private evidence; P0 `SEC1192-02` owner/RBAC private read, no-store and app-role least privilege; P0 `REL1192-03` QA v1193 lint/security/migration266/branch archive/exact-SHA Test/restore gates. P1 `SUP1192-04` schema, object namespace, scanner/re-encode and orphan lifecycle; `SUP1192-05` idempotent upload/attach API + receipt; `SUP1192-06` member/admin/Android UI and legacy compatibility; `SUP1192-07` abuse/privacy/SEO/a11y/KPI; `SUP1192-08` real-PG/edge/security/rollback regression. No runtime/DB work belongs to planning branch; implementation by separate A/B/C with QA ownership of gates.
- **External evidence gate:** Reviewed OWASP File Upload Cheat Sheet (https://cheatsheetseries.owasp.org/cheatsheets/File_Upload_Cheat_Sheet.html) and API1 object authorization (https://api-security.owasp.org/editions/2023/en/0xa1-broken-object-level-authorization/) (one publisher: OWASP), Mozilla MDN Cache-Control (https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/HTTP/Reference/Headers/Cache-Control) (one publisher: Mozilla); 2 independent publisher organizations reviewed, 0 newly accepted/deduplicated evidence records; cumulative accepted UNKNOWN; target 10,000 => BLOCKED. These are design references, not 10,000 verified independent citations.

## v2026.10.23.1190 — F675-104 Support Case State/Replay Integrity Hardening (FEATURE IMPROVEMENT / DRAFT / RELEASE HOLD)
- **Cycle and baseline:** Previous Feature Addition v1188; Current Feature Improvement v1190; Next Feature Addition v1192 (provisional). Inspected latest `origin/main` `54d5140ddb49dd3e2193a2f82b71a6a0a57cfede`, predecessor planning Draft PR #1028, open GitHub development issue #1029 and QA v1191 FAILED/HOLD. The v1188 case-tracking proposal remains unmerged. This is a docs-only stacked proposal, not evidence of implementation, Production impact or a reproduced race.
- **New source/DEV evidence (read-only):** `packages/database/migrations/196-user-admin-support-chat.sql` defines `support_threads.status` as `open|waiting_user|resolved`, `support_messages.idempotency_key UNIQUE`, and `support_threads.create_key UNIQUE`. Live DEV has 3 threads/6 messages; app role has direct UPDATE on `support_threads` and DELETE on `support_messages`. `support_create_thread` checks replay owner but not replay subject/initial body; same create key and different payload could return the original thread receipt. `support_add_message` / `admin_support_reply` SELECT dedupe key before INSERT without explicit row lock/`ON CONFLICT`; concurrent retries may surface UNIQUE violations (not proof of duplicate rows). `admin_support_set_status` accepts only target status, has no expected version/idempotency key, and updates even when a member reply changes the same thread. `frontend/src/app/support/actions.ts` and admin actions generate a new UUID on each form submission, not a stable persisted retry token. No verified case timeline API exists.
- **P0 invariant — serialized status and message timeline:** All legacy chat replies, case escalations, triage and status changes must lock the same `support_threads` row (`SELECT ... FOR UPDATE`) before checking actor/owner, current chat status, `case_version` and allowed transition. Lock order: thread -> case metadata -> escalation/receipt -> event -> outbox; no network calls inside transaction. Every successful mutation atomically updates chat status, version, immutable case event and durable receipt. Member reply after resolved reopens to `open` and appends `member_reopened`; admin resolution based on stale version returns HTTP 409 with fresh state and cannot silently overwrite a later reply.
