Admin reply vs member reply also uses versioned transition; a retry of an already committed mutation returns its original receipt, not a second state transition. Legacy routes remain operational through compatible server wrappers during flag-OFF.
- **P0 invariant — idempotency and replay fingerprint:** Keep existing unique keys; do not create duplicate indexes. For thread creation bind `create_key` to actor + normalized subject + initial body digest, and for messages bind `idempotency_key` to actor + thread + sender kind + normalized body digest. Acquire thread lock for existing thread mutations; use unique-key conflict-safe insert/reselect for new threads, where no thread row exists to lock. Same key/same fingerprint -> exactly one persisted thread/message/event/receipt; same key/different fingerprint -> 409 without revealing another member's receipt; concurrent same-key requests -> deterministic replay result rather than raw SQL 23505/500. Normalize Unicode consistently (do not silently mutate original message text); preserve exact body for audit and hash canonical request representation. Browser/Android must retain a UUID across timeout/retry, not generate a new key for each resend. Do not expose request digests or support bodies in logs.
- **P0 privilege and RBAC:** Forward-only reviewed migration by DB/Backend owner B revokes app-role direct UPDATE/DELETE on support tables and ensures restricted EXECUTE on owner/role-checked `SECURITY DEFINER` wrappers with fixed search_path. Admin queue/triage/decision actions require dedicated support permission plus admin session/step-up for sensitive escalation; generic `admin_current_roles` membership alone is not sufficient for new powers. Member case read, event cursor, escalation receipt and Android mirror must enforce thread owner in DB and Nest BFF. Unknown or wrong-owner case -> indistinguishable 404; unauthorized admin -> 403; no cross-tenant events, metadata, or timing-derived details.
- **P1 API and user contract:** Retain v1188 proposed `GET /api/v1/support/cases/:id`, `GET .../events` and `POST .../escalations`, plus admin queue/triage/decision. All new writes require `Idempotency-Key: UUID` and `If-Match: caseVersion`; return `receiptId,caseVersion,chatStatus,triageState,committedAt` from persisted transaction, with `notificationEvidence: pending|accepted|unknown|failed` (provider accepted does not mean delivered). `409` returns current version without private details to unauthorized users; `503` uncertain outcome instructs lookup by same key, never blind replay with new UUID. `GET .../receipts/:key` is owner/admin scoped and never changes state. Old `PUT /admin/support/threads/:id/status` gets a compatibility CAS gate before adding case flag, not an unversioned bypass. Member UI `/support/cases/:id` and admin `/admin/support/cases` show pending/conflict/unavailable distinctly; no success based on URL params or local optimistic state.
- **P1 schema and operations:** Reuse v1188 `support_case_meta` / `support_case_events` / `support_case_escalations` and existing chat tables; `case_version bigint CHECK >0` and `UNIQUE(thread_id,sequence)` enforce ordered immutable timeline. Add `support_case_operation_receipts(request_key uuid PRIMARY KEY, thread_id uuid FK, actor_id uuid, operation text, request_fingerprint bytea, resulting_case_version bigint, receipt_id uuid UNIQUE, committed_at timestamptz)` with immutable row and indexed owner/thread lookup. For status-only operations use same receipt contract. Index `support_case_events(thread_id,sequence DESC)` and `support_case_meta(triage_state,priority,target_at,thread_id)`; enforce no orphan events, no cross-owner escalations, and audit-verified policy version. Outbox notification intent is part of the transaction, external handoff after commit only. No automatic refunds, WLD balance changes, financial metadata leakage, arbitrary attachments, or new third-party messaging providers.
- **Member/admin, privacy and quality:** CTA `Request review` appears only for eligible owned case; disabled reason and server-derived target time are visible, target is not a guarantee. Admin `Assign / Change priority / Resolve / Review escalation` require reason, current version, and second confirmation for sensitive decisions. Case pages and API responses private/no-store/noindex/nofollow, excluded from sitemap/SEO and third-party analytics; 320px responsive and Android app-api parity, keyboard/focus and `aria-live` receipt/error, WCAG 2.2 AA, localized status names. Rate-limit proposed review requests (initial 3/case/7d, subject to real abuse evidence), throttle admin bulk changes, retain immutable redacted audit. KPI: stale-write conflicts, replay mismatches, p50/p95 first verified human reply, reopening, review response, wrong-owner denial, false success and case queue age; no support message bodies in metrics.
- **Acceptance / TDD / rollout:** RED/GREEN real PostgreSQL tests: 100 concurrent same-key thread creates/messages/escalations -> one durable object/receipt each; 100 same-key different-payload -> 409; 100 resolve-vs-member-reply and admin-vs-member-reply races -> monotonic version, ordered events and no lost reply; 100 wrong-owner member/admin attempts -> zero leaks; 10,001 equal-time events -> gap-free sequence cursor; timeout-after-commit returns original receipt; injected 23505 is normalized without duplicate writes; Unicode normalization, CSRF, HTML injection, mobile retry and feature flag rollback. Run full exact-SHA CI, real-PG negative ACL, migration266 provenance, security audit, authenticated Test E2E, Android parity, backup+isolated restore and approval before Production. Feature `support_case_tracking` default OFF; rollout read-only shadow -> limited Test -> canary -> gated Production; rollback OFF preserves existing chat, audit, receipts and case history, no destructive down-migration.
- **Prioritized backlog / ownership:** P0 `SUP1190-01` thread-create replay fingerprint + conflict-safe receipt; P0 `SUP1190-02` serialized chat/status/case version transitions; P0 `SEC1190-03` revoke direct support DML and enforce owner-scoped support RBAC; P0 `REL1190-04` QA v1191, CI/Next audit/migration266/repository 129 missing tips/exact-SHA Test/restore HOLD. P1 `SUP1190-05` operation receipt schema + cursor + notification intent; P1 `SUP1190-06` member/admin/web/Android state/CTA contracts; P1 `SUP1190-07` redacted admin/a11y/SEO/KPI and abuse control; P1 `SUP1190-08` concurrency/negative access/idempotency/rollback regression. Coordinate #1029 with Development B; Dev A wallet/P2P #1025, Dev C blog, Gemini, QA retain separate code ownership; no direct runtime deployment by planning.
- **Evidence gate:** Consulted PostgreSQL row locking <https://www.postgresql.org/docs/17/explicit-locking.html>, PostgreSQL unique constraints <https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/indexes-unique.html> and OWASP API1 authorization <https://api-security.owasp.org/editions/2023/en/0xa1-broken-object-level-authorization/>. Two independent publisher organizations (PostgreSQL Global Development Group, OWASP); newly **accepted** independent evidence records 0, cumulative deduplicated accepted UNKNOWN, target 10,000 **BLOCKED**. References reviewed are not counted as 10,000 verified evidence records.


## v2026.10.23.1188 — F675-104 Support Case Timeline, Triage and Escalation Receipts (FEATURE ADDITION / DRAFT / RELEASE HOLD)
- **Cycle / authority:** Previous Feature Improvement v1186; Current Feature Addition v1188; Next Feature Improvement v1190 (provisional). Investigated `origin/main` `54d5140ddb49dd3e2193a2f82b71a6a0a57cfede` on Debian 13.6. QA v1189 FAILED/HOLD predates this main. Repository integrity incident #1016/#1022: 129 historical unmerged branch tips are not in main; no mass restore, PR reopening, branch pruning or Production promotion is authorized. Docs-only isolated planning branch, no runtime or DB mutation.
- **Source-grounded gap:** Existing `frontend/src/app/support/page.tsx` shows member threads and replies, `frontend/src/app/admin/support/admin-support-view.tsx` manages three chat statuses, `backend/src/support/support.controller.ts` provides session/CSRF-protected chat APIs, and migration `196-user-admin-support-chat.sql` creates `support_threads`, `support_messages` and owner-checking SECURITY DEFINER functions. No case escalation, triage priority, versioned SLA target or immutable member-facing status timeline was found in those interfaces. DEV read-only check: 3 threads, 6 messages, 8 thread columns, 7 message columns, app role currently has UPDATE on support_threads and DELETE on support_messages (global P0 privilege regression). These are DEV observations, not Production incident claims.
- **Purpose / personas / scope:** Let a signed-in member understand *what happened* to a support request, whether a human has acknowledged it, what action is pending, and how to request a review when stalled. Support operator triages, responds and records a reason; authorized supervisor reviews escalations; security auditor sees redacted events. Existing chat remains intact and is the canonical message source. No automatic refund, WLD grant, account sanction, guaranteed response time, autonomous AI resolution, arbitrary file upload or new third-party messaging provider.
- **UX and CTA:** Existing `/support?thread=<uuid>` adds a clearly labeled “Case progress” tab and links to `/support/cases/:id`; user sees case number, creation, last *verified* human reply, current chat status, priority label (informational), published *target* response window (never a promise), event timeline and “Request review” CTA. The button requires a reason, shows eligibility and cooldown, and returns a persisted receipt. States `new|triaged|awaiting_operator|awaiting_member|escalation_requested|under_review|resolved|unavailable`; loading, empty, error, conflict and permission-denied states are distinct. Admin `/admin/support/cases` has priority/age filters, cursor queue, assign/acknowledge/reclassify/resolve controls with mandatory reason and second confirmation for sensitive changes. No fake “resolved” badge based only on query parameters.
- **API proposal (not implemented):** Member `GET /api/v1/support/cases/:id`, `GET /api/v1/support/cases/:id/events?cursor=&limit=20`, `POST /api/v1/support/cases/:id/escalations` with `{reason:1..1000 chars}`, `Idempotency-Key: UUID` and `If-Match: caseVersion`. Admin `GET /api/v1/admin/support/cases?state=&priority=&cursor=&limit=`, `PATCH /api/v1/admin/support/cases/:id/triage` with version, assignee, category, priority, reason, `POST /api/v1/admin/support/cases/:id/escalations/:requestId/decision`, and audited `POST .../:id/resolve`. Existing chat APIs remain unchanged. Response includes `caseId, threadId, caseVersion, chatStatus, triageState, priority, nextActor, targetAt?, targetPolicyVersion?, eventsCursor, receiptId?`. Errors: 400 validation, 401 guest, 403 admin-role refusal, 404 other-member case, 409 version/idempotency mismatch, 429 rate limit, 503 unknown write outcome with safe receipt lookup; no PII in errors. Next server BFF derives actor from session, CSRF required on writes. Android `/app-api/v1/support/cases/*` mirrors the owner-bound semantics after route-map/contract review, without trusting user_id in payload.
- **DB / indexes / constraints:** Reuse `support_threads(id,user_id,status,last_message_at)` and `support_messages(thread_id,...)`, never duplicate their messages or replace the three existing chat states. Proposed `support_case_meta(thread_id uuid PRIMARY KEY REFERENCES support_threads(id), category CHECK, priority CHECK('normal','high','urgent'), triage_state CHECK, assigned_admin_id uuid NULL, policy_version integer NOT NULL, version bigint NOT NULL DEFAULT 1, target_at timestamptz NULL, updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL)`; `support_case_events(id uuid PK, thread_id FK, sequence bigint, actor_id uuid NULL, actor_kind CHECK, kind CHECK, safe_metadata jsonb, created_at timestamptz, UNIQUE(thread_id,sequence))`; `support_case_escalations(id uuid PK, thread_id FK, requester_id FK, request_key uuid UNIQUE, payload_digest bytea, state CHECK, created_at, decided_at NULL)`; `support_case_policies(version PK, target_minutes integer CHECK >0, business_calendar text, effective_at, approved_by)`. Indexes: `(triage_state,priority,target_at,thread_id)`, `(thread_id,created_at DESC,id DESC)` for events, `(thread_id,state,created_at DESC)` for escalation queue, and `(user_id,last_message_at DESC,id DESC)` reused from migration 196. Prefer policy target 48 business hours as a configurable **proposal**, not a measured SLA.
- **Atomicity / state machine:** Lock the existing thread row, validate actor/role, version and allowed transition, then insert case event, escalation or triage change, increment version and durable notification intent within ONE PostgreSQL transaction. Same idempotency key+same request fingerprint returns the same persisted receipt; same key+different fingerprint returns 409. A concurrent resolve/reply/escalation cannot yield contradictory chat and case states; closing triage requires chat status resolved and a final reason, while a member reply reopens chat and records a new case event. Never hold DB locks across external delivery. If notification evidence is unavailable, record case transition but show notification `unknown` rather than `sent`; no money or ledger mutation.
- **Security / abuse / privacy:** Use reviewed `SECURITY DEFINER` functions with fixed `search_path`, actor identity derived from server auth, owner check inside DB and explicit admin role/step-up checks; no direct app-role INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE grants. Case IDs are unguessable UUIDs but not authorization. Member escalation proposal: one active escalation per case, 48-hour cooldown after a decision, and rate limit 5 requests/member/day, subject to QA tuning. Reject HTML/script rendering, external URL fetches and unsafe attachments; plain text only. Keep staff notes/other-member identities out of member timeline and analytics. Immutable redacted audit, retention/legal-hold policy to be approved before implementation, deletion requests handled according to existing account lifecycle policy; no irreversible schema drop.
- **SEO / mobile / accessibility:** All support, case, event and admin pages `noindex,nofollow`, no sitemap, `Cache-Control: private,no-store`, no private OpenGraph/JSON-LD payload. At 320px and Android small screens show readable case progress; keyboard reachable timeline and CTA, visible focus, `aria-live` for receipt/error, WCAG 2.2 AA and localization parity; avoid leaking case identifiers through third-party analytics/referrers. Browser and Android cannot change support case ownership.
- **KPI / operations:** Aggregate median/p95 time-to-first-human-response, open age, escalations per 100 cases, overdue *target* count, reopened cases, member satisfaction opt-in, wrong-owner denials, idempotency conflicts, false-success count and queue age. Admin sees aggregates and minimum necessary case details under RBAC; no raw message bodies, personal IDs or WLD balances in metrics. Security events go to audited channels, never to public SEO pages.
- **Tests / regression / rollout:** 100 concurrent same-key escalations => exactly one escalation/event/receipt; 100 same-key different-payload requests => 409; 100 wrong-owner reads/writes => no data; 100 admin/non-admin role negatives; 100 resolve-vs-member-reply races => coherent version and chat state; 10,001 equal-timestamp events => gap-free stable cursor; verify DST/business calendar, XSS payload, HTML escaping, CSRF, malformed UUID, missing operator, empty queue, delayed reply, timeouts, rollback, and Android/web contract parity. Feature flag `support_case_tracking` default OFF, additive forward-only migrations owned by DB developer B, no backfill until audited; shadow-read existing support chat, then limited Test, canary, and gradual opt-in after exact-SHA CI, real-PG negative grants, migration 266 provenance, backup+isolated restore, authenticated Test E2E, PR review and GitOps approval. Flag OFF disables new triage/escalations without hiding existing chat or deleting case events; rollback is non-destructive.
- **P0/P1 backlog:** P0 `SUP1188-01` owner-bound case read and truthful receipt; P0 `SEC1188-02` direct app-role DML revocation + negative tests (coordinate B and #828/#993); P0 `REL1188-03` preserve repository branch evidence #1016/#1022 and Next audit/CI/migration266/backup/exact-SHA HOLD; P1 `SUP1188-04` versioned case metadata/event/triage migration; P1 `SUP1188-05` atomic escalation + idempotency + policy targets; P1 `SUP1188-06` member/admin UI and mobile app-api; P1 `SUP1188-07` privacy/a11y/SEO/admin analytics; P1 `SUP1188-08` concurrency/negative ACL/cursor/DST/rollback QA. Development A wallet/P2P (#1025), B DB/CI, C blog, Gemini and QA own their code; this branch is docs only.
- **Independent references gate:** Reviewed OWASP API1 object authorization (<https://api-security.owasp.org/editions/2023/en/0xa1-broken-object-level-authorization/>), PostgreSQL transaction locking (<https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/explicit-locking.html>), and W3C WCAG 2.2 (<https://www.w3.org/TR/WCAG22/>): 3 independent publisher organizations, **0 newly admitted verified records**, cumulative deduplicated accepted count UNKNOWN, target 10,000 BLOCKED. No claim of passing the gate.

## v2026.10.23.1186 — F675-103 Verified Digest Dispatch Hardening (FEATURE IMPROVEMENT / DRAFT / HOLD)
- **Cycle / authority:** Previous Feature Addition v1184; Current Feature Improvement v1186; Next Feature Addition v1188 (provisional). This supplements, does not replace, F675-103 v1184. Latest main at investigation: `1a31a6f2df2b75a0accceef12662dc42d9ab2d45`; predecessor Draft PR #1014 `aed8d058`; QA v1187 FAILED/HOLD predates latest main. Planning-only stacked branch; no code, DB, balance, switch, Test or Production mutation.
- **Confirmed contract gaps:** Development `in_app_notifications.id` is UUID; `dedupe_key` has a partial UNIQUE index, but `category` is free text with no proven category→consent-purpose mapping. `notification_preferences` has `state DEFAULT 'OPTED_IN'`, nullable `consented_at`, and default `source_surface='WEB_SETTINGS'`: these defaults are NOT evidence of voluntary consent. `outbox_events` has UUID PK and retry metadata but NO UNIQUE digest idempotency key. Existing Nest notification list sorts `created_at DESC` with OFFSET and no ID tie-breaker; no digest API is implemented. Do not assert a Production incident or duplicate send from these structural findings.
- **P0 fail-closed consent & eligibility:** No optional digest external handoff unless current authenticated owner has an explicit `OPTED_IN` record for each eligible purpose AND channel, non-null verifiable `consented_at`, trusted immutable consent audit/source and policy version, no revocation, and active digest setting; DB defaults and client-supplied `source_surface` never establish consent. A reviewed, versioned `category→purpose_code` mapping and source-event verification are prerequisites; unknown category, missing event provenance, private/financially unverified amount, or ambiguous mandatory-vs-optional classification => EXCLUDE and audit redacted reason. Mandatory account/security/transaction alerts remain immediate, never deferred or silenced. Reuse existing notification preference PK; change dangerous default only through reviewed forward migration owned by F675-102, not a conflicting planning migration.
- **P0 unique dispatch invariant:** Existing `outbox_events` has no event-deduplication constraint; `notification_digest_windows.UNIQUE(user_id,channel,frequency,window_start_utc,window_end_utc)` prevents duplicate windows but does not alone prevent duplicate outbox intents. Propose `notification_digest_windows.outbox_event_id uuid UNIQUE REFERENCES outbox_events(id)` plus `notification_digest_dispatch_keys(digest_id uuid PRIMARY KEY REFERENCES notification_digest_windows(id), request_fingerprint bytea NOT NULL, outbox_event_id uuid UNIQUE NOT NULL REFERENCES outbox_events(id), created_at timestamptz NOT NULL)`, written atomically under one window row lock. Deterministic key covers user, channel, UTC closed-open window, purpose-policy version, settings version; same key+same fingerprint returns same receipt, same key+different fingerprint 409; existing notification `dedupe_key` is not a substitute. Do not add a global UNIQUE constraint to generic outbox without auditing other publishers.
- **P0 revocation vs in-flight provider handoff:** Do not promise cancellation of a request already accepted by a third-party provider. Persist per-user/channel dispatch generation and durable `CLAIMED→HANDOFF_STARTED→PROVIDER_ACCEPTED|UNKNOWN|FAILED` attempt records. Revoke immediately blocks new claims and new handoffs, marks an in-flight attempt `REVOCATION_PENDING` and shows honest pending status until acknowledged/reconciled; `revocation_effective_at` is committed only after active handoffs are drained/fenced. No DB lock is held across network provider calls. Recheck generation/consent/quiet hours just before handoff, reject stale leases, and do not blindly retry `UNKNOWN` acknowledgements. Provider acceptance ≠ delivery/read. A flag-OFF or rollback cancels new optional claims but preserves opt-outs, pending outcomes and immutable audit.
- **P1 API & owner/role boundaries:** Extend proposed owner-only `GET/PUT /api/v1/notifications/digest/preferences`, `POST .../preview`, `GET .../history` and admin retry contracts with `settingsVersion`, `consentPolicyVersion`, `dispatchGeneration`, `revocationStatus: NONE|PENDING|EFFECTIVE`, `receiptId` and `deliveryEvidence: NONE|ACCEPTED|UNKNOWN`. `If-Match` + UUID `Idempotency-Key` are mandatory for writes; stale versions and key/payload conflicts return 409, uncertain writes return 503 plus queryable receipt, wrong owner returns 404, unauthenticated 401. Server derives actor from session/app token, never from request body; admin retry requires RBAC, step-up, reason, immutable audit, and must not override consent. Web BFF and Android App API share the exact semantics; no false success from `?saved=1`.
- **P1 DB & cursor correctness:** Use owner-checked SECURITY DEFINER read/write functions with fixed `search_path`, least-privilege EXECUTE, no direct app-role DML, and tested wrong-owner denial. Link each digest item to `in_app_notifications(user_id,id)` using a reviewed composite UNIQUE/foreign-key strategy or an atomic function-enforced same-owner check, not UUID existence alone. Use `(user_id,created_at DESC,id DESC)` index and opaque signed keyset cursor for 10,001 same-timestamp history rows; no OFFSET drift. Keep scheduler `(status,scheduled_at,id)` index and bounded `SKIP LOCKED` claims. Do not copy private notification body into provider metadata, analytics or admin logs.
- **P1 deterministic schedule & UI:** Compute local DAILY/WEEKLY intervals using validated IANA timezone and closed-open UTC windows. DST missing hour shifts to first valid local instant; repeated hour selects first occurrence once; setting/timezone version change cancels stale queued windows without retroactive reclassification. Quiet-hours deferral moves optional send to first permitted instant, never mandatory alerts. Member screens `/account/notifications/digest` and `.../history` show `disabled|enabled|paused|revocation_pending|queued|accepted|unknown|failed`, Preview never sends, Save only confirms persisted receipt, Retry only when safe. Mobile >=320px, WCAG 2.2 AA keyboard/focus/status/error, Android 13+ OS permission independent of server consent, private/no-store/noindex/nofollow and sitemap exclusion.
- **P1 observability & abuse:** Redacted admin aggregate by purpose/channel and dispatch status, queue-age p95, verified consent suppression, unknown acknowledgements, dedupe conflicts, opt-out pending age, false-success count, complaints per 1,000 delivered; never publish recipient lists, message bodies or raw device tokens. Rate-limit previews (initial proposal 10/member/hour), retry budget and bulk approval; all limits require QA baseline. Data retention proposal 30 days for diagnostic events pending privacy approval, never silently delete mandatory financial audit evidence.
- **Acceptance and rollout:** 100 simultaneous window claims => exactly one dispatch key/outbox event; 100 identical PUT retries => one receipt, 100 conflicting payloads => 409; 100 wrong-owner reads/writes => no data; 100 opt-out vs handoff races => zero NEW handoffs after effective revoke and honest pending for in-flight; 100 unknown provider acknowledgements => no false delivered status or blind duplicate retry; 10,001 equal-timestamp records => zero gaps/duplicates; DST forward/back, timezone change, disabled permission, empty digest, malicious HTML, admin retry denial and flag rollback tests. Exact-SHA CI/full lint/typecheck/build, real-PG privilege negatives and migration 266 provenance, Android parity, authenticated Test E2E, backup+isolated restore and GitOps approval are required before any rollout. Keep `notification_digest` OFF; Production HOLD.
- **Reprioritized backlog:** P0 `NOTIF1186-01` fail-closed purpose/category provenance and explicit consent; P0 `NOTIF1186-02` unique dispatch/outbox key and honest provider acknowledgement; P0 `SEC1186-03` app-role grants/SECURITY DEFINER owner isolation; P0 `REL1186-04` CI/security/Migration266/backup/restore/exact-SHA gates. P1 `NOTIF1186-05` revocation/lease fence; P1 `NOTIF1186-06` keyset history and member/Android contract; P1 `NOTIF1186-07` admin privacy/SEO/a11y/KPI; P1 `NOTIF1186-08` concurrency/DST/rollback regression. Dependency PRs #994/#998/#1005/#1010/#1014 and issue #1015 stay unmerged; Dev A/B/C, Gemini and QA work remains independent.
- **Independent evidence gate:** Reviewed PostgreSQL SELECT/SKIP LOCKED, OWASP API1 object-level authorization and Android permission documentation; 3 publisher organizations reviewed this cycle, 0 newly accepted deduplicated evidence records; cumulative accepted UNKNOWN; 10,000 target BLOCKED. No claim of achieving the gate.


## v2026.10.23.1184 — F675-103 Opt-in Notification Digest & Quiet Delivery (FEATURE ADDITION / DRAFT / HOLD)
- **Cycle and authority:** Previous Feature Improvement v1182; Current Feature Addition v1184; Next Feature Improvement v1186 (provisional). Read-only baseline main `8dc8d7d34fb110db7c3b4702fe310d390165a502`, miniPC Debian 13.6; 27 open PRs; QA v1185 FAILED/HOLD is older than this main and does not certify it. EN canonical, KO semantically equivalent. Docs-only: no runtime, schema, balances, Test or Production changes.
- **Problem and dependencies:** The live source `backend/src/notification/notification.controller.ts` offers member list/unread/read operations; `notification.service.ts` lists `in_app_notifications` with offset pagination and no digest API. Development DB has 0 `in_app_notifications` and 0 `notification_preferences` rows, an existing partial UNIQUE `dedupe_key`, existing `notification_preferences` PK `(user_id,purpose_code,channel)`, and an `outbox_events` delivery-attempt model. This does not establish Production data counts. F675-101 verified inbox (#994/#998) and F675-102 consent/quiet hours (#1005/#1010) are UNMERGED drafts; this feature depends on their reviewed interfaces and must not assert them as implemented.
- **Purpose/personas and scope:** Let consenting members replace bursts of optional activity notices with one factual daily or weekly digest, while preserving immediate security, account, and essential transaction notices. Target: signed-in member, mobile app user, notification operator (aggregated diagnostics), authorized security auditor. Digest is an information presentation only: no WLD movement, no automatic claim, no advertising enrollment, no new email/push provider contract by implication. Default OFF; existing notifications continue their current behavior until opt-in is explicitly saved.
- **UX/entry/CTA/states:** Member `/account/notifications/digest` from notification settings; controls: Enable digest (explicit consent), Daily/Weekly, weekday (weekly only), local delivery hour (default proposal 09:00), IANA timezone, delivery channel, Preview, Save, Pause, Resume, View history. `/account/notifications/digest/history` shows own real receipts with cursor. `/admin/notifications/digests` shows redacted aggregates, failed dispatches and controlled retry. Guest redirects to login without leaking member data. States `disabled|editing|saving|enabled|paused|previewing|preview_ready|empty|queued|sent|failed|unknown`; display success only on server-committed receipt, never from a URL flag or local-only optimistic state. Preview is explicitly non-delivery and never asserts an actual notification or WLD reward. Unknown delivery remains unknown, not success.
- **Content and consent rules:** Digest includes only committed, owner-scoped eligible optional notifications from an exact UTC window; use event IDs, source verification and canonical detail links; omit unverified monetary amounts, fabricated event counts and unreadable source events. Critical security/transaction notices are NEVER held back or made optional by digest settings; their existing mandatory delivery path is unchanged. A member's purpose/channel consent must be `OPTED_IN` with verifiable consent evidence; missing preference, missing evidence, revoked, or unknown means NO optional external delivery. An in-app digest preview may be read without push/email consent but cannot generate a push/email send. Recheck opt-in, channel permission and quiet hours at actual dispatch; withdrawal after provider acceptance cannot be retroactively recalled and must be explained.
- **Proposed API (not implemented):** `GET /api/v1/notifications/digest/preferences` owner-only; `PUT /api/v1/notifications/digest/preferences` authenticated+consent+CSRF, `Idempotency-Key` UUID, `If-Match` version, strict `{enabled,frequency:DAILY|WEEKLY,weekday:1..7,localHour:0..23,timeZone:IANA,channel:IN_APP|PUSH|EMAIL}`; `POST /api/v1/notifications/digest/preview` owner-only, CSRF, non-persistent, rate-limited, max 20 redacted items; `GET /api/v1/notifications/digest/history?cursor=&limit=20` stable owner cursor (max 100); `GET /api/v1/admin/notifications/digests` operator RBAC; `POST /api/v1/admin/notifications/digests/:id/retry` audited operator+step-up+CSRF, only retryable and consent-valid. Next BFF routes forward session and CSRF without exposing `INTERNAL_API_TOKEN`. Android `/app-api/v1/notifications/digest/*` must map to equivalent owner-bound contract with app auth; exact paths are proposals pending route-map/contract review. Errors 400 validation, 401/403 auth, 404 not owned, 409 stale version or reused key/different payload, 429 limits, 503 uncertain; never reveal foreign membership.
- **DB design (proposed forward-only reviewed migration):** `notification_digest_settings(user_id uuid PK FK users, enabled boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT false, frequency text CHECK IN (DAILY,WEEKLY), weekday smallint CHECK 1..7, local_hour smallint CHECK 0..23, time_zone text NOT NULL validated against IANA catalog, channel text CHECK IN (IN_APP,PUSH,EMAIL), version bigint NOT NULL DEFAULT 1 CHECK >0, updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL)`; never duplicate the authoritative purpose/channel consent stored in `notification_preferences`. `notification_digest_windows(id uuid PK,user_id uuid FK,channel text,frequency text,window_start_utc timestamptz,window_end_utc timestamptz,scheduled_at timestamptz,status text CHECK IN (QUEUED,SKIPPED,SENT,FAILED,UNKNOWN,CANCELLED),request_fingerprint bytea,attempts integer CHECK >=0,version bigint,created_at,updated_at, UNIQUE(user_id,channel,frequency,window_start_utc,window_end_utc), CHECK(window_start_utc<window_end_utc))`. `notification_digest_items(digest_id uuid FK,notification_id uuid FK,PRIMARY KEY(digest_id,notification_id))` with function-enforced same-owner invariant; `notification_digest_delivery_events(id uuid PK,digest_id uuid FK,event_kind,redacted_reason,created_at)` append-only. Indices `(user_id,created_at DESC,id DESC)` for history, `(status,scheduled_at,id)` for workers, `(digest_id,created_at,id)` for audit. Do not recreate existing notification `dedupe_key` unique index. No app-role direct DML: reviewed SECURITY DEFINER functions with fixed search_path, actor binding, function EXECUTE allowlist, negative grants/RLS tests.
- **Atomic workflow / schedule:** A scheduled worker claims due windows with `FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED`, verifies owner/purpose/channel and current settings version, computes the closed-open UTC event window, persists immutable eligible notification IDs plus one deduplicated outbox intent in ONE transaction. Use deterministic idempotency key `digest:user:channel:window_start:window_end:settings_version` and request fingerprint; same key/different payload returns 409. Never hold DB locks across provider calls. Dispatch worker rechecks consent/quiet hours immediately before provider handoff; revocation locks/versions the relevant settings row and cancels queued optional sends; already accepted provider sends are recorded as `UNKNOWN/SENT` only with actual provider evidence. Provider retries use bounded exponential backoff and provider idempotency where available; unknown acknowledgement must not be reported as delivered or blindly resent. Recompute next eligible local slot from IANA timezone; DST nonexistent hour -> next valid instant, repeated hour -> first occurrence only, unique UTC window prevents duplicate sends; quiet-hour collision defers to first permitted instant without silently dropping mandatory alerts.
- **Admin/security/abuse:** Admin sees aggregate scheduled/suppressed/failed counts, queue age and hashed correlation IDs, not private bodies or recipient lists by default. Retry needs RBAC, recent step-up, CSRF, reason, immutable audit and 2-person approval for bulk sends; never allow manual bypass of revoked consent. Owner-only DB reads; BOLA/CSRF/replay tests, per-user/IP preview rate limits (initial proposal 10/hour, tunable), scheduler global budget, 20-item preview cap, no arbitrary URLs/HTML, safe internal link allowlist, no PII in logs/analytics/provider metadata, retention proposal 30 days for delivery diagnostics pending privacy review. Zero external send without consent proof. Security and financial notifications remain separate.
- **SEO/mobile/a11y:** All member, history, preview, admin and digest links `noindex,nofollow`, `Cache-Control: private,no-store`, excluded from sitemap; only a non-personal public help page may be indexed. Responsive >=320px, WCAG 2.2 AA, semantic switches, keyboard-only, accessible time-zone selector, error summary, `role=status` for confirmed saves/preview results, `role=alert` for errors, focus preserved; Android 13+ POST_NOTIFICATIONS permission is distinct from server consent and never inferred from it. Device permission denied => no push, in-app state remains available.
- **Analytics/KPIs:** Consent opt-in/out (aggregate), scheduled vs actually provider-accepted digests, duplicate-window count, suppression-after-revoke count, send-to-open rate, preview-to-opt-in, error/unknown rate, queue lag p95, per-member complaint rate; no raw event bodies or member IDs in analytics. Initial release goals: unauthorized digest access 0/100, sends after committed revocation 0/100 simulated races, duplicate dispatch 0/100 concurrent claims, false success receipts 0, missing consent sends 0, >99% correct window mapping in deterministic fixture; business engagement targets require baseline data and are not invented.
- **Test/QA and release/rollback:** RED/GREEN DB function tests for wrong-owner 100/100, 100 identical PUT retries and 100 conflicting concurrent edits, 100 parallel window claims produce one outbox intent, consent revoke vs dispatch 100 races, 10,001 same-timestamp notifications with cursor no duplicates/gaps, DST spring/fall and timezone changes, empty windows, provider timeout/unknown ack, Android permission denial, HTML injection, admin bulk denial, a11y and noindex. Exact-SHA CI/typecheck/lint, real PostgreSQL ACL negative tests, frontend/backend/app-api contract and authenticated Test E2E, migration 266 reconciliation, encrypted backup + isolated restore, runtime identity/image digest and rollback proof are mandatory. Feature flag `notification_digest` defaults OFF; staged canary only after gates; disable flag halts new optional dispatch without erasing settings, event receipts or mandatory security/transaction notices; rollback must preserve revoked consent and prevent old workers from sending. No direct Production deploy from planning.
- **Backlog:** P0 `NOTIF1184-01` owner-scoped verified source/consent boundary; P0 `SEC1184-02` app-role least privilege, DB function authorization; P0 `REL1184-03` latest-main CI/security/migration266/backup/restore/exact-SHA Test release blockers; P1 `NOTIF1184-04` versioned settings + idempotent API; P1 `NOTIF1184-05` deterministic windows, outbox and DST; P1 `NOTIF1184-06` member web + Android parity; P1 `NOTIF1184-07` admin RBAC, privacy, SEO, a11y, KPIs; P1 `NOTIF1184-08` concurrency, regression, canary and rollback. Dev A/B/C/QA/Gemini own separate implementation branches; planning owns docs only.
- **Evidence gate:** Independently reviewed publishers: OWASP API Top 10 (https://api-security.owasp.org/editions/2023/en/0x00-header/), W3C WCAG status (https://www.w3.org/WAI/WCAG22/Understanding/status-messages.html), Android notification permission (https://developer.android.com/develop/ui/compose/notifications/notification-permission), IETF RFC 5545 (https://datatracker.ietf.org/doc/html/rfc5545), PostgreSQL INSERT/ON CONFLICT (https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-insert.html): 5 distinct publisher organizations, NOT 5 accepted independent evidence records. Newly accepted/deduplicated evidence **0**; cumulative accepted **UNKNOWN**; target **10,000**; evidence gate **BLOCKED**. No false attainment claim.


## v2026.10.22.1140 — F675-92 Calculator Accuracy Reports & Verified Correction Notices (PLANNING / DRAFT)
- **Cycle:** Previous Feature Improvement v1138; Current Feature Addition v1140; Next Feature Improvement v1142 (provisional). Scope is documentation only. Baseline GitHub main `c3e62cd49da66c0b7c48223fcc81be888166f183`; local miniPC main lagged by one commit; open PRs #975/#977/#978/#981/#985; QA v1135 FAILED/HOLD (7 frontend CI failures, finance ACL/migration unresolved; 40/40 sampled ledger balances reconciled in development only). Never infer a Test/Production pass from this.
- **Purpose/personas:** Public calculator users need to flag questionable formula, tax-law year, assumptions or output; signed-in members can submit a reproducible, privacy-minimized issue; qualified editor triages, independent reviewer approves a correction, administrator publishes a verified notice. The system does NOT auto-correct historical financial results or award WLD. New feature F675-92 is independent of unmerged F675-91 saved-scenario drafts.
- **Source-observed risk:** `frontend/src/lib/capital-gains-tax-calculator.ts` subtracts `spouseGiftDeductionAmount` (default 600,000,000 KRW) directly from capital gain when a checkbox is selected, although the official NTS capital-gains computation flow does not establish such an unconditional gain deduction; legal validation is required before using the option. It also describes unexecuted loss harvesting as immediate savings and hardcodes a May 1–31 filing period, whereas NTS's 2026-02-03 notice says the 2025 overseas-stock final filing window is **2026-05-01 through 2026-06-01**. `calculator-save-action.tsx` and `calculator-retention-funnel.tsx` display successful saves, alerts and 10,000 WLD rewards without verified server receipts. These are **source and official-guidance discrepancies, not proof of actual member harm**.
- **P0 CAL1140-01:** Remove or suspend unsupported spouse-gift tax deduction and definitive 'tax saved', 'tax-free', and filing-date copy pending legal review. Display `ESTIMATE / ASSUMPTIONS UNVERIFIED` where authoritative rules cannot be established; pin country, tax year, law effective date and source revision. Compare with NTS flow (https://nts.go.kr/nts/cm/cntnts/cntntsView.do?cntntsId=7709&mi=2310), NTS 2026-02-03 filing notice (https://www.nts.go.kr/nts/na/ntt/selectNttInfo.do?mi=2307&nttSn=1348384), and Income Tax Act Art. 97-2 (https://www.law.go.kr/lsLinkCommonInfo.do?chrClsCd=010202&lsJoLnkSeq=1033246747). Reviewer must distinguish actual rule from tax-simulator simplification.
- **P0 CAL1140-02:** Restore least-privilege boundary: read-only development PostgreSQL shows `moneyverse_app` has SELECT/INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE on `support_threads`, `support_messages`, `in_app_notifications`, `outbox_events`, `privacy_data_subject_requests`, RLS off. Compare grant history and existing `SECURITY DEFINER` functions; fix only with reviewed forward-only migrations and DB-backed negative privilege tests, not ad hoc production GRANT/REVOKE.
- **P0 REL1140-03:** No release while financial ACL/migration-266 checksums, 7 CI failures, actual authenticated Test E2E, exact-SHA identity, backup/rollback and zero-downtime proofs remain unverified. Do not modify runtime, balances, deployment or feature switches from planning.
- **Member UX:** Entry on each canonical `/tools/*` result via 'Report a calculation issue' (only after signed-in session and consent); accessible 4-step flow: select `wrong_result|outdated_rule|missing_assumption|broken_ui`, show formula/version and country/year, redact preview, submit with stable idempotency UUID, receive ticket ID only after committed DB receipt. States `draft|submitting|submitted|unknown|rejected`; never fake success. Member view `/account/calculation-reports` displays own tickets; public `/tools/corrections` lists approved correction notices without private inputs; affected calculator pages display unobtrusive reviewed banner and source/effective date. Guest CTA points to public correction notices and optional sign-in, not silent data collection.
- **Admin UX:** `/admin/calculators/issues` queue, severity, duplicate group, calculator ID/formula version, evidence links, independent review and publish/withdraw actions. State machine `received -> triaged -> verifying -> approved -> published -> closed` plus `rejected` and `needs_information`; prohibit skipping review and same-actor approval for tax-sensitive correction. Expired review targets alert staff only; NEVER auto-unpublish existing content. Rollback means notice version/feature flag rollback, never silently rewriting a historical saved result.
- **API contract proposal:** `POST /api/v1/calculators/issues` authenticated+consent+CSRF with `idempotencyKey`, calculatorKey, formulaVersion, issueKind, redactedExplanation <=1000 chars, optional source URL (HTTPS allowlist). `GET /api/v1/calculators/issues?cursor=...` owner-only. `GET /api/v1/calculators/corrections?calculatorKey=...` public approved-only. `GET /api/v1/admin/calculators/issues` and `POST /api/v1/admin/calculators/issues/:id/decision` privileged admin session + CSRF + optimistic version. Errors: 400 invalid, 401/403 auth, 409 stale version/key payload mismatch, 413 oversize, 429 throttled, 503 unknown; no sensitive details in errors. Existing `/support/threads` and `in_app_notifications` should be reused where practical, not a parallel messaging platform. `/app-api/v1/*` mobile gateway must preserve session/CSRF and must not expose internal token.
- **DB proposal:** `calculator_issue_reports(id uuid PK, reporter_user_id uuid NOT NULL, calculator_key text, formula_version text, issue_kind text CHECK, redacted_description text CHECK length <=1000, idempotency_key uuid NOT NULL, request_hash bytea NOT NULL, status text CHECK, version integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 1, created_at timestamptz, updated_at timestamptz, UNIQUE(reporter_user_id,idempotency_key))`; index `(status,created_at,id)` for admin, `(reporter_user_id,created_at DESC,id DESC)` for owner. `calculator_correction_notices(id uuid PK, calculator_key, formula_version, jurisdiction, tax_year, source_url, verified_at, effective_at, approved_by, published_by, status, revision, published_at, UNIQUE(calculator_key,formula_version,revision))`; public reads only published non-private fields. `calculator_issue_events(id uuid PK, report_id FK, actor_id, event_type, prior_version, next_version, created_at)` append-only. No unreviewed attachments, PII-rich snapshots or raw financial positions. DB `SECURITY DEFINER` functions enforce actor ownership and roles; no direct DML grants to app.
- **Transaction/security:** One transaction locks issue row, checks expected version, inserts append-only event, creates deduplicated outbox notification only on real transition, and commits. `dedupe_key` unique in existing `in_app_notifications`; don't assume `outbox_events` has a logical unique event key without schema review. Rate-limit per user/IP, bot defense, sanitization, external URL SSRF allowlist, no stack traces, no analytics PII, no arbitrary screenshots, retention/erasure policy and audit-only access. OWASP error handling reference https://cheatsheetseries.owasp.org/cheatsheets/Error_Handling_Cheat_Sheet.html .
- **SEO, mobile, accessibility:** Canonical public correction index; owner/admin/private ticket URLs `noindex, no-store`, no sitemap exposure; correction banner links to a public reviewed notice only. Mobile 320px+, keyboard and screen-reader status `role=status`, labeled errors, WCAG 2.2 AA; Android app-api read parity and login/CSRF tests.
- **KPI and acceptance:** Report submit receipt rate >=99% in healthy Test, duplicate tickets <=1 per idempotency key, wrong-owner access 0/100, false 'resolved' banners 0, 95th percentile review target measured (not auto action), correction-notice read rate and re-open rate. TDD RED-GREEN tests for 100 identical retries, 100 concurrent admin transitions, 10,001-ticket stable cursor, two-reviewer separation, public PII exclusion, stale rule banner, correction withdrawal, mobile E2E, canonical/noindex, exact-SHA CI/Test, migration rollback and WLD ledger unchanged.
- **External reference gate:** This cycle reviewed NTS, National Law Information Center and OWASP (3 independent publisher domains). Approved, independently deduplicated evidence ledger **new 0; cumulative UNKNOWN / target 10,000; gate BLOCKED**. Reviewed references are NOT counted as accepted 10,000 evidence. Legal claims need dated primary-source human verification before publication.
- **Execution ownership:** Planning branch documentation only. Development A/B/C and QA own separate code branches, test-first exact-SHA gates and release promotion. Existing main docs are canonical; prior v1136/v1138 draft branches are not assumed merged.

## PostgreSQL Outbox retention optimization — v2026.10.14.622 (isolated candidate)

- Derived from the adopted v486 database architecture, not a replacement for v530 planning authority. PostgreSQL 17.11 DEV diagnosis: 957 MB DB, 213 MB Outbox, ~46,941 dead rows; expiry lookup scanned ~25,883 buffers in 36.158 ms.
- Forward-only migration 270 adds a concurrently built partial index (delivered_at, id) and per-table 5% autovacuum thresholds. Backend scheduler performs parameterized 2,000-row batches, up to 25 daily, retaining all undelivered records and the 7-day delivery retention rule.
- Isolated 260k-row synthetic Postgres Test passed SQL/index checks; backend build/typecheck and 1,165 non-DB tests passed; 391 DB-related tests skipped. This **does not** certify exact-SHA Test, Production readiness or release.
- Details: [English report](../findings/MONEYVERSE_DB_OPTIMIZATION_v2026.10.14.622.md) / [Korean](../findings/MONEYVERSE_DB_OPTIMIZATION_v2026.10.14.622.ko.md), [worklog](../worklog/2026-10-14-database-backend-optimization-v622.md).

## v2026.10.04.523 — 2026-10-04 — Central Bank / Mint / Treasury institutional separation
- Start `origin/main=c10e1582ccc0c058dff5c5356ad8b1893759f72c`; mid-work recheck detected `origin/main=065ee42204a4238c5010897212c7fc2a6c848f64`, which added treasury English/architecture documentation. The isolated branch was rebased onto that latest main before authority edits, preserving concurrent work.
- Re-read documentation governance, catalog, PROJECT_PLAN, integrated master, current treasury redistribution/fiscal specifications, AI Economy Controller, and monetary-velocity specification before integration.
- Added `CENTRAL_BANK_MINT_TREASURY_ECONOMY_CORE_SPEC.md` / `.ko.md` and adopted it into PROJECT_PLAN as current economic institutional authority.
- Separation contract: Central Bank owns monetary-policy approval; Mint is execution-only issuance/retirement; Central Treasury owns existing-WLD fiscal cash/tax/budget/expenditure; Economy Core/Settlement Ledger owns double-entry settlement, idempotency, reconciliation and supply invariants.
- Supply invariant: ordinary transfers, taxes, treasury expenditures, fully-funded loans and bond flows do not change total WLD. Only canonical mint/retire operations can change `M_total`.
- Existing treasury protected reserve is explicitly fiscal liquidity reserve, not authority to create money. Fiscal shortfall cannot auto-convert into currency issuance.
- Initial bank lending remains fully funded from existing WLD; commercial-bank deposit-money creation is deliberately excluded until a separate approved monetary-layer design exists.
- AI remains diagnostic/recommendation/bounded-auto for allowed low-risk keys only; direct mint/retire, monetary-order approval and fiscal-shortfall monetization are prohibited.
- Primary/first-party reference set includes IMF treasury-central-bank/TSA guidance, ECB issuance/production, Federal Reserve/BEP and U.S. Mint separation, Bank of Korea, Bank of England money-creation material and EVE first-party economic reporting.
- Planning/docs only. No runtime, DB, Test or Production implementation or promotion is claimed.

## v2026.09.25.437 — 2026-09-25
- Incident evidence from the Debian 13 Production VM showed PostgreSQL entering uninterruptible `D` state with repeated `kvm_async_pf_task_wait_schedule` stacks from 03:31 KST, increasing from 120s to 1,087s blocked time; the prior boot then ended without a clean shutdown and the 10:46 boot recovered system journal, the Moneyverse data filesystem journal, and PostgreSQL WAL.
- **G437-01 / P0 (Hypervisor memory continuity):** Production VM memory must not depend on aggressive host overcommit/balloon reclamation. Define and monitor a minimum guaranteed guest RAM floor, host reserve, balloon floor, swap/PSI thresholds, and forbid automatic balloon-down below the measured steady-state safety envelope.
- **G437-02 / P0 (KVM async-PF/hung-task detection):** detect `kvm_async_pf`, hung task, guest scheduling stalls, QEMU pause/reset, host OOM, storage latency and guest-agent loss at the virtualization host as well as inside the guest. A local application `/health` alone is not sufficient.
- **G437-03 / P0 (External watchdog and recovery):** an out-of-guest watchdog must probe public edge, backend, DB transaction health and guest heartbeat. On sustained VM-level failure it escalates through alert -> evidence capture -> controlled restart/failover according to a cooldown and fencing policy; it must never reboot merely because one application endpoint fails.
- **G437-04 / P0 (Database crash safety):** PostgreSQL remains on durable storage with `fsync`/WAL crash recovery intact, backups and restore evidence current, and restart automation waits for filesystem and database recovery before accepting application traffic. No auto-healer may repeatedly restart DB-dependent services while the DB is in recovery or D-state.
- **G437-05 / P1 (Host observability/evidence):** retain and correlate Proxmox/QEMU task logs, host kernel/OOM/PSI/I/O metrics, VM guest journal, PostgreSQL logs, Nginx/API availability and release identity across incidents. Preserve pre-crash evidence before automated remediation where possible.
- **G437-06 / P1 (Capacity gate):** Production/Test/AI workloads require explicit CPU/RAM/storage-I/O budgets and concurrency ceilings. Heavy local inference, builds, backups and QA must be serialized or resource-limited when they could contend with Production.
- **Acceptance gate:** Test fault-injection must cover memory pressure, guest pause/stall, database crash recovery and host/guest health disagreement; verify no ledger corruption, session continuity where the DB/session authority survives, deterministic recovery ordering, bounded restart loops and external alerting. Production promotion is blocked when host-level observability or watchdog ownership is absent.
- Start baseline `origin/main=4a4549f644972af972c47fb8f56bd9500766aa61`. Detailed authority: `docs/planning/INFRASTRUCTURE_STALL_RESILIENCE_SPEC.md` / `.ko.md` and delta `docs/planning/deltas/v2026.09.25.437.md` / `.ko.md`. Planning/docs only; no runtime mitigation, Test fault-injection or Production change is claimed.

- Revalidated the existing v400/v401 economy research authority without re-counting mirrors, translations, tracking-URL variants, or already-adopted standards. The 31,289-candidate deduplicated discovery corpus remains the broad base; v433 adds quality/provenance mapping rather than an inflated corpus claim.
- Rechecked current primary/normative evidence from the EVE Online August 2026 MER, OSRS market-intervention research, Federal Reserve settlement/liquidity research, PostgreSQL transaction isolation/locking, OWASP API Security, NIST SSDF, WCAG 2.2, OpenTelemetry semantic conventions, GitHub deployment environments, Debian 13.7 release information, and Apple platform policy.
- **G433-01 / P0 (Work/Economy):** paid work requires a server-authoritative duration or independently verifiable completion boundary; client click-to-instant-spendable-WLD is prohibited.
- **G433-02 / P0 (Ledger/Concurrency):** every balance mutation requires a durable atomic transaction, unique business idempotency key, and invariant-preserving concurrency control with bounded whole-transaction retry.
- **G433-03 / P1 (Economy):** automatic controls use a bounded multi-metric bundle—issuance, sinks, money supply, velocity, price indices, purchasing power, concentration and volume—not a single faucet/sink ratio.
- **G433-04 / P1 (Market policy):** taxes/item sinks require causal and distributional checks because intervention can raise luxury prices without reducing trade volume.
- **G433-05 / P1 (Banking/Treasury):** settlement speed is treated as a liquidity/risk parameter; faster is not assumed universally safer.
- **G433-06 / P1 (Observability):** economic policy versions, issuance/sinks, velocity, taxes, treasury flows, duplicate blocks, retries and rollbacks require correlated metrics/traces/logs.
- **G433-07 / P1 (Accessibility correction):** WCAG 2.2 SC 2.5.8 AA uses a 24x24 CSS-pixel minimum subject to defined exceptions; Moneyverse keeps 44x44 as a stronger product target, not as the WCAG normative floor.
- **G433-08..10 / P1:** protected serialized Test/Production deployment environments, Debian 13.7 freshness verification without rewriting observed 13.6 runtime absent host evidence, and mobile virtual-currency/store-policy boundaries.
- Start and mid-work main recheck: `e15a6fdd941a8f78a1a27755f148072c738eddc1` (no drift at the recorded mid-work checkpoint).
- Authoritative detail: `docs/planning/deltas/v2026.09.24.433.md` / `.ko.md`. Planning/docs only; no runtime, Test, or Production completion claim.

## v2026.09.23.406 — 2026-09-23
- Conducted exhaustive cross-reference research across academic papers, fintech architectures, and virtual economy mechanisms to formalize mitigation specs for 6 core planning domains and 8 critical gaps.
- **Key References**: Aave/Compound Kinked Jump Rate Model, LOB WebSocket Monotonic Sequence Gap Recovery (Binance/Coinbase), OSRS Grand Exchange 2% Tax & Automated Item Sink, RFC 6455 / Signal Protocol Idempotency & Cursor Pagination, CNN Multi-Factor Market Sentiment, OWASP ASVS 5.0 / NIST SP 800-63B-4 Dual-Key Rotation, Apple HIG 44px Minimum Touch Target.
- **G406-01 / P0 (Stocks)**: Monotonic sequence ID & snapshot + buffered delta resync contract for tick loss prevention.
- **G406-02 / P1 (Stocks)**: KRX standard 7-tier discrete tick size table spanning 1 WLD to 10M WLD.
- **G406-03 / P1 (Stocks)**: Multi-factor market sentiment index combining AI news (40%), price momentum (30%), volume surge (20%), and order pressure (10%).
- **G406-04 / P0 (Economy)**: 2% market transaction tax and Treasury-backed automated floor-price item buyback & permanent destruction (GE Item Sink).
- **G406-05 / P0 (Social)**: 1-on-1 direct messaging client-side UUID idempotency key `(conversation_id, client_message_id)` and mandatory cursor-based pagination.
- **G406-06 / P0 (Banking)**: Aave-style $U_{\text{optimal}}=80\%$ kinked interest rate curve and Basel III 20% statutory reserve buffer.
- **G406-07 / P1 (Auth)**: 7-day grace period dual-key overlap rotation ensuring zero session invalidation.
- **G406-08 / P1 (UX)**: 44px minimum touch target and `pb-[calc(env(safe-area-inset-bottom)+5rem)]` anti-clipping responsive layout contract.
- Authoritative spec: `docs/planning/deltas/v2026.09.23.406.ko.md` / `v2026.09.23.406.md`. English/Korean synced.

## v2026.09.30.487 — Advertising-only cash monetization
- Start/mid-work `origin/main=85508db432cd525570e26bc1820b9e637a8140fa`; no drift at the recorded checkpoint.
- User business-registration constraint: direct product sales are not permitted under the current guidance.
- **Current cash-revenue authority:** reviewed advertising only. Paid subscriptions/ad removal, digital goods/cosmetics, paid WLD/WDX, paid random items/casino value, user-paid marketplace fees, donations/memberships, paid API/B2B, affiliate/direct-sale revenue are BLOCKED until a future business-scope/tax/legal authority change explicitly re-opens them.
- **KRW 1,000,000/month ad target:** use observed Page RPM, not industry promises. `PV = 1,000,000 / PageRPM × 1,000`; scenario points are 500k PV at KRW 2k RPM, 200k at KRW 5k, 100k at KRW 10k, and 50k at KRW 20k.
- Growth authority: qualified human traffic, Search Console/index truth, audience-first calculators/guides, correct locale structure, relevant internal links and measured AdSense experiments. Self-clicks, click encouragement, incentivized ad viewing, traffic exchanges, bot impressions and low-quality purchased traffic are prohibited.
- New detailed authority: `AD_ONLY_ADVERTISING_REVENUE_SPEC.md`; PROJECT_PLAN and monetization/KR-compliance specs were updated with superseding v487 gates.
- Official evidence refresh used Google AdSense Page RPM/Auto Ads/Experiments/invalid-traffic/publisher-policy documentation and NTS platform-ad-income tax guidance. NTS one-person-media guidance is not treated as automatic classification of this website.
- Planning/docs only; no runtime, billing, AdSense-setting, Test, Production, tax-classification or revenue-result claim.
